"""Adaptive engine: per-chapter Elo/IRT ability with subject-level priors.

Model:   P(correct) = 1 / (1 + e^-(theta - b))   (1-parameter IRT / Elo)
Update:  theta += K * w * (score - P), where w comes from the response pattern
         (confidence, time taken, hints used) and K shrinks as evidence grows.
Prior:   an unseen chapter starts at its subject's ability, and every answer
         nudges the subject, so the model has an estimate for all chapters.
Policy:  pick the chapter with the best weakness + exploration + misconception
         score, then the question whose P(correct) is closest to TARGET_P.
"""
import math
import random

K_MIN = 0.4  # floor keeps tracking a student who is learning mid-session
TARGET_P = 0.6  # "desirable difficulty": right ~60% of the time (tuned via simulate)
# difficulty the student must handle on exam day; school classes default to 0 (class level)
EXAM_TARGET_B = {"JEE": 1.5, "JEEMAIN": 1.0, "NEET": 0.5, "Class 11": 0.5, "Class 12": 0.5}
GUESS_SECS, FAST_SECS, SLOW_SECS = 3, 25, 150


def p_correct(theta, b):
    return 1 / (1 + math.exp(-(theta - b)))


def level(b):
    return "Easy" if b < -0.5 else "Medium" if b < 1 else "Advanced"


def evidence(correct, secs, confidence, hints):
    """Turn a response pattern into (score, weight, misconception) for the update."""
    if secs < GUESS_SECS:
        confidence = "guess"  # too fast to have solved it
    score = max(0.0, 1 - 0.3 * hints) if correct else 0.0
    weight = {"sure": 1.2, "unsure": 1.0, "guess": 0.5}.get(confidence, 1.0)
    if correct and secs > SLOW_SECS:
        weight *= 0.7  # right but laboured: weaker evidence of mastery
    if correct and secs < FAST_SECS and confidence == "sure":
        weight *= 1.2  # fluent recall
    misconception = not correct and confidence == "sure"
    if misconception:
        weight *= 1.3  # confidently wrong is the strongest signal of a gap
    return score, weight, misconception


class Learner:
    def __init__(self, chapters):
        self.subject_of = chapters  # {chapter: subject}
        self.theta, self.n, self.flags = {}, {}, {}
        self.subj = {}
        self.history = []

    def ability(self, ch):
        return self.theta.get(ch, self.subj.get(self.subject_of[ch], 0.0))

    def readiness(self, ch, exam):
        return p_correct(self.ability(ch), EXAM_TARGET_B.get(exam, 0.0))

    def update(self, ch, b, correct, secs=30, confidence="unsure", hints=0):
        subject, theta = self.subject_of[ch], self.ability(ch)
        p = p_correct(theta, b)
        score, weight, misconception = evidence(correct, secs, confidence, hints)
        k = max(K_MIN, 0.8 / math.sqrt(1 + self.n.get(ch, 0)))
        delta = k * weight * (score - p)
        self.theta[ch] = theta + delta
        self.n[ch] = self.n.get(ch, 0) + 1
        self.subj[subject] = self.subj.get(subject, 0.0) + 0.3 * delta  # leaks to unseen chapters
        if misconception:
            self.flags[ch] = self.flags.get(ch, 0) + 1
        entry = dict(chapter=ch, subject=subject, b=b, correct=correct, p=p, score=score,
                     theta=self.theta[ch], before=theta, delta=delta, secs=round(secs, 1),
                     confidence=confidence, hints=hints, misconception=misconception)
        self.history.append(entry)
        return entry


def rank_chapters(learner, chapters, exam, recent=()):
    """Score every candidate chapter; highest first. Each row explains itself."""
    rows = []
    for ch in chapters:
        n = learner.n.get(ch, 0)
        parts = {
            "weakness": 1 - learner.readiness(ch, exam),
            "explore": 0.25 / math.sqrt(1 + n),
            "misconception": 0.25 * min(learner.flags.get(ch, 0), 2),
            "repeat_penalty": -0.6 if ch in list(recent)[-2:] else 0.0,
        }
        score = sum(parts.values()) + random.random() * 0.03  # tie-break
        rows.append(dict(chapter=ch, score=score, n=n, **parts))
    return sorted(rows, key=lambda r: r["score"], reverse=True)


def target_b(learner, ch, easier=False):
    # P(correct) = TARGET_P  <=>  b = theta - logit(TARGET_P)
    return learner.ability(ch) - math.log(TARGET_P / (1 - TARGET_P)) - (0.6 if easier else 0.0)


def pick_item(items, tb, seen):
    fresh = [q for q in items if q["id"] not in seen]
    return min(fresh, key=lambda q: abs(q["b"] - tb)) if fresh else None


# ---------- why the student is wrong, not just that they are wrong ----------

ERROR_TYPES = {
    "concept_gap": ("Concept gap", "You don't have this idea yet. Read the explanation; next is an easier question on the same concept."),
    "careless": ("Careless slip", "You know this one: your level says you should get it. Slow down and re-read before locking in."),
    "time_pressure": ("Time pressure", "Ran out of time. Practise speed: aim to decide on this type in under a minute."),
    "trap": ("Fell for a trap option", "You were sure, but picked a tempting distractor. Before answering, ask what the trap option assumes."),
}


def diagnose(p, secs, confidence, switched_from_correct=False, timed_out=False, time_frac=0.0):
    """Classify a wrong answer from the response pattern.
    p = model's P(correct) before answering; time_frac = share of the time limit used (timed mode)."""
    if timed_out or time_frac > 0.85:
        return "time_pressure"
    rushed = GUESS_SECS <= secs < FAST_SECS * 0.6 and confidence != "guess"  # under 3 s is a guess, not a slip
    if switched_from_correct or (p >= 0.65 and rushed):
        return "careless"  # had it right, or should have known it and rushed
    if confidence == "sure":
        return "trap"  # confidently chose a wrong option: the distractor did its job
    return "concept_gap"


# ---------- behaviour: fatigue and frustration over the session ----------

def wellbeing(history, skips=0, window=6):
    """'ok' | 'frustrated' | 'tired', with the signals that triggered it."""
    recent = history[-window:]
    if len(recent) >= 3 and not any(h["correct"] for h in recent[-3:]):
        return "frustrated", ["3 wrong answers in a row"]
    if len(history) < 2 * window:
        return "ok", []
    early, late = history[:-window], recent
    acc = lambda hs: sum(h["correct"] for h in hs) / len(hs)
    secs = lambda hs: sum(h["secs"] for h in hs) / len(hs)
    reasons = []
    if acc(early) - acc(late) >= 0.3:
        reasons.append(f"accuracy fell from {acc(early):.0%} to {acc(late):.0%}")
    if secs(late) > 1.5 * secs(early) and secs(late) > FAST_SECS:
        reasons.append(f"answers slowed from {secs(early):.0f}s to {secs(late):.0f}s")
    if skips >= 2:
        reasons.append(f"{skips} recent skips")
    if sum(h.get("switches", 0) >= 2 for h in late) >= 3:
        reasons.append("lots of answer switching")
    return ("tired", reasons) if len(reasons) >= 2 else ("ok", reasons)


# ---------- exam strategy: marking scheme, calibration, predicted score ----------

# (+marks for right, -marks for wrong) per single-correct MCQ
MARKING = {"JEE": (3, 1), "JEEMAIN": (4, 1), "NEET": (4, 1)}
CONF_LEVELS = ("sure", "unsure", "guess")


def expected_marks(p, exam):
    plus, minus = MARKING.get(exam, (1, 0))
    return p * plus - (1 - p) * minus


def calibration(history, exam):
    """Accuracy per confidence level, the expected marks of answering at that level, and advice."""
    out = []
    for c in CONF_LEVELS:
        hs = [h for h in history if h["confidence"] == c]
        if not hs:
            continue
        acc = sum(h["correct"] for h in hs) / len(hs)
        ev = expected_marks(acc, exam)
        advice = ("Attempt: positive expected marks." if ev > 0 else
                  "Skip these in the exam: guessing loses marks." if exam in MARKING else "No negative marking: always attempt.")
        out.append(dict(confidence=c, n=len(hs), accuracy=acc, expected=ev, advice=advice))
    return out


# ponytail: a paper is modelled as an even mix of 4 difficulty bands around the exam target;
# replace with real per-chapter weights and difficulty mix from past papers if available
PAPER_BANDS = (-2.0, -1.0, 0.0, 1.0)


def _chapter_marks(theta, exam):
    """(expected marks answering every question, answering only positive-EV ones) per question."""
    target = EXAM_TARGET_B.get(exam, 0.0)
    evs = [expected_marks(p_correct(theta, target + d), exam) for d in PAPER_BANDS]
    return sum(evs) / len(evs), sum(max(e, 0) for e in evs) / len(evs)


def predicted_score(learner, chapters, exam):
    """Expected % of max marks on a mixed-difficulty paper: answering everything vs skipping
    questions with negative expected value (the strategy gain)."""
    plus = MARKING.get(exam, (1, 0))[0]
    marks = [_chapter_marks(learner.ability(c), exam) for c in chapters]
    all_in = max(sum(a for a, _ in marks) / len(marks) / plus, 0)
    smart = sum(s for _, s in marks) / len(marks) / plus
    return dict(attempt_all=all_in, with_strategy=smart, strategy_gain=smart - all_in)


def best_gains(learner, chapters, exam, step=0.5, k=5):
    """Chapters where +step ability adds the most expected marks: where study time pays most."""
    rows = []
    for c in chapters:
        th = learner.ability(c)
        gain = _chapter_marks(th + step, exam)[1] - _chapter_marks(th, exam)[1]
        rows.append(dict(chapter=c, gain=gain, readiness=learner.readiness(c, exam)))
    return sorted(rows, key=lambda r: -r["gain"])[:k]


def daily_streak(active_days, today):
    """Consecutive ordinal days ending today or yesterday; all dates are supplied."""
    active = set(active_days)
    day = today if today in active else today - 1
    count = 0
    while day in active:
        count += 1
        day -= 1
    return count


def plan_day(learner, chapters, exam, due_reviews=0, goal=20, days_left=None):
    """A small study suggestion, never a promise of learning or an automatic quiz."""
    goal = max(1, min(200, int(goal)))
    due_reviews = max(0, int(due_reviews))
    remaining = None if days_left is None else max(0, int(days_left))
    gains = best_gains(learner, chapters, exam, k=1) if chapters else []
    chapter = gains[0]['chapter'] if gains else None
    # Near the exam, shorten new practice and leave more room for due revision.
    study = min(40, max(10, goal)) if chapter else 0
    if remaining is not None and remaining <= 1:
        study = min(study, 10)
    review = min(20, max(5, due_reviews * 2)) if due_reviews else 0
    return dict(chapter=chapter, study_minutes=study, review_minutes=review,
                due_reviews=due_reviews, goal=goal, days_left=remaining)


# ---------- simulation: adaptive vs static on synthetic students ----------

PROFILES = {
    "Uneven (2 strong, 4 weak)": [1.6, 1.3, -1.0, -1.5, -0.5, -2.0],
    "Weak all-round": [-1.5, -1.8, -1.2, -2.0, -1.6, -1.4],
    "Nearly exam-ready": [1.2, 0.8, 1.5, 0.5, 1.9, 0.9],
}


def _student_answers(rng, true_theta, ch, b):
    correct = rng.random() < p_correct(true_theta[ch], b)
    # ponytail: learning gain peaks when b matches true ability (zone of proximal development)
    true_theta[ch] += 0.15 * math.exp(-((b - true_theta[ch]) ** 2) / 2)
    return correct


def simulate(policy, start, exam="JEE", seed=0, max_q=800):
    """Questions until every chapter's TRUE readiness reaches 0.7."""
    rng = random.Random(seed)
    random.seed(seed)
    chapters = {f"Ch{i + 1}": "Sim" for i in range(len(start))}
    true_theta = dict(zip(chapters, start))
    goal = EXAM_TARGET_B.get(exam, 0.0) + math.log(0.7 / 0.3)
    learner, recent = Learner(chapters), []
    for q in range(1, max_q + 1):
        if policy == "adaptive":
            ch = rank_chapters(learner, chapters, exam, recent)[0]["chapter"]
            b = target_b(learner, ch) + rng.gauss(0, 0.3)
        else:  # static bank: fixed chapter order, difficulty spread across the bank
            ch = list(chapters)[q % len(chapters)]
            b = rng.uniform(-2, 3)
        correct = _student_answers(rng, true_theta, ch, b)
        learner.update(ch, b, correct)
        recent.append(ch)
        if all(t >= goal for t in true_theta.values()):
            return q
    return max_q


def compare(runs=20, exam="JEE"):
    out = []
    for name, start in PROFILES.items():
        a = sum(simulate("adaptive", start, exam, s) for s in range(runs)) / runs
        st = sum(simulate("static", start, exam, s) for s in range(runs)) / runs
        out.append(dict(profile=name, adaptive=round(a), static=round(st), saved=round(100 * (1 - a / st))))
    return out


# Spaced review of missed questions: a miss comes back after 1 day, then 3, then 7; three right in a row = mastered.
REVIEW_DAYS = (1, 3, 7)


def next_review(stage, correct):
    """(new stage, days until the next review), or (stage, None) once mastered. A miss restarts the ladder."""
    if not correct:
        return 0, REVIEW_DAYS[0]
    stage += 1
    return (stage, REVIEW_DAYS[stage]) if stage < len(REVIEW_DAYS) else (stage, None)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    assert daily_streak([8, 9, 10], 10) == 3
    assert daily_streak([8, 9, 10], 11) == 3
    assert daily_streak([8, 9, 10], 12) == 0
    assert daily_streak([10, 10], 10) == 1
    planning_learner = Learner({'A':'S', 'B':'S'})
    assert plan_day(planning_learner, ['A','B'], 'JEE')['chapter'] in ('A','B')
    assert plan_day(planning_learner, [], 'JEE', 2)['study_minutes'] == 0
    assert plan_day(planning_learner, ['A'], 'JEE', 2, 20, -1)['days_left'] == 0
    assert plan_day(planning_learner, ['A'], 'JEE', 2, 20, 0)['study_minutes'] == 10
    assert plan_day(planning_learner, ['A'], 'JEE', 0)['review_minutes'] == 0
    assert next_review(0, True) == (1, 3) and next_review(1, True) == (2, 7)
    assert next_review(2, True)[1] is None, "three right reviews in a row means mastered"
    assert next_review(2, False) == (0, 1), "a missed review starts the ladder again"
    L = Learner({"A": "S", "B": "S", "C": "S"})
    t0 = L.ability("A")
    L.update("A", 0.0, True, 20, "sure")
    assert L.ability("A") > t0, "correct answer must raise ability"
    assert L.ability("B") > t0, "subject prior must lift unseen chapters"
    t1 = L.ability("A")
    L.update("A", 0.0, False, 20, "sure")
    assert L.ability("A") < t1 and L.flags["A"] == 1, "confident wrong must lower ability and flag"
    g = Learner({"X": "S"}); g.update("X", 0.0, False, 1, "sure")
    s = Learner({"X": "S"}); s.update("X", 0.0, False, 30, "sure")
    assert g.ability("X") > s.ability("X"), "1-second answer is a guess, weaker penalty"
    for _ in range(3):
        L.update("C", 0.0, False, 30, "sure")
    assert rank_chapters(L, ["A", "B", "C"], "JEE")[0]["chapter"] == "C", "weakest chapter first"
    assert diagnose(0.5, 40, "unsure", timed_out=True) == "time_pressure"
    assert diagnose(0.8, 5, "unsure") == "careless"
    assert diagnose(0.3, 40, "unsure", switched_from_correct=True) == "careless"
    assert diagnose(0.3, 40, "sure") == "trap"
    assert diagnose(0.3, 40, "guess") == "concept_gap"
    h = lambda ok, s=20: dict(correct=ok, secs=s, confidence="unsure")
    assert wellbeing([h(1), h(0), h(0), h(0)])[0] == "frustrated"
    assert wellbeing([h(1, 15)] * 6 + [h(0, 60), h(1, 60)] * 3)[0] == "tired"
    assert wellbeing([h(1)] * 12)[0] == "ok"
    cal = calibration([dict(correct=False, confidence="guess")] * 5 + [dict(correct=True, confidence="guess")], "NEET")
    assert cal[0]["expected"] < 0 and "Skip" in cal[0]["advice"], "17% guesses lose marks at +4/-1 (break-even 20%)"
    ps = predicted_score(Learner({"A": "S", "B": "S"}), ["A", "B"], "JEE")
    assert ps["with_strategy"] >= ps["attempt_all"], "skipping negative-EV questions never hurts"
    res = compare(runs=10)
    assert all(r["adaptive"] < r["static"] for r in res), res
    print("engine ok", res)

================================================================================
END OF FILE: preppilot/engine.py

================================================================================
FILE: preppilot/papers.py
LINES: 344 | BYTES: 15999

"""Your own question papers: import from a Google Sheet (or a CSV file) and quiz on them adaptively.

Sheet layout (first row = headers, any order, case-insensitive):
  question | option_a | option_b | option_c | option_d | answer | chapter | subject | difficulty | explanation | hint
Required: question, option_a..option_d, answer (A-D, 1-4, or the exact option text).
Optional: chapter (default "General"), subject (default "My paper"), difficulty (easy/medium/hard or -2..2),
explanation, hint.
"""
import csv
import io
import json
import re
import secrets
import time
import urllib.request
from pathlib import Path

DIR = Path(__file__).parent / "papers"
MAX_ROWS, MAX_CELL, MAX_BYTES = 500, 2000, 2_000_000
DIFFICULTY = {"easy": -1.0, "medium": 0.0, "hard": 1.0}
ALIASES = {
    "question": ["question", "q", "questions"],
    "a": ["option_a", "option a", "a", "option1", "option 1"],
    "b": ["option_b", "option b", "b", "option2", "option 2"],
    "c": ["option_c", "option c", "c", "option3", "option 3"],
    "d": ["option_d", "option d", "d", "option4", "option 4"],
    "answer": ["answer", "correct", "correct answer", "key"],
    "chapter": ["chapter", "topic"],
    "subject": ["subject"],
    "difficulty": ["difficulty", "level"],
    "explanation": ["explanation", "solution"],
    "hint": ["hint"],
}
TEMPLATE = ("question,option_a,option_b,option_c,option_d,answer,chapter,subject,difficulty,explanation,hint\n"
            "\"What is $2 + 3$?\",4,5,6,7,B,Arithmetic,Maths,easy,\"$2 + 3 = 5$\",Count on from 2\n"
            "\"Unit of force?\",Joule,Watt,Newton,Pascal,C,Laws of Motion,Physics,medium,\"Force is measured in newtons (N).\",\"Think F = ma\"\n")

PAPERS = {}  # paper id -> paper dict


class PaperError(ValueError):
    pass


def sheet_csv_url(link):
    """Turn a Google Sheets link into its CSV export URL. Only docs.google.com is ever fetched."""
    link = link.strip()
    gid = re.search(r"[#?&]gid=(\d+)", link)
    gid = f"&gid={gid.group(1)}" if gid else ""
    published = re.fullmatch(r"https://docs\.google\.com/spreadsheets/d/e/([\w-]{20,})/.*", link)
    if published:
        return f"https://docs.google.com/spreadsheets/d/e/{published.group(1)}/pub?output=csv{gid}"
    m = re.fullmatch(r"https://docs\.google\.com/spreadsheets/d/([\w-]{20,})(/.*)?", link)
    if not m:
        raise PaperError("That doesn't look like a Google Sheets link (https://docs.google.com/spreadsheets/d/...).")
    return f"https://docs.google.com/spreadsheets/d/{m.group(1)}/export?format=csv{gid}"


def fetch_sheet(link):
    req = urllib.request.Request(sheet_csv_url(link), headers={"User-Agent": "PrepPilot"})
    try:
        with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=20) as r:
            kind = r.headers.get("Content-Type", "")
            data = r.read(MAX_BYTES + 1)
    except Exception as e:
        raise PaperError("Couldn't open the sheet. Set sharing to \"Anyone with the link: Viewer\" and try again.") from e
    if "html" in kind:  # Google returns a sign-in page for private sheets
        raise PaperError("The sheet is private. In Google Sheets: Share → General access → \"Anyone with the link\" (Viewer).")
    if len(data) > MAX_BYTES:
        raise PaperError("The sheet is too large (limit 2 MB).")
    return data.decode("utf-8-sig", errors="replace")


def _answer_index(raw, options):
    raw = raw.strip()
    bare = re.fullmatch(r"(?:option\s*)?[(\[]?\s*([A-Da-d1-4])\s*[)\].]?", raw, re.I)  # "(2)", "B)", "option c"
    if bare:
        raw = bare.group(1)
    if raw.upper() in ("A", "B", "C", "D"):
        return "ABCD".index(raw.upper())
    if raw in ("1", "2", "3", "4"):
        return int(raw) - 1
    matches = [i for i, o in enumerate(options) if o.strip().lower() == raw.lower()]
    return matches[0] if len(matches) == 1 else None


def _difficulty(raw):
    raw = raw.strip().lower()
    if not raw:
        return 0.0
    if raw in DIFFICULTY:
        return DIFFICULTY[raw]
    try:
        return max(-2.5, min(2.5, float(raw)))
    except ValueError:
        return None


def _csv_records(text):
    """CSV/Sheet with a header row -> [(label, fields)], or None if this isn't such a table."""
    rows = list(csv.reader(io.StringIO(text)))
    if not rows:
        return None
    header = [h.strip().lower() for h in rows[0]]
    col = {field: next((header.index(n) for n in names if n in header), None) for field, names in ALIASES.items()}
    if col["question"] is None:
        return None  # not a table: treat as a pasted question paper
    missing = [f for f in ("a", "b", "c", "d", "answer") if col[f] is None]
    if missing:
        nice = {"a": "option_a", "b": "option_b", "c": "option_c", "d": "option_d"}
        raise PaperError("Missing columns: " + ", ".join(nice.get(m, m) for m in missing) +
                         ". Use the template's first row as your header.")
    cell = lambda r, f: (r[col[f]].strip() if col[f] is not None and col[f] < len(r) else "")
    return [(f"Row {n}", {f: cell(r, f) for f in ALIASES}) for n, r in enumerate(rows[1:], start=2)
            if any(c.strip() for c in r)]


_QNUM = re.compile(r"^\s*(?:q(?:uestion)?\s*)?(\d+)\s*[.):\-]\s*(.*)$", re.I)
_KEY_HEADING = re.compile(r"^\s*(?:answer\s*key|answers|key)\s*[:\-]?\s*(.*)$", re.I)
_KEY_PAIR = re.compile(r"(\d+)\s*[.):\-–]?\s*\(?([A-Da-d1-4])\)?(?![\w])")
_OPT = re.compile(r"^\s*(?:\(?([a-d])[).:\]]|\(([1-4])\))\s*(.+)$", re.I)  # A) a. (b) or (1)-(4)
_DIGIT = {"1": "a", "2": "b", "3": "c", "4": "d"}
_MARK = re.compile(r"(?:^|(?<=\s))(?:\(?([a-dA-D])[).]|\(([1-4])\))\s+")


def _split_inline(line):
    """'Unit of force? (a) Joule (b) Watt (c) Newton (d) Pascal' -> ('Unit of force?', {a..d}).
    Only when markers run a, b, c... in order, so a stray 'a)' inside a question isn't split."""
    marks = list(_MARK.finditer(line))
    letters = [(m.group(1) or _DIGIT[m.group(2)]).lower() for m in marks]
    for start in range(len(marks)):
        run = letters[start:start + 4]
        if len(run) >= 2 and run == list("abcd"[:len(run)]):
            ms = marks[start:start + len(run)]
            opts = {letters[start + i]: line[m.end():(ms[i + 1].start() if i + 1 < len(ms) else len(line))].strip()
                    for i, m in enumerate(ms)}
            return line[:ms[0].start()].strip(), opts
    return line, {}
_FIELD = re.compile(r"^\s*(answer|ans|correct answer|correct|key|chapter|topic|subject|difficulty|level|"
                    r"explanation|solution|hint)\s*[:\-]\s*(.*)$", re.I)
_FIELD_NAME = {"ans": "answer", "correct answer": "answer", "correct": "answer", "key": "answer",
               "topic": "chapter", "level": "difficulty", "solution": "explanation"}


def _text_records(text):
    """A question paper pasted as plain text, the way people write one:

        1. What is 2 + 3?
        A) 4   B) 5   C) 6   D) 7
        Answer: B
        Chapter: Arithmetic          (optional; also explanation/hint/difficulty/subject)

    A 'Chapter:' or 'Subject:' line before any question applies to the questions that follow.
    An 'Answer key' section at the end ("1-B 2-C 3. (4)") fills in answers by question number,
    which is how most printed/PDF papers are laid out."""
    records, cur, defaults, key, in_key = [], None, {}, {}, False

    def finish():
        # text with no options and no answer is a title or instructions ("Each question carries 4 marks")
        if cur and (any(cur[k] for k in "abcd") or cur["answer"]):
            records.append((f"Question {cur['num'] or len(records) + 1}",
                            {**defaults, **{k: v for k, v in cur.items() if v}}))

    def new(question="", num=""):
        return {"question": question, "a": "", "b": "", "c": "", "d": "", "answer": "", "num": num}

    for line in text.replace("\r", "").split("\n"):
        if not line.strip():
            continue
        heading = _KEY_HEADING.match(line)
        if heading:
            finish()
            cur, in_key = None, True
            line = heading.group(1)
        if in_key:  # answer-key section: every "number → letter" pair on the line
            for num, ans in _KEY_PAIR.findall(line):
                key[str(int(num))] = ans
            continue
        field = _FIELD.match(line)
        if field:
            name = _FIELD_NAME.get(field.group(1).lower(), field.group(1).lower())
            if cur is None or (not cur["question"] and name in ("chapter", "subject")):
                defaults[name] = field.group(2).strip()  # section heading for the questions below
            else:
                cur[name] = field.group(2).strip()
            continue
        before, inline = _split_inline(line)
        if inline and cur is not None and not before:  # a line of options: "A) 4  B) 5  C) 6  D) 7"
            cur.update(inline)
            continue
        opt = _OPT.match(line)
        if opt and not inline and cur is not None and cur["question"]:
            cur[(opt.group(1) or _DIGIT[opt.group(2)]).lower()] = opt.group(3).strip()
            continue
        numbered = _QNUM.match(before)
        text_part = (numbered.group(2) if numbered else before).strip()
        if numbered or cur is None or any(cur[k] for k in "abcd") or cur["answer"]:
            finish()  # a new question starts after the previous one's options/answer
            cur = new(text_part, str(int(numbered.group(1))) if numbered else "")
        else:
            cur["question"] = (cur["question"] + " " + text_part).strip()  # question spans lines
        cur.update(inline)  # options written on the same line as the question
    finish()
    for _, r in records:  # answers from the key, only where the question didn't give its own
        if not r.get("answer") and r.get("num") in key:
            r["answer"] = key[r["num"]]
    return records


def parse(text, name):
    """Sheet/CSV table or pasted question paper -> (paper, problems). Raises PaperError if nothing usable."""
    if not text.strip():
        raise PaperError("Nothing to import. Paste your questions or a Google Sheets link.")
    records = _csv_records(text)
    if records is None:
        records = _text_records(text)
    if len(records) > MAX_ROWS:
        raise PaperError(f"Too many questions ({len(records)}). The limit is {MAX_ROWS}.")

    pid = secrets.token_hex(6)
    questions, errors, subjects = [], [], {}
    for n, (label, r) in enumerate(records, start=1):
        get = lambda f: (r.get(f) or "").strip()[:MAX_CELL]
        q, opts = get("question"), [get(f) for f in "abcd"]
        if not q or not all(opts):
            errors.append(f"{label}: needs a question and four options (A–D).")
            continue
        if len(set(o.lower() for o in opts)) < 4:
            errors.append(f"{label}: two options are the same.")
            continue
        key = _answer_index(get("answer"), opts)
        if key is None:
            errors.append(f"{label}: add a line like \"Answer: B\" (A–D, 1–4 or the option's exact text).")
            continue
        b = _difficulty(get("difficulty"))
        if b is None:
            errors.append(f"{label}: difficulty must be easy, medium, hard or a number.")
            continue
        subject = get("subject")[:60] or "My paper"
        chapter = get("chapter")[:80] or "General"
        if any(chapter in chs for s2, chs in subjects.items() if s2 != subject):  # unique across subjects
            chapter = f"{chapter} ({subject})"
        subjects.setdefault(subject, [])
        if chapter not in subjects[subject]:
            subjects[subject].append(chapter)
        hint = get("hint")
        questions.append(dict(
            id=f"paper-{pid}-{n}", chapter=chapter, b=b, q=q, options=opts, answer=key,
            hints=[hint or "Re-read the question and underline what is asked.", "Eliminate options you can rule out."],
            explanation=get("explanation") or f"Correct answer: {'ABCD'[key]}.",
            verified=True, key_source="uploaded", source=f"Your paper: {name}"))
    if not questions:
        raise PaperError("No questions found. " + (errors[0] if errors else
                         "Write each question with options A) to D) and a line like \"Answer: B\"."))
    paper = dict(id=pid, name=name, created=time.time(), subjects=subjects, questions=questions)
    return paper, errors


def save(paper):
    DIR.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
    (DIR / f"{paper['id']}.json").write_text(json.dumps(paper, ensure_ascii=False), encoding="utf-8")
    PAPERS[paper["id"]] = paper


def load_all():
    if DIR.exists():
        for p in DIR.glob("*.json"):
            try:
                paper = json.loads(p.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
                PAPERS[paper["id"]] = paper
            except (ValueError, KeyError):
                print("Skipping unreadable paper file:", p.name)


def get(level):
    """Paper for a level key like 'paper:<id>', else None."""
    return PAPERS.get(level[6:]) if isinstance(level, str) and level.startswith("paper:") else None


load_all()


if __name__ == "__main__":
    paper, errs = parse(TEMPLATE + "bad row,,,,,,\n\"Q\",x,y,z,w,E,,,,,\n", "Demo")
    assert len(paper["questions"]) == 2 and paper["questions"][0]["answer"] == 1 and paper["questions"][1]["answer"] == 2
    assert paper["questions"][0]["b"] == -1.0 and paper["subjects"] == {"Maths": ["Arithmetic"], "Physics": ["Laws of Motion"]}
    assert len(errs) == 2, errs
    pasted = """Chapter: Kinematics
1. A car accelerates from rest to $20$ m/s in 5 s.
What is its acceleration?
A) 2 m/s²
B) 4 m/s²
C) 5 m/s²
D) 100 m/s²
Answer: B
Explanation: a = 20/5 = 4

Q2) Unit of force?  (a) Joule (b) Watt (c) Newton (d) Pascal
Ans - c

3. Which is a vector?
A. Speed  B. Distance  C. Velocity  D. Time
"""
    paper, errs = parse(pasted, "Pasted")
    qs = paper["questions"]
    assert [q["answer"] for q in qs] == [1, 2], qs
    assert qs[0]["q"] == "A car accelerates from rest to $20$ m/s in 5 s. What is its acceleration?", qs[0]["q"]
    assert qs[0]["chapter"] == "Kinematics" and qs[0]["explanation"] == "a = 20/5 = 4"
    assert qs[1]["options"] == ["Joule", "Watt", "Newton", "Pascal"], qs[1]["options"]
    assert len(errs) == 1 and "Answer" in errs[0], errs  # question 3 has no answer line
    jee, errs = parse("""1. Speed of light in vacuum is
(1) 3e8 m/s (2) 3e6 m/s (3) 3e5 m/s (4) 300 m/s
Answer: 1
2. Which is a scalar?
(1) Force
(2) Mass
(3) Velocity
(4) Momentum
Answer: (2)
""", "JEE style")
    assert [q["answer"] for q in jee["questions"]] == [0, 1] and not errs, (jee["questions"], errs)
    pdf_style, errs = parse("""Physics Unit Test
1. Unit of force is
(a) Joule (b) Newton (c) Watt (d) Pascal
2. Which is a vector?
(1) Speed (2) Mass (3) Velocity (4) Time
3. g on Earth is about
A) 9.8 m/s² B) 1.6 m/s² C) 98 m/s² D) 0.98 m/s²
Answer Key
1-b 2. (3)
3 - A
""", "PDF style")
    assert [q["answer"] for q in pdf_style["questions"]] == [1, 2, 0] and not errs, (pdf_style["questions"], errs)
    assert pdf_style["questions"][0]["q"] == "Unit of force is"  # the title line is ignored, not an error
    assert sheet_csv_url("https://docs.google.com/spreadsheets/d/1AbCdEfGhIjKlMnOpQrStUvWxYz012345/edit#gid=77") \
        == "https://docs.google.com/spreadsheets/d/1AbCdEfGhIjKlMnOpQrStUvWxYz012345/export?format=csv&gid=77"
    for bad in ("https://evil.com/spreadsheets/d/1AbCdEfGhIjKlMnOpQrStUvWxYz012345",
                "https://docs.google.com.evil.com/spreadsheets/d/1AbCdEfGhIjKlMnOpQrStUvWxYz012345", "file:///etc/passwd"):
        try:
            sheet_csv_url(bad)
            raise AssertionError(bad)
        except PaperError:
            pass
    print("papers ok")

"""SQLite state for accounts, authentication, owned sessions, preferences and papers."""
from contextlib import contextmanager
import hashlib
import json
from pathlib import Path
import secrets
import sqlite3
import time


class Database:
    def __init__(self, path, legacy_users=None):
        self.path = Path(path)
        self.path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        with self.connection() as con:
            con.execute('PRAGMA journal_mode=WAL')
            con.executescript('''
                CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS users (
                    email TEXT PRIMARY KEY, profile TEXT NOT NULL);
                CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS auth_tokens (
                    digest TEXT PRIMARY KEY, owner TEXT NOT NULL, expires REAL NOT NULL);
                CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS preferences (
                    owner TEXT PRIMARY KEY, payload TEXT NOT NULL);
                CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS study_sessions (
                    id TEXT PRIMARY KEY, owner TEXT NOT NULL, level TEXT NOT NULL,
                    payload TEXT NOT NULL, updated REAL NOT NULL);
                CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS sessions_owner ON study_sessions(owner, updated DESC);
                CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS question_papers (
                    id TEXT PRIMARY KEY, owner TEXT NOT NULL, payload TEXT NOT NULL, created REAL NOT NULL, archived INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0);
                CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS papers_owner ON question_papers(owner, created DESC);
                CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS bookmarks (
                    owner TEXT NOT NULL, question_id TEXT NOT NULL,
                    payload TEXT NOT NULL, created REAL NOT NULL,
                    PRIMARY KEY(owner, question_id));
                CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS reviews (
                    owner TEXT NOT NULL, question_id TEXT NOT NULL, payload TEXT NOT NULL,
                    stage INTEGER NOT NULL, due REAL NOT NULL, PRIMARY KEY(owner, question_id));
                CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS complaints (
                    owner TEXT NOT NULL, question_id TEXT NOT NULL, payload TEXT NOT NULL, created REAL NOT NULL);
                CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS daily_activity (
                    owner TEXT NOT NULL, event_id TEXT NOT NULL, day TEXT NOT NULL,
                    PRIMARY KEY(owner, event_id));
                CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS activity_owner_day ON daily_activity(owner, day);
                CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS owner_activity (
                    owner TEXT PRIMARY KEY, last_seen REAL NOT NULL);
            ''')
            columns = {row['name'] for row in con.execute('PRAGMA table_info(question_papers)')}
            if 'archived' not in columns:
                con.execute('ALTER TABLE question_papers ADD COLUMN archived INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0')
            # Existing guests receive a full retention window on upgrade, even if
            # their last practice session predates authenticated-activity tracking.
            con.execute('''INSERT OR IGNORE INTO owner_activity(owner, last_seen)
                SELECT owner, ? FROM (
                    SELECT owner FROM auth_tokens UNION SELECT owner FROM preferences
                    UNION SELECT owner FROM study_sessions UNION SELECT owner FROM question_papers
                    UNION SELECT owner FROM bookmarks UNION SELECT owner FROM reviews
                    UNION SELECT owner FROM complaints UNION SELECT owner FROM daily_activity)''', (time.time(),))
            version = con.execute('PRAGMA user_version').fetchone()[0]
            if version < 6:
                con.execute('PRAGMA user_version=6')
        if legacy_users is not None:
            self.import_legacy_users(legacy_users)

    @contextmanager
    def connection(self):
        con = sqlite3.connect(self.path, timeout=15)
        con.row_factory = sqlite3.Row
        try:
            with con:
                yield con
        finally:
            con.close()

    def import_legacy_users(self, path):
        path = Path(path)
        if not path.exists():
            return
        users = json.loads(path.read_text(encoding='utf-8'))
        # Preserve hashes and salts exactly; never overwrite an existing SQLite account.
        with self.connection() as con:
            for email, user in users.items():
                if not all(k in user for k in ('name', 'email', 'level', 'salt', 'hash')):
                    raise ValueError('Legacy account data is incomplete; migration stopped without modifying its source.')
                con.execute('INSERT OR IGNORE INTO users VALUES (?, ?)', (email, json.dumps(user)))

    def users(self):
        with self.connection() as con:
            return {r['email']: json.loads(r['profile']) for r in con.execute('SELECT * FROM users')}

    def user(self, email):
        with self.connection() as con:
            row = con.execute('SELECT profile FROM users WHERE email=?', (email,)).fetchone()
        return json.loads(row['profile']) if row else None

    def add_user(self, user):
        with self.connection() as con:
            con.execute('INSERT INTO users VALUES (?, ?)', (user['email'], json.dumps(user)))

    def issue_token(self, owner, ttl=30 * 86400):
        token = secrets.token_urlsafe(32)
        now = time.time()
        with self.connection() as con:
            con.execute('INSERT INTO auth_tokens VALUES (?, ?, ?)',
                        (hashlib.sha256(token.encode()).hexdigest(), owner, now + ttl))
            con.execute('INSERT INTO owner_activity VALUES (?, ?) ON CONFLICT(owner) DO UPDATE SET last_seen=excluded.last_seen', (owner, now))
        return token

    def owner(self, token):
        if not token:
            return None
        with self.connection() as con:
            now = time.time()
            row = con.execute('SELECT owner FROM auth_tokens WHERE digest=? AND expires>?',
                (hashlib.sha256(token.encode()).hexdigest(), now)).fetchone()
            if row:
                con.execute('INSERT INTO owner_activity VALUES (?, ?) ON CONFLICT(owner) DO UPDATE SET last_seen=excluded.last_seen', (row['owner'], now))
        return row['owner'] if row else None

    def cleanup(self, now=None, guest_ttl=30 * 86400):
        """Atomically expire tokens and guests inactive for at least 30 days.

        Account identities are never candidates, regardless of practice history.
        Token issuance and every valid authenticated visit refresh last_seen.
        """
        now = time.time() if now is None else now
        with self.connection() as con:
            expired = con.execute('DELETE FROM auth_tokens WHERE expires<=?', (now,)).rowcount
            owners = [r['owner'] for r in con.execute(
                "SELECT owner FROM owner_activity WHERE owner LIKE 'guest:%' AND last_seen<=? "
                "AND owner NOT IN (SELECT email FROM users)", (now - guest_ttl,))]
            for owner in owners:
                for table in ('auth_tokens', 'preferences', 'study_sessions', 'question_papers',
                              'bookmarks', 'reviews', 'complaints', 'daily_activity', 'owner_activity'):
                    con.execute(f'DELETE FROM {table} WHERE owner=?', (owner,))
        return {'expired_tokens': expired, 'inactive_guests': len(owners)}

    def revoke(self, token):
        with self.connection() as con:
            con.execute('DELETE FROM auth_tokens WHERE digest=?', (hashlib.sha256(token.encode()).hexdigest(),))

    def get_preferences(self, owner):
        with self.connection() as con:
            row = con.execute('SELECT payload FROM preferences WHERE owner=?', (owner,)).fetchone()
        return json.loads(row['payload']) if row else None

    def save_preferences(self, owner, payload):
        with self.connection() as con:
            con.execute('INSERT INTO preferences VALUES (?, ?) ON CONFLICT(owner) DO UPDATE SET payload=excluded.payload',
                        (owner, json.dumps(payload)))

    def save_session(self, sid, owner, level, payload):
        with self.connection() as con:
            con.execute('''INSERT INTO study_sessions VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
                ON CONFLICT(id) DO UPDATE SET payload=excluded.payload,updated=excluded.updated
                WHERE study_sessions.owner=excluded.owner''',
                (sid, owner, level, json.dumps(payload, allow_nan=False), time.time()))

    def get_session(self, sid, owner):
        with self.connection() as con:
            row = con.execute('SELECT payload FROM study_sessions WHERE id=? AND owner=?', (sid, owner)).fetchone()
        return json.loads(row['payload']) if row else None

    def latest_session(self, owner, level=None):
        with self.connection() as con:
            query = 'SELECT id FROM study_sessions WHERE owner=?'
            params = [owner]
            if level:
                query += ' AND level=?'
                params.append(level)
            row = con.execute(query + ' ORDER BY updated DESC LIMIT 1', params).fetchone()
        return row['id'] if row else None

    def save_paper(self, owner, paper):
        with self.connection() as con:
            con.execute('INSERT INTO question_papers(id, owner, payload, created) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)',
                        (paper['id'], owner, json.dumps(paper), paper['created']))

    def get_paper(self, pid, owner):
        with self.connection() as con:
            row = con.execute('SELECT payload FROM question_papers WHERE id=? AND owner=?', (pid, owner)).fetchone()
        return json.loads(row['payload']) if row else None

    def papers(self, owner):
        with self.connection() as con:
            rows = con.execute('SELECT payload FROM question_papers WHERE owner=? AND archived=0 ORDER BY created DESC', (owner,)).fetchall()
        return [json.loads(row['payload']) for row in rows]

    def delete_paper(self, pid, owner):
        with self.connection() as con:
            return bool(con.execute('UPDATE question_papers SET archived=1 WHERE id=? AND owner=? AND archived=0', (pid, owner)).rowcount)


    def save_bookmark(self, owner, question_id, payload):
        with self.connection() as con:
            con.execute('''INSERT INTO bookmarks VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)
                ON CONFLICT(owner,question_id) DO NOTHING''',
                (owner, question_id, json.dumps(payload), time.time()))

    def has_bookmark(self, owner, question_id):
        with self.connection() as con:
            return bool(con.execute('SELECT 1 FROM bookmarks WHERE owner=? AND question_id=?',
                                    (owner, question_id)).fetchone())

    def bookmarks(self, owner):
        with self.connection() as con:
            rows = con.execute('SELECT question_id,payload,created FROM bookmarks WHERE owner=? ORDER BY created DESC', (owner,)).fetchall()
        return [dict(question_id=r['question_id'], **json.loads(r['payload']), created=r['created']) for r in rows]

    def add_review(self, owner, question_id, payload, due):
        """Queue a missed question; missing it again restarts its review ladder."""
        with self.connection() as con:
            con.execute('''INSERT INTO reviews VALUES (?, ?, ?, 0, ?)
                ON CONFLICT(owner,question_id) DO UPDATE SET payload=excluded.payload, stage=0, due=excluded.due''',
                (owner, question_id, json.dumps(payload), due))

    def reviews(self, owner):
        with self.connection() as con:
            rows = con.execute('SELECT question_id,payload,stage,due FROM reviews WHERE owner=? ORDER BY due', (owner,)).fetchall()
        return [dict(question_id=r['question_id'], stage=r['stage'], due=r['due'], **json.loads(r['payload'])) for r in rows]

    def review(self, owner, question_id):
        return next((r for r in self.reviews(owner) if r['question_id'] == question_id), None)

    def schedule_review(self, owner, question_id, stage, due):
        """Move a review to its next stage, or drop it once mastered (due is None)."""
        with self.connection() as con:
            if due is None:
                con.execute('DELETE FROM reviews WHERE owner=? AND question_id=?', (owner, question_id))
            else:
                con.execute('UPDATE reviews SET stage=?, due=? WHERE owner=? AND question_id=?', (stage, due, owner, question_id))

    def save_complaint(self, owner, question_id, payload):
        """Log an AI check of a disputed answer key, for the team to review later."""
        with self.connection() as con:
            con.execute('INSERT INTO complaints VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)', (owner, question_id, json.dumps(payload), time.time()))

    def remove_bookmark(self, owner, question_id):
        with self.connection() as con:
            con.execute('DELETE FROM bookmarks WHERE owner=? AND question_id=?', (owner, question_id))

    def record_activity(self, owner, event_id, day):
        """Each successful answer/review event counts once, even across retries."""
        with self.connection() as con:
            return bool(con.execute('INSERT OR IGNORE INTO daily_activity VALUES (?, ?, ?)',
                                    (owner, event_id, day)).rowcount)

    def activity_days(self, owner):
        with self.connection() as con:
            return {row['day']: row['n'] for row in con.execute(
                'SELECT day, COUNT(*) AS n FROM daily_activity WHERE owner=? GROUP BY day', (owner,))}

================================================================================
END OF FILE: preppilot/storage.py

        "Maths": ["Relations and Functions", "Inverse Trigonometric Functions", "Matrices and Determinants",
                  "Continuity and Differentiability", "Application of Derivatives", "Indefinite Integration",
                  "Definite Integration", "Area Under Curves", "Differential Equations", "Vectors", "3D Geometry",
                  "Linear Programming", "Probability"],
        "Biology": list(SYLLABUS["Biology"])[19:],
    },
}

# JEE Main uses the JEE chapter list minus the topics NTA removed from the JEE Main syllabus (2024 revision).
# Verify against the latest NTA information bulletin before the final demo.
JEE_MAIN_REMOVED = {"States of Matter", "Solid State", "Surface Chemistry", "s-Block Elements", "Metallurgy", "Polymers"}

# level key -> display name, in menu order
EXAMS = {"JEEMAIN": "JEE Main", "JEE": "JEE Advanced", "NEET": "NEET"}
LEVELS = {**{f"Class {n}": f"Class {n}" for n in CLASSES}, **EXAMS}
BANK_LEVELS = {"Class 11", "Class 12", "JEEMAIN", "JEE", "NEET"}  # where the verified PCMB bank applies


def subjects_for(level):
    """{subject: [chapters]} in syllabus order."""
    if level == "JEEMAIN":
        return {s: [c for c in chs if c not in JEE_MAIN_REMOVED] for s, chs in subjects_for("JEE").items()}
    if level in EXAMS:
        return {s: [c for c, ex in chs.items() if level in ex] for s, chs in SYLLABUS.items()
                if any(level in ex for ex in chs.values())}
    return CLASSES[int(level.split()[1])]


def chapters_for(level):
    """{chapter: subject} for every chapter at this level."""
    return {ch: subj for subj, chs in subjects_for(level).items() for ch in chs}


if __name__ == "__main__":
    for lv in LEVELS:
        subj = subjects_for(lv)
        n = sum(len(c) for c in subj.values())
        assert len(chapters_for(lv)) == n, f"{lv}: chapter name repeated across subjects"
        print(f"{lv:9} {n:3} chapters  {', '.join(subj)}")
    assert "Chemical Coordination and Integration" in CLASSES[11]["Biology"]
    assert CLASSES[12]["Biology"][0] == "Sexual Reproduction in Flowering Plants"

"""Run PrepPilot using optional private settings from QUIZBOT/.env.local."""
import argparse
import os
from pathlib import Path


def run():
    settings = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent / ".env.local"
    allowed = {"GEMINI_API_KEY", "GEMINI_MODEL", "GROQ_API_KEY", "GROQ_MODEL", "GOOGLE_CLIENT_ID"}
    if settings.exists():
        for line in settings.read_text(encoding="utf-8-sig").splitlines():
            if not line.strip() or line.lstrip().startswith("#") or "=" not in line:
                continue
            name, value = line.split("=", 1)
            name, value = name.strip(), value.strip().strip("\"'")
            if name in allowed and value:
                os.environ[name] = value
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="Start PrepPilot with private local AI settings")
    parser.add_argument("--host", default="127.0.0.1")
    parser.add_argument("--port", type=int, default=8000)
    args = parser.parse_args()
    import uvicorn
    uvicorn.run("main:app", host=args.host, port=args.port, app_dir=str(Path(__file__).resolve().parent))


if __name__ == "__main__":
    run()

"""Import real JEE Advanced (2016-2023) questions from JEEBench into questions_imported.json.

JEEBench: https://github.com/dair-iitd/jeebench (MIT license, EMNLP 2023). Answer keys are the
official JEE Advanced keys, so verify_bank.py accepts them as checked ("key_source": "official").

Only single-correct MCQs whose options split cleanly into (A)-(D) are imported; multi-correct,
integer, numeric and matrix-match questions are skipped because the quiz UI is single-choice.
Each question is mapped to a syllabus chapter by Gemini (if GEMINI_API_KEY is set) or by keywords.

Run:
  python import_jeebench.py                  # download data.zip from GitHub
  python import_jeebench.py path/to/data.zip # or use a local copy
  python import_jeebench.py --explain        # also ask Gemini for hints + a worked solution
                                             # (kept only if Gemini reaches the official answer)
"""
import io
import json
import re
import sys
import time
import urllib.request
import zipfile
from pathlib import Path

import main  # reuses the Gemini client and JSON repair
from syllabus import subjects_for

URL = "https://github.com/dair-iitd/jeebench/raw/main/data.zip"
OUT = Path(__file__).parent / "questions_imported.json"
SUBJECT = {"phy": "Physics", "chem": "Chemistry", "math": "Maths"}
CHAPTERS = subjects_for("JEE")

# fallback when Gemini is unavailable: first chapter whose keyword appears in the question wins
KEYWORDS = {
    "Physics": [
        ("Semiconductor Electronics", r"diode|transistor|logic gate|semiconductor"),
        ("Nuclei", r"nucle|radioactiv|half-life|decay|fission|fusion"),
        ("Dual Nature of Matter and Radiation", r"photoelectr|de broglie|work function|photon"),
        ("Atoms", r"hydrogen atom|bohr|spectral line|rydberg"),
        ("Wave Optics", r"interference|diffraction|young|slit|polari"),
        ("Ray Optics", r"lens|mirror|refract|prism|focal"),
        ("Electromagnetic Induction", r"induct|faraday|flux"),
        ("Alternating Current", r"\bac\b|alternating|lcr|resonan"),
        ("Moving Charges and Magnetism", r"magnetic field|solenoid|cyclotron|biot|ampere"),
        ("Capacitance", r"capacit|dielectric"),
        ("Current Electricity", r"resist|current|battery|emf|wheatstone|potentiometer"),
        ("Electrostatics", r"charge|electric field|coulomb|gauss|potential"),
        ("Thermodynamics", r"adiabatic|isotherm|heat engine|carnot|entropy"),
        ("Kinetic Theory of Gases", r"ideal gas|rms speed|degrees of freedom"),
        ("Thermal Properties of Matter", r"expansion|conduct|calorimet|radiat|specific heat"),
        ("Waves", r"wave|sound|string|organ pipe|doppler"),

        ("Mechanical Properties of Fluids", r"fluid|viscos|surface tension|bernoulli|buoyan"),

        ("Rotational Motion", r"rotat|torque|moment of inertia|angular|rolling|disc|ring"),
        ("Work, Energy and Power", r"work done|kinetic energy|collision|power"),
        ("Laws of Motion", r"friction|tension|pulley|newton"),

    ],
    "Chemistry": [
        ("Coordination Compounds", r"complex|ligand|coordination|\[.*\(nh_?3\)"),
        ("Electrochemistry", r"cell potential|electrode|e\^?\\circ|nernst|electroly"),
        ("Chemical Kinetics", r"rate constant|order of reaction|half-life|activation energy"),
        ("Chemical Thermodynamics", r"enthalpy|entropy|gibbs|\\delta h"),
        ("Ionic Equilibrium", r"\bph\b|buffer|ksp|solubility product|hydrolysis"),
        ("Chemical Equilibrium", r"equilibrium constant|k_?c|k_?p|le chatelier"),
        ("Solutions", r"osmotic|raoult|vapour pressure|molal|freezing|boiling point"),
        ("Atomic Structure", r"quantum number|orbital|electronic configuration|bohr"),
        ("Chemical Bonding", r"hybridi|vsepr|bond order|dipole|molecular orbital"),
        ("Metallurgy", r"ore|roasting|smelting|calcination|refining"),
        ("Qualitative Analysis", r"precipitate|qualitative|test for"),
        ("d- and f-Block Elements", r"transition metal|lanthan|actin|kmno|k_?2cr"),
        ("p-Block Elements", r"halogen|noble gas|boron|silic|phosph|sulph|nitrogen"),
        ("Polymers", r"polymer|nylon|monomer"),
        ("Biomolecules", r"glucose|amino acid|protein|carbohydrate|dna|vitamin"),
        ("Amines", r"amine|diazonium"),
        ("Aldehydes, Ketones and Carboxylic Acids", r"aldehyde|ketone|carboxylic|aldol|cannizzaro"),
        ("Alcohols, Phenols and Ethers", r"alcohol|phenol|ether"),

        ("Trigonometric Functions", r"\\sin|\\cos|\\tan"),
        ("Parabola", r"parabola"), ("Ellipse", r"ellipse"), ("Hyperbola", r"hyperbola"),
        ("Circles", r"circle"), ("Straight Lines", r"straight line|slope"),
        ("Sequences and Series", r"progression|series|\\sum"),
        ("Binomial Theorem", r"binomial|expansion"),
        ("Permutations and Combinations", r"arrange|ways|permutation|combination"),
        ("Quadratic Equations", r"quadratic|roots"),
        ("Sets, Relations and Functions", r"function|relation|set"),
        ("Statistics", r"mean|variance|median"),
    ],

def load(src):
    raw = Path(src).read_bytes() if src else urllib.request.urlopen(URL, timeout=120).read()
    return json.loads(zipfile.ZipFile(io.BytesIO(raw)).read("data/dataset.json"))


def split_options(text):
    """'stem\\n(A) a\\n(B) b ...' -> (stem, [a, b, c, d]) or None if it doesn't split cleanly."""
    parts = re.split(r"\n\s*\(([A-D])\)\s*", text.strip())
    if parts[1::2] != ["A", "B", "C", "D"]:
        return None
    opts = [p.strip() for p in parts[2::2]]
    return (parts[0].strip(), opts) if all(opts) and len(set(opts)) == 4 else None


def classify_keywords(subject, text):
    t = text.lower()
    return next((ch for ch, pat in KEYWORDS[subject] if re.search(pat, t)), None)


def classify_gemini(batch):
    """batch: [(i, subject, stem)] -> {i: chapter}. One Gemini call per batch."""
    out = {}
    for subject in {b[1] for b in batch}:
        rows = [(i, stem) for i, s, stem in batch if s == subject]
        listing = "\n".join(f"{i}: {stem[:600]}" for i, stem in rows)
        got = main.gemini(
            f"Classify each JEE Advanced {subject} question into exactly one chapter from this list:\n"
            f"{json.dumps(CHAPTERS[subject])}\nQuestions:\n{listing}\n"
            'Reply JSON: {"<question number>": "<chapter name exactly as listed>"}', True) or {}
        out.update({int(k): v for k, v in got.items() if str(k).isdigit() and v in CHAPTERS[subject]})
    return out


def explain(q):
    """Hints + worked solution from Gemini, kept only if its own answer matches the official key."""
    opts = "\n".join(f"{'ABCD'[i]}. {o}" for i, o in enumerate(q["options"]))
    r = main.gemini(
        "Solve this JEE Advanced question step by step, then give two progressive hints that do not reveal "
        f"the answer.\n{q['q']}\n{opts}\n"
        'Reply JSON: {"solution": str (use $...$ LaTeX), "answer": "A"|"B"|"C"|"D", "hints": [str, str]}', True)
    if isinstance(r, dict) and r.get("answer") == "ABCD"[q["answer"]] and len(r.get("hints", [])) == 2:
        return r
    return None


def run(src=None, with_explain=False):
    data = [x for x in load(src) if x["type"] == "MCQ"]
    rows, skipped = [], {"options did not split into (A)-(D)": 0, "needs a figure": 0,
                         "List-I/List-II table": 0, "no chapter match": 0}
    for x in data:
        parsed = split_options(x["question"])
        if not parsed:
            skipped["options did not split into (A)-(D)"] += 1
            continue
        if re.search(r"\b(figure|shown below|diagram)\b", x["question"], re.I):
            skipped["needs a figure"] += 1
            continue
        if re.search(r"List-?I|\\textbf|\\begin\{(tabular|array)", x["question"]):
            skipped["List-I/List-II table"] += 1  # flattened tables are unreadable in the chat card
            continue
        rows.append((x, *parsed))

    chapters = {}
    if main.GEMINI_KEY:
        batch = [(i, SUBJECT[x["subject"]], stem) for i, (x, stem, _) in enumerate(rows)]
        for k in range(0, len(batch), 15):
            chapters.update(classify_gemini(batch[k:k + 15]))
            time.sleep(2)  # stay under free-tier rate limits

    out = []
    for i, (x, stem, opts) in enumerate(rows):
        subject = SUBJECT[x["subject"]]
        ch = chapters.get(i) or classify_keywords(subject, x["question"])
        if not ch:
            skipped["no chapter match"] += 1
            continue
        key = "ABCD".index(x["gold"].strip())
        src = f"{x['description']} (official key, via JEEBench)"
        paper = re.sub(r"\W+", "-", x["description"].lower().replace("jee adv ", "")).strip("-")  # "2016-paper-1"
        q = dict(id=f"jee-{paper}-{x['subject']}-{x['index']}", chapter=ch, b=1.8, q=stem, options=opts, answer=key,
                 hints=["Identify the core principle the question is testing.",
                        "Check each option against a limiting or special case."],
                 explanation=f"Official JEE Advanced answer: ({x['gold']}). Source: {x['description']}.",
                 source=src, key_source="official")
        if with_explain and (e := explain(q)):
            q.update(hints=e["hints"], explanation=f"{e['solution']}\n\nOfficial answer: ({x['gold']}).")
            time.sleep(2)
        out.append(q)

    with open(OUT, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        f.write("[\n" + ",\n".join(json.dumps(q, ensure_ascii=False) for q in out) + "\n]\n")
    by_subject = {s: sum(f"-{k}-" in q["id"] for q in out) for k, s in SUBJECT.items()}
    print(f"Imported {len(out)} of {len(data)} single-correct JEE Advanced MCQs into {OUT.name}: {by_subject}")
    print("Skipped:", skipped)
    print("Chapter mapping:", "Gemini" if chapters else "keywords (set GEMINI_API_KEY for better mapping)")
    return out


if __name__ == "__main__":
    args = [a for a in sys.argv[1:] if not a.startswith("--")]
    run(args[0] if args else None, "--explain" in sys.argv)

================================================================================
END OF FILE: preppilot/import_jeebench.py

"""Re-check every answer key in questions.json.

Numeric questions: the answer is recomputed here from the question's data, and exactly one
option must equal it (this also catches "no correct option" and duplicate-option bugs).
Conceptual questions: a second, independent review names the correct option text.
Imported past-paper questions ("key_source": "official") carry the official exam key.
Every other question must be covered by one of the two lists, so nothing enters the bank unchecked.

Run: python verify_bank.py
"""
import json

from pathlib import Path

HERE = Path(__file__).parent
# questions_imported.json is written by import_jeebench.py (official JEE Advanced keys)
QS = [q for name in ("questions.json", "questions_imported.json") if (HERE / name).exists()
      for q in json.loads((HERE / name).read_text(encoding="utf-8"))]

# Conceptual / symbolic questions: (text that must appear in the keyed option, why it is correct)
REVIEWED = {

    "bio-inh-7": ("Codominance and multiple alleles", "IA, IB, i; AB expresses both"),

def value(option):
    """Numeric value of an option like '$4\\sqrt{10}\\,\\text{m/s}$' or '$\\frac{3}{4}$', else None."""
    s = option.replace("$", "")
    s = re.sub(r"\\text\{[^}]*\}(\^\{?-?\d+\}?)?", "", s)  # units, with any exponent
    s = s.replace(r"\%", "").replace(r"\,", "").replace(r"\dfrac", r"\frac").replace(r"\times", "*")
    s = re.sub(r"\\frac\{([^{}]*)\}\{([^{}]*)\}", r"((\1)/(\2))", s)
    s = re.sub(r"\\sqrt\{([^{}]*)\}", r"*sqrt(\1)", s)
    s = re.sub(r"\^\{(-?\d+)\}", r"**(\1)", s)
    s = re.sub(r"^\s*\*", "", s).strip()
    if not re.fullmatch(r"[\d\s.+\-*/()sqrt]+", s):
        return None
    try:  # ponytail: eval on our own repo file only, characters whitelisted above
        v = eval(s, {"__builtins__": {}, "sqrt": math.sqrt})
    except Exception:
        return None
    return float(v) if isinstance(v, (int, float)) else None


def check():
    errors, ids = [], set()
    for q in QS:
        qid, opts, key = q["id"], q["options"], q["answer"]
        if qid in ids:
            errors.append(f"{qid}: duplicate id")
        ids.add(qid)
        if len(opts) != 4 or len(set(opts)) != 4 or key not in range(4):
            errors.append(f"{qid}: needs 4 distinct options and answer 0-3")
            continue
        if q.get("key_source") == "official":
            pass  # official exam answer key; structure was checked above
        elif qid in COMPUTED:
            want = float(COMPUTED[qid])
            hits = [i for i, o in enumerate(opts) if (v := value(o)) is not None and math.isclose(v, want, rel_tol=5e-3)]
            if hits != [key]:
                errors.append(f"{qid}: computed {want:.4g}, matching options {hits}, key {key}")
        elif qid in REVIEWED:
            text, _ = REVIEWED[qid]
            # exact match wins, so "3 : 1" is not confused with "9 : 3 : 3 : 1"
            hits = [i for i, o in enumerate(opts) if o == text] or [i for i, o in enumerate(opts) if text in o]
            if hits != [key]:
                errors.append(f"{qid}: reviewed answer '{text}' is not uniquely the keyed option")
        else:
            errors.append(f"{qid}: not checked: add it to COMPUTED or REVIEWED")
    stale = (set(COMPUTED) | set(REVIEWED)) - ids
    errors += [f"{s}: check exists but question is missing" for s in sorted(stale)]
    return errors


if __name__ == "__main__":
    errs = check()
    print(f"{len(QS)} questions: {sum(q['id'] in COMPUTED for q in QS)} recomputed, "
          f"{sum(q['id'] in REVIEWED for q in QS)} reviewed, "
          f"{sum(q.get('key_source') == 'official' for q in QS)} official exam keys")
    for e in errs:
        print("FAIL", e)
    assert not errs, f"{len(errs)} problem(s)"
    print("all answer keys verified")

================================================================================
END OF FILE: preppilot/verify_bank.py
================================================================================
