<script>
document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',()=>{if(window.Chart){Chart.defaults.font.family='DM Sans';Chart.defaults.animation=false;}});
const $ = s => document.querySelector(s);
const KATEX = { delimiters: [{ left: "$$", right: "$$", display: true }, { left: "\\[", right: "\\]", display: true },
  { left: "\\(", right: "\\)", display: false }, { left: "$", right: "$", display: false }], throwOnError: false };
const S = { sid: null, exam: "JEE", examName: "", q: null, picked: null, busy: false, charts: {}, focus: "all" };
const pct = x => Math.round(x * 100) + "%";
const css = v => getComputedStyle(document.documentElement).getPropertyValue(v).trim();

function el(tag, cls, text) {
  const e = document.createElement(tag);
  if (cls) e.className = cls;
  if (text !== undefined) math(e, text);
  return e;
}

function speechButton(text) {
  if (!('speechSynthesis' in window) || !('SpeechSynthesisUtterance' in window)) return null;
  const button=el('button','btn read-aloud','🔊 Read aloud');button.type='button';
  button.onclick=()=>{
    speechSynthesis.cancel();
    const utterance=new SpeechSynthesisUtterance(String(text()||''));
    utterance.lang=document.documentElement.lang||'en';
    speechSynthesis.speak(utterance);
  };
  return button;
}
addEventListener('pagehide',()=>{if('speechSynthesis' in window)speechSynthesis.cancel();});

async function saveSummaryImage(d) {
  const canvas=document.createElement('canvas'),ctx=canvas.getContext('2d');
  if(!ctx)throw Error('Canvas is unavailable.');
  canvas.width=1200;
  const lines=[['PrepPilot · Session summary',44,true],[d.exam||'Your practice',25,false],
    [`${d.answered} answered · ${d.correct} correct · ${d.minutes} minutes`,30,false]];
  if(d.answered>=5&&d.predicted?.now!=null)lines.push(['Score estimate: '+pct(d.predicted.now)+' · practice estimate only',24,false]);
  lines.push(['Chapters practised',30,true]);
  if(!d.chapters.length)lines.push(['No chapters practised yet.',25,false]);
  d.chapters.slice(0,12).forEach(c=>lines.push([`${c.chapter}: ${c.n} answered · ${pct(c.before)} to ${pct(c.after)} estimated readiness`,25,false]));
  if(d.chapters.length>12)lines.push([`Plus ${d.chapters.length-12} more chapters.`,24,false]);
  if(d.top_error)lines.push(['Next time: '+d.top_error.label+'. '+d.top_error.fix,25,false]);
  if(d.recommended)lines.push(['Suggested next: '+d.recommended.chapter,25,false]);
  lines.push(['A study snapshot, not a validated exam prediction.',21,false]);
  const wrapped=[];
  for(const [text,size,heading] of lines){
    ctx.font=`${heading?600:400} ${size}px ${heading?'Fraunces':'DM Sans'}, sans-serif`;
    let line='';
    for(const character of String(text)){
      if(ctx.measureText(line+character).width>1040&&line){wrapped.push({text:line,size,heading});line='';}
      line+=character;
    }
    if(line)wrapped.push({text:line,size,heading});
    wrapped.push({text:'',size:12,heading:false});
  }
  canvas.height=Math.max(600,120+wrapped.reduce((height,line)=>height+line.size*1.5,0));
  const styles=getComputedStyle(document.documentElement);
  ctx.fillStyle=styles.getPropertyValue('--bg').trim()||'#0A0E1F';ctx.fillRect(0,0,canvas.width,canvas.height);
  ctx.fillStyle=styles.getPropertyValue('--accent').trim()||'#FFB547';ctx.fillRect(64,48,70,4);
  let y=100;
  for(const line of wrapped){
    ctx.font=`${line.heading?600:400} ${line.size}px ${line.heading?'Fraunces':'DM Sans'}, sans-serif`;
    ctx.fillStyle=styles.getPropertyValue(line.heading?'--accent':'--ink').trim()||'#ECEEF8';
    ctx.fillText(line.text,64,y);y+=line.size*1.5;
  }
  const blob=await new Promise(resolve=>canvas.toBlob(resolve,'image/png'));
  if(!blob)throw Error('Image could not be made.');
  const url=URL.createObjectURL(blob),download=document.createElement('a');
  download.href=url;download.download='preppilot-session-summary.png';document.body.append(download);download.click();download.remove();
  setTimeout(()=>URL.revokeObjectURL(url),1000);
}
function math(e, text) {  // textContent first, so AI output can never inject HTML
  e.textContent = String(text).replace(/\*\*(.+?)\*\*/g, "$1");  // AI Markdown bold would show as asterisks
  if (window.renderMathInElement) renderMathInElement(e, KATEX);
  return e;
}
async function api(path, body) {
  const opts = body === undefined ? {} : { method: "POST", headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" }, body: JSON.stringify(body) };
  let r;
  try { r = await fetch(path, opts); }
  catch (e) { throw Object.assign(new Error("Can't reach the PrepPilot server. Check it's running, then try again."), { offline: true }); }
  const d = await r.json().catch(() => ({}));
  if (r.status === 404 && path !== "/api/me")  // server restarted: in-memory session is gone
    throw Object.assign(new Error("Your session expired because the server restarted."), { expired: true });
  if (r.status === 423) throw Object.assign(new Error("Break time"), { brk: d.detail });  // mandatory break
  if (!r.ok) throw new Error(d.detail || r.statusText);
  return d;
}
function problem(e, canRetry = false) {
  // friendly error with a one-click way out instead of a dead end
  const m = say("⚠ " + e.message);
  const fix = e.expired ? ["Start a fresh session", () => start(S.exam)]
            : (e.offline && canRetry) ? ["Try again", () => next()] : null;
  if (fix) {
    const b = el("button", "btn primary", fix[0]);
    b.onclick = () => { b.disabled = true; fix[1](); };
    m.append(el("br"), b);
  }
}

function push(node) {
  const log = $("#log");
  log.append(node);
  log.scrollTo({ top: log.scrollHeight, behavior: calm() ? "instant" : "smooth" });  // scroll the chat only, never the page
  return node;
}
function say(text, who = "bot", extra = "") { return push(el("div", `msg ${who} ${extra}`, text)); }

async function start(exam, fresh = false) {
  clearInterval(S.brkTimer);
  $("#breakov").hidden = true;
  S.blockEnd = 0;
  S.exam = exam; S.q = null; S.focus = "all"; S.busy = false;
  $("#log").innerHTML = "";
  $("#why").innerHTML = '<p class="empty">The engine\'s reasoning for each question shows here.</p>';
  $("#dash").innerHTML = '<p class="empty">Answer a question to see your learner model.</p>';
  const providers = await api("/api/providers");
  const pr = S.prefs || {};
  if (!providers.providers.some(p => p.id === pr.ai_provider && p.available)) pr.ai_provider = providers.default;
  const seg = $("#aiprovider"); seg.replaceChildren();
  for (const p of providers.providers) {
    const b = el("button", "", p.label); b.type = "button"; b.setAttribute("role", "radio"); b.dataset.value = p.id;
    b.title = p.available ? "" : "Not set up on this server";
    b.dataset.available = String(p.available); b.disabled = true;
    b.onclick = () => switchProvider(p.id, p.label);
    seg.append(b);
  }
  markProvider(pr.ai_provider);
  const resumeKey = "pp-session:" + JSON.stringify([S.user?.email || "guest", exam, DEMO, pr]);
  // Always resume the learner's session: picking a new chapter must not wipe the model or skip a break.
  // Fallback to the last session: if preferences changed, the server starts a new learner but keeps a running break.
  const lastKey = "pp-last-session:" + (S.user?.email || "guest");
  let resumeId = "";
  try { resumeId = localStorage.getItem(resumeKey) || localStorage.getItem(lastKey) || ""; } catch (e) {}
  const d = await api("/api/session", { resume_id: resumeId, level: exam, demo: DEMO, ai_provider: pr.ai_provider,
                                        // setup choices; subjects only apply to the level they were picked for
                                        subjects: pr.level === exam ? pr.subjects || [] : [],
                                        start_ability: typeof pr.start_ability === "number" ? pr.start_ability : 0,
                                        placement: new URL(location.href).searchParams.has("placement"),
                                        block_minutes: pr.block_minutes || 40,
                                        explain_style: pr.explain_style || "steps" });
  try { localStorage.setItem(resumeKey, d.session_id); localStorage.setItem(lastKey, d.session_id); } catch (e) {}
  setClock(d.clock);
  S.sid = d.session_id; S.examName = d.exam; S.ai = d.ai; S.paper = d.paper; S.timeLimit = d.time_limit;
  enableProviders(true);
  clearInterval(S.timer);
  $("#pred").hidden = true;
  try { localStorage.setItem("pp-level", exam); } catch (e) {}
  $("#ai").textContent = d.paper ? "Your paper only" : d.ai ? "AI: " + ({gemini:"Gemini",groq:"Groq"}[d.ai_provider] || d.ai_provider) + " on" : "AI: off (bank only)";
  $("#ai").classList.toggle("on", d.ai);
  buildSide(d.subjects);
  // A chapter picked on /study (?new=1&focus=...) only switches the focus of the same session.
  const requestedFocus = fresh ? new URL(location.href).searchParams.get("focus") : null;
  if (requestedFocus) await api("/api/focus", { session_id: S.sid, focus: requestedFocus });
  S.focus = requestedFocus || d.focus || "all";
  markFocus();
  say(`${d.exam} · ${S.focus === "all" ? "Adaptive practice" : S.focus}`, "bot", "session-intro");
  await refreshDash();
  const activeFits = d.active && (!requestedFocus || [d.active.question.chapter, d.active.why.subject].includes(requestedFocus));
  if (activeFits) {
    renderQuestion(d.active.question, d.active.why, d.active.elapsed);
    renderWhy(d.active.why);
  } else next();
}

function buildSide(subjects) {
  const side = $("#side");
  side.innerHTML = "";
  const all = el("button", "btn all on", "All subjects (let PrepPilot choose)");
  all.dataset.focus = "all";
  all.onclick = () => setFocus("all");
  side.append(all);
  subjects.forEach(({ subject, chapters }) => {
    const det = el("details");
    det.open = subjects.length <= 2;
    const sum = el("summary");
    sum.dataset.focus = subject;
    const fb = el("button", "focusbtn", "Practise");
    fb.title = `Practise only ${subject}`;
    fb.onclick = e => { e.preventDefault(); setFocus(subject); };
    sum.append(el("span", "", subject), el("span", "pct", ""), fb);
    det.append(sum);
    chapters.forEach(c => {
      const b = el("button", "chap");
      b.dataset.focus = c;
      b.append(el("span", "dot"), el("span", "", c), el("span", "n", ""));
      b.onclick = () => setFocus(c);
      det.append(b);
    });
    side.append(det);
  });
}

function readinessColor(r) {
  const x = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, Number(r) || 0));
  return x < 0.5
    ? `color-mix(in srgb, var(--warn) ${Math.round(x * 200)}%, var(--bad))`
    : `color-mix(in srgb, var(--good) ${Math.round((x - 0.5) * 200)}%, var(--warn))`;
}

function updateSide(d) {
  const byName = Object.fromEntries(d.chapters.map(c => [c.chapter, c]));
  document.querySelectorAll("#side .chap").forEach(b => {
    const c = byName[b.dataset.focus];
    if (!c) return;
    const dot = b.querySelector(".dot");
    dot.style.background = readinessColor(c.readiness);
    dot.style.opacity = c.n ? 1 : 0.35;
    b.querySelector(".n").textContent = c.n ? `${pct(c.readiness)}${c.flags ? " ⚠" : ""}` : "";
    b.title = `${c.chapter}: ${pct(c.readiness)} ready${c.n ? `, ${c.n} answered` : " (estimate)"}`;
  });
  const subj = Object.fromEntries(d.subjects.map(s => [s.subject, d.chapters.some(c => c.subject === s.subject && c.n >= 3) ? pct(s.readiness) : "Starting"]));
  document.querySelectorAll("#side summary").forEach(s => s.querySelector(".pct").textContent = subj[s.dataset.focus] || "Starting");
}

function markFocus() {
  $("#focuslabel").textContent = S.focus === "all" ? "All chapters" : S.focus;
  document.querySelectorAll("#side [data-focus]").forEach(b => b.classList.toggle("on", b.dataset.focus === S.focus));
}

async function next() {
  if (S.busy) return;
  S.busy = true;
  const sid = S.sid;  // the class may be switched while this request is in flight
  const wait = say("Picking your next question…", "bot", "muted");
  try {
    const d = await api("/api/next", { session_id: sid });
    wait.remove();
    if (sid !== S.sid) return;
    setClock(d.clock);
    renderQuestion(d.question, d.why);
    renderWhy(d.why);
  } catch (e) {
    wait.remove();
    if (sid !== S.sid) return;
    if (e.brk) showBreak(e.brk); else problem(e, true);
  }
  finally { if (sid === S.sid) S.busy = false; }
}

// ---------- study rhythm: 40-min focus blocks, mandatory 10–15 min breaks ----------
const DEMO = new URLSearchParams(location.search).has("demo");  // ?demo=1: 2-min blocks, 30-s breaks
const mmss = s => `${Math.floor(s / 60)}:${String(Math.floor(s % 60)).padStart(2, "0")}`;

function setClock(c) {
  S.blockEnd = Date.now() + c.block_left * 1000;
  S.breakEnd = Date.now() + c.break_left * 1000;
  S.blockSecs = c.block_secs;
  try { localStorage.setItem("pp-clock", JSON.stringify({ block_start: c.block_start, break_until: c.break_until, demo: DEMO })); } catch (e) {}
}

const TIPS = ["Stand up and stretch your back and shoulders.", "Drink a glass of water.",
  "Look at something 20 feet away for 20 seconds: your eyes need it.", "Walk around for a couple of minutes.",
  "Skip social media: it doesn't rest your brain.", "Take 5 slow, deep breaths."];

function showBreak(b) {
  S.q = null; clearInterval(S.timer);
  document.querySelectorAll("#log .card button:not(.bookmark-question):not(.read-aloud)").forEach(x => x.disabled = true);
  setClock(b);
  const ov = $("#breakov");
  ov.hidden = false;
  $("#brkwhy").textContent = b.long
    ? "You showed signs of fatigue in this block, so this break is longer."
    : b.reason === "Break taken early" ? "Good call. Rested brains learn faster." : `${mmss(b.block_secs)} of focused practice done.`;
  $("#brkstats").textContent = b.block_answered
    ? `This block: ${b.block_answered} questions, ${pct(b.block_accuracy)} correct.` : "";
  $("#brktips").innerHTML = "";
  [...TIPS].sort(() => Math.random() - .5).slice(0, 3).forEach(t => $("#brktips").append(el("li", "", t)));
  const total = Math.max(1, b.break_left);
  clearInterval(S.brkTimer);
  S.brkTimer = setInterval(() => {
    const left = Math.max(0, (S.breakEnd - Date.now()) / 1000);
    $("#brkleft").textContent = mmss(Math.ceil(left));
    $("#brkbar").style.width = (100 * (1 - left / total)) + "%";
    if (left <= 0) {
      clearInterval(S.brkTimer);
      ov.hidden = true;
      breakChoice();
    }
  }, 250);
}

setInterval(() => {  // header clock; when the block ends, the break starts after the current question
  if (!S.blockEnd || !$("#breakov").hidden) return;
  const left = (S.blockEnd - Date.now()) / 1000;
  $("#clock").textContent = left > 0 ? `Focus ${mmss(left)}` : S.q ? "☕ Break after this question" : "☕ Break time";
  $("#clock").classList.toggle("due", left <= 0);
  $("#focus-ring-progress").setAttribute("stroke-dashoffset", String(100*(1-Math.max(0,Math.min(1,left/Math.max(1,S.blockSecs))))));
  if (left <= 0 && !S.q && !S.busy && S.sid) next();
}, 1000);

function renderQuestion(q, why, elapsed = 0) {
  if ('speechSynthesis' in window) speechSynthesis.cancel();
  S.questionAnswered = why.answered;
  S.q = q; S.picked = null;
  const card = el("div", "msg card");
  const meta = el("div", "meta");
  meta.append(el("span", "tag", why.subject), el("span", "tag", q.chapter), el("span", "tag " + why.level, why.level));
  meta.append(q.uploaded ? el("span", "tag Easy", "📄 Your question paper")
    : q.official ? el("span", "tag Easy", `✓ Real past paper · ${q.source.split(" (")[0]}`)
    : q.verified ? el("span", "tag Easy", "✓ Verified key · past-paper pattern")
    : el("span", "tag ai", "AI-generated · self-checked"));
  if (why.retry) meta.append(el("span", "tag", "Follow-up after a miss"));
  const opts = el("div", "opts");
  q.options.forEach((o, i) => {
    const b = el("button", "opt");
    b.append(el("b", "", "ABCD"[i]), el("span", "", o));
    b.onclick = () => pick(card, i);
    opts.append(b);
  });
  const conf = el("div", "conf");
  conf.append(el("span", "", "How sure are you?"));
  [["sure", "🟢 Sure"], ["unsure", "🟡 Unsure"], ["guess", "🔴 Guessing"]].forEach(([v, label]) => {
    const b = el("button", "btn", label);
    b.onclick = () => submit(card, v);
    conf.append(b);
  });
  const acts = el("div", "actions");
  const bookmark = el("button", "btn bookmark-question");
  bookmark.type = "button";
  bookmark.setAttribute("aria-pressed", String(!!q.bookmarked));
  bookmark.textContent = q.bookmarked ? "Bookmarked ✓" : "Bookmark";
  bookmark.title = "Save this question for later";
  bookmark.hidden = !q.bookmarked;  // shown once answered or skipped: a saved bookmark includes the answer
  const questionSession = S.sid;
  bookmark.onclick = async () => {
    const saved = bookmark.getAttribute("aria-pressed") === "true";
    bookmark.disabled = true;
    try {
      if (saved) {
        const response = await fetch('/api/bookmarks/' + encodeURIComponent(q.id), {method:'DELETE'});
        if (!response.ok) throw new Error('Could not remove bookmark.');
      } else {
        await api('/api/bookmarks', {session_id:questionSession, question_id:q.id});
      }
      bookmark.setAttribute("aria-pressed", String(!saved));
      bookmark.textContent = saved ? "Bookmark" : "Bookmarked ✓";
    } catch (error) { problem(error); }
    finally { bookmark.disabled = false; }
  };
  meta.append(bookmark);
  const readQuestion = speechButton(() => [q.q,...q.options.map((option,i)=>'ABCD'[i]+'. '+option)].join('. '));
  if(readQuestion)meta.append(readQuestion);
  const hb = el("button", "btn", "💡 Hint (lowers credit)");
  hb.onclick = () => hint(card);
  const sk = el("button", "btn", "Skip");
  sk.onclick = async () => {
    if (S.q !== q) return;
    S.q = null; clearInterval(S.timer);
    card.querySelectorAll("button:not(.bookmark-question):not(.read-aloud)").forEach(b => b.disabled = true);
    await api("/api/skip", { session_id: S.sid }).catch(() => {});  // skips feed fatigue detection
    bookmark.hidden = false;
    next();
  };
  acts.append(hb, sk);
  card.append(meta);
  clearInterval(S.timer);
  S.picks = []; S.limit = 0;
  if ($("#timed").checked) {  // exam mode: per-question countdown, auto-submits at zero
    S.limit = S.timeLimit;
    const bar = el("div", "tbar"), fill = el("div"), left = el("span", "tleft", `${S.limit}s`);
    bar.append(fill); card.append(bar, left);
    const end = Date.now() + Math.max(0, S.limit - elapsed) * 1000;
    S.timer = setInterval(() => {
      const s = Math.max(0, (end - Date.now()) / 1000);
      fill.style.width = (100 * s / S.limit) + "%";
      fill.classList.toggle("low", s < S.limit * 0.2);
      left.textContent = `${Math.ceil(s)}s left`;
      if (s <= 0) { clearInterval(S.timer); if (S.q === q) submit(card, S.picked === null ? "guess" : "unsure", true); }
    }, 250);
  }
  card.append(el("div", "qtext", q.q), opts, conf, acts);
  push(card);
}

function pick(card, i) {
  if (!S.q) return;
  S.picked = i;
  S.picks.push(i);
  card.querySelectorAll(".opt").forEach((b, j) => b.classList.toggle("picked", j === i));
  replay(card.querySelectorAll(".opt")[i], "press");
  card.querySelector(".conf").classList.add("show");
}

const calm = () => matchMedia("(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)").matches;
function replay(node, cls) { node.classList.remove(cls); void node.offsetWidth; node.classList.add(cls); }
function burst(node) {  // 5 small star sparks from an option
  if (calm()) return;
  for (let i = 0; i < 5; i++) {
    const sp = el("span", "spark", "⭐");
    sp.style.setProperty("--dx", `${(i - 2) * 22}px`); sp.style.animationDelay = `${i * 30}ms`;
    sp.onanimationend = () => sp.remove(); node.append(sp);
  }
}
function countUp(node, prefix, from, to) {  // Keep numeric feedback brief and optional.
  if (calm()) return;
  const t0 = performance.now();
  const step = t => { const k = Math.min(1, (t - t0) / 300);
    node.textContent = prefix + pct(from + (to - from) * (1 - (1 - k) ** 3)); if (k < 1) requestAnimationFrame(step); };
  requestAnimationFrame(step);
}

async function hint(card) {
  if (!S.q) return;
  const d = await api("/api/hint", { session_id: S.sid });
  card.querySelector(".actions").before(el("div", "hintline", d.hint ? `Hint ${d.used}: ${d.hint}` : "No more hints. Give it your best shot!"));
}

async function submit(card, confidence, timedOut = false) {
  if (!S.q || (S.picked === null && !timedOut)) return;
  const choice = S.picked ?? -1, q = S.q; S.q = null;
  clearInterval(S.timer);
  card.querySelectorAll("button:not(.bookmark-question):not(.read-aloud)").forEach(b => b.disabled = true);
  card.querySelector(".conf").classList.remove("show");
  let d;
  try {
    d = await api("/api/answer", { session_id: S.sid, choice, confidence, picks: S.picks,
                                   time_limit: S.limit, timed_out: timedOut });
  } catch (e) {
    if (e.offline) {  // keep the question so the student can just press their confidence again
      S.q = q;
      card.querySelectorAll("button:not(.bookmark-question):not(.read-aloud)").forEach(b => b.disabled = false);
      card.querySelector(".conf").classList.add("show");
    }
    return problem(e);
  }
  card.querySelector(".bookmark-question").hidden = false;
  const opts = card.querySelectorAll(".opt");
  opts[d.answer].classList.add("right");
  if (d.correct) { opts[d.answer].classList.add("wash"); burst(opts[d.answer]); }
  else {
    if (choice >= 0) { opts[choice].classList.add("wrong"); replay(opts[choice], "shake"); }
    setTimeout(() => opts[d.answer].classList.add("wash"), 150);
  }

  card.querySelector(".actions").style.display = "none";  // hint/skip are done; keep the answered card compact
  cheer(d.correct, timedOut && choice < 0);

  const r = el("div", "msg card result");
  r.append(el("h3", d.correct ? "ok" : "no", d.correct ? "✅ Correct"
    : timedOut && choice < 0 ? `⏱ Time's up · Answer ${"ABCD"[d.answer]}` : `❌ Answer ${"ABCD"[d.answer]}`));
  if (d.diagnosis) {  // one compact line: what kind of mistake + the fix
    const dg = el("p", "diag " + d.diagnosis.type);
    dg.append(el("strong", "", d.diagnosis.label + " · "), el("span", "", d.diagnosis.fix));
    r.append(dg);
  }
  r.append(el("div", "expl", d.explanation));
  const readExplanation=speechButton(()=>d.explanation);if(readExplanation)r.append(readExplanation);
  const ch = card.querySelector(".tag:nth-child(2)").textContent;
  const shown = (S.questionAnswered || 0) >= 3, est = `${ch}: estimate ${pct(d.readiness_before)} → `;
  const meta = [shown ? est + pct(d.readiness_after) : `${ch}: building your estimate`, `${d.secs}s`];
  if (d.guess) meta.push("counted as a guess");
  if (d.retry_next) meta.push("easier follow-up next");
  if (d.review_in) meta.push(`added to mistake review, back in ${inWords(d.review_in)}`);
  const eng = el("p", "engine");
  const estSpan = el("span", d.delta >= 0 ? "up" : "down", meta[0]);
  eng.append(estSpan, " · " + meta.slice(1).join(" · "));
  if (shown) countUp(estSpan, est, d.readiness_before, d.readiness_after);
  r.append(eng);
  const acts = el("div", "actions");
  const nb = el("button", "btn primary", "Next question →");
  nb.onclick = () => { nb.disabled = true; next(); };
  acts.append(nb);
  if (!d.correct && S.ai) {  // personalised AI explanation only when asked for
    const wb = el("button", "btn", "🧠 Why did I get this wrong?");
    wb.onclick = () => {
      wb.disabled = true; wb.textContent = "Thinking…";
      const box = el("div", "hintline");
      api("/api/explain", { session_id: S.sid })
        .then(x => { if (x.explanation) { math(box, x.explanation); const read=speechButton(()=>x.explanation);if(read)box.append(read);acts.before(box); } wb.remove(); })
        .catch(() => { wb.disabled = false; wb.textContent = "🧠 Why did I get this wrong?"; });
    };
    acts.append(wb);
  }
  const rb = el("button", "linkbtn", "⚑ Report question");
  const answeredSession = S.sid;
  rb.onclick = async () => {
    rb.disabled = true;
    try {
      await api("/api/report", { session_id: answeredSession, answer_id: d.answer_id });
      rb.textContent = "Reported. This answer no longer counts.";
      if (answeredSession === S.sid) await refreshDash();
    } catch (e) { rb.disabled = false; problem(e); }
  };
  acts.append(rb);
  r.append(acts);
  push(r);
  if (d.placement_done) {  // onboarding check finished: show the starting levels, then go to the hub
    try { S.prefs.placed = true; localStorage.setItem("pp-prefs", JSON.stringify(S.prefs)); } catch (e) {}
    const m = say("🧪 Placement check done. Your starting level: " +
      d.placement_done.map(x => `${x.subject} ${pct(x.readiness)}`).join(" · ") + ". I'll adapt from here.", "bot", "coach");
    const go = el("button", "btn primary", "Go to my study space →");
    go.onclick = () => { location.href = "/study"; };
    m.append(el("br"), go);
  }
  if (d.coach && !d.placement_done) say(d.coach, "bot", "coach");
  if (d.intervention) {
    const m = say((d.intervention.state === "frustrated" ? "💙 " : "☕ ") + d.intervention.message, "bot", "coach care");
    if (d.intervention.state === "tired") {
      const bb = el("button", "btn primary", "Take my break now");
      bb.onclick = () => { bb.disabled = true; api("/api/break", { session_id: S.sid }).catch(e => e.brk && showBreak(e.brk)); };
      m.append(el("br"), bb);
    }
  }
  refreshDash();
}

function renderWhy(w) {
  const p = $("#why");
  const previous = p.childNodes.length ? p.cloneNode(true) : null;
  p.innerHTML = "";
  p.append(el("h2", "", "Chosen chapter"), el("div", "big", w.chapter));
  if(!calm()) {
    if(previous){
      previous.removeAttribute('id');previous.setAttribute('aria-hidden','true');previous.inert=true;
      Object.assign(previous.style,{position:'absolute',inset:'0',pointerEvents:'none'});p.style.position='relative';p.append(previous);
      previous.animate([{opacity:1},{opacity:0}],{duration:150}).finished.then(()=>previous.remove(),()=>previous.remove());
    }
    p.animate([{opacity:0},{opacity:1}],{duration:150});
    p.querySelector('.big').animate([{transform:'scale(1)'},{transform:'scale(1.035)'},{transform:'scale(1)'}],{duration:250});
  }
  const parts = w.score_parts;
  const top = Object.entries(parts).filter(([k]) => k !== "repeat_penalty").sort((a, b) => b[1] - a[1])[0][0];
  let reason = w.placement ? `Placement check, question ${6 - w.placement} of 5: a medium question from ${w.subject}, to find your starting level.`
    : w.boost ? `${w.boost}: an easier question (about ${pct(w.p)} chance) from one of your stronger chapters, to rebuild momentum.`
    : w.retry ? `Your last ${w.chapter} answer looked like ${w.last_error ? `a "${w.last_error.toLowerCase()}"` : "a gap"}, so this is an easier follow-up on the same concept.`
    : top === "misconception" ? `You were confidently wrong here before, so it gets extra priority.`
    : w.answered === 0 ? `Starting estimate—answer a few questions to help me understand your ${w.subject} skills.`
    : w.answered < 3 ? "I’m still building your starting estimate. A few more answers will help guide your practice."
    : `Your recent answers suggest this chapter would benefit from more practice.`;
  p.append(el("p", "reason", reason));

  // the three numbers a student cares about; the engine internals live in a collapsible section
  const g = el("div", "grid");
  [["Readiness estimate", w.answered < 3 ? "Building…" : pct(w.readiness)], ["Answered here", w.answered], ["Level", w.level]]
    .forEach(([k, v]) => { const s = el("div", "stat"); s.append(el("small", "", k), el("strong", "", String(v))); g.append(s); });
  p.append(g);

  const det = el("details", "engine-details");
  det.open = S.engineOpen || false;
  det.ontoggle = () => { S.engineOpen = det.open; };
  det.append(el("summary", "", "Engine details"));
  p.append(det);
  const add = (...nodes) => det.append(...nodes);
  const g2 = el("div", "grid");
  [["Ability θ", w.ability.toFixed(2)], ["Target b", w.target_b.toFixed(2)], ["Question b", w.b.toFixed(2)],
   ["Answered here", w.answered]]
    .forEach(([k, v]) => { const s = el("div", "stat"); s.append(el("small", "", k), el("strong", "", String(v))); g2.append(s); });
  add(el("p", "legend", "Difficulty is set so you have about a " + pct(w.p) + " chance: hard enough to learn from, easy enough not to guess."), g2);

  add(el("h2", "", "Priority score breakdown"));
  const labels = { weakness: "Weakness", explore: "Exploration", misconception: "Misconception", repeat_penalty: "Recently asked" };
  Object.entries(parts).forEach(([k, v]) => {
    const row = el("div", "bar");
    const track = el("div", "track"), fill = el("div", "fill" + (v < 0 ? " neg" : ""));
    fill.style.width = Math.min(100, Math.abs(v) * 100) + "%";
    track.append(fill);
    row.append(el("span", "", labels[k]), track, el("span", "", v.toFixed(2)));
    add(row);
  });

  add(el("h2", "", `Top candidates (of ${w.pool} chapters)`));
  const ol = el("ol", "cands");
  w.candidates.forEach(c => { const li = el("li"); li.append(el("span", "", c.chapter), el("span", "", c.score.toFixed(2))); ol.append(li); });
  add(ol);
  if (!w.retry && w.chapter !== w.candidates[0].chapter)
    add(el("p", "legend", "Chosen from chapters tied within 0.05 of the top score, preferring one with a question ready."));
  add(el("p", "legend", `Source details: ${w.source}.`));
  const sourceLabel = S.q?.uploaded ? "Your paper · user-supplied answer key"
    : S.q?.official ? w.source : S.q?.verified ? "Original practice question · checked answer key"
    : "AI-generated · second-solve checked";
  p.append(el("p", "legend", sourceLabel));
}

function inWords(secs) {
  for (const [unit, name] of [[86400, "day"], [3600, "hour"], [60, "minute"]])
    if (secs >= unit * 0.95) { const n = Math.round(secs / unit); return `${n} ${name}${n === 1 ? "" : "s"}`; }
  return "under a minute";
}

// Live adaptation: skill estimate vs each question's difficulty, redrawn (animated) after every answer.
function drawAdapt(history) {
  const recent = history.slice(-20), last = history[history.length - 1], note = $("#adaptnote");
  $(".adapt-chart").hidden = history.length < 3;
  note.replaceChildren();
  if (history.length < 3) { note.textContent = `Answer ${3-history.length} more to draw your learning line`; return; }
  if (!last) { note.textContent = "Answer a question and watch the next one adjust to you."; return; }
  const up = last.delta >= 0;
  note.append(last.correct ? "✓ Right" : "✗ Missed", " → ", el("span", up ? "up" : "down",
    `${last.chapter} skill ${up ? "+" : "−"}${Math.abs(last.delta).toFixed(2)}`),
    up ? ". Its next question aims a little harder." : ". Its next question eases off.");
  if (last.delta) note.append(el("span", "adapt-chip " + (last.delta > 0 ? "harder" : "easier"), last.delta > 0 ? "↑ harder" : "↓ easier"));
  S.adaptHist = recent;
  if (!window.Chart) return;
  const points = recent.map(h => h.correct ? css("--good") : css("--bad"));
  if (S.charts.adapt) {
    const c = S.charts.adapt;
    c.data.labels = recent.map(h => h.i);
    c.data.datasets[0].data = recent.map(h => h.theta);
    c.data.datasets[1].data = recent.map(h => h.b);
    c.data.datasets[1].pointBackgroundColor = points;
    return c.update();
  }
  S.charts.adapt = new Chart($("#adaptcv"), {
    type: "line",
    data: { labels: recent.map(h => h.i), datasets: [
      { label: "Your skill", data: recent.map(h => h.theta), borderColor: css("--accent"), borderWidth: 2, tension: .35, pointRadius: 0 },
      { label: "Question difficulty", data: recent.map(h => h.b), showLine: false, pointRadius: 5, pointHoverRadius: 7,
        pointBackgroundColor: points, backgroundColor: css("--muted"), borderColor: "transparent" } ] },
    options: {
      maintainAspectRatio: false, animation: false,
      plugins: { legend: { labels: { color: css("--muted"), usePointStyle: true, boxWidth: 8, font: { size: 11 } } },
                 tooltip: { callbacks: { title: c => `Question ${c[0].label}`,
                   afterLabel: c => { const h = S.adaptHist[c.dataIndex]; return `${h.chapter} · ${h.correct ? "right" : "missed"}`; } } } },
      scales: { x: { ticks: { color: css("--muted"), font: { family: "DM Sans", size: 10 } }, grid: { display: false } },
                y: { suggestedMin:-2, suggestedMax:2, ticks: { color: css("--muted"), font: { family: "DM Sans", size: 10 } }, grid: { color: "rgba(143, 168, 255, 0.22)" } } },
    },
  });
}

async function refreshDash() {
  const d = await api(`/api/dashboard?session_id=${S.sid}`);
  updateSide(d);
  drawAdapt(d.history);
  const p = $("#dash");
  p.innerHTML = "";
  const pr = d.predicted, mk = d.marking;
  $("#pred").textContent = `Score estimate: ${pct(pr.with_strategy)}${mk ? " of max marks" : ""}`;
  $("#pred").hidden = d.stats.answered < 5;

  // Compact Progress tab: numbers first, one short line per idea, details only where they help a decision.
  const g = el("div", "grid");
  [["Answered", d.stats.answered], ["Accuracy", pct(d.stats.accuracy)], ["Avg time", d.stats.avg_secs + "s"]]
    .forEach(([k, v]) => { const s = el("div", "stat"); s.append(el("small", "", k), el("strong", "", String(v))); g.append(s); });
  p.append(el("h2", "", "This session"), g);

  // predicted score
  p.append(el("h2", "", "Score estimate"));
  if (d.stats.answered < 5) p.append(el("p", "legend", `Shows after 5 answers (${5 - d.stats.answered} to go).`));
  else {
    const ps = el("div", "predict");
    ps.append(el("div", "big", pct(pr.with_strategy)), el("div", "legend", mk ? `of max marks · +${mk[0]} / −${mk[1]} marking` : "predicted score"));
    if (pr.strategy_gain > 0.005) ps.append(el("div", "up", `+${pct(pr.strategy_gain)} by skipping risky questions`));
    p.append(ps);
    if (d.predicted_log.length > 1) {
      const box = el("div", "chartbox small"), cv = el("canvas");
      box.append(cv); p.append(box);
      if (S.charts.pred) S.charts.pred.destroy();
      if (window.Chart) S.charts.pred = new Chart(cv, {
        type: "line",
        data: { labels: d.predicted_log.map((_, i) => i + 1),
                datasets: [{ data: d.predicted_log.map(x => Math.round(x * 1000) / 10), borderColor: css("--accent"), tension: .3, pointRadius: 0 }] },
        options: { maintainAspectRatio: false, animation: false, plugins: { legend: { display: false } },
                   scales: { x: { ticks: { color: css("--muted") }, grid: { display: false } },
                             y: { ticks: { color: css("--muted"), callback: v => v + "%" }, grid: { color: "rgba(143, 168, 255, 0.22)" } } } },
      });
    }
  }

  // focus / fatigue: one line
  const wb = d.wellbeing;
  const fm = el("div", "focusrow " + wb.state);
  fm.append(el("strong", "", { ok: "🟢 Focused", tired: "🟠 Getting tired", frustrated: "🔴 Frustrated" }[wb.state]),
            el("span", "", wb.reasons.length ? wb.reasons.join("; ") : "Steady speed and accuracy."));
  p.append(el("h2", "", "Focus"), fm);

  // why the student is wrong
  const wrong = d.errors.reduce((a, e) => a + e.n, 0);
  p.append(el("h2", "", "Mistakes"));
  if (!wrong) p.append(el("p", "legend", "No wrong answers yet."));
  d.errors.filter(e => e.n).sort((a, b) => b.n - a.n).forEach(e => {
    const row = el("div", "bar"), track = el("div", "track"), fill = el("div", "fill err-" + e.type);
    fill.style.width = (100 * e.n / wrong) + "%"; track.append(fill);
    row.append(el("span", "", e.label), track, el("span", "", String(e.n)));
    p.append(row);
  });
  const top = d.errors.filter(e => e.n).sort((a, b) => b.n - a.n)[0];
  if (top) p.append(el("p", "legend", "Tip: " + top.fix.split(".")[0] + "."));

  // confidence calibration + negative-marking strategy, as a 3-column verdict
  p.append(el("h2", "", "Confidence vs reality"));
  if (!d.calibration.length) p.append(el("p", "legend", "Rate how sure you are after each answer to see this."));
  else {
    const t = el("table", "calib");
    const hr = el("tr");
    ["You said", "Right", "In the exam"].forEach(h => hr.append(el("th", "", h)));
    t.append(hr);
    const name = { sure: "🟢 Sure", unsure: "🟡 Unsure", guess: "🔴 Guessing" };
    d.calibration.forEach(c => {
      const tr = el("tr");
      tr.append(el("td", "", `${name[c.confidence]} (${c.n})`), el("td", "", pct(c.accuracy)),
                el("td", c.expected >= 0 ? "up" : "down", c.expected >= 0 ? "Attempt" : "Skip"));
      t.append(tr);
    });
    p.append(t);
    if (mk) p.append(el("p", "legend", `Attempt only if you're right over ${pct(mk[1] / (mk[0] + mk[1]))} of the time.`));
  }

  p.append(el("h2", "", "Subject readiness"));
  d.subjects.forEach(s => {
    const row = el("div", "bar"), track = el("div", "track"), fill = el("div", "fill");
    fill.style.width = pct(s.readiness); fill.style.background = readinessColor(s.readiness); track.append(fill);
    const subjectAttempts = d.chapters.filter(c => c.subject === s.subject).reduce((sum, c) => sum + c.n, 0);
    if (subjectAttempts < 3) fill.style.width = "0%";
    row.append(el("span", "", s.subject), track, el("span", "", subjectAttempts < 3 ? "Starting" : pct(s.readiness)));
    p.append(row);
  });

  p.append(el("h2", "", "Ability vs difficulty"));
  const box = el("div", "chartbox"), cv = el("canvas");
  box.append(cv); p.append(box);
  if (S.charts.theta) S.charts.theta.destroy();
  if (window.Chart) S.charts.theta = new Chart(cv, {
    type: "line",
    data: {
      labels: d.history.map(h => h.i),
      datasets: [
        { label: "Your ability", data: d.history.map(h => h.theta), borderColor: css("--accent"), tension: .3, pointRadius: 0 },
        { label: "Question difficulty", data: d.history.map(h => h.b), showLine: false, pointRadius: 5,
          pointBackgroundColor: d.history.map(h => h.correct ? css("--good") : css("--bad")), borderColor: "transparent" },
      ],
    },
    options: {
      maintainAspectRatio: false, animation: false,
      plugins: { legend: { labels: { color: css("--muted"), boxWidth: 12 } },
                 tooltip: { callbacks: { afterLabel: c => d.history[c.dataIndex].chapter } } },
      scales: { x: { ticks: { color: css("--muted") }, grid: { display: false } },
                y: { ticks: { color: css("--muted") }, grid: { color: "rgba(143, 168, 255, 0.22)" } } },
    },
  });

  // where study time pays most: top 3 names only
  p.append(el("h2", "", "Study next"));
  const ul2 = el("ol", "cands");
  d.gains.slice(0, 3).forEach(gn => { const li = el("li"); li.append(el("span", "", gn.chapter), el("span", "up", `+${gn.gain.toFixed(2)}`)); ul2.append(li); });
  p.append(ul2, el("p", "legend", mk ? "Marks gained per question as you improve." : "Accuracy gained per question as you improve."));

  p.append(el("h2", "", "Syllabus map"));
  const map = el("div", "map");
  const bySubj = {};
  d.chapters.forEach(c => (bySubj[c.subject] ||= []).push(c));
  Object.entries(bySubj).forEach(([s, chs]) => {
    map.append(el("h3", "", s));
    const tiles = el("div", "tiles");
    chs.forEach(c => {
      const t = el("button", "tile" + (c.n ? "" : " prior") + (c.flags ? " flag" : "") + (S.focus === c.chapter ? " focus" : ""));
      t.style.background = readinessColor(c.readiness);
      t.title = `${c.chapter}: ${pct(c.readiness)} ready${c.n ? `, ${c.n} answered` : " (estimate from subject prior)"}`;
      t.setAttribute("aria-label", t.title);
      t.onclick = () => setFocus(c.chapter);
      tiles.append(t);
    });
    map.append(tiles);
  });
  map.append(el("p", "legend", "Colours show model estimates. Faded chapters have not been assessed; a red ring marks a confident wrong answer."));
  p.append(map);
  const full = el("a", "btn", "Open full progress page →"); full.href = "/progress"; full.style.marginTop = "16px";
  p.append(full);
}

async function setFocus(f) {
  try {
    const d = await api("/api/focus", { session_id: S.sid, focus: f });
    S.focus = f;
    markFocus();
    document.body.classList.remove("side-open");
    say(f === "all" ? `Focus: whole syllabus (${d.pool} chapters).` : `Focus: ${f} (${d.pool} chapter${d.pool === 1 ? "" : "s"}).`);
    // swap an unanswered question for one from the new focus
    if (S.q) { S.q = null; clearInterval(S.timer); [...document.querySelectorAll(".msg.card:not(.result)")].pop().querySelectorAll("button:not(.bookmark-question):not(.read-aloud)").forEach(b => b.disabled = true); }
    next();
  } catch (e) { say("⚠ " + e.message); }
}

function tab(name) {
  document.querySelectorAll(".tabs button").forEach(b => b.classList.toggle("on", b.dataset.tab === name));
  document.querySelectorAll(".panel").forEach(p => p.classList.toggle("on", p.id === name));
}

$("#ask").onsubmit = async e => {
  e.preventDefault();
  const text = $("#input").value.trim();
  if (!text) return;
  $("#input").value = "";
  say(text, "user");
  const [cmd, ...rest] = text.split(" ");
  const arg = rest.join(" ").trim();
  const card = [...document.querySelectorAll(".msg.card:not(.result)")].pop();
  if (cmd === "/hint") return S.q ? hint(card) : say("No active question.");
  if (cmd === "/skip" || cmd === "/next") return S.q ? card.querySelector(".actions .btn:last-child").click() : next();
  if (cmd === "/focus") return setFocus(arg || "all");
  if (cmd === "/dashboard") return tab("dash");
  if (cmd === "/why") return tab("why");
  if (cmd === "/sim") return (location.href = "/progress");
  const wait = say("Thinking…", "bot", "muted");
  try { const d = await api("/api/ask", { session_id: S.sid, text }); wait.remove(); say(d.reply); }
  catch (err) { wait.remove(); say("⚠ " + err.message); }
};

document.querySelectorAll(".tabs button").forEach(b => b.onclick = () => tab(b.dataset.tab));
$("#level").onchange = e => start(e.target.value);
$("#sidebtn").onclick = () => document.body.classList.toggle("side-open");

// ---------- after a break: continue, or switch to the recommended chapter ----------
async function breakChoice() {
  const m = say(`Break over. New ${mmss(S.blockSecs)} focus block starts now.`, "bot", "coach");
  const row = el("div", "actions");
  const cont = el("button", "btn primary", S.focus === "all" ? "Continue →" : `Continue ${S.focus} →`);
  cont.onclick = () => { row.querySelectorAll("button").forEach(b => b.disabled = true); next(); };
  row.append(cont);
  m.append(row);
  try {
    const b = await api(`/api/brief?${new URLSearchParams({ level: S.exam, session_id: S.sid })}`);
    const rc = b.recommended;
    if (rc && rc.chapter !== S.focus) {
      const sw = el("button", "btn", `Switch to ${rc.chapter} (recommended)`);
      sw.onclick = () => { row.querySelectorAll("button").forEach(x => x.disabled = true); setFocus(rc.chapter); };
      row.append(sw);
    }
  } catch (e) {}
}

// ---------- end of session: what changed, the main mistake pattern, what to do next ----------
async function showSummary() {
  let d;
  try { d = await api(`/api/summary?session_id=${S.sid}`); } catch (e) { return problem(e); }
  const box = $("#sumbody");
  box.replaceChildren();
  if (!d.answered) {
    box.append(el("p", "reason", "You haven't answered anything yet this session. Answer a few questions and come back to see your progress."));
  } else {
    const g = el("div", "grid");
    [["Answered", d.answered], ["Correct", pct(d.correct / d.answered)], ["Time", `${d.minutes} min`]]
      .forEach(([k, v]) => { const st = el("div", "stat"); st.append(el("small", "", k), el("strong", "", String(v))); g.append(st); });
    box.append(g);
    if (d.predicted.start !== null) {
      const diff = d.predicted.now - d.predicted.start;
      box.append(el("p", "reason", `Predicted score: ${pct(d.predicted.start)} → ${pct(d.predicted.now)}` +
        (Math.abs(diff) >= 0.005 ? ` (${diff > 0 ? "+" : "−"}${pct(Math.abs(diff))})` : "")));
    }
    box.append(el("h2", "", "Chapters you practised"));
    d.chapters.slice(0, 5).forEach(c => {
      const row = el("div", "bar"), track = el("div", "track"), fill = el("div", "fill");
      fill.style.width = pct(c.after); track.append(fill);
      row.append(el("span", "", c.chapter), track, el("span", c.after >= c.before ? "up" : "down", `${pct(c.before)}→${pct(c.after)}`));
      box.append(row);
    });
    if (d.top_error) {
      box.append(el("h2", "", "Watch out for"));
      const tip = el("p", "diag " + d.top_error.type);
      tip.append(el("strong", "", `${d.top_error.label} (${d.top_error.n}×) · `), el("span", "", d.top_error.fix));
      box.append(tip);
    }
  }
  const acts = $("#sumacts");
  acts.replaceChildren();
  const image=el('button','btn','Save as image');image.type='button';
  image.onclick=async()=>{
    image.disabled=true;
    try{await saveSummaryImage(d);}catch(error){box.append(el('p','reason','Could not save the image. Please try again.'));}
    finally{image.disabled=false;}
  };acts.append(image);
  if (d.recommended) {
    const nb = el("button", "btn primary", `Study ${d.recommended.chapter} next`);
    nb.onclick = () => { $("#sumov").hidden = true; setFocus(d.recommended.chapter); };
    acts.append(nb);
  }
  const home = el("a", "btn", "🏠 Back to home"); home.href = "/study";
  const keep = el("button", "btn", "Keep practising");
  keep.onclick = () => { $("#sumov").hidden = true; };
  acts.append(home, keep);
  $("#sumov").hidden = false;
}
$("#endbtn").onclick = () => { $("#quiztools").open = false; showSummary(); };


// ---------- account: signed-in user or guest; anyone else goes to /login ----------
async function signOut() {
  try {
    const response = await fetch("/api/logout", {method:"POST"});
    if (!response.ok) throw new Error("Could not sign out. Please try again.");
    ["pp-user", "pp-token", "pp-prefs", "pp-papers"].forEach(k => localStorage.removeItem(k));
    location.href = "/login";
  } catch (error) { problem(error); }
}
$("#userbtn").onclick = e => {
  e.stopPropagation();
  $("#quiztools").open = false;
  const m = $("#usermenu");
  m.hidden = !m.hidden;
  $("#userbtn").setAttribute("aria-expanded", String(!m.hidden));
};
document.addEventListener("click", e => { if (!e.target.closest(".user")) $("#usermenu").hidden = true; });
$("#logout").onclick = signOut;

window.addEventListener("load", async () => {
  let account;
  try { account = await restoreAccount(); } catch (error) { problem(error); return; }
  if (!account) return;
  S.user = account.user; S.prefs = account.preferences;
  if (!S.prefs) { location.replace("/setup"); return; }
  $("#timed").checked = !!S.prefs.timed;
  $("#userbtn").textContent = (S.user.name || "?").trim()[0].toUpperCase();
  $("#uname").textContent = S.user.name;
  $("#uemail").textContent = S.user.guest ? "Guest practice · this browser" : S.user.email;
  $("#logout").textContent = S.user.guest ? "Sign in / create account" : "Log out";
  // uploaded question papers appear in the level picker as their own group
  let mine = [];
  try { mine = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem("pp-papers") || "[]"); } catch (e) {}
  if (mine.length) {
    const g = document.createElement("optgroup"); g.label = "My question papers";
    mine.forEach(p => g.append(new Option("📄 " + p.name, p.level)));
    $("#level").append(g);
  }
  let lv = "JEE";
  try { lv = localStorage.getItem("pp-level") || lv; } catch (e) {}
  if (![...$("#level").options].some(o => o.value === lv)) lv = "JEE";
  $("#level").value = lv;
  const url = new URL(location.href);
  const fresh = url.searchParams.has("new");
  url.searchParams.delete("new");
  start(lv, fresh).then(() => history.replaceState(null, "", url)).catch(e => problem(e));
});
$("#quiztools").addEventListener("toggle", () => { if ($("#quiztools").open) $("#usermenu").hidden = true; });
document.addEventListener("click", e => { if (!e.target.closest("#quiztools")) $("#quiztools").open = false; });
document.addEventListener("keydown", e => {
  if (e.key === "Escape" && $("#quiztools").open) { $("#quiztools").open = false; $("#quiztools summary").focus(); }
  else if (e.key === "Escape" && !$("#usermenu").hidden) { $("#usermenu").hidden = true; $("#userbtn").focus(); }
});
$("#timed").onchange = async e => {
  const previous = S.prefs.timed;
  S.prefs.timed = e.target.checked;
  try { await savePreferences(S.prefs); }
  catch (error) { S.prefs.timed = previous; e.target.checked = previous; problem(error); }
};
$("#showbuddy").onchange = e => {
  $("#buddy").hidden = !e.target.checked;
  try { localStorage.setItem("pp-buddy-visible", String(e.target.checked)); } catch (error) {}
};
try { $("#showbuddy").checked = localStorage.getItem("pp-buddy-visible") !== "false"; } catch (error) {}
$("#buddy").hidden = !$("#showbuddy").checked;
function markProvider(id) {
  document.querySelectorAll("#aiprovider button").forEach(b => b.setAttribute("aria-checked", String(b.dataset.value === id)));
}
function enableProviders(on) {
  document.querySelectorAll("#aiprovider button").forEach(b => { b.disabled = !on || b.dataset.available !== "true"; });
}
async function switchProvider(provider, label) {
  const previous = S.prefs.ai_provider, sid = S.sid;
  if (provider === previous) return;
  enableProviders(false); markProvider(provider);
  try {
    const d = await api("/api/provider", {session_id: sid, ai_provider: provider});
    if (sid !== S.sid) return;
    S.ai = d.ai; S.prefs.ai_provider = d.ai_provider;
    try {
      localStorage.setItem("pp-prefs", JSON.stringify(S.prefs));
      if (S.user.email && !S.user.guest) localStorage.setItem("pp-prefs:" + S.user.email, JSON.stringify(S.prefs));
      const key = "pp-session:" + JSON.stringify([S.user?.email || "guest", S.exam, DEMO, S.prefs]);
      localStorage.setItem(key, sid);
    } catch (storageError) { /* The active selection still works when browser storage is unavailable. */ }
    $("#ai").textContent = S.paper ? "Your paper only" : d.ai ? "AI: " + label + " on" : "AI: off (bank only)";
    $("#ai").classList.toggle("on", d.ai);
    say("AI tutor: " + label + ". Your current question and learning progress are kept.");
    refreshDash();
  } catch (error) { markProvider(previous); problem(error); }
  finally { enableProviders(true); }
}
$("#breaknow").onclick = () => { $("#quiztools").open = false; return S.sid && api("/api/break", { session_id: S.sid }).catch(e => e.brk && showBreak(e.brk)); };

// Painted chibi study buddy: one picture per mood, CSS motion, reacts to clicks and to every answer
const CHEERS = {
  right: ["Very good, you got that right!", "Nailed it! 🎉", "Yes! Your brain is warming up.", "Correct! Keep going.", "Sharp! 💡"],
  streak: ["That's {n} in a row! 🔥", "{n} straight. You're on fire! 🔥", "Unstoppable: {n} in a row!"],
  wrong: ["Don't worry, you'll get it next time.", "Mistakes are how we learn. 💪", "Close one. Read the fix and try the next!",
          "That one was tricky. You've got this.", "No stress, every expert missed this once."],
  time: ["Time ran out, that's okay. Take a breath. 🌱", "No rush, you'll be quicker next time."],
  poke: ["Hehe, that tickles!", "Wheee!", "Hi! Ready for the next one?", "You're doing great!", "Let's learn something cool!", "Boop! 👋"],
};
const BUDDIES = { pip: "Pip", mika: "Mika", leo: "Leo" };
let streak = 0, sayTimer, moveTimer, pokes = 0;
const anyOf = a => a[Math.floor(Math.random() * a.length)];
const pose = mood => document.querySelectorAll("#chibi .pose").forEach(img => img.classList.toggle("on", img.dataset.mood === mood));
function buddySay(line) {
  $("#bubble").textContent = line; $("#bubble").classList.add("show");
  clearTimeout(sayTimer); sayTimer = setTimeout(() => $("#bubble").classList.remove("show"), 4500);
}
function act(move, mood = "happy", sparks = 0) {
  const b = $("#buddy");
  b.classList.remove("jump", "dance", "giggle", "squish", "sad", "land"); void b.offsetWidth;  // restart the animation
  b.classList.add(move); pose(mood);
  for (let i = 0; i < sparks; i++) {
    const sp = el("span", "spark", anyOf(["✨", "⭐", "💖", "🎉"]));
    sp.style.setProperty("--dx", `${Math.round(Math.random() * 70 - 35)}px`); sp.style.animationDelay = `${i * 90}ms`;
    sp.onanimationend = () => sp.remove(); $(".chibiwrap").append(sp);
  }
  clearTimeout(moveTimer);
  moveTimer = setTimeout(() => { b.classList.remove(move); pose("idle"); }, mood === "sad" ? 3000 : 1900);
}
function cheer(correct, timeUp) {
  streak = correct ? streak + 1 : 0;
  if (!correct) { act("sad", "sad"); return buddySay(anyOf(timeUp ? CHEERS.time : CHEERS.wrong)); }
  const onFire = streak >= 3 && streak % 2;
  act(onFire ? "dance" : "jump", "happy", onFire ? 6 : 3);
  buddySay(onFire ? anyOf(CHEERS.streak).replace("{n}", streak) : anyOf(CHEERS.right));
}
function setBuddy(id) {
  $("#buddy").dataset.char = id; pose("idle");
  $("#chibi").setAttribute("aria-label", `${BUDDIES[id]}, your study buddy. Click to play, drag or use arrow keys to move.`);
  // all three poses stay loaded and stacked, so a mood change is a crossfade, never a flash
  document.querySelectorAll("#chibi .pose").forEach(img => { img.src = `/buddy/${id}-${img.dataset.mood}.png?v=2`; });
  try { localStorage.setItem("pp-buddy", id); } catch {}
}
let dragged = false;
$("#chibi").onclick = () => {
  if (dragged) { dragged = false; return; }  // the click that ends a drag is not a poke
  act(["jump", "giggle", "squish", "dance"][pokes++ % 4], "happy", 2);
  buddySay(pokes === 1 ? `Hi, I'm ${BUDDIES[$("#buddy").dataset.char]}! 👋` : anyOf(CHEERS.poke));
};
$("#chibi").onmousedown = e => e.preventDefault();  // no focus ring on mouse clicks, only for keyboard users
$("#charbtn").onclick = () => {
  const ids = Object.keys(BUDDIES), next = ids[(ids.indexOf($("#buddy").dataset.char) + 1) % ids.length];
  setBuddy(next); act("squish", "happy", 3); buddySay(`Hi, I'm ${BUDDIES[next]}! 👋`);
};
// Drag the buddy anywhere (mouse, touch or pen), or move it with arrow keys; the spot is remembered per browser.
function placeBuddy(x, y, remember = true) {
  const b = $("#buddy"), r = b.getBoundingClientRect();
  x = Math.max(0, Math.min(innerWidth - r.width, x)); y = Math.max(0, Math.min(innerHeight - r.height, y));
  Object.assign(b.style, { left: `${x}px`, top: `${y}px`, right: "auto", bottom: "auto" });
  b.classList.toggle("flip", x + r.width / 2 < innerWidth / 2);  // speech bubble on the side with room
  if (remember) try { localStorage.setItem("pp-buddy-pos", JSON.stringify({ x: x / innerWidth, y: y / innerHeight })); } catch {}
}
let drag = null, tiltTimer;
$("#chibi").addEventListener("pointerdown", e => {
  if (e.button !== 0) return;
  const r = $("#buddy").getBoundingClientRect();
  drag = { id: e.pointerId, sx: e.clientX, sy: e.clientY, dx: e.clientX - r.left, dy: e.clientY - r.top, lastX: e.clientX, moving: false };
  $("#chibi").setPointerCapture(e.pointerId);  // keep getting moves even if a fast flick leaves the buddy
});
$("#chibi").addEventListener("pointermove", e => {
  if (!drag || e.pointerId !== drag.id) return;
  if (!drag.moving) {
    if (Math.hypot(e.clientX - drag.sx, e.clientY - drag.sy) < 6) return;  // small wobble is still a click
    drag.moving = true; $("#buddy").classList.add("dragging");
  }
  placeBuddy(e.clientX - drag.dx, e.clientY - drag.dy, false);
  const tilt = Math.max(-14, Math.min(14, (e.clientX - drag.lastX) * 1.2));  // swings with the motion, like being carried
  drag.lastX = e.clientX;
  $(".sprite").style.setProperty("--tilt", `${tilt.toFixed(1)}deg`);
  clearTimeout(tiltTimer); tiltTimer = setTimeout(() => $(".sprite").style.setProperty("--tilt", "0deg"), 90);
});
const endDrag = e => {
  if (!drag || e.pointerId !== drag.id) return;
  if (drag.moving) {
    dragged = true; $("#buddy").classList.remove("dragging"); $(".sprite").style.removeProperty("--tilt");
    const r = $("#buddy").getBoundingClientRect(); placeBuddy(r.left, r.top);
    act("land", "idle");
  }
  drag = null;
};
$("#chibi").addEventListener("pointerup", endDrag);
$("#chibi").addEventListener("pointercancel", endDrag);
$("#chibi").addEventListener("keydown", e => {
  const step = e.shiftKey ? 60 : 20, move = { ArrowLeft: [-step, 0], ArrowRight: [step, 0], ArrowUp: [0, -step], ArrowDown: [0, step] }[e.key];
  if (!move) return;
  e.preventDefault();
  const r = $("#buddy").getBoundingClientRect(); placeBuddy(r.left + move[0], r.top + move[1]);
});
function restoreBuddyPlace() {
  let spot; try { spot = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem("pp-buddy-pos") || "null"); } catch {}
  if (spot) placeBuddy(spot.x * innerWidth, spot.y * innerHeight, false);
}
$("#buddyhome").onclick = () => {
  try { localStorage.removeItem("pp-buddy-pos"); } catch {}
  Object.assign($("#buddy").style, { left: "", top: "", right: "", bottom: "" }); $("#buddy").classList.remove("flip");
  $("#quiztools").open = false; act("squish", "happy");
};
addEventListener("resize", restoreBuddyPlace);
let saved; try { saved = localStorage.getItem("pp-buddy"); } catch {}
setBuddy(BUDDIES[saved] ? saved : "pip");
restoreBuddyPlace();
document.querySelector("#chibi .pose").addEventListener("load", restoreBuddyPlace);  // size is known once the picture loads
let leanFrame, scrollBias = 0;
$("#log").addEventListener("scroll", () => {
  if (matchMedia("(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)").matches) return;
  scrollBias = Math.max(-3, Math.min(3, -($("#log").scrollTop % 120) / 40));
  $("#buddy").style.setProperty("--py", `${scrollBias.toFixed(1)}px`);
}, { passive: true });
document.addEventListener("pointermove", e => {  // leans and shifts a little toward the pointer
  if (leanFrame || drag || matchMedia("(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)").matches) return;
  leanFrame = requestAnimationFrame(() => {
    leanFrame = 0;
    const r = $("#buddy").getBoundingClientRect(),
          dx = e.clientX - (r.left + r.width / 2),
          dy = e.clientY - (r.top + r.height / 2);
    $("#chibi").style.setProperty("--lean", `${Math.max(-5, Math.min(5, dx / 80)).toFixed(1)}deg`);
    $("#buddy").style.setProperty("--px", `${Math.max(-4.5, Math.min(4.5, dx / 95)).toFixed(1)}px`);
    $("#buddy").style.setProperty("--py", `${Math.max(-3.5, Math.min(3.5, dy / 120 + scrollBias)).toFixed(1)}px`);
  });
}, { passive: true });
</script>
<div id="sumov" hidden role="dialog" aria-modal="true" aria-labelledby="sumtitle">

<script>
const $ = id => document.getElementById(id);
const params = new URLSearchParams(location.search);
let mode = "signin";
let panelOpen = false;
let lastTrigger = null;

const heroStage = $("hero-stage");
const galaxyViewport = $("galaxy-viewport");
const galaxyHost = $("galaxy-demo");
const authPanel = $("auth-panel");
const panelBackdrop = $("panel-backdrop");
const openSigninBtn = $("open-signin");
const openSignupBtn = $("open-signup");
const closePanelBtn = $("close-panel");

function isMobileSheet() {
  return window.innerWidth <= 768;
}

function isReducedMotion() {
  return Boolean(window.matchMedia && window.matchMedia("(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)").matches);
}

function syncPanelA11y() {
  if (!panelOpen) {
    authPanel.setAttribute("aria-hidden", "true");
    authPanel.inert = true;
    authPanel.removeAttribute("aria-modal");
    authPanel.setAttribute("role", "complementary");
    return;
  }
  authPanel.setAttribute("aria-hidden", "false");
  authPanel.inert = false;
  if (isMobileSheet()) {
    authPanel.setAttribute("role", "dialog");
    authPanel.setAttribute("aria-modal", "true");
  } else {
    authPanel.setAttribute("role", "complementary");
    authPanel.removeAttribute("aria-modal");
  }
}

function setMode(m) {
  mode = m;
  $("tab-signin").classList.toggle("active", m === "signin");
  $("tab-signin").setAttribute("aria-selected", String(m === "signin"));
  $("tab-signup").classList.toggle("active", m === "signup");
  $("tab-signup").setAttribute("aria-selected", String(m === "signup"));
  $("signup-fields").hidden = m !== "signup";
  $("level-field").hidden = m !== "signup";
  $("password").autocomplete = m === "signup" ? "new-password" : "current-password";
  $("submit").textContent = m === "signup" ? "Create account →" : "Sign in →";
  $("panel-heading").textContent = m === "signup" ? "Create your account" : "Welcome back";
  $("panel-sub").textContent = m === "signup" ? "Free forever. Takes 20 seconds." : "Sign in to continue your practice.";
  showError("");
}

function refitGalaxyForState(animate) {
  const fullW = heroStage.clientWidth || window.innerWidth;
  const targetWidth = panelOpen && !isMobileSheet() ? Math.max(320, fullW - 420) : fullW;
  if (window.PrepPilotGalaxy && typeof window.PrepPilotGalaxy.refit === "function") {
    window.PrepPilotGalaxy.refit(galaxyHost, {
      targetWidth,
      animate: Boolean(animate && !isReducedMotion()),
      duration: 300,
      snap: !animate || isReducedMotion()
    });
  }
}

let animTimer = null;
function markAnimating(animate) {
  clearTimeout(animTimer);
  if (!animate || isReducedMotion()) {
    heroStage.classList.remove("is-animating");
    return;
  }
  heroStage.classList.add("is-animating");
  animTimer = setTimeout(() => heroStage.classList.remove("is-animating"), 340);
}

function openPanel(targetMode, triggerEl = null, animate = true) {
  if (targetMode) setMode(targetMode);
  if (triggerEl) lastTrigger = triggerEl;
  panelOpen = true;
  markAnimating(animate);
  heroStage.classList.add("panel-open");
  syncPanelA11y();
  refitGalaxyForState(animate);

  const firstInput = mode === "signup" ? $("name") : $("email");
  if (firstInput) firstInput.focus({ preventScroll: true });
}

syncPanelA11y();

function closePanel(animate = true) {
  if (!panelOpen) return;
  panelOpen = false;
  markAnimating(animate);
  heroStage.classList.remove("panel-open");
  syncPanelA11y();
  refitGalaxyForState(animate);

  if (lastTrigger && typeof lastTrigger.focus === "function") {
    lastTrigger.focus({ preventScroll: true });
  }
}

openSigninBtn.onclick = () => openPanel("signin", openSigninBtn, true);
openSignupBtn.onclick = () => openPanel("signup", openSignupBtn, true);
closePanelBtn.onclick = () => closePanel(true);
panelBackdrop.onclick = () => closePanel(true);

/* Clicking dimmed/empty background area on the galaxy viewport when panel is open also restores full-width galaxy */
let stageDownPos = null;
galaxyViewport.addEventListener("pointerdown", e => {
  if (!panelOpen) return;
  if (e.target.closest("#buddy") || e.target.closest(".galaxy-planet-label") || e.target.closest(".galaxy-panel") || e.target.closest(".galaxy-reset")) {
    stageDownPos = null;
    return;
  }
  stageDownPos = { x: e.clientX, y: e.clientY };
});
galaxyViewport.addEventListener("pointerup", e => {
  if (!panelOpen || !stageDownPos) return;
  const dist = Math.hypot(e.clientX - stageDownPos.x, e.clientY - stageDownPos.y);
  stageDownPos = null;
  if (dist < 5 && e.target.classList.contains("galaxy-canvas")) {
    closePanel(true);
  }
});

$("tab-signin").onclick = () => {
  setMode("signin");
  $("email").focus({ preventScroll: true });
};
$("tab-signup").onclick = () => {
  setMode("signup");
  $("name").focus({ preventScroll: true });
};

/* Esc closes the panel; Tab traps focus when the panel covers content on phones */
window.addEventListener("keydown", e => {
  if (!panelOpen) return;
  if (e.key === "Escape") {
    e.preventDefault();
    closePanel(true);
    return;
  }
  if (e.key === "Tab" && isMobileSheet()) {
    const focusables = [...authPanel.querySelectorAll(
      'button:not([hidden]):not([disabled]), input:not([hidden]):not([disabled]), select:not([hidden]):not([disabled]), [tabindex]:not([tabindex="-1"])'
    )].filter(el => el.offsetParent !== null);
    if (!focusables.length) return;
    const first = focusables[0];
    const last = focusables[focusables.length - 1];
    if (e.shiftKey && document.activeElement === first) {
      e.preventDefault();
      last.focus();
    } else if (!e.shiftKey && document.activeElement === last) {
      e.preventDefault();
      first.focus();
    }
  }
});

window.addEventListener("resize", () => {
  if (panelOpen) syncPanelA11y();
  refitGalaxyForState(false);
});

function showError(msg) {
  const el = $("err");
  el.textContent = msg;
  el.classList.toggle("show", Boolean(msg));
}

$("form").onsubmit = async (e) => {
  e.preventDefault();
  const email = $("email").value.trim(), password = $("password").value, name = $("name").value.trim();
  if (mode === "signup" && !name) return showError("Enter your name.");
  if (!/^[^@\s]+@[^@\s]+\.[^@\s]+$/.test(email)) return showError("Enter a valid email address.");
  if (password.length < 8) return showError("Password must be at least 8 characters.");
  $("submit").disabled = true; showError("");
  try {
    const body = mode === "signup" ? { name, email, password, level: $("level").value } : { email, password };
    const r = await fetch(mode === "signup" ? "/api/signup" : "/api/login",
      { method: "POST", headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" }, body: JSON.stringify(body) });
    const d = await r.json();
    if (!r.ok) throw new Error(d.detail || "Something went wrong.");
    await finishSignIn();
  } catch (err) { showError(err.message); }
  $("submit").disabled = false;
};

$("show").onclick = () => {
  const pw = $("password"), hidden = pw.type === "password";
  pw.type = hidden ? "text" : "password";
  $("show").textContent = hidden ? "Hide" : "Show";
  $("show").setAttribute("aria-label", hidden ? "Hide password" : "Show password");
};

async function continueAsGuest(e) {
  const btn = e && e.currentTarget;
  if (btn) btn.disabled = true;
  try {
    const current = await restoreAccount();
    if (current && current.user && current.user.guest) { await finishSignIn(); return; }
    const response = await fetch("/api/guest", { method: "POST" });
    if (!response.ok) throw new Error("Could not start guest practice.");
    await finishSignIn();
  } catch (error) {
    if (panelOpen) showError(error.message);
    if (btn) btn.disabled = false;
  }
}
$("guest").onclick = continueAsGuest;
$("hero-guest").onclick = continueAsGuest;
$("hero-signin").onclick = () => openPanel("signin", $("hero-signin"), true);

/* Signed-in check & deep links (/login, /?login=1, /?mode=signup) */
const wantDeepLinkSignup = params.get("mode") === "signup";
const wantDeepLinkSignin =
  location.pathname === "/login" ||
  params.has("login") ||
  params.get("mode") === "signin";

(async () => {
  let state = null;
  try { state = await restoreAccount(); } catch {}
  if (state && state.user && !state.user.guest) {
    const target = state.preferences ? "/study" : "/setup";
    location.replace(target);
    return;
  }

  if (wantDeepLinkSignup) {
    openPanel("signup", openSignupBtn, false);
  } else if (wantDeepLinkSignin) {
    openPanel("signin", openSigninBtn, false);
  }
})();

/* Pip buddy interaction: hop, sparkle, and rotate through 3 product lines */
(() => {
  const buddy = $("buddy");
  const bubble = $("bubble");
  const img = $("buddy-img");
  if (!buddy || !bubble || !img) return;

  const lines = [
    "Hi, I’m Pip. We’ll start with your study plan.",
    "Your progress grows one chapter at a time.",
    "Your focus time is yours to choose."
  ];
  let lineIdx = 0;
  let swapTimer = null;

  function triggerPip(e) {
    if (e) e.stopPropagation();
    lineIdx = (lineIdx + 1) % lines.length;
    bubble.textContent = lines[lineIdx];

    buddy.classList.remove("hop", "sparkle");
    bubble.classList.remove("pop");
    void buddy.offsetWidth;
    buddy.classList.add("hop", "sparkle");
    bubble.classList.add("pop");

    img.src = "/buddy/pip-happy.png?v=2";
    clearTimeout(swapTimer);
    swapTimer = setTimeout(() => {
      img.src = "/buddy/pip-idle.png?v=2";
    }, 900);
  }

  buddy.addEventListener("click", triggerPip);
  buddy.addEventListener("keydown", e => {
    if (e.key === "Enter" || e.key === " ") {
      e.preventDefault();
      triggerPip(e);
    }
  });
})();
</script>
</body>
</html>
