<script>
const $ = s => document.querySelector(s);
function el(tag,cls,text) { const e=document.createElement(tag);if(cls)e.className=cls;if(text!==undefined)e.textContent=text;return e; }
let user, prefs, catalog, selected;
const level=()=>prefs.level||'JEE';
function openQuiz(lv,chapter) {  // every way into the quiz goes through here so the level is always right
 try{localStorage.setItem('pp-level',lv);}catch(e){}
 location.href='/app'+(chapter?'?'+new URLSearchParams({new:'1',focus:chapter}):'');
}
function nextCard(kicker,title,sub,primary,onclick) {
 const b=el('button','next-card'+(primary?' primary':''));b.append(el('span','kicker',kicker),el('b','',title),el('span','',sub));b.onclick=onclick;
 if(kicker==='Due for review')b.classList.add('due-attention');
 requestAnimationFrame(()=>window.ppMotion?.(b,[{opacity:0,transform:'translateY(6px)'},{opacity:1,transform:'translateY(0)'}],{delay:Math.min(200,document.querySelectorAll('.next-card').length*40)}));return b;
}
async function loadNextUp(subjects) {
 if(prefs.start_ability==='check'&&!prefs.placed) $('#nextup').append(nextCard('Optional','Find your starting point','A 5-question check, whenever you’re ready.',false,()=>{location.href='/app?placement=1';}));
 let sid='';try{sid=localStorage.getItem('pp-last-session:'+(user.email||'guest'))||'';}catch(e){}
 const r=await fetch('/api/brief?'+new URLSearchParams({level:level(),session_id:sid,subjects:subjects.map(s=>s.subject).join(','),ai_provider:prefs.ai_provider||''}));
 if(!r.ok)return;const d=await r.json();const box=$('#nextup');
 if(d.continue)box.append(nextCard('Continue',d.continue.chapter||'Your last quiz',
   d.continue.answered+' answered'+(d.continue.active?' · a question is waiting':''),false,()=>openQuiz(level())));
 if(d.recommended){const rc=d.recommended;box.append(nextCard('Recommended next',rc.chapter,
   rc.subject+' · '+(rc.practised?'Suggested from your practice so far':'Suggested starting chapter · not assessed yet'),true,()=>openQuiz(level(),rc.chapter)));}
}
async function loadReviews() {  // spaced review of missed questions
 const r=await fetch('/api/reviews');if(!r.ok)return;const d=await r.json();
 if(!d.due.length&&!d.later)return;
 const go=()=>{location.href='/review';};
 const n=d.due.length;
 $('#nextup').append(n?nextCard('Due for review',n+' missed question'+(n===1?'':'s'),'Spaced repetition · answer them again to lock them in',false,go)
  :nextCard('Mistake review','All caught up',d.later+' scheduled · next in '+inWords(d.next_in),false,go));
}
async function loadDaily() {
 const response=await fetch('/api/daily');
 if(!response.ok)throw Error('Daily progress could not load. Please reload.');
 const d=await response.json(), box=$('#daily-study');box.replaceChildren();box.hidden=false;
 box.append(el('h2','',`${d.streak} day${d.streak===1?'':'s'} · ${d.answered}/${d.goal} today`),el('p','muted','Questions and reviews · UTC day'));
 const meter=el('progress');meter.max=d.goal;meter.value=Math.min(d.answered,d.goal);meter.setAttribute('aria-label','Daily question goal');box.append(meter);
 if(d.exam_date){
   const plan=d.plan, title=el('strong','daily-plan',`${d.days_left} day${d.days_left===1?'':'s'} left`);
   box.append(title,el('p','',`Today: ${plan.study_minutes} min${plan.chapter?' '+plan.chapter:' practice'}${plan.review_minutes?', '+plan.review_minutes+' min review':''}`));
   if(plan.chapter){const go=el('button','btn','Open planned chapter');go.onclick=()=>openQuiz(level(),plan.chapter);box.append(go);}
 }
 if(d.milestone){
   const key='pp-streak-celebration:'+user.email+':'+d.today;
   let seen=false;try{seen=localStorage.getItem(key)==='1';}catch(e){}
   if(!seen){
     const cheer=el('div','daily-cheer'),pip=el('img');pip.src='/buddy/pip-happy.png?v=2';pip.alt='Pip celebrating';pip.width=52;pip.height=64;
     cheer.append(pip,el('span','',`${d.milestone} days of showing up. Nice work!`));box.append(cheer);
     window.ppMotion?.(cheer,[{opacity:0,transform:'translateY(5px)'},{opacity:1,transform:'translateY(0)'}]);
     try{localStorage.setItem(key,'1');}catch(e){}
   }
 }
 const heat=$('#heatmap');heat.replaceChildren();
 for(const subject of new Set(d.chapters.map(c=>c.subject))){
   const group=el('section','heatmap-group');group.append(el('h3','',subject));const tiles=el('div','heatmap-tiles');
   d.chapters.filter(c=>c.subject===subject).forEach(c=>{
     const state=!c.n?'unassessed':c.readiness>=.7?'ready':c.readiness>=.4?'building':'needs-practice';
     const tile=el('button','heatmap-tile '+state,c.chapter);tile.type='button';
     tile.setAttribute('aria-label',c.chapter+': '+(!c.n?'not assessed':Math.round(c.readiness*100)+'% estimated readiness'));
     tile.title=tile.getAttribute('aria-label');tile.onclick=()=>openQuiz(level(),c.chapter);tiles.append(tile);
   });group.append(tiles);heat.append(group);
 }
}
function inWords(secs){for(const [u,n] of [[86400,'day'],[3600,'hour'],[60,'minute']])if(secs>=u*.95){const v=Math.round(secs/u);return v+' '+n+(v===1?'':'s');}return 'under a minute';}
function loadPapers() {
 let list=[];try{list=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('pp-papers')||'[]');}catch(e){}
 const box=$('#papers');
 for(const p of list){const b=el('button','subject-card paper-card');
  b.append(el('span','subject-icon','▤'),el('span','arrow','↗'),el('b','',p.name),el('small','',p.questions+' questions'));
  b.onclick=()=>openQuiz(p.level);box.append(b);}
 const add=el('a','subject-card add-card');add.href='/papers?add=1';add.append(el('b','','+ Add a question paper'),el('small','','Paste questions or upload a file'));box.append(add);
}
try { user=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('pp-user')||'null');prefs=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('pp-prefs')||'null'); } catch(e) {}
const symbols={
  Physics:['φ','var(--subject-blue)'],
  Chemistry:['⚗','var(--subject-violet)'],
  Maths:['∑','var(--accent-text)'],
  Biology:['❧','var(--good)'],
  Science:['⚛','var(--subject-blue)'],
  English:['Aa','var(--subject-violet)']
};
function renderChapters() {
 const box=$('#chapters');box.replaceChildren();
 const term=$('#search').value.trim().toLowerCase();
 const matches=selected.chapters.filter(ch=>ch.chapter.toLowerCase().includes(term));
 $('#chaptercount').textContent=matches.length+' chapters · Choose where your quiz begins';
 for(const ch of matches) {
  const card=el('article','chapter');
  card.append(el('span','chapter-number',String(selected.chapters.indexOf(ch)+1).padStart(2,'0')));
  const text=el('div','chapter-text');text.append(el('h3','',ch.chapter),el('p','',ch.questions?ch.questions+(ch.questions===1?' bank question':' bank questions'):catalog.ai?'AI practice available':'AI needed · currently unavailable'));
  const start=el('button','btn', 'Start quiz →');start.disabled=!ch.questions&&!catalog.ai;
  start.setAttribute('aria-label','Start quiz: '+ch.chapter);
  start.onclick=()=>openQuiz(level(),ch.chapter);
  card.append(text,start);box.append(card);
 }
 if(!matches.length)box.append(el('p','status','No chapters match your search.'));
}
function choose(subject) {
 selected=subject;$('#search').value='';$('#chaptertitle').textContent=subject.subject+' chapters';$('#chapter-section').hidden=false;
 document.querySelectorAll('.subject-card').forEach(b=>b.setAttribute('aria-pressed',String(b.dataset.subject===subject.subject)));
 renderChapters();
}
async function load() {
 const account=await restoreAccount();if(!account)return;user=account.user;prefs=account.preferences;
 if(!user){location.replace('/login');return;}if(!prefs){location.replace('/setup');return;}
 $('#greeting').textContent='Hi, '+user.name.split(' ')[0];$('#avatar').textContent=user.name[0].toUpperCase();
 $('#minutes').textContent=(prefs.block_minutes||40)+' min';
 const providerResponse=await fetch('/api/providers');if(!providerResponse.ok)throw Error('Could not load AI settings. Please reload.');
 const providerConfig=await providerResponse.json();
 if(!providerConfig.providers.some(p=>p.id===prefs.ai_provider&&p.available)) {
  prefs.ai_provider=providerConfig.default;
  try {localStorage.setItem('pp-prefs',JSON.stringify(prefs));}catch(e){}
 }
 const response=await fetch('/api/catalog?'+new URLSearchParams({level:level(),ai_provider:prefs.ai_provider}));if(!response.ok)throw Error('Could not load your syllabus. Please try again.');
 catalog=await response.json();$('#levelname').textContent=catalog.name;$('#crumb').textContent=catalog.name;
 $('#settings').textContent=catalog.name+' · '+({'-1':'Just starting','0':'Comfortable','1':'Confident'}[String(prefs.start_ability??0)]||'Adaptive practice');
 const subjects=catalog.subjects.filter(s=>!prefs.subjects?.length||prefs.subjects.includes(s.subject));
 for(const subject of subjects) {
  const [symbol,color]=symbols[subject.subject]||['▤','var(--subject-blue)'];
  const button=el('button','subject-card');button.dataset.subject=subject.subject;button.setAttribute('aria-pressed','false');
  button.style.setProperty('--tint','color-mix(in srgb, '+color+' 12%, transparent)');button.style.setProperty('--color',color);
  button.append(el('span','subject-icon',symbol),el('span','arrow','↗'),el('b','',subject.subject),el('small','',subject.chapters.length+' chapters'));
  button.onclick=()=>{
   choose(subject);
   const section=$('#chapter-section');
   if(section.getBoundingClientRect().top>innerHeight-160) section.scrollIntoView({behavior:matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)').matches?'instant':'smooth',block:'start'});
  };$('#subjects').append(button);
 }
 if(subjects.length)choose(subjects[0]);else throw Error('Choose your subjects in Preferences to get started.');
 loadPapers();loadNextUp(subjects).then(loadReviews).then(()=>$('#nextup').append(nextCard('AI tutor','Your AI tutor','Ask a question or add your notes',false,()=>{location.href='/tutor';}))).catch(()=>{});
 $('#status').hidden=true;
 loadDaily().catch(error=>{const box=$('#daily-study');box.hidden=false;box.replaceChildren(el('p','muted',error.message));});
 if(user.guest) showWelcome();
}
function showWelcome() {
 const key='pp-welcome:'+user.email;
 try {if(localStorage.getItem(key))return;}catch(e){}
 const steps=[['This is your study space','Look around first. Nothing starts until you choose a quiz.'],['Choose your next chapter','Pick a subject below, then choose a chapter. You set the pace.'],['More than a quiz','Use the menu for your tutor, saved questions and progress. Your profile holds Preferences.']];
 const dialog=el('dialog','welcome-guide');dialog.setAttribute('aria-labelledby','guide-title');
 const count=el('small'),title=el('h2'),copy=el('p'),actions=el('div','guide-actions');title.id='guide-title';
 const skip=el('button','btn','Explore on my own'),next=el('button','btn primary','Next');
 let step=0; const paint=()=>{count.textContent=(step+1)+' of '+steps.length;title.textContent=steps[step][0];copy.textContent=steps[step][1];next.textContent=step===steps.length-1?'Explore my dashboard':'Next';};
 const done=()=>{try{localStorage.setItem(key,'1');}catch(e){}dialog.close();};
 skip.onclick=done;next.onclick=()=>{if(step===steps.length-1)done();else{step++;paint();}};
 dialog.addEventListener('cancel',()=>{try{localStorage.setItem(key,'1');}catch(e){}});
 actions.append(skip,next);dialog.append(count,title,copy,actions);document.body.append(dialog);paint();dialog.showModal();
}
$('#search').oninput=renderChapters;
load().catch(e=>{$('#status').hidden=false;$('#status').textContent=e.message;});
</script>
</body></html>

<script>
const $ = s => document.querySelector(s);
const LIMIT = 30000;  // matches NOTES_LIMIT in main.py
const mathOptions = {delimiters:[{left:'$$',right:'$$',display:true},{left:'\\[',right:'\\]',display:true},{left:'\\(',right:'\\)',display:false},{left:'$',right:'$',display:false}],throwOnError:false};
function el(tag, cls, text) {const e=document.createElement(tag);if(cls)e.className=cls;if(text!==undefined)e.textContent=text;return e;}
function math(e, text) {e.textContent=String(text).replace(/\*\*(.+?)\*\*/g,'$1');if(window.renderMathInElement)renderMathInElement(e,mathOptions);return e;}
let provider = "", history = [], count = 10;

async function post(path, body) {
  const r = await fetch(path, {method:'POST', headers:{'Content-Type':'application/json'}, body:JSON.stringify(body)});
  const d = await r.json().catch(() => ({}));
  if (!r.ok) throw Error(d.detail || 'Something went wrong. Please try again.');
  return d;
}
const notes = () => $('#notes').value.trim();
function ready(needNotes = true) {
  $('#err').textContent = '';
  if (needNotes && notes().length < 80) { $('#err').textContent = 'Add a bit more from your notes first (at least a paragraph).'; return false; }
  if (!provider) { $('#err').textContent = 'The AI tutor needs Gemini or Groq set up on the server.'; return false; }
  return true;
}
async function busy(button, label, work) {
  const was = button.textContent; button.disabled = true; button.textContent = label;
  try { await work(); } catch (e) { $('#err').textContent = e.message; }
  finally { button.disabled = false; button.textContent = was; }
}

// notes: kept in this browser only, with a live size meter
function meter() {
  const n = notes().length, m = $('#meter');
  m.textContent = `${n.toLocaleString()} / ${LIMIT.toLocaleString()} characters` + (n > LIMIT ? ' · only the first part is used' : '');
  m.classList.toggle('over', n > LIMIT);
  try { localStorage.setItem('pp-notes', $('#notes').value); localStorage.setItem('pp-notes-name', $('#name').value); } catch (e) {}
}
$('#notes').oninput = meter; $('#name').oninput = meter;
$('#file').onchange = async () => {
  const f = $('#file').files[0]; $('#file').value = ''; if (!f) return;
  const label = $('label[for="file"]'), was = label.textContent; label.textContent = 'Reading ' + f.name + '…';
  try {
    $('#notes').value = await fileText(f);
    if (!$('#name').value.trim()) $('#name').value = f.name.replace(/\.[a-z]+$/i, '');
    meter(); $('#err').textContent = '';
  } catch (e) { $('#err').textContent = e.message || "Couldn't read that file."; }
  label.textContent = was;
};

// tabs
document.querySelectorAll('[role=tab]').forEach(tab => tab.onclick = () => {
  document.querySelectorAll('[role=tab]').forEach(t => {
    const on = t === tab; t.setAttribute('aria-selected', String(on)); $('#' + t.getAttribute('aria-controls')).hidden = !on;
  });
});

// explain
$('#explain').onclick = () => ready() && busy($('#explain'), 'Reading your notes…', async () => {
  const d = await post('/api/notes/explain', {notes: notes(), ai_provider: provider});
  const box = $('#explained'); box.replaceChildren();
  if (d.summary) box.append(math(el('p', 'summary'), d.summary));
  const list = el('div', 'concepts');
  d.concepts.forEach(c => { const card = el('article', 'concept'); card.append(math(el('h3'), c.title), math(el('p'), c.explanation)); list.append(card); });
  box.append(list);
});

// ask (the last few turns go along so follow-ups make sense)
$('#askform').onsubmit = e => {
  e.preventDefault();
  const q = $('#question').value.trim();
  if (!q || !ready(false)) return;  // questions work with or without notes
  const chat = $('#chat'); chat.querySelector('.empty')?.remove();
  chat.append(el('div', 'msg me', q)); $('#question').value = '';
  const wait = el('div', 'msg tutor', 'Thinking…'); chat.append(wait); chat.scrollTop = chat.scrollHeight;
  busy($('#askform button'), 'Asking…', async () => {
    try {
      const d = await post('/api/notes/ask', {notes: notes(), ai_provider: provider, question: q, history: history.slice(-6)});
      math(wait, d.reply);
      history.push({role: 'user', text: q}, {role: 'tutor', text: d.reply});
    } catch (err) { wait.remove(); throw err; }
    chat.scrollTop = chat.scrollHeight;
  });
};

// quiz
document.querySelectorAll('#count .chip').forEach(c => c.onclick = () => {
  count = +c.dataset.n;
  document.querySelectorAll('#count .chip').forEach(x => x.setAttribute('aria-checked', String(x === c)));
});
$('#makequiz').onclick = () => ready() && busy($('#makequiz'), 'Writing and checking questions…', async () => {
  const d = await post('/api/notes/quiz', {notes: notes(), ai_provider: provider, count, name: $('#name').value.trim()});
  const box = $('#quizdone'); box.replaceChildren();
  const done = el('div', 'done');
  done.append(el('strong', '', `✓ ${d.questions} question${d.questions === 1 ? '' : 's'} ready`),
    el('span', 'empty', `"${d.name}"${d.dropped ? ` · ${d.dropped} dropped because the check disagreed` : ''}`), el('br'));
  const go = el('button', 'btn primary', 'Start quiz →');
  go.onclick = () => { try { localStorage.setItem('pp-level', d.level); } catch (e) {} location.href = '/app?new=1'; };
  done.append(go); box.append(done);
});

(async () => {
  try { $('#notes').value = localStorage.getItem('pp-notes') || ''; $('#name').value = localStorage.getItem('pp-notes-name') || ''; } catch (e) {}
  meter();
  if (notes().length < 80) $('#tab-ask').click();
  const account = await restoreAccount(); if (!account) return;
  const d = await (await fetch('/api/providers')).json();
  const wanted = account.preferences?.ai_provider;
  const usable = d.providers.filter(p => p.available && p.id !== 'none');
  const pick = usable.find(p => p.id === wanted) || usable[0];
  provider = pick ? pick.id : '';
  $('#provider').textContent = pick ? `Your tutor: ${pick.label}. Change it in Preferences.`
    : 'The AI tutor needs Gemini or Groq set up on the server (GEMINI_API_KEY or GROQ_API_KEY). Bank quizzes still work.';
  document.querySelectorAll('#explain, #askform button, #makequiz').forEach(b => { b.disabled = !pick; });
})().catch(e => { $('#provider').textContent = e.message; });
</script>
</body></html>

.step.done .num { background: var(--accent); color: var(--accent-ink); border-color: var(--accent); }

        transition: border-color .15s, background .15s, box-shadow .15s; }
@media (hover: hover) { .chip:hover:not(:disabled) { border-color: color-mix(in srgb, var(--accent) 55%, var(--line)); box-shadow: inset 0 1px 0 var(--accent); } }

@media (max-width: 1000px) { .cards[data-pref="start_ability"] { grid-template-columns: repeat(2, 1fr); } }

        transition: border-color .15s, background .15s, box-shadow .15s; }
@media (hover: hover) { .card:hover { border-color: color-mix(in srgb, var(--accent) 55%, var(--line)); box-shadow: inset 0 1px 0 var(--accent); } }
.card[aria-pressed="true"] { border-color: var(--accent); background: var(--sel); box-shadow: inset 0 1px 0 var(--accent); }

       backdrop-filter: blur(10px); border-top: 1px solid var(--line); }

  main.wrap { padding-top: 28px; }
  .summary { display: none; }

    <div class="body chips" id="subjects"></div>
  </section>

<script>
(async () => {
 const account = await restoreAccount();
 if (!account) return;

const $ = s => document.querySelector(s);
const user = account.user;
const saved = account.preferences || {};
const P = { ...saved, level: saved.level || (user && user.level) || "", subjects: [...(saved.subjects || [])], start_ability: saved.start_ability ?? 0,
            block_minutes: saved.block_minutes || 40, explain_style: saved.explain_style || "steps", timed: !!saved.timed, placed: !!saved.placed, ai_provider: saved.ai_provider || "" };
P.daily_goal = saved.daily_goal ?? 20;
P.exam_date = saved.exam_date || '';
P.ai_fallback = !!saved.ai_fallback;
$('#ai-fallback').checked = P.ai_fallback;
$('#ai-fallback').onchange = event => { P.ai_fallback = event.target.checked; };
$('#daily-goal').value = P.daily_goal;
$('#exam-date').value = P.exam_date;
$('#daily-goal').oninput = event => { P.daily_goal = Number(event.target.value); update(); };
$('#exam-date').oninput = event => { P.exam_date = event.target.value; update(); };
if (account.preferences) {
  document.querySelector('.brand').href = '/study';
  document.querySelector('.intro h1').textContent = 'Your preferences';
}
let LEVELS = [];
let providersReady = false;
let saving = false;
let currentStep = 0;
const steps = [...document.querySelectorAll('main .step')];
const stepNames = ['Class or exam', 'Subjects', 'Starting level', 'Focus time', 'Explanations', 'Timed mode', 'AI tutor'];
steps.forEach((step, index) => {
  const heading = step.querySelector('h2');
  heading.id = `setup-heading-${index}`;
  heading.tabIndex = -1;
  step.setAttribute('aria-labelledby', heading.id);
  step.querySelector('.num').setAttribute('aria-hidden', 'true');
});

if (user) $("#hello").textContent = user.guest ? "Guest" : `Hi, ${user.name.split(" ")[0]}`;

function el(tag, cls, text) { const e = document.createElement(tag); if (cls) e.className = cls; if (text != null) e.textContent = text; return e; }

function renderLevels() {
  const groups = [["School · Primary", [1, 2, 3, 4, 5]], ["School · Middle", [6, 7, 8]], ["School · Secondary", [9, 10]],
                  ["School · Senior secondary", [11, 12]], ["Competitive exams", ["JEEMAIN", "JEE", "NEET"]]];
  const box = $("#levels");
  box.innerHTML = "";
  groups.forEach(([label, items]) => {
    box.append(el("div", "group-label", label));
    const chips = el("div", "chips");
    items.forEach(it => {
      const key = typeof it === "number" ? `Class ${it}` : it;
      const lv = LEVELS.find(l => l.level === key);
      if (!lv) return;
      const b = el("button", "chip", lv.name);
      b.dataset.level = key;
      b.append(el("small", "", lv.subjects.map(s => s.subject).join(" · ")));
      b.setAttribute("aria-pressed", String(P.level === key));
      b.onclick = () => {
        const changed = P.level !== key;
        if (changed) P.subjects = [];
        P.level = key;
        box.querySelectorAll('[data-level]').forEach(button => button.setAttribute('aria-pressed', String(button.dataset.level === key)));
        renderSubjects(changed); update();
      };
      chips.append(b);
    });
    box.append(chips);
  });
}

function renderSubjects(defaultAll = false) {
  const box = $("#subjects");
  box.innerHTML = "";
  const lv = LEVELS.find(l => l.level === P.level);
  if (!lv) { box.append(el("span", "muted", "Pick a class or exam first.")); return; }
  const valid = lv.subjects.map(s => s.subject);
  P.subjects = P.subjects.filter(s => valid.includes(s));
  if (defaultAll && !P.subjects.length) P.subjects = [...valid];
  lv.subjects.forEach(({ subject, chapters }) => {
    const b = el("button", "chip", subject);
    b.append(el("small", "", `${chapters} chapters`));
    b.setAttribute("aria-pressed", String(P.subjects.includes(subject)));
    b.onclick = () => {
      P.subjects = P.subjects.includes(subject) ? P.subjects.filter(s => s !== subject) : [...P.subjects, subject];
      b.setAttribute('aria-pressed', String(P.subjects.includes(subject))); update();
    };
    box.append(b);
  });
}

document.querySelectorAll(".cards[data-pref]").forEach(group => {
  const key = group.dataset.pref;
  const sync = () => group.querySelectorAll(".card").forEach(c => c.setAttribute("aria-pressed", String(String(P[key]) === c.dataset.value)));
  group.querySelectorAll(".card").forEach(c => c.onclick = () => {
    P[key] = isNaN(c.dataset.value) ? c.dataset.value : Number(c.dataset.value);
    sync(); update();
  });
  sync();
});
$("#timed").checked = P.timed;
$("#timed").onchange = e => { P.timed = e.target.checked; update(); };

function update() {
  const lv = LEVELS.find(l => l.level === P.level);
  $("#s-level").classList.toggle("done", !!lv);
  $("#s-subjects").classList.toggle("done", P.subjects.length > 0);
  ["s-start", "s-rhythm", "s-style"].forEach(id => $("#" + id).classList.add("done"));
  const validSubjects = !!lv && P.subjects.length > 0 && P.subjects.every(subject => lv.subjects.some(s => s.subject === subject));
  const goal = P.daily_goal ?? 20;
  const goalValid = Number.isInteger(goal) && goal >= 1 && goal <= 200;
  const ready = currentStep === 0 ? !!lv : currentStep === 1 ? validSubjects : currentStep === 3 ? goalValid : currentStep === steps.length - 1 ? !!lv && validSubjects && providersReady && goalValid : true;
  $('#go').disabled = saving || !ready;
  $('#back').disabled = saving;
  $('#go').textContent = saving ? 'Saving…' : currentStep === steps.length - 1 ? 'Open my dashboard →' : 'Continue →';
  $('#summary').textContent = !ready ? currentStep === 0 ? 'Choose a class or exam.' : currentStep === 1 ? 'Choose at least one subject.' : !goalValid ? 'Choose a daily goal from 1 to 200.' : 'Waiting for AI tutor options.' : currentStep === steps.length - 1 ? 'Save your choices and explore.' : 'You can change these later.';
}

function showStep(index, focus = true) {
  currentStep = index;
  steps.forEach((step, at) => { step.hidden = at !== index; });
  $('#back').hidden = index === 0;
  $('#step-progress').textContent = `Step ${index + 1} of ${steps.length} · ${stepNames[index]}`;
  $('#setup-error').textContent = '';
  update();
  if (focus) steps[index].querySelector('h2').focus();
}
$('#back').onclick = () => { if (!saving && currentStep > 0) showStep(currentStep - 1); };
function goHome() { location.href = '/study'; }

$("#go").onclick = async () => {
  if ($('#go').disabled) return;
  if (currentStep < steps.length - 1) { showStep(currentStep + 1); return; }
  saving = true;
  $('#setup-error').textContent = '';
  update();
  try {
    const persisted = await savePreferences(P);
    Object.assign(P, persisted);
  } catch (error) {
    $('#setup-error').textContent = error.message;
    saving = false;
    update();
    return;
  }
  try {
    localStorage.setItem("pp-prefs", JSON.stringify(P));
    if (user && user.email && !user.guest) localStorage.setItem("pp-prefs:" + user.email, JSON.stringify(P));
    localStorage.setItem("pp-level", P.level);
  } catch (e) {}
  goHome();
};

fetch("/api/providers").then(r => {if (!r.ok) throw new Error("Provider settings unavailable. Please reload."); return r.json();}).then(d => {
  if (!d.providers.some(p => p.id === P.ai_provider && p.available)) {
    P.ai_provider = d.providers.find(p => p.id === d.default && p.available)?.id || d.providers.find(p => p.available)?.id || '';
  }
  const box = $("#aiprovider"); box.replaceChildren();
  const blurb = { gemini: "Google's model", groq: "Fast open models", none: "Verified questions, no AI" };
  for (const p of d.providers) {
    const b = el("button", "chip"); b.type = "button"; b.setAttribute("role", "radio"); b.dataset.value = p.id;
    b.append(p.label, el("small", "", p.available ? blurb[p.id] || "" : "Not set up on this server"));
    b.disabled = !p.available;
    b.onclick = () => { P.ai_provider = p.id; markProvider(); };
    box.append(b);
  }
  providersReady = d.providers.some(p => p.id === P.ai_provider && p.available);
  markProvider();
  $("#providerstatus").textContent = providersReady ? 'You can change this during a quiz.' : 'No AI tutor options are available. Please reload.';
  update();
}).catch(e => {$("#providerstatus").textContent = e.message; update();});
function markProvider() {
  document.querySelectorAll("#aiprovider .chip").forEach(b => {
    const on = b.dataset.value === P.ai_provider;
    b.setAttribute("aria-pressed", String(on)); b.setAttribute("aria-checked", String(on));
    b.tabIndex = on && !b.disabled ? 0 : -1;
  });
}
$('#aiprovider').onkeydown = event => {
  const buttons = [...document.querySelectorAll('#aiprovider button:not(:disabled)')];
  const at = buttons.indexOf(document.activeElement);
  if (at < 0 || !['ArrowLeft', 'ArrowRight', 'ArrowUp', 'ArrowDown', 'Home', 'End'].includes(event.key)) return;
  event.preventDefault();
  const next = event.key === 'Home' ? 0 : event.key === 'End' ? buttons.length - 1 : (at + (['ArrowLeft', 'ArrowUp'].includes(event.key) ? -1 : 1) + buttons.length) % buttons.length;
  buttons[next].click(); buttons[next].focus();
};

fetch("/api/levels").then(r => { if (!r.ok) throw new Error('Could not load classes and exams. Please reload.'); return r.json(); }).then(d => {
  LEVELS = d;
  if (!LEVELS.some(l => l.level === P.level)) P.level = "";
  renderLevels(); renderSubjects(true); update();
}).catch(error => { $('#setup-error').textContent = error.message; });
showStep(0, false);

})().catch(error => { document.body.append(document.createTextNode(error.message)); });
</script>
</body>
</html>

================================================================================
END OF FILE: preppilot/setup.html

<script>
(async () => {
 const account = await restoreAccount();
 if (!account) return;

const $ = s => document.querySelector(s);
function el(tag, cls, text) { const e = document.createElement(tag); if (cls) e.className = cls; if (text != null) e.textContent = text; return e; }
let user = null;
try { user = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem("pp-user") || "null"); } catch (e) {}
if (!user) location.replace("/login");

function savedPapers() { try { return JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem("pp-papers") || "[]"); } catch (e) { return []; } }
function storePapers(list) { try { localStorage.setItem("pp-papers", JSON.stringify(list)); } catch (e) {} }

function startPaper(p) {
  try { localStorage.setItem("pp-level", p.level); } catch (e) {}
  location.href = "/app?new=1";
}

function renderSaved() {
  const list = savedPapers();
  $("#saved-section").hidden = !list.length;
  const box = $("#saved"); box.replaceChildren();
  list.forEach(p => {
    const row = el("div", "paper"), info = el("div");
    info.append(el("b", "", p.name), el("span", "muted", `${p.questions} questions`));
    const go = el("button", "btn", "Start quiz →"); go.onclick = () => startPaper(p);
    const rm = el("button", "linkbtn", "Remove from list");
    rm.onclick = async () => {
      rm.disabled = true;
      try {
        const response = await fetch('/api/papers/' + encodeURIComponent(p.paper_id), {method:'DELETE'});
        if (!response.ok) throw new Error('Could not remove the paper.');
        storePapers(savedPapers().filter(x => x.paper_id !== p.paper_id)); renderSaved();
      } catch (error) { $('#err').textContent = error.message; rm.disabled = false; }
    };
    row.append(info, go, rm); box.append(row);
  });
}

// Files are read in the browser by extract.js and their text fills the box, so the user sees what will be imported.
$("#file").onchange = async () => {
  const f = $("#file").files[0];
  $("#file").value = "";
  if (!f) return;
  $("#err").textContent = "";
  const kind = (f.name.split(".").pop() || "").toLowerCase();
  const label = $('label[for="file"]'), was = label.textContent;
  label.textContent = "Reading " + f.name + "…";
  try {
    $("#content").value = await fileText(f);
    if (!$("#name").value.trim()) $("#name").value = f.name.replace(/\.(csv|txt|pdf|docx|pptx)$/i, "");
  } catch (e) { $("#err").textContent = e.message || "Couldn't read that file."; }
  label.textContent = was;
};

$("#form").onsubmit = async e => {
  e.preventDefault();
  const content = $("#content").value.trim(), name = $("#name").value.trim();
  $("#err").textContent = ""; $("#result").hidden = true;
  if (!content) return $("#err").textContent = "Paste your questions (or a Google Sheets link) first.";
  $("#go").disabled = true; $("#go").textContent = "Reading your questions…";
  try {
    const r = await fetch("/api/papers/import", { method: "POST", headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" }, body: JSON.stringify({ name, content }) });
    const d = await r.json().catch(() => ({}));
    if (!r.ok) throw new Error(d.detail || "Import failed.");
    storePapers([d, ...savedPapers().filter(x => x.paper_id !== d.paper_id)].slice(0, 20));
    renderSaved();
    $("#r-title").textContent = `✓ ${d.questions} question${d.questions === 1 ? "" : "s"} ready`;
    const chapters = d.subjects.reduce((n, s) => n + s.chapters.length, 0);
    $("#r-detail").textContent = `"${d.name}" · ${chapters} chapter${chapters === 1 ? "" : "s"}. Rename it any time by importing again.`;
    const warn = $("#r-warn"); warn.replaceChildren();
    warn.hidden = !d.error_count;
    if (d.error_count) {
      warn.append(`${d.error_count} question${d.error_count === 1 ? " was" : "s were"} skipped. Fix and import again if you want ${d.error_count === 1 ? "it" : "them"}:`);
      const ul = el("ul"); d.errors.forEach(x => ul.append(el("li", "", x))); warn.append(ul);
    }
    $("#r-start").onclick = () => startPaper(d);
    $("#result").hidden = false;
    $("#result").scrollIntoView({ behavior: "smooth", block: "center" });
  } catch (err) { $("#err").textContent = err.message; }
  $("#go").disabled = false; $("#go").textContent = "Make my quiz →";
};
renderSaved();

})().catch(error => { document.body.append(document.createTextNode(error.message)); });
</script>
</body>
</html>

<script>
document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',()=>{if(window.Chart){Chart.defaults.font.family='DM Sans';Chart.defaults.animation=false;}});
const $ = s => document.querySelector(s);
const pct = x => Math.round(x * 100) + "%";
const css = v => getComputedStyle(document.documentElement).getPropertyValue(v).trim();
function readinessColor(r) {
  const x = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, Number(r) || 0));
  return x < 0.5
    ? `color-mix(in srgb, var(--warn) ${Math.round(x * 200)}%, var(--bad))`
    : `color-mix(in srgb, var(--good) ${Math.round((x - 0.5) * 200)}%, var(--warn))`;
}
function el(tag, cls, text) { const e = document.createElement(tag); if (cls) e.className = cls; if (text != null) e.textContent = text; return e; }
function bar(label, value, right, fillCls = "fill", useReadinessScale = false) {
  const row = el("div", "bar"), track = el("div", "track"), fill = el("div", fillCls);
  fill.style.width = Math.max(0, Math.min(100, value * 100)) + "%";
  if (useReadinessScale) fill.style.background = readinessColor(value);
  track.append(fill);
  row.append(el("span", "", label), track, right instanceof Node ? right : el("span", "", right));
  return row;
}
let user = null, level = "JEE";
try { user = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem("pp-user") || "null"); level = localStorage.getItem("pp-level") || level; } catch (e) {}


async function load() {
  const account = await restoreAccount(); if (!account) return; user = account.user;
  let sid = "";
  try { sid = localStorage.getItem("pp-last-session:" + (user.email || "guest")) || ""; } catch (e) {}
  const r = sid ? await fetch(`/api/dashboard?session_id=${encodeURIComponent(sid)}`) : null;
  if (!r || !r.ok) {
    $("#status").replaceChildren(el("p", "empty", "No saved practice yet for this learner. Start a quiz and your progress shows up here."));
    const go = el("a", "btn primary", "Start practising →"); go.href = "/study"; $("#status").append(go);
    $("#content").hidden = false;
    document.querySelectorAll("#content > section:not(#simcard):not(#galaxycard)").forEach(x => x.hidden = true);
    return;
  }
  const d = await r.json();
  $("#title").textContent = "Your progress";
  $("#level").textContent = d.exam;
  const mk = d.marking;
  const buildingEstimate = d.stats.answered < 5;
  $("#pred").textContent = buildingEstimate ? "Building estimate" : pct(d.predicted.with_strategy);
  if(!buildingEstimate)window.ppCount?.($("#pred"),d.predicted.with_strategy*100);
  $("#predsub").textContent = buildingEstimate ? "Answer a few more questions to see a rough score estimate. This is not a validated exam prediction." : (mk ? `of max marks on a ${d.exam} paper (+${mk[0]} / −${mk[1]})` : `on a ${d.exam} test`) +
    (d.predicted.strategy_gain > 0.005 ? ` · +${pct(d.predicted.strategy_gain)} from skipping likely-wrong questions` : "");
  if (!buildingEstimate && window.Chart && d.predicted_log.length > 1) new Chart($("#predchart"), {
    type: "line",
    data: { labels: d.predicted_log.map((_, i) => i + 1),
            datasets: [{ data: d.predicted_log.map(x => Math.round(x * 1000) / 10), borderColor: css("--accent"), tension: .3, pointRadius: 0 }] },
    options: { maintainAspectRatio: false, animation: false, plugins: { legend: { display: false } },
               scales: { x: { title: { display: true, text: "Questions answered", color: css("--muted") }, ticks: { color: css("--muted") }, grid: { display: false } },
                         y: { ticks: { color: css("--muted"), callback: v => v + "%" }, grid: { color: "rgba(143, 168, 255, 0.22)" } } } },
  });
  else $("#predchart").parentElement.replaceWith(el("p", "legend", "A rough trend appears after at least five answers."));

  [["Answered", d.stats.answered], ["Accuracy", pct(d.stats.accuracy)], ["Avg time", d.stats.avg_secs + "s"], ["Chapters", d.stats.chapters_touched]]
    .forEach(([k, v]) => { const s = el("div", "stat"); s.append(el("small", "", k), el("strong", "", String(v))); $("#stats").append(s); });
  d.subjects.forEach(s => {
    const attempts = d.chapters.filter(c => c.subject === s.subject).reduce((sum, c) => sum + c.n, 0);
    $("#subjects").append(bar(s.subject, attempts < 3 ? 0 : s.readiness, attempts < 3 ? "Starting" : pct(s.readiness), "fill", true));
  });

  const practised = d.chapters.filter(c => c.n).sort((a, b) => a.readiness - b.readiness);
  if (!practised.length) $("#chapters").append(el("p", "empty", "Nothing practised yet."));
  practised.forEach(c => $("#chapters").append(bar(c.chapter, c.readiness, `${pct(c.readiness)}${c.flags ? " ⚠" : ""}`, "fill", true)));
  if (practised.length) $("#chapters").append(el("p", "legend", "Exam-readiness per chapter, weakest first. ⚠ = a confident wrong answer."));

  const wrong = d.errors.reduce((a, e) => a + e.n, 0);
  if (!wrong) $("#errors").append(el("p", "empty", "No wrong answers yet."));
  d.errors.filter(e => e.n).sort((a, b) => b.n - a.n).forEach(e => $("#errors").append(bar(e.label, e.n / wrong, String(e.n), "fill err-" + e.type)));
  const top = d.errors.filter(e => e.n).sort((a, b) => b.n - a.n)[0];
  if (top) $("#errors").append(el("p", "legend", `Fix for ${top.label.toLowerCase()}: ${top.fix}`));

  if (!d.calibration.length) $("#calib").append(el("p", "empty", "Rate your confidence on each answer to see this."));
  else {
    const t = el("table"), hr = el("tr");
    ["You said", "Right", mk ? "Marks / question" : "Accuracy", "Strategy"].forEach(h => hr.append(el("th", "", h)));
    t.append(hr);
    const name = { sure: "🟢 Sure", unsure: "🟡 Unsure", guess: "🔴 Guessing" };
    d.calibration.forEach(c => {
      const tr = el("tr");
      tr.append(el("td", "", `${name[c.confidence]} (${c.n})`), el("td", "", pct(c.accuracy)),
        el("td", c.expected >= 0 ? "up" : "down", mk ? (c.expected >= 0 ? "+" : "") + c.expected.toFixed(2) : pct(c.accuracy)), el("td", "", c.advice));
      t.append(tr);
    });
    $("#calib").append(t);
    if (mk) $("#calib").append(el("p", "legend", `Attempting pays only if you're right more than ${pct(mk[1] / (mk[0] + mk[1]))} of the time.`));
  }

  const servable = new Set(d.chapters.filter(c => c.banked).map(c => c.chapter));
  d.gains.forEach(g => {
    const row = el("div", "bar"), go = el("a", "btn", "Practise →");
    if (!d.ai && !servable.has(g.chapter)) { go.removeAttribute("href"); go.textContent = "Needs AI"; go.style.opacity = ".5"; }
    if (go.textContent !== "Needs AI") go.href = "/app?" + new URLSearchParams({ new: "1", focus: g.chapter });
    row.style.gridTemplateColumns = "minmax(0,1fr) 70px auto";
    row.append(el("span", "", `${g.chapter} · ${d.chapters.some(c => c.chapter === g.chapter && c.n >= 3) ? pct(g.readiness) + " estimated" : "Building estimate"}`), el("span", "up", `+${g.gain.toFixed(2)}`), go);
    $("#gains").append(row);
  });
  $("#gains").append(el("p", "legend", mk ? "Extra expected marks per question if your ability there rises one step." : "Extra expected accuracy per question if your ability there rises one step."));

  $("#status").hidden = true;
  $("#content").hidden = false;
}

$("#runsim").onclick = async () => {
  const btn = $("#runsim");
  btn.disabled = true; btn.textContent = "Simulating…";
  const exam = ["JEE", "JEEMAIN", "NEET"].includes(level) ? level : "JEE";
  const d = await (await fetch(`/api/simulate?exam=${exam}`)).json();
  const avg = Math.round(d.results.reduce((a, r) => a + r.saved, 0) / d.results.length);
  $("#simhead").textContent = `Adaptive reaches ${d.exam} readiness in ${avg}% fewer questions on average.`;
  if (window.Chart) new Chart($("#simchart"), {
    type: "bar",
    data: { labels: d.results.map(r => r.profile), datasets: [
      { label: "Adaptive (PrepPilot)", data: d.results.map(r => r.adaptive), backgroundColor: css("--accent"), borderRadius: 6 },
      { label: "Static bank", data: d.results.map(r => r.static), backgroundColor: css("--muted"), borderRadius: 6 }] },
    options: { maintainAspectRatio: false, plugins: { legend: { labels: { color: css("--muted"), boxWidth: 12 } } },
               scales: { x: { ticks: { color: css("--muted") }, grid: { display: false } },
                         y: { title: { display: true, text: "Questions to readiness", color: css("--muted") }, ticks: { color: css("--muted") }, grid: { color: "rgba(143, 168, 255, 0.22)" } } } },
  });
  btn.remove();
};
window.addEventListener("load", () => load().then(()=>window.ppRevealBars?.(document)).catch(e => { $("#status").textContent = "Couldn't load your progress. Is the server running?"; }));

<span class="eyebrow">01 · SPACED REPETITION</span>
<h1>Mistake review</h1>
<p id="mastered-counter" class="muted" aria-live="polite">Mastered this visit: 0</p>
<p class="lead" id="count">Questions you missed come back at growing gaps until they stick.</p>
<ul class="ladder" aria-label="How reviews are spaced"><li>Missed in a quiz → back in <b>1 day</b></li><li>Right → <b>3 days</b></li><li>Right again → <b>7 days</b></li><li>Right a third time → <b>mastered</b></li><li>Any miss → back to <b>1 day</b></li></ul>

<script>
const $ = s => document.querySelector(s);
const mathOptions = {delimiters:[{left:'$$',right:'$$',display:true},{left:'\\[',right:'\\]',display:true},{left:'\\(',right:'\\)',display:false},{left:'$',right:'$',display:false}],throwOnError:false};
function el(tag, cls, text) {const e=document.createElement(tag);if(cls)e.className=cls;if(text!==undefined)e.textContent=text;return e;}
function math(e, text) {e.textContent=text;if(window.renderMathInElement)renderMathInElement(e,mathOptions);return e;}
function inWords(secs) {
 for (const [unit, name] of [[86400,'day'],[3600,'hour'],[60,'minute']])
  if (secs >= unit * 0.95) { const n = Math.round(secs / unit); return n + ' ' + name + (n === 1 ? '' : 's'); }
 return 'under a minute';
}
const STAGES = ['First review', 'Second review', 'Final review'];
function card(item) {
 const box = el('article', 'review'), meta = el('div', 'meta');
 meta.append(el('span', '', [item.level_name, item.subject, item.chapter].filter(Boolean).join(' · ')), el('span', 'stage', STAGES[item.stage] || 'Review'));
 box.append(meta, math(el('div', 'question-text'), item.q));
 const options = el('div', 'options');
 item.options.forEach((text, i) => {
  const b = el('button'); b.type = 'button';
  b.append(el('b', '', 'ABCD'[i]), math(el('span'), text));
  b.onclick = () => answer(item, box, i);
  options.append(b);
 });
 box.append(options);
 return box;
}
async function answer(item, box, choice) {
 const buttons = [...box.querySelectorAll('.options button')];
 buttons.forEach(b => b.disabled = true);
 try {
  const r = await fetch('/api/reviews/' + encodeURIComponent(item.question_id), {method:'POST', headers:{'Content-Type':'application/json'}, body:JSON.stringify({choice})});
  const d = await r.json().catch(() => ({}));
  if (!r.ok) throw Error(d.detail || 'Could not save this answer. Please try again.');
  buttons[d.answer].classList.add('right');
  if (!d.correct) buttons[choice].classList.add('wrong');
  box.classList.add(d.correct ? 'done-right' : 'done-wrong');
  const out = el('div', 'outcome');
  out.append(el('strong', d.correct ? 'ok' : 'no', d.mastered ? '🎉 Mastered. This one is off your list.'
   : d.correct ? `✓ Right. Next review in ${inWords(d.next_in)}.` : `✗ Not yet. It comes back in ${inWords(d.next_in)}.`));
  out.append(math(el('p'), d.explanation || 'No explanation was supplied.'));
  box.append(out);
  window.ppMotion?.(box,[{transform:'rotateY(-8deg)',opacity:.45},{transform:'rotateY(0)',opacity:1}],{duration:300});
  if(d.mastered) {
    mastered+=1;$('#mastered-counter').textContent='Mastered this visit: '+mastered;
    if(!matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)').matches) {
      const star=el('span','mastered-flight','★'),from=box.getBoundingClientRect(),to=$('#mastered-counter').getBoundingClientRect();
      star.setAttribute('aria-hidden','true');star.style.left=from.left+'px';star.style.top=from.top+'px';document.body.append(star);
      const flight=window.ppMotion?.(star,[{transform:'translate(0,0) scale(1)',opacity:1},{transform:`translate(${to.left-from.left}px,${to.top-from.top}px) scale(.4)`,opacity:0}],{duration:350});
      if(flight)flight.finished.finally(()=>star.remove());else star.remove();
    }
  }
  left -= 1; summary();
 } catch (error) { buttons.forEach(b => b.disabled = false); $('#status').hidden = false; $('#status').textContent = error.message; }
}
let left = 0, later = 0, mastered = 0;
function summary() {
 $('#count').textContent = left ? `${left} question${left === 1 ? '' : 's'} due now${later ? ` · ${later} scheduled later` : ''}.`
  : 'All caught up for now. Missed questions come back at growing gaps until they stick.';
}
async function load() {
 const account = await restoreAccount(); if (!account) return;
 const r = await fetch('/api/reviews');
 if (!r.ok) throw Error('Could not load your review list. Please reload.');
 const d = await r.json();
 left = d.due.length; later = d.later;
 $('#status').hidden = true; summary();
 const list = $('#list');
 if (!d.due.length) {
  const empty = el('div', 'empty', d.later ? `Nothing due right now. Your next review is in ${inWords(d.next_in)}.`
   : 'No mistakes to review yet. Questions you miss in a quiz will appear here, spaced out so they stick.');
  empty.append(el('br'));
  const go = el('a', 'btn', 'Back to practice'); go.href = '/study'; empty.append(go);
  list.append(empty); return;
 }
 d.due.forEach(item => list.append(card(item)));
}
load().catch(error => { $('#status').textContent = error.message; const retry = el('button', 'btn', 'Try again'); retry.onclick = () => location.reload(); $('#status').append(el('br'), retry); });
</script>
</body></html>

<script>
const $ = selector => document.querySelector(selector);
let bookmarks = [];
const mathOptions = {delimiters:[{left:'$$',right:'$$',display:true},{left:'\\[',right:'\\]',display:true},{left:'\\(',right:'\\)',display:false},{left:'$',right:'$',display:false}],throwOnError:false};
function el(tag, cls, text) {const e=document.createElement(tag);if(cls)e.className=cls;if(text!==undefined)e.textContent=text;return e;}

function render() {
 const list=$('#saved-list');list.replaceChildren();
 $('#count').textContent=bookmarks.length+' bookmarked question'+(bookmarks.length===1?'':'s');
 const term=$('#search').value.trim().toLowerCase();
 const matches=bookmarks.filter(b=>[b.question.q,b.question.chapter,b.subject,b.level_name].join(' ').toLowerCase().includes(term));
 if(!matches.length) {
  const empty=el('div','empty',bookmarks.length?'No saved questions match your search.':'No bookmarked questions yet. Save a question during a quiz and it will appear here.');
  if(!bookmarks.length){empty.append(el('br'));const link=el('a','btn','Go to study space');link.href='/study';empty.append(link);}
  list.append(empty);return;
 }
 for(const saved of matches) {
  const q=saved.question, card=el('article','saved-question');
  const header=el('div','saved-head');
  header.append(el('div','saved-meta',[saved.level_name,saved.subject,q.chapter].filter(Boolean).join(' · ')));
  const remove=el('button','remove-bookmark','Remove bookmark');remove.type='button';
  remove.onclick=async()=>{
   remove.disabled=true;
   try {
    const response=await fetch('/api/bookmarks/'+encodeURIComponent(saved.question_id),{method:'DELETE'});
    if(!response.ok)throw Error('Could not remove this bookmark. Please try again.');
    bookmarks=bookmarks.filter(b=>b.question_id!==saved.question_id);render();
   }catch(error){$('#status').hidden=false;$('#status').textContent=error.message;remove.disabled=false;}
  };
  header.append(remove);card.append(header,math(el('div','question-text'),q.q));
  const options=el('ul','saved-options');
  q.options.forEach((text,index)=>{const item=el('li');item.append(el('b','','ABCD'[index]),math(el('span'),text));options.append(item);});
  card.append(options);
  const details=el('details','solution');details.append(el('summary','','Show answer and explanation'));
  details.append(math(el('p','correct'),'Answer: '+'ABCD'[q.answer]+' — '+q.options[q.answer]),math(el('p'),q.explanation||'No explanation was supplied.'));
  card.append(details);list.append(card);
 }
}
async function load() {
 $('#status').hidden=false;$('#status').textContent='Loading your bookmarks…';
 const account=await restoreAccount();if(!account)return;
 const response=await fetch('/api/bookmarks');
 if(!response.ok)throw Error('Could not load your bookmarks. Please reload.');
 bookmarks=await response.json();$('#status').hidden=true;render();
}
$('#search').oninput=render;
load().catch(error=>{$('#status').textContent=error.message;const retry=el('button','btn','Try again');retry.onclick=()=>location.reload();$('#status').append(el('br'),retry);});
</script>

<h1>Think an answer key is wrong?</h1>

<script>
const $ = s => document.querySelector(s);
const mathOptions = {delimiters:[{left:'$$',right:'$$',display:true},{left:'\\[',right:'\\]',display:true},{left:'\\(',right:'\\)',display:false},{left:'$',right:'$',display:false}],throwOnError:false};
function el(tag, cls, text) {const e=document.createElement(tag);if(cls)e.className=cls;if(text!==undefined)e.textContent=text;return e;}
function math(e, text) {e.textContent=String(text).replace(/\*\*(.+?)\*\*/g,'$1');if(window.renderMathInElement)renderMathInElement(e,mathOptions);return e;}
const TITLES = { key_correct: '✓ The answer key looks right', key_wrong: '✗ The answer key looks wrong', flawed: '⚠ The question looks flawed', unclear: '? Not conclusive' };
let sid = null;
function card(item) {
  const box = el('article', 'item'), meta = el('div', 'meta');
  [item.level_name, item.subject, item.chapter, item.source, `you were ${item.you_were}`].filter(Boolean).forEach(t => meta.append(el('span', 'tag', t)));
  box.append(meta, math(el('div', 'qtext'), item.q));
  const opts = el('ul', 'opts');
  item.options.forEach((o, i) => { const li = el('li', i === item.answer ? 'key' : ''); li.append(el('b', '', 'ABCD'[i] + (i === item.answer ? ' (key)' : '')), math(el('span'), o)); opts.append(li); });
  box.append(opts);
  const why = el('textarea'); why.placeholder = 'Optional: why do you think the key is wrong?'; why.maxLength = 500;
  why.setAttribute('aria-label', 'Why you think the key is wrong (optional)');
  const go = el('button', 'btn primary', 'Check with AI'); go.type = 'button';
  const row = el('div', 'row'); row.append(go);
  box.append(why, row);
  go.onclick = async () => {
    go.disabled = true; go.textContent = 'Solving twice…';
    try {
      const r = await fetch('/api/check', {method:'POST', headers:{'Content-Type':'application/json'}, body:JSON.stringify({session_id: sid, question_id: item.question_id, reason: why.value})});
      const d = await r.json().catch(() => ({}));
      if (!r.ok) throw Error(d.detail || 'The check failed. Please try again.');
      box.querySelector('.verdict')?.remove();
      const v = el('div', 'verdict ' + d.verdict);
      v.append(el('strong', '', TITLES[d.verdict] || 'Result'), el('span', '', d.message),
               math(el('p'), 'AI working: ' + (d.working || '—')));
      if (d.hidden) v.append(el('p', '', 'Removed from your practice, and your progress was recalculated without it.'));
      box.append(v);
      go.textContent = d.hidden ? 'Removed' : 'Check again'; go.disabled = d.hidden;
    } catch (e) { go.disabled = false; go.textContent = 'Check with AI'; $('#status').hidden = false; $('#status').textContent = e.message; }
  };
  return box;
}
(async () => {
  const account = await restoreAccount(); if (!account) return;
  const r = await fetch('/api/check/recent');
  if (!r.ok) throw Error('Could not load your recent questions. Please reload.');
  const d = await r.json(); sid = d.session_id; $('#status').hidden = true;
  const list = $('#list');
  if (!d.questions.length) {
    const empty = el('div', 'empty', 'No answered questions yet. Answer a few in a quiz, then come back to check any you disagree with.');
    empty.append(el('br')); const a = el('a', 'btn', 'Start practising'); a.href = '/app'; empty.append(a); list.append(empty); return;
  }
  if (!d.ai) { $('#status').hidden = false; $('#status').textContent = 'Checking needs an AI tutor. Turn on Groq or Gemini in the quiz Options first.'; }
  d.questions.forEach(item => list.append(card(item)));
})().catch(e => { $('#status').textContent = e.message; });
</script>
</body></html>

/* PrepPilot Knowledge Galaxy — 3D space scene (Three.js r128 from cdnjs).
 *
 * Overview mode:
 *   - Subject planets with procedural 3D fBm band/swirl surface shaders, key-light terminator,
 *     fresnel atmosphere rim, additive halo behind the planet, and optional gradient ring.
 *   - Each planet is surrounded by 1-2 rotating asteroid-style particle dust belts (~300 particles
 *     on desktop, ~150 on phones, in a single BufferGeometry Points object per planet).
 *   - Each chapter owns an arc segment of the belt coloured red -> amber -> green by readiness,
 *     or soft pearl-silver when unassessed (n == 0). Misconceptions (flags > 0) pulse as bright
 *     coral sparks inside the chapter's arc.
 *   - Hovering a belt arc shows the chapter tooltip; clicking a planet, belt arc, or label pill
 *     (or zooming in close) enters Focused mode.
 *
 * Focused mode:
 *   - Camera eases in over ~900 ms while other planetary systems dim to 30% and hide their labels.
 *   - The focused planet's particle belt condenses over ~600 ms into key-lit 3D moon spheres
 *     distributed across 2-3 inclined (5-25 deg), slightly elliptical rings with chapter-seeded
 *     irregular phases, ±15% seeded size jitter, and Kepler-like orbital speeds.
 *   - Each moon casts a 40-degree comet-like gradient trail behind it (max opacity ~0.25) with
 *     near/far depth fading and hardware depth occlusion behind the planet.
 *   - Back button, Escape key, or clicking empty space reverses the condensation and returns
 *     smoothly to the galaxy overview.
 */
(function () {
  if (window.__ppGalaxyLoaded) return;
  window.__ppGalaxyLoaded = true;

  const REDUCED_MOTION =
    window.matchMedia && window.matchMedia("(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)").matches;

  const SUBJECT_THEMES = {
    Physics: {
      hex: "#3b82f6",
      deep: [0.05, 0.14, 0.38],
      mid: [0.16, 0.42, 0.88],
      bright: [0.42, 0.74, 1.0],
      rim: [0.55, 0.85, 1.0],
    },
    Chemistry: {
      hex: "#a855f7",
      deep: [0.18, 0.06, 0.36],
      mid: [0.52, 0.20, 0.84],
      bright: [0.80, 0.48, 1.0],
      rim: [0.88, 0.65, 1.0],
    },
    Maths: {
      hex: "#f59e0b",
      deep: [0.36, 0.14, 0.03],
      mid: [0.85, 0.46, 0.06],
      bright: [1.0, 0.76, 0.28],
      rim: [1.0, 0.86, 0.50],
    },
    Biology: {
      hex: "#10b981",
      deep: [0.03, 0.26, 0.19],
      mid: [0.06, 0.62, 0.44],
      bright: [0.32, 0.90, 0.68],
      rim: [0.55, 0.98, 0.80],
    },
    Science: {
      hex: "#06b6d4",
      deep: [0.04, 0.22, 0.32],
      mid: [0.05, 0.58, 0.72],
      bright: [0.38, 0.88, 0.96],
      rim: [0.62, 0.95, 1.0],
    },
    English: {
      hex: "#ec4899",
      deep: [0.34, 0.06, 0.20],
      mid: [0.80, 0.20, 0.52],
      bright: [0.98, 0.52, 0.76],
      rim: [1.0, 0.72, 0.88],
    },
    "Social Science": {
      hex: "#eab308",
      deep: [0.32, 0.20, 0.04],
      mid: [0.78, 0.56, 0.08],
      bright: [0.98, 0.82, 0.32],
      rim: [1.0, 0.90, 0.55],
    },
    EVS: {
      hex: "#22c55e",
      deep: [0.05, 0.28, 0.12],
      mid: [0.12, 0.66, 0.30],
      bright: [0.42, 0.92, 0.54],
      rim: [0.65, 0.98, 0.74],
    },
  };
  const FALLBACK_KEYS = ["Physics", "Chemistry", "Maths", "Biology", "Science", "English"];

  function themeFor(subject, idx) {
    return SUBJECT_THEMES[subject] || SUBJECT_THEMES[FALLBACK_KEYS[idx % FALLBACK_KEYS.length]];
  }

  function clamp(v, lo, hi) {
    return Math.max(lo, Math.min(hi, v));
  }

  function lerp(a, b, t) {
    return a + (b - a) * t;
  }

  function easeInOutCubic(t) {
    return t < 0.5 ? 4 * t * t * t : 1 - Math.pow(-2 * t + 2, 3) / 2;
  }

  function easeOutCubic(t) {
    return 1 - Math.pow(1 - t, 3);
  }

  function pct(x) {
    return Math.round((x || 0) * 100) + "%";
  }

  // Deterministic hash & PRNG seeded by chapter/subject name so orbits and jitter stay stable across reloads
  function hashSeed(str) {
    let h = 2166136261 >>> 0;
    for (let i = 0; i < str.length; i++) {
      h ^= str.charCodeAt(i);
      h = Math.imul(h, 16777619);
    }
    return h >>> 0;
  }

  function makeRng(seedStr) {
    let a = hashSeed(seedStr) || 1;
    return function () {
      a |= 0;
      a = (a + 0x6d2b79f5) | 0;
      let t = Math.imul(a ^ (a >>> 15), 1 | a);
      t = (t + Math.imul(t ^ (t >>> 7), 61 | t)) ^ t;
      return ((t ^ (t >>> 14)) >>> 0) / 4294967296;
    };
  }

  // Readiness colour encoding: --bad (#FF6B6B) -> --warn (#FFB547) -> --good (#3DDC97); pearl-silver if not assessed yet.
  function moonColorRGB(readiness, assessed) {
    if (!assessed) {
      return { r: 0.74, g: 0.82, b: 0.95 }; // soft pearl / silver-blue
    }
    const r = clamp(readiness || 0, 0, 1);
    if (r >= 0.7) {
      const t = clamp((r - 0.7) / 0.3, 0, 1);
      return {
        r: lerp(0.22, 0.239, t),
        g: lerp(0.82, 0.863, t),
        b: lerp(0.55, 0.592, t),
      };
    }
    if (r >= 0.4) {
      const t = (r - 0.4) / 0.3;
      return {
        r: lerp(1.0, 0.239, t),
        g: lerp(0.710, 0.863, t),
        b: lerp(0.278, 0.592, t),
      };
    }
    const t = r / 0.4;
    return {
      r: 1.0,
      g: lerp(0.420, 0.710, t),
      b: lerp(0.420, 0.278, t),
    };
  }

  function el(tag, cls, text) {
    const node = document.createElement(tag);
    if (cls) node.className = cls;
    if (text !== undefined && text !== null) node.textContent = text;
    return node;
  }

  function webglAvailable() {
    try {
      const c = document.createElement("canvas");
      return !!(
        window.WebGLRenderingContext &&
        (c.getContext("webgl") || c.getContext("experimental-webgl"))
      );
    } catch {
      return false;
    }
  }

  function openChapterQuiz(level, chapter) {
    if (level) localStorage.setItem("pp-level", level);
    const p = new URLSearchParams({ new: "1" });
    if (chapter && chapter !== "all") p.set("focus", chapter);
    location.href = "/app?" + p.toString();
  }

  let glowTextureCache = null;
  function getGlowTexture(THREE) {
    if (glowTextureCache) return glowTextureCache;
    const c = document.createElement("canvas");
    c.width = 128;
    c.height = 128;
    const ctx = c.getContext("2d");
    const g = ctx.createRadialGradient(64, 64, 0, 64, 64, 64);
    g.addColorStop(0.0, "rgba(255, 255, 255, 1.0)");
    g.addColorStop(0.2, "rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.78)");
    g.addColorStop(0.48, "rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.26)");
    g.addColorStop(0.78, "rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.05)");
    g.addColorStop(1.0, "rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.0)");
    ctx.fillStyle = g;
    ctx.fillRect(0, 0, 128, 128);
    glowTextureCache = new THREE.CanvasTexture(c);
    return glowTextureCache;
  }

  function injectStyles() {

  function renderListFallback(listEl, data) {
    listEl.replaceChildren();
    const chapters = data.chapters || [];
    if (!chapters.length) {
      listEl.append(el("p", "muted", "No chapters available for this level yet."));
      return;
    }
    for (const ch of chapters) {
      const row = el("div", "galaxy-list-item");
      const meta = el("div", "galaxy-list-meta");
      meta.append(el("strong", "", ch.chapter));
      const statusText =
        ch.n > 0
          ? `${ch.subject} · ${pct(ch.readiness)} ready · ${ch.n} answered${ch.flags ? " · misconception flagged" : ""}`
          : `${ch.subject} · not assessed yet`;
      meta.append(el("small", "", statusText));

      const right = el("div", "galaxy-actions");
      const badgeClass =
        ch.n === 0 ? "" : ch.readiness >= 0.7 ? " ready" : ch.readiness >= 0.4 ? " mid" : " low";
      right.append(
        el("span", "galaxy-badge" + badgeClass, ch.n > 0 ? pct(ch.readiness) : "Unassessed")
      );
      const btn = el("button", "btn ghost small", "Practise");
      btn.type = "button";
      btn.onclick = () => openChapterQuiz(data.level, ch.chapter);
      right.append(btn);

      row.append(meta, right);
      listEl.append(row);
    }
  }

  // ---------- Shaders ----------

  const NEBULA_VERT = `
    varying vec2 vUv;
    void main() {
      vUv = uv;
      gl_Position = vec4(position.xy, 0.9999, 1.0);
    }
  `;

  const NEBULA_FRAG = `
    precision mediump float;
    varying vec2 vUv;
    uniform float uTime;
    uniform float uAspect;
    uniform vec2 uCamShift;

    float hash(vec2 p) {
      return fract(sin(dot(p, vec2(127.1, 311.7))) * 43758.5453123);
    }
    float noise(vec2 p) {
      vec2 i = floor(p);
      vec2 f = fract(p);
      vec2 u = f * f * (3.0 - 2.0 * f);
      return mix(
        mix(hash(i + vec2(0.0, 0.0)), hash(i + vec2(1.0, 0.0)), u.x),
        mix(hash(i + vec2(0.0, 1.0)), hash(i + vec2(1.0, 1.0)), u.x),
        u.y
      );
    }
    float fbm(vec2 p) {
      float v = 0.0;
      float a = 0.5;
      mat2 rot = mat2(0.8, -0.6, 0.6, 0.8);
      for (int i = 0; i < 4; i++) {
        v += a * noise(p);
        p = rot * p * 2.02 + vec2(1.7, 9.2);
        a *= 0.5;
      }
      return v;
    }

    void main() {
      vec2 uv = (vUv - 0.5) * vec2(uAspect, 1.0);
      vec2 p = uv * 1.65 + uCamShift * 0.22;
      float t = uTime * 0.025;

      vec2 q = vec2(fbm(p + vec2(t, -t * 0.6)), fbm(p + vec2(3.4, 1.2) - t * 0.5));
      float n1 = fbm(p + q * 1.4 + vec2(0.0, t * 0.4));
      float n2 = fbm(p * 1.3 - q * 1.1 + vec2(-t * 0.3, 2.1));
      float n3 = fbm(p * 0.9 + vec2(q.y, -q.x) * 1.2);

      vec3 base = vec3(0.0196, 0.0235, 0.0588); // #05060f
      vec3 violet = vec3(0.36, 0.18, 0.66);
      vec3 teal = vec3(0.07, 0.44, 0.56);
      vec3 magenta = vec3(0.52, 0.15, 0.44);

      float vMask = smoothstep(0.32, 0.78, n1);
      float tMask = smoothstep(0.36, 0.80, n2);
      float mMask = smoothstep(0.40, 0.82, n3);

      float bandDist = abs(uv.y * 0.85 - uv.x * 0.32 + (n1 - 0.5) * 0.45);
      float band = exp(-bandDist * 2.4);

      vec3 col = base;
      col += violet * vMask * (0.16 + 0.14 * band);
      col += teal * tMask * (0.13 + 0.11 * band);
      col += magenta * mMask * (0.10 + 0.10 * band);

      float vig = 1.0 - smoothstep(0.45, 1.35, length(uv));
      col *= (0.82 + 0.18 * vig);
      gl_FragColor = vec4(col, 1.0);
    }
  `;

  const STAR_VERT = `
    attribute float aSize;
    attribute float aPhase;
    attribute vec3 aColor;
    varying vec3 vColor;
    varying float vAlpha;
    uniform float uTime;
    uniform float uPixelRatio;
    uniform float uOpacity;
    void main() {
      vColor = aColor;
      float twinkle = 0.72 + 0.28 * sin(uTime * 2.4 + aPhase);
      vAlpha = twinkle * uOpacity;
      vec4 mvPos = modelViewMatrix * vec4(position, 1.0);
      float rawSize = aSize * uPixelRatio * (135.0 / max(-mvPos.z, 36.0)) * (0.90 + 0.18 * twinkle);
      gl_PointSize = clamp(rawSize, 1.6 * uPixelRatio, 9.5 * uPixelRatio);
      gl_Position = projectionMatrix * mvPos;
    }
  `;

  const STAR_FRAG = `
    precision mediump float;
    varying vec3 vColor;
    varying float vAlpha;
    void main() {
      vec2 c = gl_PointCoord - vec2(0.5);
      float d = length(c) * 2.0;
      if (d > 1.0) discard;
      float core = exp(-d * d * 5.2);
      float halo = exp(-d * 1.9) * 0.58;
      float alpha = clamp(core + halo, 0.0, 1.0) * vAlpha;
      vec3 col = mix(vColor, vec3(1.0), core * 0.65);
      gl_FragColor = vec4(col, alpha);
    }
  `;

  const PLANET_VERT = `
    varying vec3 vLocalPos;
    varying vec3 vWorldNormal;
    varying vec3 vViewDir;
    varying float vViewDist;
    void main() {
      vLocalPos = position;
      vec4 worldPos = modelMatrix * vec4(position, 1.0);
      vWorldNormal = normalize(mat3(modelMatrix) * normal);
      vec4 mvPos = viewMatrix * worldPos;
      vViewDir = normalize(-mvPos.xyz);
      vViewDist = -mvPos.z;
      gl_Position = projectionMatrix * mvPos;
    }
  `;

  const PLANET_FRAG = `
    precision mediump float;
    varying vec3 vLocalPos;
    varying vec3 vWorldNormal;
    varying vec3 vViewDir;
    varying float vViewDist;
    uniform vec3 uDeep;
    uniform vec3 uMid;
    uniform vec3 uBright;
    uniform vec3 uRim;
    uniform vec3 uLightDir;
    uniform float uTime;
    uniform float uDim;

    float hash(vec3 p) {
      p = fract(p * 0.3183099 + vec3(0.1, 0.2, 0.3));
      p *= 17.0;
      return fract(p.x * p.y * p.z * (p.x + p.y + p.z));
    }
    float noise3(vec3 x) {
      vec3 i = floor(x);
      vec3 f = fract(x);
      f = f * f * (3.0 - 2.0 * f);
      return mix(
        mix(mix(hash(i + vec3(0,0,0)), hash(i + vec3(1,0,0)), f.x),
            mix(hash(i + vec3(0,1,0)), hash(i + vec3(1,1,0)), f.x), f.y),
        mix(mix(hash(i + vec3(0,0,1)), hash(i + vec3(1,0,1)), f.x),
            mix(hash(i + vec3(0,1,1)), hash(i + vec3(1,1,1)), f.x), f.y),
        f.z
      );
    }
    float fbm3(vec3 p) {
      float v = 0.0;
      float a = 0.5;
      for (int i = 0; i < 3; i++) {
        v += a * noise3(p);
        p = p * 2.05 + vec3(1.3, 2.7, -1.9);
        a *= 0.5;
      }
      return v;
    }

    void main() {
      vec3 p = normalize(vLocalPos);
      float swirl = fbm3(p * 2.8 + vec3(uTime * 0.05, -uTime * 0.03, 0.0));
      float bands = 0.5 + 0.5 * sin(p.y * 8.5 + swirl * 4.2);
      vec3 surf = mix(uDeep, uMid, smoothstep(0.15, 0.75, bands));
      surf = mix(surf, uBright, smoothstep(0.55, 0.92, swirl * bands));

      float ndl = dot(normalize(vWorldNormal), normalize(uLightDir));
      float light = smoothstep(-0.35, 0.85, ndl);
      vec3 litColor = surf * (0.14 + 0.94 * light);

      vec3 viewNormal = normalize(mat3(viewMatrix) * vWorldNormal);
      float fresnel = pow(1.0 - max(dot(viewNormal, vViewDir), 0.0), 2.4);
      litColor += uRim * fresnel * (0.55 + 0.45 * max(ndl, 0.0));

      float fogFactor = 1.0 - exp(-0.00018 * vViewDist * vViewDist);
      vec3 spaceBg = vec3(0.0196, 0.0235, 0.0588);
      vec3 finalCol = mix(litColor * uDim, spaceBg, clamp(fogFactor, 0.0, 0.72));
      gl_FragColor = vec4(finalCol, 1.0);
    }
  `;

  const RING_VERT = `
    varying vec2 vPos;
    void main() {
      vPos = position.xy;
      gl_Position = projectionMatrix * modelViewMatrix * vec4(position, 1.0);
    }
  `;

  const RING_FRAG = `
    precision mediump float;
    varying vec2 vPos;
    uniform vec3 uColorInner;
    uniform vec3 uColorOuter;
    uniform float uDim;
    void main() {
      float r = length(vPos);
      float t = clamp((r - 1.48) / (2.32 - 1.48), 0.0, 1.0);
      float edge = smoothstep(0.0, 0.16, t) * (1.0 - smoothstep(0.74, 1.0, t));
      float band = 0.62 + 0.38 * sin(t * 32.0);
      vec3 col = mix(uColorInner, uColorOuter, t);
      gl_FragColor = vec4(col * uDim, edge * band * 0.30 * uDim);
    }
  `;

  // Particle dust belt shader (asteroid-belt style in overview; condenses into moons on focus)
  const BELT_VERT = `
    attribute float aSize;
    attribute vec3 aColor;
    attribute float aAlpha;
    attribute float aSpark;
    attribute float aPhase;
    varying vec3 vColor;
    varying float vAlpha;
    varying float vSpark;
    uniform float uTime;
    uniform float uPixelRatio;
    uniform float uDim;
    uniform float uFade;
    uniform float uCenterViewZ;

    void main() {
      vec4 mvPos = modelViewMatrix * vec4(position, 1.0);
      float viewDist = max(-mvPos.z, 4.0);
      // Depth fade: particles on the far side of the planet are dimmer and smaller than the near side
      float relZ = mvPos.z - uCenterViewZ; // positive = closer to camera than planet center
      float depthBoost = clamp(0.68 + 0.38 * (relZ / 3.8), 0.36, 1.18);
      float fogAtten = exp(-0.00024 * viewDist * viewDist);

      float pulse = 1.0;
      vec3 col = aColor;
      if (aSpark > 0.5) {
        // Misconception coral spark pulsing inside the belt
        float s = 0.5 + 0.5 * sin(uTime * 4.6 + aPhase);
        pulse = 1.25 + 0.75 * s;
        col = mix(vec3(1.0, 0.34, 0.28), vec3(1.0, 0.74, 0.58), s);
      } else {
        pulse = 0.86 + 0.14 * sin(uTime * 2.1 + aPhase);
      }

      vColor = col * uDim * mix(0.75, 1.15, clamp((relZ + 3.5) / 7.0, 0.0, 1.0));
      vAlpha = aAlpha * depthBoost * fogAtten * uFade * (0.35 + 0.65 * uDim) * (aSpark > 0.5 ? (0.82 + 0.18 * sin(uTime * 4.6 + aPhase)) : 1.0);
      vSpark = aSpark;

      float pt = aSize * pulse * depthBoost * uPixelRatio * (86.0 / viewDist);
      gl_PointSize = clamp(pt, 1.75 * uPixelRatio, (aSpark > 0.5 ? 11.5 : 7.2) * uPixelRatio);
      gl_Position = projectionMatrix * mvPos;
    }
  `;

  const BELT_FRAG = `
    precision mediump float;
    varying vec3 vColor;
    varying float vAlpha;
    varying float vSpark;
    void main() {
      vec2 c = gl_PointCoord - vec2(0.5);
      float d = length(c) * 2.0;
      if (d > 1.0) discard;
      float core = exp(-d * d * (vSpark > 0.5 ? 4.2 : 6.0));
      float halo = exp(-d * 2.1) * (vSpark > 0.5 ? 0.75 : 0.42);
      float a = clamp(core + halo, 0.0, 1.0) * vAlpha;
      vec3 col = mix(vColor, vec3(1.0, 0.97, 0.94), core * (vSpark > 0.5 ? 0.65 : 0.35));
      gl_FragColor = vec4(col, a);
    }
  `;

  // Shaded 3D moon sphere lit by the same key light as the planets + readiness emissive glow
  const MOON_VERT = `
    varying vec3 vWorldNormal;
    varying vec3 vViewNormal;
    varying vec3 vViewDir;
    varying float vViewDist;
    void main() {
      vec4 worldPos = modelMatrix * vec4(position, 1.0);
      vWorldNormal = normalize(mat3(modelMatrix) * normal);
      vViewNormal = normalize(normalMatrix * normal);
      vec4 mvPos = viewMatrix * worldPos;
      vViewDir = normalize(-mvPos.xyz);
      vViewDist = -mvPos.z;
      gl_Position = projectionMatrix * mvPos;
    }
  `;

  const MOON_FRAG = `
    precision mediump float;
    varying vec3 vWorldNormal;
    varying vec3 vViewNormal;
    varying vec3 vViewDir;
    varying float vViewDist;
    uniform vec3 uColor;
    uniform vec3 uLightDir;
    uniform float uOpacity;
    uniform float uDim;
    void main() {
      // Key light lit side and curved terminator shadow
      float ndl = dot(normalize(vWorldNormal), normalize(uLightDir));
      float diffuse = smoothstep(-0.30, 0.85, ndl);
      float spec = pow(max(dot(reflect(-normalize(uLightDir), normalize(vWorldNormal)), vViewDir), 0.0), 14.0) * 0.28;

      // Dark side retains subtle readiness emissive glow; lit side is bright and spherical
      vec3 darkSide = uColor * 0.24;
      vec3 litSide = mix(uColor, vec3(1.0, 0.99, 0.96), 0.30) * 1.12 + vec3(spec);
      vec3 col = mix(darkSide, litSide, diffuse);

      // Fresnel readiness aura rim
      float center = max(dot(normalize(vViewNormal), vViewDir), 0.0);
      float rim = pow(1.0 - center, 2.0);
      col += uColor * rim * (0.38 + 0.32 * max(ndl, 0.0));

      // Distance & fog falloff into deep space (#05060f)
      float fogFactor = 1.0 - exp(-0.00024 * vViewDist * vViewDist);
      vec3 spaceBg = vec3(0.0196, 0.0235, 0.0588);
      col = mix(col * uDim, spaceBg, clamp(fogFactor, 0.0, 0.72));
      gl_FragColor = vec4(col, uOpacity);
    }
  `;

  // Comet-like 40 deg orbit trail + ultra-faint depth-faded ring shader
  const TRAIL_VERT = `
    attribute float aTrailAlpha;
    varying float vAlpha;
    varying float vDepthBoost;
    uniform float uCenterViewZ;
    void main() {
      vec4 mvPos = modelViewMatrix * vec4(position, 1.0);
      float relZ = mvPos.z - uCenterViewZ; // positive = near half of orbit
      vDepthBoost = clamp(0.55 + 0.50 * (relZ / 3.4), 0.22, 1.08);
      float fogAtten = exp(-0.00024 * mvPos.z * mvPos.z);
      vAlpha = aTrailAlpha * vDepthBoost * fogAtten;
      gl_Position = projectionMatrix * mvPos;
    }
  `;

  const TRAIL_FRAG = `
    precision mediump float;
    varying float vAlpha;
    varying float vDepthBoost;
    uniform vec3 uColor;
    uniform float uOpacity;
    uniform float uDim;
    void main() {
      float a = vAlpha * uOpacity * uDim;
      if (a < 0.003) discard;
      vec3 col = uColor * (0.75 + 0.25 * vDepthBoost) * uDim;
      gl_FragColor = vec4(col, a);
    }
  `;

  function createScene(stage, canvas, labelsBox, tooltip, panel, resetBtn, data, playEntrance) {
    const THREE = window.THREE;
    const isPhone = window.innerWidth <= 640;
    const glowTex = getGlowTexture(THREE);

    const renderer = new THREE.WebGLRenderer({
      canvas,
      antialias: true,
      alpha: false,
      powerPreference: "high-performance",
    });
    renderer.setPixelRatio(Math.min(window.devicePixelRatio || 1, 2));
    renderer.setClearColor(0x05060f, 1);

    const scene = new THREE.Scene();
    scene.fog = new THREE.FogExp2(0x05060f, 0.0085);
    const camera = new THREE.PerspectiveCamera(44, 1, 0.1, 240);

    // 1. Full-screen deep space nebula background plane
    const nebulaMat = new THREE.ShaderMaterial({
      vertexShader: NEBULA_VERT,
      fragmentShader: NEBULA_FRAG,
      uniforms: {
        uTime: { value: 0 },
        uAspect: { value: 1.5 },
        uCamShift: { value: new THREE.Vector2(0, 0) },
      },
      depthTest: false,
      depthWrite: false,
    });
    const nebulaQuad = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.PlaneGeometry(2, 2), nebulaMat);
    nebulaQuad.frustumCulled = false;
    nebulaQuad.renderOrder = -100;
    scene.add(nebulaQuad);

    // 2. Three parallax star layers (tiny, small, bright)
    const starLayers = [];
    const starSpecs = [
      { count: isPhone ? 140 : 280, sizeMin: 1.1, sizeMax: 1.8, rMin: 64, rMax: 108, parallax: 0.06 },
      { count: isPhone ? 70 : 140, sizeMin: 1.9, sizeMax: 2.9, rMin: 54, rMax: 86, parallax: 0.14 },
      { count: isPhone ? 18 : 38, sizeMin: 3.1, sizeMax: 4.5, rMin: 48, rMax: 74, parallax: 0.22 },
    ];
    const starPalette = [
      new THREE.Color("#e8f0ff"),
      new THREE.Color("#c7d8ff"),
      new THREE.Color("#ffe9c7"),
      new THREE.Color("#e0c8ff"),
      new THREE.Color("#a8f0f8"),
    ];

    for (const spec of starSpecs) {
      const pos = new Float32Array(spec.count * 3);
      const sizes = new Float32Array(spec.count);
      const phases = new Float32Array(spec.count);
      const cols = new Float32Array(spec.count * 3);
      for (let i = 0; i < spec.count; i++) {
        const r = spec.rMin + Math.random() * (spec.rMax - spec.rMin);
        const th = Math.random() * Math.PI * 2;
        const ph = Math.acos(2 * Math.random() - 1);
        pos[i * 3] = r * Math.sin(ph) * Math.cos(th);
        pos[i * 3 + 1] = r * Math.cos(ph) * 0.72;
        pos[i * 3 + 2] = r * Math.sin(ph) * Math.sin(th);
        sizes[i] = spec.sizeMin + Math.random() * (spec.sizeMax - spec.sizeMin);
        phases[i] = Math.random() * Math.PI * 2;
        const c = starPalette[i % starPalette.length];
        cols[i * 3] = c.r;
        cols[i * 3 + 1] = c.g;
        cols[i * 3 + 2] = c.b;
      }
      const geo = new THREE.BufferGeometry();
      geo.setAttribute("position", new THREE.BufferAttribute(pos, 3));
      geo.setAttribute("aSize", new THREE.BufferAttribute(sizes, 1));
      geo.setAttribute("aPhase", new THREE.BufferAttribute(phases, 1));
      geo.setAttribute("aColor", new THREE.BufferAttribute(cols, 3));
      const mat = new THREE.ShaderMaterial({
        vertexShader: STAR_VERT,
        fragmentShader: STAR_FRAG,
        uniforms: {
          uTime: { value: 0 },
          uPixelRatio: { value: Math.min(window.devicePixelRatio || 1, 2) },
          uOpacity: { value: playEntrance && !REDUCED_MOTION && !data.isDemo ? 0 : 1 },
        },
        transparent: true,
        depthTest: true,
        depthWrite: false,
        blending: THREE.AdditiveBlending,
      });
      const points = new THREE.Points(geo, mat);
      points.renderOrder = -50;
      scene.add(points);
      starLayers.push({ points, mat, parallax: spec.parallax });
    }

    // 3. Occasional faint shooting star (desktop/tablet only, every 8-12 s)
    let shootingStar = null;
    if (!isPhone) {
      const segs = 16;
      const shPos = new Float32Array(segs * 3);
      const shCol = new Float32Array(segs * 3);
      for (let i = 0; i < segs; i++) {
        const f = Math.pow(1 - i / (segs - 1), 1.8) * 0.75;
        shCol[i * 3] = 0.82 * f;
        shCol[i * 3 + 1] = 0.92 * f;
        shCol[i * 3 + 2] = 1.0 * f;
      }
      const shGeo = new THREE.BufferGeometry();
      shGeo.setAttribute("position", new THREE.BufferAttribute(shPos, 3));
      shGeo.setAttribute("color", new THREE.BufferAttribute(shCol, 3));
      const shMat = new THREE.LineBasicMaterial({
        vertexColors: true,
        transparent: true,
        opacity: 0.75,
        blending: THREE.AdditiveBlending,
        depthWrite: false,
      });
      const shLine = new THREE.Line(shGeo, shMat);
      shLine.visible = false;
      scene.add(shLine);
      shootingStar = {
        line: shLine,
        posAttr: shGeo.getAttribute("position"),
        active: false,
        nextAt: performance.now() + (8000 + Math.random() * 4000),
        startAt: 0,
        duration: 780,
        origin: new THREE.Vector3(),
        vel: new THREE.Vector3(),
      };
    }

    // 4. Subject planets, overview particle dust belts, and focused 3D moons
    const galaxyGroup = new THREE.Group();
    scene.add(galaxyGroup);

    const bySubject = new Map();
    data.subjects.forEach(s => bySubject.set(s.subject, { readiness: s.readiness, chapters: [] }));
