    data.chapters.forEach(c => {
      if (!bySubject.has(c.subject)) bySubject.set(c.subject, { readiness: 0.5, chapters: [] });
      bySubject.get(c.subject).chapters.push(c);
    });
    const subjectEntries = [...bySubject.entries()];
    const count = subjectEntries.length || 1;
    const baseSpread = count === 1 ? 0 : count === 2 ? 6.8 : count === 3 ? 8.2 : 9.2;
    const spreadX = baseSpread * 1.24;
    const spreadZ = baseSpread * 0.80;

    const keyLightDir = new THREE.Vector3(0.65, 0.72, 0.55).normalize();
    const sharedSphere = new THREE.SphereGeometry(1, 28, 20);
    const planetRadius = 1.24;
    const planetGeo = new THREE.SphereGeometry(planetRadius, 40, 30);
    const planetRingGeo = new THREE.RingGeometry(1.48, 2.32, 64);

    const planetMeshes = [];
    const planetNodes = [];
    const pulsingRings = [];

    labelsBox.replaceChildren();

    subjectEntries.forEach(([subject, info], idx) => {
      const rng = makeRng("planet:" + subject + ":" + idx);
      let px = 0;
      let py = 0;
      let pz = 0;
      if (count === 4) {
        // Distinct 4-quadrant layout: Top-Right, Bottom-Right, Bottom-Left, Top-Left
        const quadCoords = [
          { x: 0.84, y: 1.35, z: -0.62 },  // idx 0 (Physics): Top-Right
          { x: 0.88, y: -1.55, z: 0.68 },  // idx 1 (Chemistry): Bottom-Right
          { x: -0.88, y: -1.45, z: 0.64 }, // idx 2 (Maths): Bottom-Left
          { x: -0.82, y: 1.45, z: -0.66 }, // idx 3 (Biology): Top-Left
        ];
        const q = quadCoords[idx];
        px = q.x * spreadX;
        py = q.y;
        pz = q.z * spreadZ;
      } else if (count > 1) {
        const angle = (idx / count) * Math.PI * 2 - Math.PI * 0.28;
        px = Math.cos(angle) * spreadX;
        pz = Math.sin(angle) * spreadZ;
        py = pz < 0 ? 1.15 : -1.15;
      }

      const theme = themeFor(subject, idx);
      const systemGroup = new THREE.Group();
      systemGroup.position.set(px, py, pz);
      galaxyGroup.add(systemGroup);

      // Procedural planet surface + fresnel atmosphere shader (opaque depth buffer occluder)
      const planetMat = new THREE.ShaderMaterial({
        vertexShader: PLANET_VERT,
        fragmentShader: PLANET_FRAG,
        uniforms: {
          uDeep: { value: new THREE.Color(theme.deep[0], theme.deep[1], theme.deep[2]) },
          uMid: { value: new THREE.Color(theme.mid[0], theme.mid[1], theme.mid[2]) },
          uBright: { value: new THREE.Color(theme.bright[0], theme.bright[1], theme.bright[2]) },
          uRim: { value: new THREE.Color(theme.rim[0], theme.rim[1], theme.rim[2]) },
          uLightDir: { value: keyLightDir },
          uTime: { value: idx * 10 },
          uDim: { value: 1.0 },
        },
        depthTest: true,
        depthWrite: true,
      });
      const planetMesh = new THREE.Mesh(planetGeo, planetMat);
      planetMesh.rotation.z = (idx % 2 === 0 ? 1 : -1) * 0.16;
      planetMesh.renderOrder = 1;
      systemGroup.add(planetMesh);
      planetMeshes.push(planetMesh);

      // Additive glow halo sprite placed slightly behind the planet center along view ray
      const haloMat = new THREE.SpriteMaterial({
        map: glowTex,
        color: new THREE.Color(theme.bright[0], theme.bright[1], theme.bright[2]),
        transparent: true,
        opacity: 0.50,
        blending: THREE.AdditiveBlending,
        depthTest: true,
        depthWrite: false,
      });
      const haloSprite = new THREE.Sprite(haloMat);
      haloSprite.scale.set(4.8, 4.8, 1);
      haloSprite.renderOrder = 0;
      systemGroup.add(haloSprite);

      // Thin translucent gradient ring system on 1-2 planets
      let ringMat = null;
      if (idx % 2 === 0) {
        ringMat = new THREE.ShaderMaterial({
          vertexShader: RING_VERT,
          fragmentShader: RING_FRAG,
          uniforms: {
            uColorInner: { value: new THREE.Color(theme.bright[0], theme.bright[1], theme.bright[2]) },
            uColorOuter: { value: new THREE.Color(theme.rim[0], theme.rim[1], theme.rim[2]) },
            uDim: { value: 1.0 },
          },
          side: THREE.DoubleSide,
          transparent: true,
          depthTest: true,
          depthWrite: false,
          blending: THREE.AdditiveBlending,
        });
        const pRing = new THREE.Mesh(planetRingGeo, ringMat);
        pRing.rotation.x = Math.PI / 2 - 0.34;
        pRing.rotation.y = idx === 0 ? -0.22 : 0.22;
        pRing.renderOrder = 2;
        systemGroup.add(pRing);
      }

      // Glass pill label with coloured subject dot (rendered via textContent)
      const labelBtn = el("button", "galaxy-planet-label");
      labelBtn.type = "button";
      const dot = el("i", "galaxy-planet-dot");
      dot.style.background = theme.hex;
      dot.style.color = theme.hex;
      const hasSubjectAnswers = info.chapters.some(c => c.n >= 3);
      const labelText = el(
        "span",
        "",
        hasSubjectAnswers ? `${subject} · ${pct(info.readiness)}` : subject
      );
      labelBtn.append(dot, labelText);
      labelBtn.title = `Focus on ${subject}`;
      labelsBox.append(labelBtn);

      // Spread chapters over 2-3 natural inclined elliptical rings (focused view)
      const chs = info.chapters.length
        ? info.chapters
        : [{ chapter: subject, subject, readiness: info.readiness || 0.5, n: 0, flags: 0 }];
      const numRings = chs.length <= 2 ? 1 : chs.length <= 9 ? 2 : 3;
      const ringBuckets = Array.from({ length: numRings }, () => []);
      chs.forEach((ch, cIdx) => {
        ringBuckets[cIdx % numRings].push(ch);
      });

      const moonsGroup = new THREE.Group();
      moonsGroup.visible = false; // Moons only exist as active visible meshes when focused!
      systemGroup.add(moonsGroup);

      const planetMoons = [];
      const ringDefs = [];
      let outerBeltRadius = 3.2;

      ringBuckets.forEach((ringChapters, rIdx) => {
        const rRng = makeRng("ring:" + subject + ":" + rIdx);
        // Radii: inner ~2.35, mid ~3.28, outer ~4.18
        const rx = 2.35 + rIdx * 0.92 + (rRng() - 0.5) * 0.14;
        const eccentricity = 0.85 + rRng() * 0.08; // slightly elliptical
        const rz = rx * eccentricity;
        if (rx + 0.55 > outerBeltRadius) outerBeltRadius = rx + 0.55;

        // Inclinations between 6 deg (0.105 rad) and 23 deg (0.40 rad)
        const incDeg = 7 + rIdx * 7 + rRng() * 6;
        const incRad = (incDeg * Math.PI) / 180;
        const tiltSignX = rIdx % 2 === 0 ? 1 : -1;
        const tiltSignZ = (idx + rIdx) % 2 === 0 ? -1 : 1;
        const tiltX = tiltSignX * incRad * 0.82;
        const tiltZ = tiltSignZ * incRad * 0.58;
        const yawY = rIdx * 0.65 + idx * 0.4;

        const orbitEuler = new THREE.Euler(tiltX, yawY, tiltZ, "XYZ");
        const orbitQuat = new THREE.Quaternion().setFromEuler(orbitEuler);

        // Kepler-like angular speed: omega proportional to 1 / rx^1.5, calm and slow
        const dirSign = rIdx % 2 === 0 ? 1 : -1;
        const keplerSpeed = (0.34 / Math.pow(rx, 1.5)) * dirSign;

        // Faint depth-faded whisper ring (no hard white wire)
        const whisperSegs = 96;
        const whisperPos = new Float32Array(whisperSegs * 3);
        const whisperAlpha = new Float32Array(whisperSegs);
        const vTmp = new THREE.Vector3();
        for (let s = 0; s < whisperSegs; s++) {
          const a = (s / whisperSegs) * Math.PI * 2;
          vTmp.set(Math.cos(a) * rx, 0, Math.sin(a) * rz).applyQuaternion(orbitQuat);
          whisperPos[s * 3] = vTmp.x;
          whisperPos[s * 3 + 1] = vTmp.y;
          whisperPos[s * 3 + 2] = vTmp.z;
          whisperAlpha[s] = 0.055;
        }
        const whisperGeo = new THREE.BufferGeometry();
        whisperGeo.setAttribute("position", new THREE.BufferAttribute(whisperPos, 3));
        whisperGeo.setAttribute("aTrailAlpha", new THREE.BufferAttribute(whisperAlpha, 1));
        const whisperMat = new THREE.ShaderMaterial({
          vertexShader: TRAIL_VERT,
          fragmentShader: TRAIL_FRAG,
          uniforms: {
            uColor: { value: new THREE.Color(theme.rim[0], theme.rim[1], theme.rim[2]) },
            uOpacity: { value: 1.0 },
            uDim: { value: 1.0 },
            uCenterViewZ: { value: -20.0 },
          },
          transparent: true,
          depthTest: true,
          depthWrite: false,
          blending: THREE.AdditiveBlending,
        });
        const whisperLoop = new THREE.LineLoop(whisperGeo, whisperMat);
        whisperLoop.renderOrder = 2;
        moonsGroup.add(whisperLoop);

        // Irregular chapter-seeded moon phases along this ring (never evenly spaced clones)
        const rawAngles = ringChapters.map((ch, cIdx) => {
          const cRng = makeRng("moon:" + subject + ":" + ch.chapter);
          // Irregular jitter up to ±42% of slot width
          const slot = (cIdx / ringChapters.length) * Math.PI * 2;
          const slotWidth = (Math.PI * 2) / ringChapters.length;
          return slot + (cRng() - 0.5) * slotWidth * 0.72 + rIdx * 1.13;
        });

        ringChapters.forEach((ch, cIdx) => {
          const cRng = makeRng("size:" + subject + ":" + ch.chapter);
          const baseAngle = rawAngles[cIdx];
          const assessed = (ch.n || 0) > 0;
          const rgb = moonColorRGB(ch.readiness, assessed);
          const moonColor = new THREE.Color(rgb.r, rgb.g, rgb.b);

          // Moon size from questions practised + ±15% seeded jitter
          const jitter = 0.85 + cRng() * 0.30;
          const moonRadius = (0.21 + Math.min(ch.n || 0, 12) * 0.026) * jitter;

          // Key-lit 3D sphere with terminator and readiness emissive glow
          const moonMat = new THREE.ShaderMaterial({
            vertexShader: MOON_VERT,
            fragmentShader: MOON_FRAG,
            uniforms: {
              uColor: { value: moonColor },
              uLightDir: { value: keyLightDir },
              uOpacity: { value: assessed ? 0.98 : 0.45 },
              uDim: { value: 1.0 },
            },
            transparent: true,
            depthTest: true,
            depthWrite: true,
          });
          const moon = new THREE.Mesh(sharedSphere, moonMat);
          moon.scale.setScalar(moonRadius);
          moon.renderOrder = 3;

          // Small emissive readiness aura sprite (occluded when behind planet)
          const mGlowMat = new THREE.SpriteMaterial({
            map: glowTex,
            color: moonColor,
            transparent: true,
            opacity: assessed ? 0.58 : 0.26,
            blending: THREE.AdditiveBlending,
            depthTest: true,
            depthWrite: false,
          });
          const mGlow = new THREE.Sprite(mGlowMat);
          mGlow.scale.set(2.8, 2.8, 1);
          mGlow.renderOrder = 4;
          moon.add(mGlow);

          // Misconception pulsing coral ring when flags > 0
          if ((ch.flags || 0) > 0) {
            const cRingGeo = new THREE.RingGeometry(1.48, 2.15, 36);
            const cRingMat = new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({
              color: 0xff5f52,
              side: THREE.DoubleSide,
              transparent: true,
              opacity: 0.90,
              blending: THREE.AdditiveBlending,
              depthTest: true,
              depthWrite: false,
            });
            const cRingMesh = new THREE.Mesh(cRingGeo, cRingMat);
            cRingMesh.rotation.x = Math.PI / 2;
            cRingMesh.renderOrder = 4;
            moon.add(cRingMesh);
            pulsingRings.push({ mesh: cRingMesh, mat: cRingMat, phase: cIdx * 1.1 });
          }

          // 40-degree comet-like gradient trail behind the moon (max opacity ~0.25, depth-faded)
          const trailPtsCount = 28;
          const trailPositions = new Float32Array(trailPtsCount * 3);
          const trailAlphas = new Float32Array(trailPtsCount);
          const maxTrailAlpha = assessed ? 0.25 : 0.16;
          for (let tIdx = 0; tIdx < trailPtsCount; tIdx++) {
            const frac = tIdx / (trailPtsCount - 1);
            trailAlphas[tIdx] = Math.pow(1 - frac, 1.85) * maxTrailAlpha;
          }
          const trailGeo = new THREE.BufferGeometry();
          trailGeo.setAttribute("position", new THREE.BufferAttribute(trailPositions, 3));
          trailGeo.setAttribute("aTrailAlpha", new THREE.BufferAttribute(trailAlphas, 1));
          const trailColor = assessed
            ? moonColor.clone().lerp(new THREE.Color(theme.rim[0], theme.rim[1], theme.rim[2]), 0.25)
            : new THREE.Color(theme.rim[0], theme.rim[1], theme.rim[2]);
          const trailMat = new THREE.ShaderMaterial({
            vertexShader: TRAIL_VERT,
            fragmentShader: TRAIL_FRAG,
            uniforms: {
              uColor: { value: trailColor },
              uOpacity: { value: 1.0 },
              uDim: { value: 1.0 },
              uCenterViewZ: { value: -20.0 },
            },
            transparent: true,
            depthTest: true,
            depthWrite: false,
            blending: THREE.AdditiveBlending,
          });
          const trailLine = new THREE.Line(trailGeo, trailMat);
          trailLine.renderOrder = 2;
          moonsGroup.add(trailLine);

          moon.userData = {
            chapter: ch,
            planetNode: null, // assigned below
            rx,
            rz,
            orbitQuat,
            baseAngle,
            currentAngle: baseAngle,
            dirSign,
            speed: keplerSpeed,
            baseScale: moonRadius,
            assessed,
            moonColor,
            moonMat,
            glowMat: mGlowMat,
            glowSprite: mGlow,
            trailPosAttr: trailGeo.getAttribute("position"),
            trailMat,
            trailPtsCount,
            whisperMat,
          };
          moonsGroup.add(moon);
          planetMoons.push(moon);
        });

        ringDefs.push({ rx, rz, orbitQuat, whisperMat });
      });

      // Build the planet's particle dust belts (1-2 belts in a SINGLE BufferGeometry Points object per planet)
      // About 300 particles per planet on desktop, 150 on phones.
      const numBelts = chs.length > 6 ? 2 : 1;
      const beltConfigs =
        numBelts === 2
          ? [
              {
                rMid: 2.38,
                rHalfWidth: 0.36,
                ecc: 0.90,
                quat: new THREE.Quaternion().setFromEuler(
                  new THREE.Euler(
                    (idx % 2 === 0 ? 1 : -1) * 0.20,
                    idx * 0.5,
                    (idx % 2 === 0 ? -1 : 1) * 0.12
                  )
                ),
                speed: 0.085,
                chapters: [],
              },
              {
                rMid: 3.42,
                rHalfWidth: 0.42,
                ecc: 0.88,
                quat: new THREE.Quaternion().setFromEuler(
                  new THREE.Euler(
                    (idx % 2 === 0 ? -1 : 1) * 0.28,
                    idx * 0.5 + 0.7,
                    (idx % 2 === 0 ? 1 : -1) * 0.16
                  )
                ),
                speed: -0.058,
                chapters: [],
              },
            ]
          : [
              {
                rMid: 2.68,
                rHalfWidth: 0.46,
                ecc: 0.89,
                quat: new THREE.Quaternion().setFromEuler(
                  new THREE.Euler((idx % 2 === 0 ? 1 : -1) * 0.22, idx * 0.4, 0.12)
                ),
                speed: 0.075,
                chapters: [],
              },
            ];

      planetMoons.forEach((m, mIdx) => {
        const bIdx = numBelts === 2 ? mIdx % 2 : 0;
        beltConfigs[bIdx].chapters.push(m);
      });
      beltConfigs.forEach(bc => {
        if (bc.rMid + bc.rHalfWidth + 0.35 > outerBeltRadius) {
          outerBeltRadius = bc.rMid + bc.rHalfWidth + 0.35;
        }
      });

      // Assign arc segments in each belt to chapters
      const chapterArcs = [];
      beltConfigs.forEach((bc, bIdx) => {
        const nCh = bc.chapters.length || 1;
        const arcSpan = (Math.PI * 2) / nCh;
        bc.chapters.forEach((moonMesh, localIdx) => {
          const a0 = localIdx * arcSpan;
          const a1 = (localIdx + 1) * arcSpan;
          moonMesh.userData.beltIdx = bIdx;
          moonMesh.userData.arcStart = a0;
          moonMesh.userData.arcEnd = a1;
          chapterArcs.push({
            beltIdx: bIdx,
            arcStart: a0,
            arcEnd: a1,
            moon: moonMesh,
            chapter: moonMesh.userData.chapter,
          });
        });
      });

      const particleCount = isPhone ? 156 : 312;
      const bPositions = new Float32Array(particleCount * 3);
      const bSizes = new Float32Array(particleCount);
      const bColors = new Float32Array(particleCount * 3);
      const bAlphas = new Float32Array(particleCount);
      const bSparks = new Float32Array(particleCount);
      const bPhases = new Float32Array(particleCount);
      const particleMeta = [];

      for (let pIdx = 0; pIdx < particleCount; pIdx++) {
        const arcEntry = chapterArcs[pIdx % chapterArcs.length];
        const ch = arcEntry.chapter;
        const moon = arcEntry.moon;
        const bc = beltConfigs[arcEntry.beltIdx];
        const assessed = (ch.n || 0) > 0;
        const rgb = moonColorRGB(ch.readiness, assessed);

        // Leave a subtle 6% gap at segment boundaries so chapter arcs read cleanly
        const margin = (arcEntry.arcEnd - arcEntry.arcStart) * 0.04;
        const u = margin + rng() * (arcEntry.arcEnd - arcEntry.arcStart - 2 * margin);
        const baseBeltAngle = arcEntry.arcStart + u;
        // Gaussian-like radial & vertical scatter for natural asteroid dust belt
        const gaussR = (rng() + rng() - 1) * bc.rHalfWidth;
        const gaussY = (rng() + rng() - 1) * 0.14;

        // Misconception chapters get pulsing coral sparks inside their belt arc
        const isSpark = (ch.flags || 0) > 0 && pIdx % Math.max(2, chapterArcs.length) < 2;

        bColors[pIdx * 3] = isSpark ? 1.0 : rgb.r;
        bColors[pIdx * 3 + 1] = isSpark ? 0.36 : rgb.g;
        bColors[pIdx * 3 + 2] = isSpark ? 0.30 : rgb.b;

        bSizes[pIdx] = isSpark
          ? 3.8 + rng() * 1.8
          : assessed
          ? 1.95 + rng() * 1.65
          : 1.45 + rng() * 1.35;
        bAlphas[pIdx] = isSpark ? 1.0 : assessed ? 0.78 + rng() * 0.22 : 0.48 + rng() * 0.28;
        bSparks[pIdx] = isSpark ? 1.0 : 0.0;
        bPhases[pIdx] = rng() * Math.PI * 2;

        // Swirl offset when condensing onto the target moon
        const swirlAngle = rng() * Math.PI * 2;
        const swirlR = 0.18 + rng() * 0.45;
        particleMeta.push({
          beltIdx: arcEntry.beltIdx,
          baseBeltAngle,
          rOffset: gaussR,
          yOffset: gaussY,
          moon,
          swirlX: Math.cos(swirlAngle) * swirlR,
          swirlY: (rng() - 0.5) * 0.35,
          swirlZ: Math.sin(swirlAngle) * swirlR,
        });
      }

      const beltGeo = new THREE.BufferGeometry();
      beltGeo.setAttribute("position", new THREE.BufferAttribute(bPositions, 3));
      beltGeo.setAttribute("aSize", new THREE.BufferAttribute(bSizes, 1));
      beltGeo.setAttribute("aColor", new THREE.BufferAttribute(bColors, 3));
      beltGeo.setAttribute("aAlpha", new THREE.BufferAttribute(bAlphas, 1));
      beltGeo.setAttribute("aSpark", new THREE.BufferAttribute(bSparks, 1));
      beltGeo.setAttribute("aPhase", new THREE.BufferAttribute(bPhases, 1));

      const beltMat = new THREE.ShaderMaterial({
        vertexShader: BELT_VERT,
        fragmentShader: BELT_FRAG,
        uniforms: {
          uTime: { value: 0 },
          uPixelRatio: { value: Math.min(window.devicePixelRatio || 1, 2) },
          uDim: { value: 1.0 },
          uFade: { value: 1.0 },
          uCenterViewZ: { value: -20.0 },
        },
        transparent: true,
        depthTest: true,
        depthWrite: false,
        blending: THREE.AdditiveBlending,
      });

      const beltPoints = new THREE.Points(beltGeo, beltMat);
      beltPoints.renderOrder = 2;
      systemGroup.add(beltPoints);

      const planetNode = {
        idx,
        subject,
        theme,
        basePos: new THREE.Vector3(px, py, pz),
        targetPos: new THREE.Vector3(px, py, pz),
        group: systemGroup,
        mesh: planetMesh,
        haloSprite,
        planetMat,
        haloMat,
        ringMat,
        label: labelBtn,
        moonsGroup,
        moons: planetMoons,
        ringDefs,
        beltConfigs,
        chapterArcs,
        beltPoints,
        beltPosAttr: beltGeo.getAttribute("position"),
        beltMat,
        particleMeta,
        outerBeltRadius,
        condense: data.isDemo ? 0.82 : 0, // 0 = dust belt (overview), 1 = condensed into individual moons (focused)
        targetCondense: data.isDemo ? 0.82 : 0,
      };
      planetMoons.forEach(m => {
        m.userData.planetNode = planetNode;
      });
      planetMesh.userData.planetNode = planetNode;
      planetNodes.push(planetNode);
    });

    // 5. Exact perspective camera framing including the outermost belt + label pill of every system
    let theta = 0.22;
    let phi = 0.96;
    let distance = 24;
    let targetTheta = theta;
    let targetPhi = phi;
    let targetDistance = distance;
    let framedDistance = 24;
    let framedTargetY = 0;
    const lookTarget = new THREE.Vector3(0, 0, 0);
    const desiredTarget = new THREE.Vector3(0, 0, 0);

    function computeFramedDistance(overrideW, overrideH, snapPositions = true) {
      const w = Math.max(280, overrideW || stage.clientWidth || 640);
      const h = Math.max(320, overrideH || stage.clientHeight || 560);
      const aspect = w / h;

      // Adapt FOV and constellation aspect smoothly so narrow/portrait stages keep planets large and clear
      camera.fov = aspect < 0.85 ? 50 : aspect < 1.15 ? 47 : 44;
      camera.aspect = aspect;
      camera.updateProjectionMatrix();

      const tPortrait = clamp((1.22 - aspect) / 0.72, 0, 1);
      const scaleX = lerp(1.0, 0.36, tPortrait);
      const scaleY = lerp(1.0, 1.76, tPortrait);
      const scaleZ = lerp(1.0, 0.72, tPortrait);
      for (const pNode of planetNodes) {
        pNode.targetPos.set(
          pNode.basePos.x * scaleX,
          pNode.basePos.y * scaleY,
          pNode.basePos.z * scaleZ
        );
        if (snapPositions || REDUCED_MOTION) {
          pNode.group.position.copy(pNode.targetPos);
        }
      }

      const vFov = (camera.fov * Math.PI) / 180;
      const hFov = 2 * Math.atan(Math.tan(vFov / 2) * aspect);
      const tanV = Math.tan(Math.max(0.18, vFov / 2));
      const tanH = Math.tan(Math.max(0.18, hFov / 2));

      // Camera basis at default overview angles (theta=0.22, phi=0.96)
      const defTheta = 0.22;
      const defPhi = 0.96;
      const forward = new THREE.Vector3(
        -Math.sin(defPhi) * Math.sin(defTheta),
        -Math.cos(defPhi),
        -Math.sin(defPhi) * Math.cos(defTheta)
      ).normalize();
      const right = new THREE.Vector3()
        .crossVectors(forward, new THREE.Vector3(0, 1, 0))
        .normalize();
      const up = new THREE.Vector3().crossVectors(right, forward).normalize();

      // Safe screen box: keep planets and labels out of top chip, side edges, and bottom legend + Pip strip (>= 80 px)
      const topSafePx = data.isDemo ? 68 : 44;
      const bottomSafePx = data.isDemo ? (w < 880 ? 174 : 118) : 64;
      const sideSafePx = w < 520 ? 34 : 76;

      const marginH = clamp(1 - (2 * sideSafePx) / w, 0.52, 0.88);
      const ndcTop = 1 - (2 * topSafePx) / h;
      const ndcBottom = -1 + (2 * bottomSafePx) / h;
      const ndcMid = (ndcTop + ndcBottom) * 0.5;
      const marginV = clamp((ndcTop - ndcBottom) * 0.5, 0.36, 0.86);

      let reqDist = 11.0;
      const sampleVec = new THREE.Vector3();
      for (const pNode of planetNodes) {
        const center = pNode.targetPos;
        const r = pNode.outerBeltRadius + (aspect < 0.85 ? 0.85 : 0.45);
        // Sample 12 points around the 3D outer belt envelope + top label pill offset
        for (let i = 0; i < 12; i++) {
          const a = (i / 12) * Math.PI * 2;
          for (const yOff of [-1.35, 0.0, 2.85]) {
            sampleVec.set(
              center.x + Math.cos(a) * r,
              center.y + yOff,
              center.z + Math.sin(a) * r
            );
            const xCam = sampleVec.dot(right);
            const yCam = sampleVec.dot(up);
            const zCam = -sampleVec.dot(forward); // positive = closer to camera than origin

            const dH = zCam + Math.abs(xCam) / (marginH * tanH);
            const dV = zCam + Math.abs(yCam) / (marginV * tanV);
            if (dH > reqDist) reqDist = dH;
            if (dV > reqDist) reqDist = dV;
          }
        }
      }
      reqDist = clamp(reqDist, 11.0, 82.0);
      const targetY = -((ndcMid * reqDist * tanV) / Math.max(0.5, up.y));
      return { dist: reqDist, targetY };
    }

    // Smooth camera transition (~900 ms ease-in-out)
    let camTransition = null;
    function animateCameraTo(newTargetVec, newDist, newPhi, durationMs = 400) {
      durationMs = Math.min(400, durationMs);
      if (REDUCED_MOTION) {
        lookTarget.copy(newTargetVec);
        desiredTarget.copy(newTargetVec);
        distance = targetDistance = newDist;
        if (newPhi !== undefined) phi = targetPhi = newPhi;
        camTransition = null;
        return;
      }
      camTransition = {
        startTime: performance.now(),
        duration: durationMs,
        fromTarget: lookTarget.clone(),
        toTarget: newTargetVec.clone(),
        fromDist: distance,
        toDist: newDist,
        fromPhi: phi,
        toPhi: newPhi !== undefined ? newPhi : targetPhi,
      };
      desiredTarget.copy(newTargetVec);
      targetDistance = newDist;
      if (newPhi !== undefined) targetPhi = newPhi;
    }

    let focusedPlanet = null;
    let hoveredMoon = null;
    let hoveredChapterArc = null;
    let selectedMoon = null;

    function formatTooltipText(ch) {
      if (data.isDemo) {
        return ch.n > 0
          ? `${ch.chapter} · ${Math.round(ch.readiness * 100)}% ready`
          : `${ch.chapter} · not assessed yet`;
      }
      const readyPart = ch.n > 0 ? `${Math.round(ch.readiness * 100)}% ready` : "not assessed yet";
      return `${ch.chapter} · ${ch.subject} · ${readyPart} · ${ch.n || 0} answered`;
    }

    function projectToStage(worldVec) {
      const v = worldVec.clone().project(camera);
      const rect = canvas.getBoundingClientRect();
      return {
        x: (v.x * 0.5 + 0.5) * rect.width,
        y: (-v.y * 0.5 + 0.5) * rect.height,
        visible: v.z < 1,
      };
    }

    function showTooltipAtWorld(ch, theme, worldVec) {
      tooltip.style.setProperty("--subj-accent", theme.hex);
      tooltip.textContent = formatTooltipText(ch);
      tooltip.hidden = false;
      const pos = projectToStage(worldVec);
      const rect = canvas.getBoundingClientRect();
      const clampedX = clamp(pos.x, 96, Math.max(96, rect.width - 96));
      const clampedY = clamp(pos.y, 48, Math.max(48, rect.height - 16));
      tooltip.style.left = clampedX + "px";
      tooltip.style.top = clampedY + "px";
    }

    function showTooltipForMoon(moon) {
      const wp = new THREE.Vector3();
      moon.getWorldPosition(wp);
      showTooltipAtWorld(moon.userData.chapter, moon.userData.planetNode.theme, wp);
    }

    function openChapterPanel(moon) {
      selectedMoon = moon;
      const ch = moon.userData.chapter;
      const pNode = moon.userData.planetNode;
      if (isPhone) {
        tooltip.hidden = true;
      } else {
        showTooltipForMoon(moon);
      }

      const planetWorld = new THREE.Vector3();
      pNode.group.getWorldPosition(planetWorld);
      const sysScreen = projectToStage(planetWorld);
      const rect = canvas.getBoundingClientRect();
      panel.classList.toggle("side-left", sysScreen.x > rect.width * 0.52);
      panel.style.setProperty("--subj-accent", pNode.theme.hex);

      panel.replaceChildren();
      const top = el("div", "galaxy-panel-top");
      const badgeClass =
        ch.n === 0 ? "" : ch.readiness >= 0.7 ? " ready" : ch.readiness >= 0.4 ? " mid" : " low";
      const badge = el(
        "span",
        "galaxy-badge" + badgeClass,
        ch.n > 0 ? `${pct(ch.readiness)} ready` : "Not assessed yet"
      );
      const close = el("button", "galaxy-close", "×");
      close.type = "button";
      close.setAttribute("aria-label", "Close chapter panel");
      close.onclick = e => {
        e.stopPropagation();
        selectedMoon = null;
        panel.hidden = true;
        tooltip.hidden = true;
      };
      top.append(badge, close);

      const title = el("strong", "", ch.chapter);
      const meta = el(
        "p",
        "",
        `${ch.subject} · ${ch.n || 0} ${ch.n === 1 ? "question" : "questions"} answered`
      );
      panel.append(top, title, meta);
      if (ch.flags > 0) {
        panel.append(
          el(
            "p",
            "flag-note",
            `⚠ ${ch.flags} confident misconception${ch.flags === 1 ? "" : "s"} flagged`
          )
        );
      }
      const btn = el("button", "btn primary", "Practise →");
      btn.type = "button";
      btn.onclick = () => {
        btn.disabled=true;
        if (REDUCED_MOTION) {openChapterQuiz(data.level,ch.chapter);return;}
        const point=new THREE.Vector3();moon.getWorldPosition(point);
        animateCameraTo(point,5.5,targetPhi,400);
        setTimeout(()=>openChapterQuiz(data.level,ch.chapter),400);
      };
      panel.append(btn);
      panel.hidden = false;
    }

    function focusPlanet(pNode, initialMoon = null) {
      focusedPlanet = pNode;
      resetBtn.hidden = false;
      hoveredChapterArc = null;

      planetNodes.forEach(n => {
        n.targetCondense = n === pNode ? 1 : 0;
        if (REDUCED_MOTION) n.condense = n.targetCondense;
      });

      const wp = new THREE.Vector3();
      pNode.group.getWorldPosition(wp);

      // Compute zoom distance that frames this planet's outermost moon ring cleanly
      const vFov = (camera.fov * Math.PI) / 180;
      const hFov = 2 * Math.atan(Math.tan(vFov / 2) * camera.aspect);
      const minHalfFov = Math.min(vFov / 2, hFov / 2);
      const fitPlanetDist = clamp(
        ((pNode.outerBeltRadius + (isPhone ? 1.85 : 0.85)) * 1.16) / Math.tan(Math.max(0.2, minHalfFov)),
        7.4,
        17.5
      );
      if (isPhone) {
        // Shift camera lookTarget slightly below planet so the planet + moon rings sit in the upper half above the card
        wp.y -= 1.15;
      }
      animateCameraTo(wp, fitPlanetDist, 0.90, 900);

      // Pick either the requested moon or the highest-priority chapter moon to show in the Practise card
      const chosenMoon =
        initialMoon ||
        pNode.moons.find(m => (m.userData.chapter.flags || 0) > 0) ||
        pNode.moons.find(m => (m.userData.chapter.n || 0) > 0) ||
        pNode.moons[0];
      if (chosenMoon) {
        if (data.isDemo) {
          selectedMoon = chosenMoon;
          showTooltipForMoon(chosenMoon);
        } else {
          openChapterPanel(chosenMoon);
        }
      }
    }

    function resetCamera() {
      focusedPlanet = null;
      selectedMoon = null;
      hoveredMoon = null;
      hoveredChapterArc = null;
      panel.hidden = true;
      tooltip.hidden = true;
      resetBtn.hidden = true;
      const defCondense = data.isDemo ? 0.82 : 0;
      planetNodes.forEach(n => {
        n.targetCondense = defCondense;
        if (REDUCED_MOTION) n.condense = defCondense;
      });
      animateCameraTo(new THREE.Vector3(0, framedTargetY, 0), framedDistance, 0.96, 900);
    }
    resetBtn.onclick = resetCamera;

    // Escape key reverses focused view back to overview
    function onKeyDown(e) {
      if (e.key === "Escape" && (focusedPlanet || selectedMoon)) {
        resetCamera();
      }
    }
    window.addEventListener("keydown", onKeyDown);

    planetNodes.forEach(node => {
      node.label.onclick = e => {
        e.stopPropagation();
        if (focusedPlanet === node) {
          resetCamera();
        } else {
          focusPlanet(node);
        }
      };
    });

    // Raycasting & pointer controls (planets, focused moons, and overview belt arcs)
    const raycaster = new THREE.Raycaster();
    const pointerNDC = new THREE.Vector2();
    const pointers = new Map();
    let dragStart = null;
    let isDragging = false;
    let pinchStartDist = 0;
    let pinchStartCamDist = distance;
    let beltTimeAccum = 0;

    function setRayFromClient(clientX, clientY) {
      const rect = canvas.getBoundingClientRect();
      if (!rect.width || !rect.height) return false;
      pointerNDC.x = ((clientX - rect.left) / rect.width) * 2 - 1;
      pointerNDC.y = -((clientY - rect.top) / rect.height) * 2 + 1;
      raycaster.setFromCamera(pointerNDC, camera);
      return true;
    }

    function pickFocusedMoon(clientX, clientY) {
      if (data.isDemo && !focusedPlanet) {
        if (!setRayFromClient(clientX, clientY)) return null;
        const allMoons = planetNodes.flatMap(n => n.moons);
        const hits = raycaster.intersectObjects(allMoons, false);
        return hits.length ? hits[0].object : null;
      }
      if (!focusedPlanet || focusedPlanet.condense < 0.35) return null;
      if (!setRayFromClient(clientX, clientY)) return null;
      const hits = raycaster.intersectObjects(focusedPlanet.moons, false);
      return hits.length ? hits[0].object : null;
    }

    function pickPlanetSphere(clientX, clientY) {
      if (!setRayFromClient(clientX, clientY)) return null;
      const hits = raycaster.intersectObjects(planetMeshes, false);
      return hits.length ? hits[0].object.userData.planetNode : null;
    }

    // Ray-plane intersection with each planet's dust belt(s) in Overview mode
    const invQuatTmp = new THREE.Quaternion();
    const localRayOrigin = new THREE.Vector3();
    const localRayDir = new THREE.Vector3();
    const hitLocal = new THREE.Vector3();
    const hitWorld = new THREE.Vector3();

    function pickOverviewBeltArc(clientX, clientY) {
      if (!setRayFromClient(clientX, clientY)) return null;
      let bestHit = null;
      let bestDistToCam = Infinity;

      for (const pNode of planetNodes) {
        if (pNode.condense > 0.5) continue;
        const sysPos = pNode.group.position;

        // First make sure the ray isn't occluded by the front hemisphere of the planet
        for (let bIdx = 0; bIdx < pNode.beltConfigs.length; bIdx++) {
          const bc = pNode.beltConfigs[bIdx];
          invQuatTmp.copy(bc.quat).invert();
          localRayOrigin.copy(raycaster.ray.origin).sub(sysPos).applyQuaternion(invQuatTmp);
          localRayDir.copy(raycaster.ray.direction).applyQuaternion(invQuatTmp);
          if (Math.abs(localRayDir.y) < 1e-4) continue;
          const t = -localRayOrigin.y / localRayDir.y;
          if (t <= 0 || t >= bestDistToCam) continue;

          hitLocal.copy(localRayOrigin).addScaledVector(localRayDir, t);
          const rx = hitLocal.x;
          const rz = hitLocal.z / bc.ecc;
          const r = Math.hypot(rx, rz);
          if (Math.abs(r - bc.rMid) > bc.rHalfWidth + 0.38) continue;

          // Compute angle in the rotating belt frame
          let ang = Math.atan2(rz, rx) - beltTimeAccum * bc.speed;
          ang = ((ang % (Math.PI * 2)) + Math.PI * 2) % (Math.PI * 2);

          const arcMatch = pNode.chapterArcs.find(
            ca => ca.beltIdx === bIdx && ang >= ca.arcStart && ang <= ca.arcEnd
          );
          if (arcMatch) {
            hitWorld.copy(hitLocal).applyQuaternion(bc.quat).add(sysPos);
            bestDistToCam = t;
            bestHit = {
              planetNode: pNode,
              moon: arcMatch.moon,
              chapter: arcMatch.chapter,
              worldPoint: hitWorld.clone(),
            };
          }
        }
      }
      return bestHit;
    }

    function checkAutoFocusByZoom() {
      // Zooming near a planet automatically condenses its belt into moons; zooming out returns to overview
      if (!focusedPlanet && targetDistance < framedDistance * 0.62) {
        let bestNode = planetNodes[0];
        let bestScreenDist = Infinity;
        const cx = (lastStageW || canvas.clientWidth) * 0.5;
        const cy = (lastStageH || canvas.clientHeight) * 0.5;
        const wp = new THREE.Vector3();
        for (const pNode of planetNodes) {
          pNode.group.getWorldPosition(wp);
          const sp = projectToStage(wp);
          const d = Math.hypot(sp.x - cx, sp.y - cy);
          if (d < bestScreenDist) {
            bestScreenDist = d;
            bestNode = pNode;
          }
        }
        if (bestNode) focusPlanet(bestNode);
      } else if (focusedPlanet && targetDistance > framedDistance * 0.82) {
        resetCamera();
      }
    }

    canvas.addEventListener("pointerdown", e => {
      pointers.set(e.pointerId, { x: e.clientX, y: e.clientY });
      canvas.setPointerCapture(e.pointerId);
      if (pointers.size === 1) {
        dragStart = { x: e.clientX, y: e.clientY, theta: targetTheta, phi: targetPhi };
        isDragging = false;
      } else if (pointers.size === 2) {
        const pts = [...pointers.values()];
        pinchStartDist = Math.hypot(pts[0].x - pts[1].x, pts[0].y - pts[1].y);
        pinchStartCamDist = targetDistance;
      }
    });

    canvas.addEventListener("pointermove", e => {
      if (pointers.has(e.pointerId)) {
        pointers.set(e.pointerId, { x: e.clientX, y: e.clientY });
      }
      if (pointers.size === 2) {
        camTransition = null;
        const pts = [...pointers.values()];
        const dist = Math.hypot(pts[0].x - pts[1].x, pts[0].y - pts[1].y);
        if (pinchStartDist > 10) {
          targetDistance = clamp(pinchStartCamDist * (pinchStartDist / dist), 5.5, 48);
          checkAutoFocusByZoom();
        }
        return;
      }
      if (pointers.size === 1 && dragStart) {
        const dx = e.clientX - dragStart.x;
        const dy = e.clientY - dragStart.y;
        if (!isDragging && Math.hypot(dx, dy) > 5) {
          isDragging = true;
          camTransition = null;
        }
        if (isDragging) {
          targetTheta = dragStart.theta - dx * 0.0075;
          targetPhi = clamp(dragStart.phi - dy * 0.0065, 0.28, Math.PI * 0.48);
          return;
        }
      }
      if (e.pointerType !== "touch") {
        if (focusedPlanet) {
          const moonHit = pickFocusedMoon(e.clientX, e.clientY);
          hoveredMoon = moonHit;
          hoveredChapterArc = null;
          canvas.style.cursor = moonHit ? "pointer" : "grab";
          if (isPhone && !panel.hidden) {
            tooltip.hidden = true;
          } else if (moonHit) {
            showTooltipForMoon(moonHit);
          } else if (selectedMoon) {
            showTooltipForMoon(selectedMoon);
          } else {
            tooltip.hidden = true;
          }
        } else {
          const demoMoonHit = data.isDemo ? pickFocusedMoon(e.clientX, e.clientY) : null;
          hoveredMoon = demoMoonHit;
          const arcHit = demoMoonHit ? null : pickOverviewBeltArc(e.clientX, e.clientY);
          const planetHit = pickPlanetSphere(e.clientX, e.clientY);
          hoveredChapterArc = arcHit;
          if (demoMoonHit) {
            canvas.style.cursor = "pointer";
            showTooltipForMoon(demoMoonHit);
          } else if (arcHit) {
            canvas.style.cursor = "pointer";
            showTooltipAtWorld(arcHit.chapter, arcHit.planetNode.theme, arcHit.worldPoint);
          } else if (planetHit) {
            canvas.style.cursor = "pointer";
            if (!data.isDemo) tooltip.hidden = true;
          } else {
            canvas.style.cursor = "grab";
            if (data.isDemo && selectedMoon) showTooltipForMoon(selectedMoon);
            else tooltip.hidden = true;
          }
        }
      }
    });

    function endPointer(e) {
      const wasTap = pointers.size === 1 && dragStart && !isDragging;
      pointers.delete(e.pointerId);
      if (pointers.size === 0) dragStart = null;
      if (!wasTap) return;

      if (focusedPlanet) {
        const moonHit = pickFocusedMoon(e.clientX, e.clientY);
        if (moonHit) {
          openChapterPanel(moonHit);
          return;
        }
        const planetHit = pickPlanetSphere(e.clientX, e.clientY);
        if (planetHit && planetHit !== focusedPlanet) {
          focusPlanet(planetHit);
          return;
        }
        if (!planetHit) {
          // Clicking empty space reverses condensation back to galaxy overview
          resetCamera();
        }
      } else {
        const arcHit = pickOverviewBeltArc(e.clientX, e.clientY);
        if (arcHit) {
          focusPlanet(arcHit.planetNode, arcHit.moon);
          return;
        }
        const planetHit = pickPlanetSphere(e.clientX, e.clientY);
        if (planetHit) {
          focusPlanet(planetHit);
        }
      }
    }
    canvas.addEventListener("pointerup", endPointer);
    // the browser cancels a touch when it takes over to scroll the page: that is never a tap on a planet
    canvas.addEventListener("pointercancel", e => {
      pointers.delete(e.pointerId);
      if (pointers.size === 0) { dragStart = null; isDragging = false; }
    });
    canvas.addEventListener("pointerleave", () => {
      if (!selectedMoon) tooltip.hidden = true;
      hoveredMoon = null;
      hoveredChapterArc = null;
    });

    let zoomCheckQueued = false;
    canvas.addEventListener(
      "wheel",
      e => {
        if (stage.closest('[data-journey="1"]')) return;
        e.preventDefault();
        camTransition = null;
        targetDistance = clamp(targetDistance + e.deltaY * 0.015, 5.5, 48);
        if (!zoomCheckQueued) {  // wheels fire many events per frame: check focus once per frame
          zoomCheckQueued = true;
          requestAnimationFrame(() => { zoomCheckQueued = false; checkAutoFocusByZoom(); });
        }
      },
      { passive: false }
    );

    // Resize & auto-fit bounding envelope
    let lastStageW = 0;
    let lastStageH = 0;

    function resize(opts = {}) {
      const actualW = stage.clientWidth || 640;
      const actualH = stage.clientHeight || 560;
      const targetW = opts.targetWidth || actualW;
      const targetH = opts.targetHeight || actualH;
      const animate = Boolean(opts.animate && !REDUCED_MOTION);

      lastStageW = actualW;
      lastStageH = actualH;
      renderer.setSize(actualW, actualH, false);

      const prevFramed = framedDistance;
      const framed = computeFramedDistance(targetW, targetH, !animate);
      camera.aspect = actualW / actualH;
      camera.updateProjectionMatrix();
      nebulaMat.uniforms.uAspect.value = actualW / actualH;

      framedDistance = framed.dist;
      framedTargetY = framed.targetY;

      if (!focusedPlanet) {
        desiredTarget.set(0, framedTargetY, 0);
        if (animate) {
          animateCameraTo(desiredTarget, framedDistance, targetPhi, opts.duration || 300);
          if (!running) start();
        } else {
          if (!camTransition) {
            lookTarget.copy(desiredTarget);
          }
          if (Math.abs(targetDistance - prevFramed) < 12.0 || opts.snap) {
            targetDistance = framedDistance;
            if (!entranceActive && !camTransition) distance = framedDistance;
          }
          if (REDUCED_MOTION && !running) {
            running = true;
            frame(performance.now());
          }
        }
      }
    }

    // 6. Entrance choreography (1.6s glide-in on first IntersectionObserver view)
    let entranceActive = Boolean(playEntrance && !REDUCED_MOTION && !data.isDemo);
    let entranceStart = 0;
    const ENTRANCE_DURATION = 1600;

    let running = false;
    let rafId = 0;
    let lastTs = performance.now();
    const tmpVec = new THREE.Vector3();
    const tmpViewVec = new THREE.Vector3();
    const camRight = new THREE.Vector3();
    const camUp = new THREE.Vector3();
    const camForward = new THREE.Vector3();
    const beltPosTmp = new THREE.Vector3();
    const moonTargetTmp = new THREE.Vector3();

    // Reduced-motion resize can draw immediately; initialize the frame state first.
    resize({ snap: true });
    if (entranceActive) {
      distance = framedDistance * 1.65;
      targetDistance = framedDistance;
      theta = 0.68;
      targetTheta = 0.22;
      planetNodes.forEach(p => p.group.scale.setScalar(0.001));
    } else {
      distance = targetDistance = framedDistance;
      lookTarget.copy(desiredTarget);
    }

    function frame(now) {
      if (!running) return;
      const dt = Math.min((now - lastTs) / 1000, 0.05);
      lastTs = now;
      const elapsedSec = now * 0.001;
      beltTimeAccum += dt;

      // Keep WebGL canvas size & projection aspect locked to live stage width during CSS width transitions
      const curW = stage.clientWidth || 640;
      const curH = stage.clientHeight || 560;
      if (curW !== lastStageW || curH !== lastStageH) {
        lastStageW = curW;
        lastStageH = curH;
        renderer.setSize(curW, curH, false);
        camera.aspect = curW / curH;
        camera.updateProjectionMatrix();
        nebulaMat.uniforms.uAspect.value = curW / curH;
      }

      // Entrance choreography (1.6 s)
      let entranceProgress = 1;
      if (entranceActive) {
        if (!entranceStart) entranceStart = now;
        entranceProgress = clamp((now - entranceStart) / ENTRANCE_DURATION, 0, 1);
        const eased = easeOutCubic(entranceProgress);
        const starFade = clamp(entranceProgress / 0.45, 0, 1);
        starLayers.forEach(l => {
          l.mat.uniforms.uOpacity.value = starFade;
        });
        distance = lerp(framedDistance * 1.65, framedDistance, eased);
        theta = lerp(0.68, 0.22, eased);
        targetTheta = theta;
        targetDistance = distance;
        if (entranceProgress >= 1) {
          entranceActive = false;
          starLayers.forEach(l => {
            l.mat.uniforms.uOpacity.value = 1;
          });
        }
      }

      // Camera 900ms ease-in-out transition vs manual drag/zoom damping
      if (camTransition) {
        const t = clamp((now - camTransition.startTime) / camTransition.duration, 0, 1);
        const e = easeInOutCubic(t);
        lookTarget.lerpVectors(camTransition.fromTarget, camTransition.toTarget, e);
        distance = lerp(camTransition.fromDist, camTransition.toDist, e);
        phi = lerp(camTransition.fromPhi, camTransition.toPhi, e);
        if (t >= 1) camTransition = null;
      } else if (!entranceActive) {
        if (!isDragging && pointers.size === 0 && !focusedPlanet && !REDUCED_MOTION) {
          targetTheta += dt * 0.032; // calm idle galaxy rotation
        }
        theta += (targetTheta - theta) * 0.12;
        phi += (targetPhi - phi) * 0.12;
        distance += (targetDistance - distance) * 0.12;
        lookTarget.lerp(desiredTarget, 0.12);
      }

      camera.position.set(
        lookTarget.x + distance * Math.sin(phi) * Math.sin(theta),
        lookTarget.y + distance * Math.cos(phi),
        lookTarget.z + distance * Math.sin(phi) * Math.cos(theta)
      );
      camera.lookAt(lookTarget);
      camera.updateMatrixWorld();

      // Extract camera basis vectors for label placement & halo positioning
      camera.matrixWorld.extractBasis(camRight, camUp, camForward);

      // Update nebula & parallax star layers
      nebulaMat.uniforms.uTime.value = elapsedSec;
      nebulaMat.uniforms.uCamShift.value.set(theta * 0.35, (phi - 0.96) * 0.35);
      starLayers.forEach(layer => {
        layer.mat.uniforms.uTime.value = elapsedSec;
        layer.points.rotation.y = -theta * layer.parallax;
        layer.points.rotation.x = -(phi - 0.96) * layer.parallax * 0.6;
      });

      // Occasional faint shooting star (desktop only, every 8-12 s)
      if (shootingStar && !REDUCED_MOTION) {
        if (!shootingStar.active && now >= shootingStar.nextAt) {
          shootingStar.active = true;
          shootingStar.startAt = now;
          shootingStar.line.visible = true;
          const angle = Math.random() * Math.PI * 2;
          shootingStar.origin.set(
            Math.cos(angle) * 28,
            10 + Math.random() * 8,
            Math.sin(angle) * 22 - 14
          );
          shootingStar.vel.set(
            -16 - Math.random() * 10,
            -9 - Math.random() * 5,
            (Math.random() - 0.5) * 10
          );
        }
        if (shootingStar.active) {
          const st = (now - shootingStar.startAt) / shootingStar.duration;
          if (st >= 1) {
            shootingStar.active = false;
            shootingStar.line.visible = false;
            shootingStar.nextAt = now + 8000 + Math.random() * 4000;
          } else {
            const arr = shootingStar.posAttr.array;
            const segs = arr.length / 3;
            for (let i = 0; i < segs; i++) {
              const backT = Math.max(0, st - i * 0.018);
              arr[i * 3] = shootingStar.origin.x + shootingStar.vel.x * backT;
              arr[i * 3 + 1] = shootingStar.origin.y + shootingStar.vel.y * backT;
              arr[i * 3 + 2] = shootingStar.origin.z + shootingStar.vel.z * backT;
            }
            shootingStar.posAttr.needsUpdate = true;
          }
        }
      }

      // Update planets, particle dust belts, condensation transition (600 ms), and focused moons
      const condenseStep = REDUCED_MOTION ? 1.0 : dt / 0.60; // 600 ms condensation

      planetNodes.forEach((pNode, idx) => {
        if (!REDUCED_MOTION) {
          pNode.group.position.lerp(pNode.targetPos, 0.16);
        } else {
          pNode.group.position.copy(pNode.targetPos);
        }
        if (entranceActive) {
          const delay = idx * 0.10;
          const pProg = clamp((entranceProgress - delay) / (0.68 - delay * 0.5), 0, 1);
          pNode.group.scale.setScalar(Math.max(0.001, easeOutCubic(pProg)));
        } else {
          pNode.group.scale.setScalar(1);
        }

        // Smooth 600ms condensation progress (0 = dust belt, 1 = condensed moons)
        if (pNode.condense < pNode.targetCondense) {
          pNode.condense = Math.min(pNode.targetCondense, pNode.condense + condenseStep);
        } else if (pNode.condense > pNode.targetCondense) {
          pNode.condense = Math.max(pNode.targetCondense, pNode.condense - condenseStep);
        }
        const cEased = easeInOutCubic(pNode.condense);

        // Dim other systems to 30% when one planet is focused
        const targetSysDim = !focusedPlanet || focusedPlanet === pNode ? 1.0 : 0.30;
        const curDim = lerp(pNode.planetMat.uniforms.uDim.value, targetSysDim, 0.14);

        pNode.planetMat.uniforms.uTime.value = elapsedSec + idx * 10;
        pNode.planetMat.uniforms.uDim.value = curDim;
        pNode.haloMat.opacity = 0.48 * curDim;
        if (pNode.ringMat) pNode.ringMat.uniforms.uDim.value = curDim;
        if (!REDUCED_MOTION) pNode.mesh.rotation.y += dt * 0.20;

        // Keep halo sprite slightly behind planet center along camera forward so it never washes out planet surface
        pNode.haloSprite.position.copy(camForward).multiplyScalar(-0.35);

        // Planet center view-space Z for near/far depth fading
        pNode.group.getWorldPosition(tmpVec);
        tmpViewVec.copy(tmpVec).applyMatrix4(camera.matrixWorldInverse);
        const centerViewZ = tmpViewVec.z;
        pNode.beltMat.uniforms.uCenterViewZ.value = centerViewZ;
        pNode.beltMat.uniforms.uTime.value = elapsedSec;
        pNode.beltMat.uniforms.uDim.value = curDim;

        // Step moon orbital positions (needed when condensing or focused)
        const showMoons = pNode.condense > 0.08;
        pNode.moonsGroup.visible = showMoons;

        if (showMoons || pNode.condense > 0.001) {
          const moonAlphaScale = clamp((cEased - 0.18) / 0.82, 0, 1);
          const moonSizeScale = lerp(0.35, 1.0, moonAlphaScale);

          pNode.moons.forEach(moon => {
            const u = moon.userData;
            if (!REDUCED_MOTION) {
              u.currentAngle = u.baseAngle + beltTimeAccum * u.speed;
            } else {
              u.currentAngle = u.baseAngle;
            }

            moonTargetTmp
              .set(Math.cos(u.currentAngle) * u.rx, 0, Math.sin(u.currentAngle) * u.rz)
              .applyQuaternion(u.orbitQuat);
            moon.position.copy(moonTargetTmp);

            const isHovered = moon === hoveredMoon;
            const isSelected = moon === selectedMoon;
            const hoverMult = isHovered || isSelected ? 1.4 : 1.0;
            const targetScale = u.baseScale * hoverMult * moonSizeScale;
            moon.scale.setScalar(lerp(moon.scale.x, targetScale, 0.22));

            u.moonMat.uniforms.uDim.value = curDim;
            u.moonMat.uniforms.uOpacity.value = (u.assessed ? 0.98 : 0.45) * moonAlphaScale;
            u.glowMat.opacity =
              (isHovered || isSelected ? 0.90 : u.assessed ? 0.56 : 0.25) *
              curDim *
              moonAlphaScale;

            // Update 40-degree comet-like gradient trail behind the moon
            u.trailMat.uniforms.uCenterViewZ.value = centerViewZ;
            u.trailMat.uniforms.uDim.value = curDim;
            u.trailMat.uniforms.uOpacity.value =
              (isHovered || isSelected ? 1.35 : 1.0) * moonAlphaScale;
            u.whisperMat.uniforms.uCenterViewZ.value = centerViewZ;
            u.whisperMat.uniforms.uDim.value = curDim * moonAlphaScale;

            const tArr = u.trailPosAttr.array;
            const countPts = u.trailPtsCount;
            const arcLenRad = (40 * Math.PI) / 180; // 40 deg comet tail
            for (let tIdx = 0; tIdx < countPts; tIdx++) {
              const frac = tIdx / (countPts - 1);
              const tAngle = u.currentAngle - u.dirSign * frac * arcLenRad;
              beltPosTmp
                .set(Math.cos(tAngle) * u.rx, 0, Math.sin(tAngle) * u.rz)
                .applyQuaternion(u.orbitQuat);
              tArr[tIdx * 3] = beltPosTmp.x;
              tArr[tIdx * 3 + 1] = beltPosTmp.y;
              tArr[tIdx * 3 + 2] = beltPosTmp.z;
            }
            u.trailPosAttr.needsUpdate = true;
          });
        }

        // Update particle dust belt positions (rotates in overview; flows & condenses into moon orbs on focus)
        const showBelt = pNode.condense < 0.98;
        pNode.beltPoints.visible = showBelt;
        if (showBelt) {
          pNode.beltMat.uniforms.uFade.value = clamp(1.0 - Math.pow(cEased, 1.6), 0, 1);
          const posArr = pNode.beltPosAttr.array;
          const metas = pNode.particleMeta;
          for (let i = 0; i < metas.length; i++) {
            const m = metas[i];
            const bc = pNode.beltConfigs[m.beltIdx];
            const curBeltAng =
              m.baseBeltAngle + (REDUCED_MOTION ? 0 : beltTimeAccum * bc.speed);
            const r = bc.rMid + m.rOffset;
            beltPosTmp
              .set(Math.cos(curBeltAng) * r, m.yOffset, Math.sin(curBeltAng) * r * bc.ecc)
              .applyQuaternion(bc.quat);

            if (cEased > 0.001) {
              // Flow particle smoothly into its chapter's 3D moon orb
              const mPos = m.moon.position;
              const swirlDecay = 1.0 - cEased;
              const tx = mPos.x + m.swirlX * swirlDecay;
              const ty = mPos.y + m.swirlY * swirlDecay;
              const tz = mPos.z + m.swirlZ * swirlDecay;
              posArr[i * 3] = lerp(beltPosTmp.x, tx, cEased);
              posArr[i * 3 + 1] = lerp(beltPosTmp.y, ty, cEased);
              posArr[i * 3 + 2] = lerp(beltPosTmp.z, tz, cEased);
            } else {
              posArr[i * 3] = beltPosTmp.x;
              posArr[i * 3 + 1] = beltPosTmp.y;
              posArr[i * 3 + 2] = beltPosTmp.z;
            }
          }
          pNode.beltPosAttr.needsUpdate = true;
        }

        // Position planet glass pill label ABOVE the outermost belt so it never overlaps moons;
        // hide labels of other planets when one planet is focused.
        const hideLabel = Boolean(focusedPlanet && focusedPlanet !== pNode);
        const setLabel = (opacity, x, y) => {  // write the DOM only when something changed
          const key = `${opacity}|${x}|${y}`;
          if (pNode._labelKey === key) return;
          pNode._labelKey = key;
          pNode.label.style.opacity = opacity;
          pNode.label.style.pointerEvents = opacity === "1" ? "auto" : "none";
          if (x !== null) pNode.label.style.transform = `translate3d(${x}px, ${y}px, 0) translate(-50%, -100%)`;
        };
        if (hideLabel) {
          setLabel("0", null, null);
        } else {
          pNode.group.getWorldPosition(tmpVec);
          const upOffset = focusedPlanet === pNode ? pNode.outerBeltRadius * 0.62 + 0.65 : 2.15;
          const labelWorld = tmpVec.clone().addScaledVector(camUp, upOffset);
          const sp = projectToStage(labelWorld);
          const minTop = data.isDemo ? 60 : isPhone && focusedPlanet === pNode ? 78 : 26;
          const maxBottom = data.isDemo
            ? Math.max(minTop, curH - (curW < 880 ? 168 : 112))
            : Math.max(minTop, curH - 36);
          const clampedX = clamp(sp.x, 56, Math.max(56, curW - 56));
          const clampedY = clamp(sp.y - 4, minTop, maxBottom);
          setLabel(sp.visible ? "1" : "0", Math.round(clampedX), Math.round(clampedY));
        }
      });

      // Keep tooltip anchored to active moon
      const activeMoon = hoveredMoon || selectedMoon;
      if (activeMoon && !tooltip.hidden && (focusedPlanet || data.isDemo)) {
        const wp = new THREE.Vector3();
        activeMoon.getWorldPosition(wp);
        const pos = projectToStage(wp);
        tooltip.style.left = clamp(pos.x, 96, Math.max(96, curW - 96)) + "px";
        tooltip.style.top = clamp(pos.y, 48, Math.max(48, curH - 16)) + "px";
      }

      // Pulse misconception coral rings in focused view
      if (!REDUCED_MOTION) {
        pulsingRings.forEach(r => {
          const s = 1 + 0.16 * Math.sin(elapsedSec * 3.4 + r.phase);
          r.mesh.scale.set(s, s, 1);
          r.mat.opacity = 0.58 + 0.34 * Math.sin(elapsedSec * 3.4 + r.phase);
        });
      }

      renderer.render(scene, camera);
      if (REDUCED_MOTION && data.isDemo && !camTransition && !isDragging) {
        running = false;
        return;
      }
      rafId = requestAnimationFrame(frame);
    }

    function start() {
      if (running) return;
      running = true;
      lastTs = performance.now();
      resize();
      rafId = requestAnimationFrame(frame);
    }

    function stop() {
      running = false;
      if (rafId) cancelAnimationFrame(rafId);
    }

    window.addEventListener("resize", () => {
      if (running || REDUCED_MOTION) resize({ snap: true });
    });

    // Read-only geometry helper (used by layout checks): projected planet + belt footprint in stage pixels
    function planetScreenBoxes() {
      const out = [];
      const c = new THREE.Vector3();
      const edge = new THREE.Vector3();
      for (const pNode of planetNodes) {
        pNode.group.getWorldPosition(c);
        const sp = projectToStage(c);
        edge.copy(c).addScaledVector(camRight, pNode.outerBeltRadius);
        const ep = projectToStage(edge);
        const rPx = Math.abs(ep.x - sp.x);
        out.push({
          subject: pNode.subject,
          x: sp.x,
          y: sp.y,
          planetRadiusPx: rPx * (1.24 / pNode.outerBeltRadius),
          beltRadiusPx: rPx,
        });
      }
      return out;
    }

    return { start, stop, refit: resize, planetScreenBoxes,
      focusSubject(subject) {
        const planet = planetNodes.find(p => p.subject === subject);
        if (planet) { focusPlanet(planet); selectedMoon = null; panel.hidden = true; tooltip.hidden = true; }
      }
    };
  }

  async function fetchGalaxyData() {
    let state = null;
    try {
      const r = await fetch("/api/state");
      if (r.ok) state = await r.json();
    } catch {}

    const level =
      (state && state.preferences && state.preferences.level) ||
      (state && state.user && state.user.level) ||
      localStorage.getItem("pp-level") ||
      "JEE";
    const chosenSubjects =
      (state && state.preferences && state.preferences.subjects) || [];

    const sid = state && state.last_session_id;
    if (sid) {
      try {
        const dr = await fetch("/api/dashboard?session_id=" + encodeURIComponent(sid));
        if (dr.ok) {
          const dash = await dr.json();
          if (dash.chapters && dash.chapters.length) {
            const hasAnswered = dash.chapters.some(c => (c.n || 0) > 0);
            return {
              level,
              hasSession: hasAnswered,
              chapters: dash.chapters,
              subjects: dash.subjects || [],
            };
          }
        }
      } catch {}
    }

    try {
      const cr = await fetch("/api/catalog?level=" + encodeURIComponent(level));
      if (cr.ok) {
        const cat = await cr.json();
        const subjList = Array.isArray(cat.subjects) ? cat.subjects : [];
        const filtered = chosenSubjects.length
          ? subjList.filter(s => chosenSubjects.includes(s.subject))
          : subjList;
        const subjects = [];
        const chapters = [];
        for (const sObj of filtered) {
          subjects.push({ subject: sObj.subject, readiness: 0.5 });
          for (const chObj of sObj.chapters || []) {
            const chName = typeof chObj === "string" ? chObj : chObj.chapter;
            chapters.push({
              chapter: chName,
              subject: sObj.subject,
              readiness: 0.5,
              n: 0,
              flags: 0,
              banked: Boolean(chObj && chObj.questions > 0),
            });
          }
        }
        return { level, hasSession: false, chapters, subjects };
      }
    } catch {}

    return { level, hasSession: false, chapters: [], subjects: [] };
  }

  // One sample learner drives both the landing galaxy and its sample report cards.
  const SAMPLE_LEARNER = {
    level: "JEE",
    hasSession: true,
    isDemo: true,
    subjects: [],
    chapters: [
      { chapter: "Kinematics", subject: "Physics", readiness: 0.62, n: 8, flags: 0, banked: true },
      { chapter: "Laws of Motion", subject: "Physics", readiness: 0.44, n: 6, flags: 1, banked: true },
      { chapter: "Work, Energy and Power", subject: "Physics", readiness: 0.78, n: 9, flags: 0, banked: true },
      { chapter: "Rotational Motion", subject: "Physics", readiness: 0.31, n: 5, flags: 1, banked: true },
      { chapter: "Thermodynamics", subject: "Physics", readiness: 0.74, n: 7, flags: 0, banked: true },
      { chapter: "Electrostatics", subject: "Physics", readiness: 0.5, n: 0, flags: 0, banked: true },
      { chapter: "Chemical Bonding", subject: "Chemistry", readiness: 0.76, n: 9, flags: 0, banked: true },
      { chapter: "Thermochemistry", subject: "Chemistry", readiness: 0.36, n: 5, flags: 1, banked: true },
      { chapter: "Equilibrium", subject: "Chemistry", readiness: 0.58, n: 7, flags: 0, banked: true },
      { chapter: "Electrochemistry", subject: "Chemistry", readiness: 0.81, n: 8, flags: 0, banked: true },
      { chapter: "Organic Basics", subject: "Chemistry", readiness: 0.29, n: 4, flags: 0, banked: true },
      { chapter: "Coordination Compounds", subject: "Chemistry", readiness: 0.5, n: 0, flags: 0, banked: true },
      { chapter: "Calculus", subject: "Maths", readiness: 0.82, n: 10, flags: 0, banked: true },
      { chapter: "Vectors and 3D", subject: "Maths", readiness: 0.66, n: 7, flags: 0, banked: true },
      { chapter: "Matrices & Determinants", subject: "Maths", readiness: 0.74, n: 8, flags: 0, banked: true },
      { chapter: "Complex Numbers", subject: "Maths", readiness: 0.38, n: 5, flags: 1, banked: true },
      { chapter: "Conic Sections", subject: "Maths", readiness: 0.52, n: 6, flags: 0, banked: true },
      { chapter: "Probability", subject: "Maths", readiness: 0.5, n: 0, flags: 0, banked: true },
      { chapter: "Genetics", subject: "Biology", readiness: 0.79, n: 9, flags: 0, banked: true },
      { chapter: "Cell: The Unit of Life", subject: "Biology", readiness: 0.64, n: 7, flags: 0, banked: true },
      { chapter: "Plant Physiology", subject: "Biology", readiness: 0.34, n: 4, flags: 1, banked: true },
      { chapter: "Ecology", subject: "Biology", readiness: 0.72, n: 6, flags: 0, banked: true },
      { chapter: "Biomolecules", subject: "Biology", readiness: 0.5, n: 0, flags: 0, banked: true },
    ],
  };
  // Subject readiness = mean of practised chapters, the same figure the report cards show.
  SAMPLE_LEARNER.subjects = ["Physics", "Chemistry", "Maths", "Biology"].map(subject => {
    const done = SAMPLE_LEARNER.chapters.filter(c => c.subject === subject && c.n > 0);
    return { subject, readiness: done.reduce((t, c) => t + c.readiness, 0) / done.length };
  });

  async function mountGalaxy(host) {
    if (!host || host.dataset.galaxyMounted === "1") return;
    host.dataset.galaxyMounted = "1";
    injectStyles();

    const isDemo = host.dataset.galaxy === "demo";
    const data = isDemo ? SAMPLE_LEARNER : await fetchGalaxyData();
    host.replaceChildren();
    host.classList.add(isDemo ? "galaxy-demo-host" : "galaxy-sec");

    const toggleBtn = el("button", "btn ghost small", "List view");
    toggleBtn.type = "button";

    if (!isDemo) {
      const head = el("div", "galaxy-head");
      const headCopy = el("div", "galaxy-head-copy");
      headCopy.append(el("h2", "", "Your knowledge galaxy"));
      headCopy.append(
        el(
          "p",
          "galaxy-sub",
          "Scroll through your subjects. Select a planet to explore its chapters."
        )
      );
      if (!data.hasSession) {
        headCopy.append(
          el(
            "p",
            "galaxy-unassessed-note",
            "Your progress appears here after you practise."
          )
        );
      }

      const actions = el("div", "galaxy-actions");
      if (!data.hasSession && host.dataset.journey !== "1") {
        const ctaBtn = el("button", "btn primary small", "Start practising →");
        ctaBtn.type = "button";
        ctaBtn.onclick = () => openChapterQuiz(data.level, "all");
        actions.append(ctaBtn);
      }
      actions.append(toggleBtn);
      head.append(headCopy, actions);
      host.append(head);
    }

    const can3D = isDemo
      ? webglAvailable() && Boolean(window.THREE)
      : !REDUCED_MOTION && webglAvailable() && Boolean(window.THREE);
    const stage = el("div", "galaxy-stage");
    const canvas = el("canvas", "galaxy-canvas");
    canvas.setAttribute(
      "aria-label",
      "Interactive 3D knowledge galaxy of subjects and chapters"
    );
    const labelsBox = el("div", "galaxy-labels");
    const tooltip = el("div", "galaxy-tooltip");
    tooltip.hidden = true;
    const panel = el("div", "galaxy-panel");
    panel.hidden = true;
    const resetBtn = el("button", "galaxy-reset", "← Back to galaxy");
    resetBtn.type = "button";
    resetBtn.hidden = true;

    if (isDemo) {
      const sampleTag = el("div", "galaxy-sample-tag", "Sample learner");
      stage.append(sampleTag);
    }
    stage.append(canvas, labelsBox, tooltip, panel, resetBtn);

    const legend = el("div", "galaxy-legend");
    const items = isDemo
      ? [
          ["#3DDC97", "Ready (≥70%)"],
          ["#FFB547", "Building"],
          ["#FF6B6B", "Needs focus"],
          ["#8D95B8", "Not assessed"],
        ]
      : [
          ["#3DDC97", "Ready (≥70%)"],
          ["#FFB547", "Building"],
          ["#FF6B6B", "Needs focus"],
          ["#8D95B8", "Not assessed yet"],
        ];
    for (const [col, text] of items) {
      const s = el("span");
      const d = el("i", "galaxy-dot");
      d.style.background = col;
      s.append(d, document.createTextNode(text));
      legend.append(s);
    }
    const flagSpan = el("span");
    flagSpan.append(
      el("i", "galaxy-ring-key"),
      document.createTextNode(isDemo ? "Misconception" : "Misconception spark / ring")
    );
    legend.append(flagSpan);
    if (!isDemo) {
      const sizeSpan = el("span", "", "· Click a planet or belt to condense into chapter moons");
      legend.append(sizeSpan);
    }

    function journeyReports(onFocus) {
      const reports = el("div", "journey-reports");
      data.subjects.forEach(subject => {
        const chapters = data.chapters.filter(c => c.subject === subject.subject);
        const practised = chapters.filter(c => c.n > 0);
        const section = el("section", "journey-stop");
        section.dataset.subject = subject.subject;
        section.append(el("p", "journey-label", isDemo ? "Sample report" : "Your progress"), el("h2", "", subject.subject));
        const answered = practised.reduce((n,c) => n+c.n,0);
        const ready = answered >= 3 ? Math.round(practised.reduce((n,c) => n+c.readiness,0)/practised.length*100) : null;
        section.append(el("strong", "journey-score", ready === null ? answered ? "Getting started" : "A fresh start" : ready + "%"));
        section.append(el("p", "", ready === null ? answered ? "Answer a few more questions to see your estimate." : "No practice recorded yet." : "Estimated readiness · " + practised.length + " chapters practised"));
        if (ready !== null) {
          const meter = el("div", "journey-meter"), fill = el("span");
          fill.style.width = Math.max(0, Math.min(100, ready)) + "%"; meter.append(fill); section.append(meter);
          const weak = [...practised].sort((a,b) => a.readiness-b.readiness)[0];
          section.append(el("p", "journey-next", "Next focus: " + weak.chapter));
        }
        const go = el("button", "btn", isDemo ? "Build your study space" : "Choose a chapter");
        go.type = "button";
        go.onclick = () => {
          if (isDemo) { location.href = "/login?mode=signup"; return; }
          const card = [...document.querySelectorAll(".subject-card")].find(b => b.dataset.subject === subject.subject);
          if (card) { card.click(); document.querySelector("#chapter-section").scrollIntoView({behavior: REDUCED_MOTION ? "instant" : "smooth"}); }
        };
        section.append(go); reports.append(section);
      });
      if (onFocus && "IntersectionObserver" in window) {
        const observer = new IntersectionObserver(entries => {
          const active = entries.find(e => e.isIntersecting);
          if (active) onFocus(active.target.dataset.subject);
        }, {rootMargin:"-20% 0px -35% 0px",threshold:0});
        [...reports.children].forEach(section => observer.observe(section));
      }
      return reports;
    }
    const listEl = el("div", "galaxy-list");
    renderListFallback(listEl, data);

    if (!can3D) {
      stage.hidden = true;
      toggleBtn.hidden = true;
      listEl.hidden = false;
      host.append(host.dataset.journey === "1" ? journeyReports(null) : listEl, legend);
      return;
    }

    listEl.hidden = true;
    if (isDemo) {
      stage.append(legend);
      host.append(stage, listEl);
    } else {
      host.append(stage, listEl, legend);
    }

    let controller = null;
    let inView = false;
    let showingList = false;
    let entrancePlayed = false;

    function ensureScene() {
      if (!controller) {
        const playEntrance = !entrancePlayed;
        entrancePlayed = true;
        controller = createScene(
          stage,
          canvas,
          labelsBox,
          tooltip,
          panel,
          resetBtn,
          data,
          playEntrance
        );
        host._galaxyController = controller;
      }
      return controller;
    }

    function syncRunState() {
      if (!inView || document.hidden || showingList) {
        if (controller) controller.stop();
      } else {
        ensureScene().start();
      }
    }

    let journey;
    if (host.dataset.journey === "1") {
      journey = el("div", "galaxy-journey");
      host.append(journey);
      journey.append(stage, journeyReports(subject => {
        if (!showingList && !document.hidden) ensureScene().focusSubject(subject);
      }));
    }

    toggleBtn.onclick = () => {
      showingList = !showingList;
      stage.hidden = showingList;
      if (journey) journey.hidden = showingList;
      listEl.hidden = !showingList;
      toggleBtn.textContent = showingList ? "3D galaxy view" : "List view";
      syncRunState();
    };

    if ("IntersectionObserver" in window) {
      const io = new IntersectionObserver(
        entries => {
          for (const entry of entries) {
            inView = entry.isIntersecting;
          }
          syncRunState();
        },
        { threshold: 0.08 }
      );
      io.observe(host);
    } else {
      inView = true;
      syncRunState();
    }

    document.addEventListener("visibilitychange", syncRunState);
  }

  function refitGalaxy(hostOrSelector, opts = {}) {
    const host =
      typeof hostOrSelector === "string"
        ? document.querySelector(hostOrSelector)
        : hostOrSelector || document.querySelector("[data-galaxy]");
    if (host && host._galaxyController && typeof host._galaxyController.refit === "function") {
      host._galaxyController.refit(opts);
    }
  }

  function boot() {
    document.querySelectorAll("[data-galaxy]").forEach(mountGalaxy);
  }

  window.PrepPilotGalaxy = { mount: mountGalaxy, refresh: boot, refit: refitGalaxy };

  if (document.readyState === "loading") {
    document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", boot);
  } else {
    boot();
  }
})();

================================================================================
END OF FILE: preppilot/galaxy.js
