/* The server cookie is the identity; browser values below are display caches only. */
(function () {
  const nativeFetch = window.fetch.bind(window);
  function clearActiveCache() {
    try { ['pp-user','pp-token','pp-prefs','pp-papers'].forEach(k => localStorage.removeItem(k)); } catch (e) {}
  }
  window.fetch = async function (...args) {
    const response = await nativeFetch(...args);
    const fallbackNotice = response.headers.get('X-PrepPilot-AI-Notice');
    if (fallbackNotice) {
      let notice = document.querySelector('#ai-fallback-notice');
      if (!notice) {
        notice = document.createElement('div');notice.id='ai-fallback-notice';notice.setAttribute('role','status');
        Object.assign(notice.style,{position:'fixed',bottom:'calc(90px + env(safe-area-inset-bottom))',left:'16px',right:'16px',zIndex:'90',padding:'14px',borderRadius:'12px',background:'var(--panel)',color:'var(--ink)',border:'1px solid var(--accent)'});
        document.body.append(notice);
      }
      notice.replaceChildren(document.createTextNode(fallbackNotice+' '));
      const close=document.createElement('button');close.type='button';close.className='btn';close.textContent='Dismiss';close.onclick=()=>notice.remove();notice.append(close);
    }
    const input = args[0] instanceof Request ? args[0].url : String(args[0]);
    const url = new URL(input, location.href);
    if (response.status === 401 && url.origin === location.origin && url.pathname.startsWith('/api/') &&
        !['/api/login','/api/signup'].includes(url.pathname)) {
      clearActiveCache();
      if (!['/', '/login'].includes(location.pathname)) location.replace('/login');
    }
    return response;
  };
  window.restoreAccount = async function () {
    const response = await fetch('/api/state');
    if (response.status === 401) return null;
    if (!response.ok) throw new Error('Could not restore your saved study progress. Please reload.');
    const state = await response.json();
    try {
      const previous = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('pp-user') || 'null');
      const sameUser = previous && previous.email === state.user.email;
      localStorage.setItem('pp-user', JSON.stringify(state.user));
      localStorage.removeItem('pp-token');
      // A stale session pointer must never override the authenticated server state.
      Object.keys(localStorage).filter(k => k.startsWith('pp-session:')).forEach(k => localStorage.removeItem(k));
      const lastKey = 'pp-last-session:' + state.user.email;
      if (state.last_session_id) localStorage.setItem(lastKey, state.last_session_id);
      else localStorage.removeItem(lastKey);
      if (state.preferences) {
        localStorage.setItem('pp-prefs', JSON.stringify(state.preferences));
        localStorage.setItem('pp-prefs:' + state.user.email, JSON.stringify(state.preferences));
        if (!sameUser || !localStorage.getItem('pp-level')) localStorage.setItem('pp-level', state.preferences.level);
      } else {
        localStorage.removeItem('pp-prefs');
        localStorage.setItem('pp-level', state.user.level || 'JEE');
      }
      localStorage.setItem('pp-papers', JSON.stringify(state.papers));
    } catch (e) { /* Data stays saved on the server even if browser storage is blocked. */ }
    return state;
  };
  window.finishSignIn = async function () {
    const state = await window.restoreAccount();
    if (!state || !state.user) throw new Error('Please sign in again to continue.');
    // Saved server preferences mean onboarding is complete, across browsers and providers.
    location.replace(state.preferences ? '/study' : '/setup');
  };
  window.savePreferences = async function (preferences) {
    const response = await fetch('/api/preferences', {method:'PUT',headers:{'Content-Type':'application/json'},body:JSON.stringify(preferences)});
    const data = await response.json();
    if (!response.ok) throw new Error(data.detail || 'Could not save preferences.');
    return data;
  };

  // Shared paper picker keeps navigation in place until the learner chooses a paper.
  let paperDialog;
  function node(tag, className, text) {
    const element = document.createElement(tag);
    if (className) element.className = className;
    if (text !== undefined) element.textContent = text;
    return element;
  }
  function createPaperDialog() {
    const style = node('style');
    style.textContent = `
      #my-papers-dialog { width:min(580px,calc(100vw - 32px));max-height:calc(100dvh - 48px);padding:0;
        border:1px solid var(--line,#252E55);border-radius:16px;background:var(--panel,#11172E);color:var(--ink,#ECEEF8);
        box-shadow:0 24px 80px #0006;font:inherit; }
      #my-papers-dialog::backdrop { background:#05071288;backdrop-filter:blur(4px); }
      #my-papers-dialog .paper-picker-head { padding:24px 24px 16px;display:flex;align-items:flex-start;gap:16px; }
      #my-papers-dialog h2 { font-size:24px;margin:0 0 6px; }
      #my-papers-dialog p { margin:0;font-size:14px;color:var(--muted,#8D95B8); }
      #my-papers-dialog .paper-picker-close { margin-left:auto;flex-shrink:0;border:1px solid var(--line,#252E55);border-radius:50%;
        background:var(--panel,#11172E);color:inherit;width:34px;height:34px;cursor:pointer;font-size:21px; }
      #my-papers-dialog .paper-picker-list { list-style:none;margin:0;padding:0 24px 20px;display:grid;gap:10px;
        max-height:min(50dvh,420px);overflow-y:auto; }
      #my-papers-dialog .paper-picker-row { width:100%;display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:space-between;gap:16px;
        border:1px solid var(--line,#252E55);background:var(--raised,#18203D);color:inherit;border-radius:12px;padding:16px;text-align:left;cursor:pointer; }
      #my-papers-dialog .paper-picker-row:hover { border-color:var(--accent,#FFB547);box-shadow:inset 0 1px 0 var(--accent,#FFB547); }
      #my-papers-dialog .paper-picker-row strong { display:block;font-size:15px;overflow-wrap:anywhere; }
      #my-papers-dialog .paper-picker-row small { display:block;margin-top:4px;color:var(--muted,#8D95B8);font-size:12px;font-family:var(--font-mono,monospace); }
      #my-papers-dialog .paper-picker-start { font-size:13px;flex-shrink:0;font-weight:600;color:var(--accent,#FFB547); }
      #my-papers-dialog .paper-picker-footer { padding:18px 24px;border-top:1px solid var(--line,#252E55); }
      #my-papers-dialog .paper-picker-add { display:block;text-align:center;text-decoration:none;border-radius:12px;padding:12px;
        font-weight:600;background:var(--accent,#FFB547);color:var(--accent-ink,#1A1200); }
      #my-papers-dialog .paper-picker-message { padding:22px 0;line-height:1.6; }
      #my-papers-dialog button:focus-visible,#my-papers-dialog a:focus-visible { outline:2px solid var(--nebula,#8FA8FF);outline-offset:3px; }
      @media(max-width:480px) { #my-papers-dialog .paper-picker-head{padding:20px 18px 14px;}
        #my-papers-dialog .paper-picker-list{padding:0 18px 18px;}#my-papers-dialog .paper-picker-footer{padding:16px 18px;}
        #my-papers-dialog .paper-picker-row{padding:14px;gap:10px;} }
    `;
    document.head.append(style);
    const dialog = node('dialog'); dialog.id = 'my-papers-dialog';
    dialog.setAttribute('aria-labelledby', 'paper-picker-title');
    dialog.setAttribute('aria-describedby', 'paper-picker-description');
    const header = node('div', 'paper-picker-head');
    const title = node('div');
    const heading = node('h2', '', 'My papers'); heading.id = 'paper-picker-title';
    const description = node('p', '', 'Choose a previously uploaded paper to begin your quiz.'); description.id = 'paper-picker-description';
    title.append(heading, description);
    const close = node('button', 'paper-picker-close', '×'); close.type = 'button';close.setAttribute('aria-label', 'Close my papers');
    close.onclick = () => dialog.close();header.append(title, close);
    const list = node('ul', 'paper-picker-list');list.setAttribute('aria-label', 'Previously uploaded papers');
    const footer = node('div', 'paper-picker-footer');
    const add = node('a', 'paper-picker-add', '+ Add new paper'); add.href = '/papers?add=1';footer.append(add);
    dialog.append(header, list, footer);
    dialog.addEventListener('click', event => {
      const bounds = dialog.getBoundingClientRect();
      if (event.target === dialog && (event.clientX < bounds.left || event.clientX > bounds.right || event.clientY < bounds.top || event.clientY > bounds.bottom)) dialog.close();
    });
    document.body.append(dialog);
    return dialog;
  }
  async function showPapers() {
    paperDialog = paperDialog || createPaperDialog();
    const dialog = paperDialog, list = dialog.querySelector('.paper-picker-list');
    list.replaceChildren(node('li', 'paper-picker-message', 'Loading your papers…'));
    list.setAttribute('aria-busy', 'true');
    if (!dialog.open) dialog.showModal();
    try {
      const response = await fetch('/api/papers');
      if (response.status === 401) return;
      if (!response.ok) throw new Error('Could not load your papers. Please try again.');
      const papers = await response.json();
      list.replaceChildren();
      if (!papers.length) list.append(node('li', 'paper-picker-message', 'No papers uploaded yet. Add your first paper below.'));
      for (const paper of papers) {
        const item = node('li');
        const button = node('button', 'paper-picker-row');button.type = 'button';
        const text = node('span');text.append(node('strong', '', paper.name), node('small', '', paper.questions + (paper.questions === 1 ? ' question' : ' questions')));
        button.append(text, node('span', 'paper-picker-start', 'Start quiz →'));
        button.onclick = () => {
          try {localStorage.setItem('pp-level', paper.level);} catch (error) {}
          location.href = '/app?new=1';
        };
        item.append(button);list.append(item);
      }
    } catch (error) {
      const item = node('li', 'paper-picker-message');item.append(node('p', '', error.message));
      const retry = node('button', 'btn', 'Try again');retry.type = 'button';retry.onclick = showPapers;item.append(retry);list.replaceChildren(item);
    } finally { list.setAttribute('aria-busy', 'false'); }
  }
  document.addEventListener('click', event => {
    const link = event.target.closest('a[data-paper-picker]');
    if (!link || event.button !== 0 || event.ctrlKey || event.metaKey || event.shiftKey || event.altKey) return;
    event.preventDefault();showPapers();
  });
})();

/* Account menu: any [data-account-menu] button opens it (avatar on the home hub and other pages). */
(function () {
  let menu, opener;
  const LINKS = [
    ['Study space', '/study'], ['AI tutor', '/tutor'], ['Mistake review', '/review'],
    ['Bookmarked questions', '/bookmarks'], ['Progress', '/progress'], ['My question papers', '/papers', true],
    ['Check a question with AI', '/check'], ['Preferences', '/setup'],
  ];
  function node(tag, className, text) {
    const element = document.createElement(tag);
    if (className) element.className = className;
    if (text !== undefined) element.textContent = text;
    return element;
  }
  function build() {
    const style = node('style');
    style.textContent = `
      .acct-menu { position:fixed;z-index:60;min-width:250px;max-width:calc(100vw - 24px);padding:8px;border-radius:14px;
        background:var(--panel,#11172E);color:var(--ink,#ECEEF8);border:1px solid var(--line,#252E55);
        box-shadow:0 18px 48px #0006;font:inherit;font-size:14px;overflow-y:auto; }
      .acct-menu[hidden] { display:none; }
      .acct-head { padding:10px 12px 12px;border-bottom:1px solid var(--line,#252E55);margin-bottom:6px; }
      .acct-head strong { display:block;font-size:15px; }
      .acct-head span { display:block;color:var(--muted,#8D95B8);font-size:12px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;margin-top:2px; }
      .acct-menu a, .acct-menu button { display:block;width:100%;text-align:left;padding:9px 12px;border-radius:9px;border:0;
        background:none;color:inherit;font:inherit;text-decoration:none;cursor:pointer; }
      .acct-menu a:hover, .acct-menu button:hover, .acct-menu a:focus-visible, .acct-menu button:focus-visible {
        background:var(--sel,#18203D);outline:none; }
      .acct-menu hr { border:0;border-top:1px solid var(--line,#252E55);margin:6px 0; }
      .acct-menu .acct-out { color:var(--bad,#FF6B6B); }
      [data-account-menu] { cursor:pointer;border:0;font:inherit; }`;
    document.head.append(style);
    menu = node('div', 'acct-menu'); menu.id = 'acct-menu'; menu.setAttribute('role', 'menu'); menu.hidden = true;
    document.body.append(menu);
    menu.addEventListener('keydown', event => {
      const items = [...menu.querySelectorAll('[role=menuitem]')], at = items.indexOf(document.activeElement);
      if (event.key === 'Escape') { close(); opener && opener.focus(); }
      else if (event.key === 'ArrowDown' || event.key === 'ArrowUp') {
        event.preventDefault();
        items[(at + (event.key === 'ArrowDown' ? 1 : items.length - 1)) % items.length].focus();
      } else if (event.key === 'Tab') close();
    });
  }
  function fill() {
    let user = null;
    try { user = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('pp-user') || 'null'); } catch (e) {}
    menu.replaceChildren();
    const head = node('div', 'acct-head');
    head.append(node('strong', '', user ? user.name : 'PrepPilot'),
      node('span', '', !user ? '' : user.guest ? 'Guest · progress saved in this browser' : user.email));
    menu.append(head);
    for (const [label, href, picker] of LINKS) {
      const link = node('a', '', label); link.href = href; link.setAttribute('role', 'menuitem');
      if (picker) { link.dataset.paperPicker = ''; link.setAttribute('aria-haspopup', 'dialog'); }
      link.addEventListener('click', close);
      menu.append(link);
    }
    menu.append(node('hr'));
    if (user && user.guest) {
      const join = node('a', '', 'Create an account to keep progress'); join.href = '/?mode=signup'; join.setAttribute('role', 'menuitem');
      menu.append(join);
    }
    const out = node('button', 'acct-out', user && user.guest ? 'Leave guest session' : 'Sign out');
    out.type = 'button'; out.setAttribute('role', 'menuitem');
    out.onclick = async () => {
      out.disabled = true; out.textContent = 'Signing out…';
      try {
        const response = await fetch('/api/logout', { method: 'POST' });
        if (!response.ok) throw new Error();
        try { ['pp-user', 'pp-token', 'pp-prefs', 'pp-papers'].forEach(k => localStorage.removeItem(k)); } catch (e) {}
        location.href = '/';
      } catch (e) { out.disabled = false; out.textContent = 'Could not sign out. Try again'; }
    };
    menu.append(out);
  }
  function open(button) {
    menu || build();
    fill(); opener = button; menu.hidden = false;
    const r = button.getBoundingClientRect(), width = Math.max(250, menu.offsetWidth);
    menu.style.top = `${r.bottom + 8}px`;
    menu.style.maxHeight = `${Math.max(100, innerHeight - r.bottom - 20)}px`;
    menu.style.left = `${Math.max(12, Math.min(innerWidth - width - 12, r.right - width))}px`;
    button.setAttribute('aria-expanded', 'true');
    menu.querySelector('[role=menuitem]').focus();
  }
  function close() {
    if (!menu || menu.hidden) return;
    menu.hidden = true;
    if (opener) opener.setAttribute('aria-expanded', 'false');
  }
  document.addEventListener('click', event => {
    const button = event.target.closest('[data-account-menu]');
    if (button) { event.preventDefault(); menu && !menu.hidden ? close() : open(button); return; }
    if (menu && !menu.hidden && !menu.contains(event.target)) close();
  });
  addEventListener('resize', close);
  addEventListener('scroll', close, { passive: true });
})();

/* Shared navigation for study pages; the quiz retains its focused header. */
(async function () {
  const pages = ['/study', '/tutor', '/review', '/bookmarks', '/progress', '/papers', '/setup', '/check'];
  if (!pages.includes(location.pathname) || document.querySelector('.shared-sidebar')) return;
  let state;
  try { state = await restoreAccount(); } catch (error) { return; }
  if (!state?.user) return;

  const links = [
    ['Study space', '/study'], ['AI tutor', '/tutor'], ['Mistake review', '/review'],
    ['Bookmarked questions', '/bookmarks'], ['Progress', '/progress'],
    ['My question papers', '/papers'], ['Preferences', '/setup'], ['Check a question', '/check'],
  ];
  const node = (tag, className, text) => {
    const element = document.createElement(tag);
    if (className) element.className = className;
    if (text !== undefined) element.textContent = text;
    return element;
  };
  function link(label, href) {
    const element = node('a', 'shared-link', label);
    element.href = href;
    if (href === location.pathname) element.setAttribute('aria-current', 'page');
    if (href === '/papers') { element.dataset.paperPicker = ''; element.setAttribute('aria-haspopup', 'dialog'); }
    return element;
  }
  const style = node('style');
  style.textContent = `
    body.shared-navigation {padding-left:220px;}
    .shared-navigation .shared-header,.shared-navigation > .topbar {position:sticky;top:0;z-index:30;min-height:72px;margin-left:-220px;}

    .shared-navigation .shared-old-nav {display:none!important;}

    .shared-more::backdrop {background:#05081799;}
    .shared-more h2 {font:600 24px var(--font-heading);margin:0 0 12px;}
    .shared-more .shared-close {display:block;margin:12px 0 0 auto;}
    @media(max-width:760px) {
      body.shared-navigation {padding-left:0;padding-bottom:calc(82px + env(safe-area-inset-bottom));}
      .shared-navigation .shared-header,.shared-navigation > .topbar {margin-left:0;min-height:64px;}
      .shared-header {padding:12px 16px;gap:10px;}
      .shared-account > span {display:none;}
      .shared-sidebar {display:none;}
      .shared-tabs {display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(6,minmax(0,1fr));position:fixed;bottom:0;left:0;right:0;z-index:45;padding:6px 4px calc(6px + env(safe-area-inset-bottom));border-top:1px solid var(--line);background:var(--panel);}
      .shared-tabs .shared-link,.shared-tabs button {min-width:0;margin:0;padding:14px 2px;text-align:center;font:500 11px/1.4 var(--font-sans);border:0;border-radius:8px;background:none;color:var(--muted);text-decoration:none;}
      .shared-tabs .shared-link[aria-current="page"],.shared-tabs button[aria-current="page"] {color:var(--accent-text);background:var(--sel);box-shadow:none;}
      .shared-navigation main.wrap {width:calc(100% - 32px);}
      .shared-navigation .bar {left:0;bottom:calc(58px + env(safe-area-inset-bottom));}
      .shared-navigation.shared-setup {padding-bottom:calc(170px + env(safe-area-inset-bottom));}
    }
  `;
  document.head.append(style);

  // Keep hub IDs and controls intact; other pages share one small header.
  const hubHeader = document.querySelector('body > header.topbar');
  if (!hubHeader) {
    const header = node('header', 'shared-header');
    const brand = node('a', 'brand', 'Prep'); brand.href = '/study'; brand.append(node('span', '', 'Pilot'));
    const account = node('div', 'shared-account');
    account.append(node('span', '', state.user.guest ? 'Guest' : state.user.name));
    const avatar = node('button', 'shared-avatar', (state.user.name || 'P').slice(0, 1).toUpperCase());
    avatar.type = 'button'; avatar.dataset.accountMenu = '';
    avatar.setAttribute('aria-label', 'Account menu'); avatar.setAttribute('aria-haspopup', 'menu'); avatar.setAttribute('aria-expanded', 'false');
    account.append(avatar); header.append(brand, account); document.body.prepend(header);
  }
  const sidebar = node('nav', 'shared-sidebar');sidebar.setAttribute('aria-label', 'Study navigation');
  links.forEach(([label, href]) => sidebar.append(link(label, href)));
  const tabs = node('nav', 'shared-tabs');tabs.setAttribute('aria-label', 'Mobile study navigation');
  [['Home', '/study'], ['Practise', '/app'], ['Review', '/review'], ['Tutor', '/tutor'], ['Progress', '/progress']]
    .forEach(([label, href]) => tabs.append(link(label, href)));
  const more = node('button', '', 'More');more.type = 'button';more.setAttribute('aria-haspopup', 'dialog');
  if (['/bookmarks', '/papers', '/setup', '/check'].includes(location.pathname)) more.setAttribute('aria-current', 'page');
  let dialog;
  more.onclick = () => {
    if (!dialog) {
      dialog = node('dialog', 'shared-more');
      const title = node('h2', '', 'More');title.id = 'shared-more-title';dialog.setAttribute('aria-labelledby', title.id);dialog.append(title);
      links.forEach(([label, href]) => {
        const item = link(label, href);item.addEventListener('click', () => dialog.close());dialog.append(item);
      });
      const close = node('button', 'btn shared-close', 'Close');close.type = 'button';close.onclick = () => dialog.close();dialog.append(close);
      document.body.append(dialog);
    }
    dialog.showModal();
  };
  tabs.append(more);document.body.append(sidebar, tabs);
  document.querySelectorAll('body > nav.nav, .layout > aside[aria-label="Study navigation"]').forEach(old => old.classList.add('shared-old-nav'));
  document.body.classList.add('shared-navigation');
  if (location.pathname === '/setup') document.body.classList.add('shared-setup');
})();

/* Installable app: registers the service worker and wires any [data-install] button. */
(function () {
  if ("serviceWorker" in navigator) {
    addEventListener("load", () => navigator.serviceWorker.register("/sw.js").catch(() => {}));
  }
  let prompt = null;
  const standalone = () => matchMedia("(display-mode: standalone)").matches || navigator.standalone === true;
  const buttons = () => document.querySelectorAll("[data-install]");
  const show = visible => buttons().forEach(b => { b.hidden = !visible; });
  addEventListener("beforeinstallprompt", event => {  // Chrome, Edge and Android offer the native install sheet
    event.preventDefault(); prompt = event; show(!standalone());
  });
  addEventListener("appinstalled", () => { prompt = null; show(false); });
  document.addEventListener("click", async event => {
    const button = event.target.closest("[data-install]");
    if (!button) return;
    if (prompt) {
      prompt.prompt();
      await prompt.userChoice.catch(() => null);
      prompt = null; show(false);
    } else {  // Safari has no install prompt: explain the manual step instead
      alert("To install PrepPilot, open your browser's Share menu and choose \"Add to Home Screen\".");
    }
  });
  // iPhone and iPad Safari never fire beforeinstallprompt, so offer the manual route there.
  const iOS = /iphone|ipad|ipod/i.test(navigator.userAgent) || (navigator.platform === "MacIntel" && navigator.maxTouchPoints > 1);
  document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", () => show(iOS && !standalone()));

  // Shared pointer-driven 3D tilt (up to ~6 deg) and soft light glare for cards.
  const TILT_CARDS = ".subject-card, .next-card, .paper-card, .feature, .step, .mock, [data-tilt]";
  const allowTilt = () => matchMedia("(hover: hover) and (prefers-reduced-motion: no-preference)").matches;
  let frame = 0, lastEvent = null, currentCard = null;
  function clearTilt(card) {
    if (!card) return;
    ["--rx", "--ry", "--gx", "--gy"].forEach(prop => card.style.removeProperty(prop));
  }
  document.addEventListener("pointermove", event => {
    if (event.pointerType === "touch" || !allowTilt()) return;
    lastEvent = event;
    if (frame) return;
    frame = requestAnimationFrame(() => {
      frame = 0;
      const e = lastEvent;
      if (!e) return;
      const card = e.target && e.target.closest ? e.target.closest(TILT_CARDS) : null;
      if (currentCard && currentCard !== card) clearTilt(currentCard);
      currentCard = card;
      if (!card || card.disabled) return;
      const rect = card.getBoundingClientRect();
      if (!rect.width || !rect.height) return;
      const px = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, (e.clientX - rect.left) / rect.width));
      const py = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, (e.clientY - rect.top) / rect.height));
      const limit = 4;
      card.style.setProperty("--rx", `${((0.5 - py) * 2 * limit).toFixed(2)}deg`);
      card.style.setProperty("--ry", `${((px - 0.5) * 2 * limit).toFixed(2)}deg`);
      card.style.setProperty("--gx", `${(px * 100).toFixed(1)}%`);
      card.style.setProperty("--gy", `${(py * 100).toFixed(1)}%`);
    });
  }, { passive: true });
  document.addEventListener("pointerleave", () => { if (currentCard) { clearTilt(currentCard); currentCard = null; } }, { passive: true });
  window.addEventListener("blur", () => { if (currentCard) { clearTilt(currentCard); currentCard = null; } });
})();
// Reusable loading placeholders retain status roles and error text.
addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',()=>{
  const loading=[...document.querySelectorAll('[role="status"],.status,.empty')].filter(e=>/^Loading/.test(e.textContent.trim()));
  loading.forEach(element=>{
    element.classList.add('loading-skeleton');element.setAttribute('aria-label',element.textContent);
    const observer=new MutationObserver(()=>{
      if(!/^Loading/.test(element.textContent.trim())||element.hidden){
        element.classList.remove('loading-skeleton');element.removeAttribute('aria-label');observer.disconnect();
        if(!matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)').matches)element.animate([{opacity:0},{opacity:1}],{duration:200});
      }
    });observer.observe(element,{childList:true,subtree:true,characterData:true,attributes:true,attributeFilter:['hidden']});
  });
});
// Motion stays optional and never delays saving learner work.
window.ppMotion = (element, frames, options = {}) => {
  if (!element || matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)').matches) return null;
  return element.animate(frames, {duration:200, easing:'ease-out', ...options});
};
window.ppCount = (element, value) => {
  if (!element) return;
  if (matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)').matches) { element.textContent=Math.round(value)+'%'; return; }
  const start=performance.now();
  function frame(now) {const t=Math.min(1,(now-start)/300);element.textContent=Math.round(value*t)+'%';if(t<1)requestAnimationFrame(frame);}
  requestAnimationFrame(frame);
};
window.ppRevealBars = root => {
  if (matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)').matches || !('IntersectionObserver' in window)) return;
  const observer=new IntersectionObserver(entries=>entries.forEach(entry=>{
    if (!entry.isIntersecting) return;
    entry.target.style.opacity='1';
    ppMotion(entry.target,[{transform:'scaleX(0)'},{transform:'scaleX(1)'}],{duration:300});
    observer.unobserve(entry.target);
  }));
  root.querySelectorAll('.fill').forEach(fill=>{fill.style.transformOrigin='left';fill.style.opacity='0';observer.observe(fill);});
};
addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',()=>{
  if(location.pathname!='/study'||matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)').matches)return;
  const seen=new WeakSet();
  const reveal=()=>document.querySelectorAll('.subject-card,.paper-card').forEach((card,index)=>{
    if(seen.has(card))return;seen.add(card);
    ppMotion(card,[{opacity:0,transform:'translateY(6px)'},{opacity:1,transform:'translateY(0)'}],{delay:Math.min(index,5)*40});
  });
  new MutationObserver(reveal).observe(document.querySelector('main')||document.body,{childList:true,subtree:true});reveal();
});

/* PrepPilot service worker: makes the site installable and lets it open offline.
   Pages: network first, last good copy when offline, else the offline page.
   Static files and CDN libraries: served from cache, refreshed in the background.
   /api/ is never cached: learning data must always be live and owned by the signed-in learner. */
const CACHE = "preppilot-v17";
const PRECACHE = ["/offline.html", "/manifest.webmanifest", "/site.css", "/account.js", "/nav.js", "/extract.js", "/pwa.js", "/galaxy.js", "/icons/icon-192.png",
                  "/buddy/pip-idle.png?v=2", "/buddy/pip-happy.png?v=2", "/buddy/pip-sad.png?v=2"];

self.addEventListener("install", event => {
  event.waitUntil(caches.open(CACHE).then(cache => cache.addAll(PRECACHE)).then(() => self.skipWaiting()));
});

self.addEventListener("activate", event => {
  event.waitUntil(caches.keys()
    .then(keys => Promise.all(keys.filter(key => key !== CACHE).map(key => caches.delete(key))))
    .then(() => self.clients.claim()));
});

const STATIC_HOSTS = ["cdnjs.cloudflare.com", "fonts.googleapis.com", "fonts.gstatic.com"];

self.addEventListener("fetch", event => {
  const request = event.request, url = new URL(request.url);
  if (request.method !== "GET") return;
  const sameOrigin = url.origin === self.location.origin;
  if (sameOrigin && url.pathname.startsWith("/api/")) return;

  if (request.mode === "navigate") {
    event.respondWith(fetch(request)
      .then(response => {
        if (response.ok) { const copy = response.clone(); caches.open(CACHE).then(cache => cache.put(request, copy)); }
        return response;
      })
      .catch(() => caches.match(request).then(cached => cached || caches.match("/offline.html"))));
    return;
  }

  if (sameOrigin || STATIC_HOSTS.includes(url.hostname)) {
    event.respondWith(caches.open(CACHE).then(cache => cache.match(request).then(cached => {
      const fresh = fetch(request).then(response => {
        if (response.ok || response.type === "opaque") cache.put(request, response.clone());
        return response;
      }).catch(() => cached);
      return cached || fresh;
    })));
  }
});

FILE: preppilot/google-signin.js

/* "Continue with Google": renders Google's button into any [data-google-signin] slot when the server has a client ID.
   Google's script loads from accounts.google.com (required by Google Identity Services), only on pages with a slot. */
(async function () {
  const slots = [...document.querySelectorAll('[data-google-signin]')];
  if (!slots.length) return;
  let config;
  try { config = await (await fetch('/api/auth/config')).json(); } catch (e) { return; }
  if (!config.google_client_id) return;  // not configured: the slot stays hidden
  const loaded = await new Promise(resolve => {
    const script = document.createElement('script');
    script.src = 'https://accounts.google.com/gsi/client'; script.async = true;
    script.onload = () => resolve(true); script.onerror = () => resolve(false);
    document.head.append(script);
  });
  if (!loaded || !window.google?.accounts?.id) return;
  const note = slot => slot.nextElementSibling?.matches('[data-google-error]') ? slot.nextElementSibling
    : slot.insertAdjacentElement('afterend', Object.assign(document.createElement('p'), { role: 'alert' }));
  google.accounts.id.initialize({
    client_id: config.google_client_id,
    callback: async ({ credential }) => {
      slots.forEach(slot => { const n = note(slot); n.dataset.googleError = ''; n.textContent = 'Signing you in…'; });
      try {
        const response = await fetch('/api/auth/google', { method: 'POST', headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify({ credential }) });
        const data = await response.json().catch(() => ({}));
        if (!response.ok) throw new Error(data.detail || 'Google sign-in failed. Please try again.');
        await finishSignIn();
      } catch (error) {
        slots.forEach(slot => { note(slot).textContent = error.message; });
      }
    },
  });
  const dark = matchMedia('(prefers-color-scheme: dark)').matches;
  for (const slot of slots) {
    slot.hidden = false;
    google.accounts.id.renderButton(slot, { theme: dark ? 'filled_black' : 'outline', size: 'large', shape: 'pill',
      text: 'continue_with', logo_alignment: 'left', width: Math.min(Math.max(slot.clientWidth, 240), 400) });
  }
})();

/* Read PDF, Word, PowerPoint and text files in the browser (shared by the papers page and the AI tutor).
   Libraries load from cdnjs only when a file of that type is picked. */
const CDN = "https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/";
const loaded = {};
function loadScript(src) {
  return loaded[src] ||= new Promise((ok, fail) => {
    const sc = document.createElement("script"); sc.src = src; sc.onload = ok;
    sc.onerror = () => fail(new Error("Couldn't load the file reader. Check your internet connection."));
    document.head.append(sc);
  });
}
async function pdfText(file) {
  await loadScript(CDN + "pdf.js/3.11.174/pdf.min.js");
  pdfjsLib.GlobalWorkerOptions.workerSrc = CDN + "pdf.js/3.11.174/pdf.worker.min.js";
  const pdf = await pdfjsLib.getDocument({ data: await file.arrayBuffer() }).promise;
  let out = "";
  for (let i = 1; i <= pdf.numPages; i++) {
    const page = await (await pdf.getPage(i)).getTextContent();
    // PDF.js can split a visually spaced row into separate items (e.g. A) ... B) ...).
    out += page.items.map(it => it.str + (it.hasEOL ? "\n" : " ")).join("") + "\n";
  }
  return out;
}
async function docxText(file) {
  await loadScript(CDN + "mammoth/1.6.0/mammoth.browser.min.js");
  return (await mammoth.extractRawText({ arrayBuffer: await file.arrayBuffer() })).value;
}
async function pptxText(file) {  // each slide's text, paragraph by paragraph, in slide order
  await loadScript(CDN + "jszip/3.10.1/jszip.min.js");
  const zip = await JSZip.loadAsync(await file.arrayBuffer());
  const slides = Object.keys(zip.files).filter(n => /^ppt\/slides\/slide\d+\.xml$/.test(n))
    .sort((a, b) => parseInt(a.match(/\d+/)[0]) - parseInt(b.match(/\d+/)[0]));
  let out = "";
  for (const name of slides) {
    const xml = new DOMParser().parseFromString(await zip.file(name).async("string"), "application/xml");
    for (const para of xml.getElementsByTagNameNS("*", "p")) {
      const line = [...para.getElementsByTagNameNS("*", "t")].map(t => t.textContent).join("");
      if (line.trim()) out += line + "\n";
    }
    out += "\n";
  }
  return out;
}
async function fileText(file) {  // any supported file -> tidy plain text, or a clear error
  const kind = (file.name.split(".").pop() || "").toLowerCase();
  if (["ppt", "doc"].includes(kind)) throw new Error(`Old .${kind} files can't be read. Open it and use "Save as" .${kind}x, then upload that.`);
  const text = kind === "pdf" ? await pdfText(file) : kind === "docx" ? await docxText(file) : kind === "pptx" ? await pptxText(file) : await file.text();
  if (!text.trim()) throw new Error(kind === "pdf"
    ? "This PDF has no selectable text. It's probably a scan (photos of pages). Copy the text from a text PDF or type it in."
    : "That file is empty.");
  return text.replace(/[ \t]+\n/g, "\n").replace(/\n{3,}/g, "\n\n").trim();
}

"""Focused regression checks. Run: python -B test_regressions.py"""
import copy
import os
from pathlib import Path
import tempfile
import unittest
from unittest.mock import patch
from concurrent.futures import ThreadPoolExecutor
import sys

local_dependencies = Path(__file__).parent / '.runtime'
if not local_dependencies.exists():
    local_dependencies = Path(__file__).parent / '.test-deps'
if local_dependencies.exists():
    sys.path.insert(0, str(local_dependencies))
os.environ.pop('GROQ_API_KEY', None)
os.environ.pop('GEMINI_API_KEY', None)  # deterministic offline checks
from fastapi.testclient import TestClient
import main
import engine
from storage import Database
from fastapi.responses import Response


class RegressionTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        self.original_db = main.DB
        self.test_directory = tempfile.TemporaryDirectory()
        main.DB = Database(Path(self.test_directory.name) / "test.sqlite3")
        main.SESSIONS.clear()
        self.client = TestClient(main.app)
        self.bank = copy.deepcopy(main.BANK)
        self.client.post("/api/guest")

    def tearDown(self):
        main.BANK.clear()
        main.BANK.update(self.bank)
        self.client.close()
        main.SESSIONS.clear()
        main.DB = self.original_db
        self.test_directory.cleanup()

    def post(self, path, payload):
        response = self.client.post('/api/' + path, json=payload)
        self.assertEqual(response.status_code, 200, response.text)
        return response.json()

    def session(self):
        return self.post('session', {'level': 'JEE'})['session_id']

    def answered(self, sid):
        q = self.post('next', {'session_id': sid})['question']
        state = main.SESSIONS[sid]
        state['asked_at'] -= 30
        result = self.post('answer', {'session_id': sid, 'choice': state['current']['answer'], 'confidence': 'sure'})
        return q, result

    def test_report_old_answer_preserves_shared_bank_and_replays_model(self):
        sid = self.session()
        q1, a1 = self.answered(sid)
        q2, a2 = self.answered(sid)
        retained = copy.deepcopy(main.SESSIONS[sid]['learner'].history[1])
        counts = {ch: len(pool) for ch, pool in main.BANK.items()}
        self.post('report', {'session_id': sid, 'answer_id': a1['answer_id']})
        s = main.SESSIONS[sid]
        self.assertEqual(counts, {ch: len(pool) for ch, pool in main.BANK.items()})
        self.assertEqual([h['answer_id'] for h in s['learner'].history], [a2['answer_id']])
        expected = engine.Learner(s['chapters'])
        expected.subj = dict(s['initial_subj'])
        expected.update(retained['chapter'], retained['b'], retained['correct'], retained['secs'], retained['confidence'], retained['hints'])
        self.assertEqual(s['learner'].theta, expected.theta)
        self.assertEqual(s['learner'].subj, expected.subj)
        self.assertEqual(len(s['pred_log']), 1)
        self.assertNotIn(q1['id'], [q['id'] for q in main.items(s, q1['chapter'])])
        original_cookies = dict(self.client.cookies)
        self.client.post("/api/guest")
        other = main.SESSIONS[self.session()]
        self.assertIn(q1['id'], [q['id'] for q in main.items(other, q1['chapter'])])
        self.client.cookies = original_cookies
        self.post('report', {'session_id': sid, 'answer_id': a1['answer_id']})
        self.assertEqual(len(s['learner'].history), 1)

    def test_report_first_answer_restores_unseen_prior(self):
        sid = self.session()
        q, a = self.answered(sid)
        self.post('report', {'session_id': sid, 'answer_id': a['answer_id']})
        learner = main.SESSIONS[sid]['learner']
        self.assertEqual(learner.theta, {})
        self.assertEqual(learner.history, [])
        self.assertEqual(main.SESSIONS[sid]['pred_log'], [])
        self.assertEqual(main.BANK[q['chapter']], self.bank[q['chapter']])

    def test_resume_preserves_progress_question_and_break(self):
        sid = self.session()
        self.answered(sid)
        q = self.post('next', {'session_id': sid})['question']
        resumed = self.post('session', {'level': 'JEE', 'resume_id': sid})
        self.assertEqual(resumed['session_id'], sid)
        self.assertEqual(resumed['active']['question']['id'], q['id'])
        self.assertEqual(len(main.SESSIONS[sid]['learner'].history), 1)
        self.assertEqual(self.client.post('/api/break', json={'session_id': sid}).status_code, 423)
        self.assertGreater(self.post('session', {'level': 'JEE', 'resume_id': sid})['clock']['break_left'], 0)
        changed = self.post('session', {'level': 'NEET', 'resume_id': sid})
        self.assertNotEqual(changed['session_id'], sid)

    def test_new_session_ignores_stale_browser_clock(self):
        import time
        result = self.post('session', {'level': 'JEE', 'block_start': time.time() - 86400,
                                      'break_until': time.time() + 600, 'block_minutes': 40})
        self.assertEqual(result['clock']['break_left'], 0)
        self.assertGreater(result['clock']['block_left'], 2395)
        self.post('next', {'session_id': result['session_id']})

    def test_selected_focus_duration_and_server_break(self):
        result = self.post('session', {'level': 'JEE', 'block_minutes': 25})
        sid = result['session_id']
        self.assertEqual(result['clock']['block_secs'], 1500)
        self.assertEqual(result['clock']['break_left'], 0)
        self.post('next', {'session_id': sid})
        self.post('answer', {'session_id': sid, 'choice': 0})
        main.SESSIONS[sid]['block_start'] -= 1501
        response = self.client.post('/api/next', json={'session_id': sid})
        self.assertEqual(response.status_code, 423)
        self.assertGreater(response.json()['detail']['break_left'], 370)

    def test_no_break_before_any_practice_and_time_away_counts_as_rest(self):
        sid = self.post('session', {'level': 'JEE', 'block_minutes': 40})['session_id']
        main.SESSIONS[sid]['block_start'] -= 3000  # session opened long ago, nothing answered yet
        self.post('next', {'session_id': sid})  # must serve a question, not a break
        self.assertLess(main.time.time() - main.SESSIONS[sid]['block_start'], 5)
        self.post('answer', {'session_id': sid, 'choice': 0})
        main.SESSIONS[sid]['block_start'] -= 1200
        main.SESSIONS[sid]['last_practice_at'] -= 900  # 15 minutes away: longer than a 10-minute short break
        resumed = self.post('session', {'level': 'JEE', 'block_minutes': 40, 'resume_id': sid})
        self.assertEqual(resumed['session_id'], sid)
        self.assertGreater(resumed['clock']['block_left'], 2390)

    def test_catalog_and_selected_chapter_quiz(self):
        catalog = self.client.get('/api/catalog?level=JEE')
        self.assertEqual(catalog.status_code, 200)
        chapter = next(ch['chapter'] for subject in catalog.json()['subjects']
                       for ch in subject['chapters'] if ch['questions'])
        sid = self.session()
        self.post('focus', {'session_id': sid, 'focus': chapter})
        self.assertEqual(self.post('next', {'session_id': sid})['question']['chapter'], chapter)
        self.assertEqual(self.client.get('/api/catalog?level=invalid').status_code, 400)
        self.assertEqual(self.client.get('/study').status_code, 200)

    def test_switching_chapter_keeps_learner_and_break(self):
        # /study "Start quiz" resumes the same session and only changes its focus.
        sid = self.session()
        self.answered(sid)
        resumed = self.post('session', {'level': 'JEE', 'resume_id': sid})
        self.assertEqual(resumed['session_id'], sid)
        self.post('focus', {'session_id': sid, 'focus': 'Laws of Motion'})
        self.assertEqual(len(main.SESSIONS[sid]['learner'].history), 1)
        self.assertEqual(self.post('next', {'session_id': sid})['question']['chapter'], 'Laws of Motion')

    def test_changed_preferences_cannot_skip_a_running_break(self):
        sid = self.session()
        self.assertEqual(self.client.post('/api/break', json={'session_id': sid}).status_code, 423)
        changed = self.post('session', {'level': 'NEET', 'resume_id': sid})
        self.assertNotEqual(changed['session_id'], sid)
        self.assertGreater(changed['clock']['break_left'], 0)
        self.assertEqual(self.client.post('/api/next', json={'session_id': changed['session_id']}).status_code, 423)

    def test_jee_main_level(self):
        from syllabus import JEE_MAIN_REMOVED, subjects_for
        main_chapters = {c for chs in subjects_for('JEEMAIN').values() for c in chs}
        adv_chapters = {c for chs in subjects_for('JEE').values() for c in chs}
        self.assertEqual(main_chapters, adv_chapters - JEE_MAIN_REMOVED)
        self.assertEqual(engine.MARKING['JEEMAIN'], (4, 1))
        self.assertTrue(engine.EXAM_TARGET_B['NEET'] < engine.EXAM_TARGET_B['JEEMAIN'] < engine.EXAM_TARGET_B['JEE'])
        result = self.post('session', {'level': 'JEEMAIN'})
        self.assertEqual(result['exam'], 'JEE Main')
        self.assertEqual(result['marking'], [4, 1])
        self.assertEqual(self.client.get('/api/catalog?level=JEEMAIN').status_code, 200)
        q = self.post('next', {'session_id': result['session_id']})['question']
        self.assertIn(q['chapter'], main_chapters)

    def test_uploaded_question_paper(self):
        import papers
        csv_text = papers.TEMPLATE + '"Speed of light?",3e8 m/s,3e6 m/s,300 m/s,3 m/s,1,Optics,Physics,hard,,\n'
        r = self.client.post('/api/papers/import', json={'name': 'Mock 1', 'csv_text': csv_text})
        self.assertEqual(r.status_code, 200, r.text)
        d = r.json()
        try:
            self.assertEqual(d['questions'], 3)
            session = self.post('session', {'level': d['level']})
            self.assertEqual(session['exam'], '📄 Mock 1')
            self.assertFalse(session['ai'])  # uploaded papers never mix in AI questions
            sid, seen = session['session_id'], set()
            for _ in range(3):
                q = self.post('next', {'session_id': sid})['question']
                self.assertTrue(q['uploaded'])
                seen.add(q['id'])
                self.post('answer', {'session_id': sid, 'choice': main.SESSIONS[sid]['current']['answer']})
            self.assertEqual(len(seen), 3)
            self.assertEqual(self.client.get(f"/api/papers/{d['paper_id']}").json()['questions'], 3)
        finally:
            papers.PAPERS.pop(d['paper_id'], None)
            (papers.DIR / f"{d['paper_id']}.json").unlink(missing_ok=True)

    def test_pasted_question_paper_without_name(self):
        import papers
        text = "1. Unit of force?\nA) Joule\nB) Newton\nC) Watt\nD) Pascal\nAnswer: B\n\n2. 2 + 2 = ? (1) 3 (2) 4 (3) 5 (4) 6\nAns: (2)\n"
        r = self.client.post('/api/papers/import', json={'content': text})
        self.assertEqual(r.status_code, 200, r.text)
        d = r.json()
        try:
            self.assertEqual((d['questions'], d['error_count']), (2, 0))
            self.assertTrue(d['name'].startswith('My paper'))
        finally:
            papers.PAPERS.pop(d['paper_id'], None)
            (papers.DIR / f"{d['paper_id']}.json").unlink(missing_ok=True)
        bad_link = self.client.post('/api/papers/import', json={'content': 'https://example.com/sheet'})
        self.assertEqual(bad_link.status_code, 400)

    def test_paper_import_rejects_bad_input(self):
        bad = [
            {'name': 'X', 'sheet_url': 'https://evil.example.com/spreadsheets/d/abcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyz'},
            {'name': 'X', 'csv_text': 'question,answer\nQ,A\n'},          # missing option columns
            {'name': '', 'csv_text': 'question\n'},                        # no name
            {'name': 'X'},                                                 # neither link nor file
        ]
        for body in bad:
            self.assertEqual(self.client.post('/api/papers/import', json=body).status_code, 400, body)
        self.assertEqual(self.client.post('/api/session', json={'level': 'paper:doesnotexist'}).status_code, 400)

    def test_placement_check_spreads_subjects_then_stops(self):
        sid = self.post('session', {'level': 'JEE', 'placement': True})['session_id']
        subjects = []
        for i in range(5):
            why = self.post('next', {'session_id': sid})['why']
            self.assertEqual(why['placement'], 5 - i)
            subjects.append(why['subject'])
            result = self.post('answer', {'session_id': sid, 'choice': main.SESSIONS[sid]['current']['answer']})
        self.assertEqual(len(set(subjects[:3])), 3)  # one question per subject before repeating
        self.assertEqual(result['placement_left'], 0)
        self.assertEqual({x['subject'] for x in result['placement_done']}, {'Physics', 'Chemistry', 'Maths'})
        self.assertEqual(self.post('next', {'session_id': sid})['why']['placement'], 0)

    def test_placement_request_does_not_resume_normal_quiz(self):
        normal = self.session()
        checked = self.post('session', {'level': 'JEE', 'resume_id': normal, 'placement': True})
        sid = checked['session_id']
        self.assertNotEqual(sid, normal)
        self.assertEqual(main.SESSIONS[sid]['placement'], 5)
        resumed = self.post('session', {'level': 'JEE', 'resume_id': sid, 'placement': True})
        self.assertEqual(resumed['session_id'], sid)
        self.assertEqual(self.post('next', {'session_id': sid})['why']['placement'], 5)

    def test_report_preserves_placement_prior_and_restores_missing_answer(self):
        sid = self.post('session', {'level': 'JEE', 'placement': True})['session_id']
        self.answered(sid)
        _, reported = self.answered(sid)
        s = main.SESSIONS[sid]
        retained = copy.deepcopy(s['learner'].history[0])
        expected = engine.Learner(s['chapters'])
        expected.subj = dict(s['initial_subj'])
        h = expected.update(retained['chapter'], retained['b'], retained['correct'],
                            retained['secs'], retained['confidence'], retained['hints'])
        expected.subj[h['subject']] += 0.7 * h['delta']
        self.post('report', {'session_id': sid, 'answer_id': reported['answer_id']})
        self.assertEqual(s['learner'].theta, expected.theta)
        self.assertEqual(s['learner'].subj, expected.subj)
        self.assertEqual(s['placement'], 4)
        self.assertEqual(len(s['pred_log']), 1)
        self.post('report', {'session_id': sid, 'answer_id': reported['answer_id']})
        self.assertEqual(s['placement'], 4)
        for _ in range(4):
            _, result = self.answered(sid)
        self.assertIsNotNone(result['placement_done'])
        resumed = self.post('session', {'level': 'JEE', 'resume_id': sid})
        self.assertEqual(resumed['session_id'], sid)
        self.assertEqual(s['placement'], 0)
        # Reporting a placement answer after the check also reopens the missing slot.
        self.post('report', {'session_id': sid, 'answer_id': retained['answer_id']})
        self.assertEqual(s['placement'], 1)

    def test_hub_brief_and_session_summary(self):
        fresh = self.client.get('/api/brief', params={'level': 'NEET'}).json()
        self.assertIsNone(fresh['continue'])
        self.assertTrue(main.BANK.get(fresh['recommended']['chapter']))  # ties prefer chapters with real questions
        sid = self.session()
        self.assertEqual(self.client.get('/api/summary', params={'session_id': sid}).json()['answered'], 0)
        self.answered(sid)
        brief = self.client.get('/api/brief', params={'level': 'JEE', 'session_id': sid}).json()
        self.assertEqual(brief['continue']['answered'], 1)
        summary = self.client.get('/api/summary', params={'session_id': sid}).json()
        self.assertEqual((summary['answered'], summary['correct']), (1, 1))
        self.assertEqual(len(summary['chapters']), 1)
        self.assertIsNotNone(summary['recommended'])
        for path in ('/progress', '/study', '/papers'):
            self.assertEqual(self.client.get(path).status_code, 200)

    def test_report_requires_answer_in_same_session(self):
        sid = self.session()
        _, answer = self.answered(sid)
        # A separate guest cannot report an answer owned by the first guest.
        self.client.post("/api/guest")
        other = self.session()
        result = self.client.post('/api/report', json={'session_id': other, 'answer_id': answer['answer_id']})
        self.assertEqual(result.status_code, 404)
        self.assertEqual(len(main.SESSIONS[sid]['learner'].history), 1)

    def test_parallel_signups_keep_all_accounts(self):
        original = main.USERS_FILE
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(dir=Path(__file__).parent) as temporary:
            try:
                main.USERS_FILE = Path(temporary) / 'users.json'
                def create(i):
                    return main.signup(main.Signup(name='Student', email=f'student{i}@example.com', password='password123'), Response())
                with ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=8) as workers:
                    list(workers.map(create, range(12)))
                self.assertEqual(len(main._users()), 12)
                profile = main.login(main.Login(email='student0@example.com', password='password123'), Response())
                self.assertEqual(profile['user']['email'], 'student0@example.com')
            finally:
                main.USERS_FILE = original
                pass

    def test_chapter_without_questions_explains_next_step(self):
        with patch.multiple(main, GEMINI_KEY=None, GROQ_KEY=None):
            sid = self.post('session', {'level': 'JEE', 'ai_provider': 'none'})['session_id']
            self.post('focus', {'session_id': sid, 'focus': 'Mechanical Properties of Fluids'})
            response = self.client.post('/api/next', json={'session_id': sid})
        self.assertEqual(response.status_code, 400)
        self.assertIn('no verified questions', response.json()['detail'])
        self.assertNotIn('questions.json', response.json()['detail'])

    def test_installable_app_files(self):
        for path in ('/manifest.webmanifest', '/sw.js', '/pwa.js', '/offline.html', '/icons/icon-512.png', '/favicon.ico'):
            self.assertEqual(self.client.get(path).status_code, 200, path)
        self.assertTrue(self.client.get('/sw.js').headers['content-type'].startswith('application/javascript'))
        self.assertEqual(self.client.get('/manifest.webmanifest').json()['start_url'], '/study')
        for path in ('/icons/..%2Fmain.py', '/icons/icon.svg'):
            self.assertEqual(self.client.get(path).status_code, 404, path)


if __name__ == '__main__':
    unittest.main()

"""Persistence, migration and ownership regression checks (temporary SQLite databases)."""
import copy
import hashlib
import json
from pathlib import Path
import sys
import tempfile
import time
import unittest

for directory in ('.runtime', '.test-deps'):
    location = Path(__file__).parent / directory
    if location.exists():
        sys.path.insert(0, str(location))
        break
from fastapi.testclient import TestClient
import main
from storage import Database


class PersistenceTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        self.directory = tempfile.TemporaryDirectory()
        self.path = Path(self.directory.name) / 'state.sqlite3'
        self.original_db = main.DB
        main.DB = Database(self.path)
        main.SESSIONS.clear()
        self.bank = copy.deepcopy(main.BANK)
        self.client = TestClient(main.app)
        self.client.post('/api/guest')

    def tearDown(self):
        self.client.close()
        main.DB = self.original_db
        main.SESSIONS.clear()
        main.BANK.clear()
        main.BANK.update(self.bank)
        self.directory.cleanup()

    def post(self, path, body):
        response = self.client.post('/api/' + path, json=body)
        self.assertEqual(response.status_code, 200, response.text)
        return response.json()

    def account(self, email='first@example.com', client=None):
        target = client or self.client
        response = target.post('/api/signup', json={'name':'Student', 'email':email, 'password':'test-password', 'level':'JEE'})
        self.assertEqual(response.status_code, 200, response.text)
        self.assertNotIn('token', response.json())
        self.assertIn('HttpOnly', response.headers['set-cookie'])

    def session(self):
        return self.post('session', {'level':'JEE', 'ai_provider':'none'})['session_id']

    def answered(self, sid):
        self.post('next', {'session_id':sid})
        s = main.SESSIONS[sid]
        s['asked_at'] -= 30
        return self.post('answer', {'session_id':sid, 'choice':s['current']['answer'], 'confidence':'sure'})

    def restart(self):
        main.SESSIONS.clear()
        main.DB = Database(self.path)

    def test_progress_active_question_and_hint_survive_reopen(self):
        self.account()
        sid = self.session()
        self.answered(sid)
        q = self.post('next', {'session_id':sid})['question']
        self.post('hint', {'session_id':sid})
        before = self.client.get('/api/dashboard', params={'session_id':sid}).json()
        self.restart()
        after = self.client.get('/api/dashboard', params={'session_id':sid}).json()
        self.assertEqual(before, after)
        resumed = self.post('session', {'level':'JEE', 'ai_provider':'none', 'resume_id':sid})
        self.assertEqual(resumed['session_id'], sid)
        self.assertEqual(resumed['active']['question']['id'], q['id'])
        self.assertEqual(main.SESSIONS[sid]['hints'], 1)
        result = self.post('answer', {'session_id':sid, 'choice':main.SESSIONS[sid]['current']['answer']})
        self.post('report', {'session_id':sid, 'answer_id':result['answer_id']})
        self.restart()
        self.assertEqual(self.client.get('/api/dashboard', params={'session_id':sid}).json()['stats']['answered'], 1)

    def test_preferences_papers_and_progress_restore_on_another_login(self):
        self.account()
        prefs = {'level':'JEE','subjects':['Maths'],'start_ability':0,'block_minutes':25,'ai_provider':'none','explain_style':'short'}
        saved = self.client.put('/api/preferences', json=prefs)
        self.assertEqual(saved.status_code, 200, saved.text)
        sid = self.session()
        self.answered(sid)
        paper = self.post('papers/import', {'content':'1. 2 + 3?\nA) 4 B) 5 C) 6 D) 7\nAnswer: B'})
        self.restart()
        with TestClient(main.app) as other_device:
            self.assertEqual(other_device.get('/api/state').status_code, 401)
            self.assertEqual(other_device.post('/api/login', json={'email':'first@example.com','password':'test-password'}).status_code, 200)
            state = other_device.get('/api/state').json()
            self.assertEqual(state['preferences']['block_minutes'], 25)
            self.assertEqual(state['last_session_id'], sid)
            self.assertEqual(state['papers'][0]['paper_id'], paper['paper_id'])
            resumed = other_device.post('/api/session', json={'level':'JEE','ai_provider':'none','resume_id':sid})
            self.assertEqual(resumed.json()['session_id'], sid)

    def test_guest_and_accounts_cannot_access_other_learners(self):
        self.account()
        sid = self.session()
        paper = self.post('papers/import', {'content':'1. 2 + 3?\nA) 4 B) 5 C) 6 D) 7\nAnswer: B'})
        with TestClient(main.app) as other:
            self.account('second@example.com', other)
            for route in ('dashboard','summary'):
                self.assertEqual(other.get('/api/'+route, params={'session_id':sid}).status_code, 404)
            for route, body in [('next',{}),('hint',{}),('skip',{}),('break',{}),('answer',{'choice':0}),
                                ('report',{'answer_id':'anything'}),('focus',{'focus':'all'}),('provider',{'ai_provider':'none'}),('ask',{'text':'Help'})]:
                self.assertEqual(other.post('/api/'+route,json={'session_id':sid,**body}).status_code, 404, route)
            self.assertEqual(other.post('/api/session',json={'level':'JEE','resume_id':sid}).status_code, 404)
            self.assertEqual(other.get('/api/brief',params={'level':'JEE','session_id':sid}).status_code, 404)
            self.assertEqual(other.get('/api/papers/'+paper['paper_id']).status_code, 404)
            self.assertEqual(other.delete('/api/papers/'+paper['paper_id']).status_code, 404)
            self.assertEqual(other.post('/api/session',json={'level':paper['level']}).status_code, 400)
            self.assertEqual(other.get('/api/papers').json(), [])
            self.assertIsNone(other.get('/api/state').json()['last_session_id'])

    def test_unauthenticated_requests_are_rejected(self):
        with TestClient(main.app) as anonymous:
            for route in ('state','me','papers','dashboard','summary'):
                self.assertEqual(anonymous.get('/api/'+route).status_code, 401)
            self.assertEqual(anonymous.post('/api/session',json={}).status_code, 401)
            self.assertEqual(anonymous.post('/api/papers/import',json={'content':'x'}).status_code, 401)
            self.assertEqual(anonymous.get('/api/catalog').status_code, 200)

    def test_break_and_placement_report_survive_reopen(self):
        sid = self.post('session', {'level':'JEE','placement':True,'ai_provider':'none'})['session_id']
        result = self.answered(sid)
        self.restart()
        self.post('report', {'session_id':sid,'answer_id':result['answer_id']})
        self.restart()
        self.client.get('/api/dashboard', params={'session_id':sid})
        self.assertEqual(main.SESSIONS[sid]['placement'], 5)
        self.assertEqual(self.client.post('/api/break',json={'session_id':sid}).status_code, 423)
        until = main.SESSIONS[sid]['break_until']
        self.restart()
        response = self.client.post('/api/next',json={'session_id':sid})
        self.assertEqual(response.status_code, 423)
        self.assertEqual(main.SESSIONS[sid]['break_until'], until)

    def test_logout_expiry_and_stored_token_hash(self):
        self.account()
        raw = self.client.cookies.get(main.AUTH_COOKIE)
        self.assertIsNotNone(main.DB.owner(raw))
        with main.DB.connection() as con:
            digests = [r['digest'] for r in con.execute('SELECT digest FROM auth_tokens')]
        self.assertNotIn(raw, digests)
        self.assertIn(hashlib.sha256(raw.encode()).hexdigest(), digests)
        self.assertEqual(self.client.post('/api/logout').status_code, 200)
        self.restart()
        with TestClient(main.app, cookies={main.AUTH_COOKIE:raw}) as revoked:
            self.assertEqual(revoked.get('/api/state').status_code, 401)
        expired = main.DB.issue_token('account:first@example.com', ttl=-1)
        with TestClient(main.app, cookies={main.AUTH_COOKIE:expired}) as old:
            self.assertEqual(old.get('/api/state').status_code, 401)

    def test_legacy_account_migration_is_idempotent_and_preserves_source(self):
        salt = '11' * 16
        user = {'name':'Existing','email':'existing@example.com','level':'NEET','salt':salt,'hash':main._hash('old-password',salt)}
        legacy = Path(self.directory.name)/'users.json'
        legacy.write_text(json.dumps({user['email']:user}),encoding='utf-8')
        original = legacy.read_bytes()
        migrated = Database(Path(self.directory.name)/'migration.sqlite3', legacy_users=legacy)
        migrated.import_legacy_users(legacy)
        self.assertEqual(migrated.users(), {user['email']:user})
        self.assertEqual(legacy.read_bytes(), original)
        main.DB = migrated
        self.assertEqual(self.client.post('/api/login',json={'email':user['email'],'password':'old-password'}).status_code, 200)
        self.assertEqual(self.client.post('/api/login',json={'email':user['email'],'password':'wrong-password'}).status_code, 401)

    def test_invalid_legacy_import_rolls_back_without_changing_source(self):
        legacy = Path(self.directory.name)/'broken-users.json'
        good = {'name':'Good','email':'good@example.com','level':'JEE','salt':'11'*16,'hash':'unchanged'}
        legacy.write_text(json.dumps({'good@example.com':good,'bad@example.com':{'name':'Incomplete'}}),encoding='utf-8')
        original = legacy.read_bytes()
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
            main.DB.import_legacy_users(legacy)
        self.assertIsNone(main.DB.user('good@example.com'))
        self.assertEqual(legacy.read_bytes(), original)

    def test_paper_archive_preserves_existing_practice(self):
        paper = self.post('papers/import', {'content':'1. 2 + 3?\nA) 4 B) 5 C) 6 D) 7\nAnswer: B'})
        sid = self.post('session', {'level':paper['level'],'ai_provider':'none'})['session_id']
        self.assertEqual(self.client.delete('/api/papers/'+paper['paper_id']).status_code, 200)
        self.assertEqual(self.client.get('/api/papers').json(), [])
        self.restart()
        self.assertEqual(self.client.get('/api/dashboard',params={'session_id':sid}).status_code, 200)
        self.post('next', {'session_id':sid})

    def test_returning_after_long_absence_keeps_learning_and_starts_fresh_focus(self):
        sid = self.session()
        self.answered(sid)
        s = main.SESSIONS[sid]
        s['last_practice_at'] = time.time() - 86400
        s['block_start'] = time.time() - 86400
        main.save_session(sid, s)
        self.restart()
        resumed = self.post('session', {'level':'JEE','ai_provider':'none','resume_id':sid})
        self.assertEqual(resumed['session_id'], sid)
        self.assertEqual(resumed['clock']['break_left'], 0)
        self.assertGreater(resumed['clock']['block_left'], 2395)
        self.assertEqual(len(main.SESSIONS[sid]['learner'].history), 1)

    def test_cross_origin_mutations_are_rejected(self):
        self.assertEqual(self.client.post('/api/session',json={},headers={'Origin':'https://other.example'}).status_code, 403)


if __name__ == '__main__':
    unittest.main()

"""Google sign-in and AI question checks (Google and the AI are faked; runs offline)."""
import copy
from pathlib import Path
import sys
import tempfile
import time
import unittest
from unittest.mock import patch

for directory in ('.runtime', '.test-deps'):
    location = Path(__file__).parent / directory
    if location.exists():
        sys.path.insert(0, str(location))
        break
from fastapi.testclient import TestClient
import main
from storage import Database

CLIENT = 'test-client.apps.googleusercontent.com'


def claims(**over):
    base = {'aud': CLIENT, 'iss': 'https://accounts.google.com', 'email': 'Asha@Example.com', 'email_verified': 'true',
            'exp': str(int(time.time()) + 600), 'sub': '1234', 'name': 'Asha'}
    return {**base, **over}


class Base(unittest.TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        self.temp = tempfile.TemporaryDirectory()
        self.original_db = main.DB
        main.DB = Database(Path(self.temp.name) / 'extras.sqlite3')
        self.keys = patch.multiple(main, GEMINI_KEY=None, GROQ_KEY='test-groq', GOOGLE_CLIENT_ID=CLIENT)
        self.keys.start()
        main.SESSIONS.clear()
        self.bank = copy.deepcopy(main.BANK)
        self.client = TestClient(main.app)

    def tearDown(self):
        self.client.close()
        main.DB = self.original_db
        main.SESSIONS.clear()
        main.BANK.clear(); main.BANK.update(self.bank)
        self.keys.stop()
        self.temp.cleanup()


class GoogleSignInTests(Base):
    def google(self, info, client=None):
        with patch.object(main, 'google_token_info', return_value=info):
            return (client or self.client).post('/api/auth/google', json={'credential': 'token'})

    def test_new_google_user_is_created_and_signed_in(self):
        self.assertEqual(self.client.get('/api/auth/config').json(), {'google_client_id': CLIENT})
        response = self.google(claims())
        self.assertEqual(response.status_code, 200, response.text)
        self.assertEqual(response.json()['user']['email'], 'asha@example.com')
        self.assertEqual(self.client.get('/api/me').json()['name'], 'Asha')

    def test_rejects_wrong_audience_unverified_or_expired(self):
        for bad in (claims(aud='someone-else'), claims(email_verified='false'), claims(exp='1'), claims(iss='evil.example')):
            self.assertEqual(self.google(bad).status_code, 401)
        with patch.object(main, 'google_token_info', side_effect=ValueError('bad token')):
            self.assertEqual(self.client.post('/api/auth/google', json={'credential': 'x'}).status_code, 401)

    def test_links_existing_password_account_and_blocks_other_google_account(self):
        self.client.post('/api/signup', json={'name': 'Asha', 'email': 'asha@example.com', 'password': 'password123', 'level': 'NEET'})
        with TestClient(main.app) as other:
            response = self.google(claims(), other)
            self.assertEqual(response.json()['user']['level'], 'NEET')
        with TestClient(main.app) as third:  # a Google-created account remembers which Google account made it
            self.google(claims(email='g@example.com', sub='A'), third)
            self.assertEqual(self.google(claims(email='g@example.com', sub='B'), third).status_code, 401)

    def test_password_login_refuses_google_only_account(self):
        self.google(claims())
        with TestClient(main.app) as other:
            self.assertEqual(other.post('/api/login', json={'email': 'asha@example.com', 'password': 'whatever1'}).status_code, 401)

    def test_google_returning_user_restores_first_sign_in_preferences(self):
        self.assertEqual(self.google(claims()).status_code, 200)
        self.assertIsNone(self.client.get('/api/state').json()['preferences'])
        prefs = {'level':'NEET', 'subjects':['Biology'], 'start_ability':0.0, 'block_minutes':25, 'ai_provider':'none'}
        saved = self.client.put('/api/preferences', json=prefs)
        self.assertEqual(saved.status_code, 200, saved.text)
        self.client.post('/api/logout')
        main.SESSIONS.clear()
        main.DB = Database(Path(self.temp.name) / 'extras.sqlite3')
        with TestClient(main.app) as returning:
            self.assertEqual(self.google(claims(), returning).status_code, 200)
            self.assertEqual(returning.get('/api/state').json()['preferences'], saved.json())

    def test_disabled_without_client_id(self):
        with patch.object(main, 'GOOGLE_CLIENT_ID', ''):
            self.assertEqual(self.client.get('/api/auth/config').json(), {'google_client_id': None})
            self.assertEqual(self.client.post('/api/auth/google', json={'credential': 'x'}).status_code, 404)

    def test_malformed_google_claims_are_rejected_without_signing_in(self):
        for bad in (None, [], claims(exp='invalid'), claims(exp='nan'), claims(exp='inf'),
                    claims(email=None), claims(email=''), claims(sub=None), claims(sub=''),
                    {k: v for k, v in claims().items() if k != 'email'}):
            with self.subTest(claims=bad):
                self.assertEqual(self.google(bad).status_code, 401)
                self.assertEqual(self.client.get('/api/me').status_code, 401)


class QuestionCheckTests(Base):
    def setUp(self):
        super().setUp()
        self.client.post('/api/guest')
        # practise with AI off (no background generation or network), then let the check use Groq
        self.sid = self.client.post('/api/session', json={'level': 'JEE', 'ai_provider': 'none'}).json()['session_id']
        self.client.post('/api/next', json={'session_id': self.sid})
        self.q = main.SESSIONS[self.sid]['current']
        self.client.post('/api/answer', json={'session_id': self.sid, 'choice': (self.q['answer'] + 1) % 4})
        main.SESSIONS[self.sid]['ai_provider'] = 'groq'

    def check(self, answers, reason=''):
        replies = iter([{'working': 'w', 'answer': a} for a in answers])
        with patch.object(main, 'ai_call', side_effect=lambda *a, **k: next(replies)):
            return self.client.post('/api/check', json={'session_id': self.sid, 'question_id': self.q['id'], 'reason': reason})

    def test_recent_lists_answered_question_with_key(self):
        d = self.client.get('/api/check/recent').json()
        self.assertEqual(d['session_id'], self.sid)
        self.assertEqual(d['questions'][0]['question_id'], self.q['id'])
        self.assertEqual(d['questions'][0]['you_were'], 'wrong')

    def test_ai_agrees_with_key(self):
        d = self.check([self.q['answer']]).json()
        self.assertEqual((d['verdict'], d['hidden']), ('key_correct', False))

    def test_two_matching_disagreements_hide_question_and_replay(self):
        other = (self.q['answer'] + 2) % 4
        d = self.check([other, other], 'I got a different value').json()
        self.assertEqual((d['verdict'], d['hidden']), ('key_wrong', True))
        s = main.SESSIONS[self.sid]
        self.assertIn(self.q['id'], s['reported'])
        self.assertEqual(len(s['learner'].history), 0)
        self.assertEqual(self.client.get('/api/check/recent').json()['questions'], [])

    def test_inconsistent_checks_are_unclear(self):
        a, b = (self.q['answer'] + 1) % 4, (self.q['answer'] + 2) % 4
        d = self.check([a, b]).json()
        self.assertEqual((d['verdict'], d['hidden']), ('unclear', False))

    def test_two_no_correct_option_results_hide_and_log_flawed_question(self):
        response = self.check([-1, -1])
        self.assertEqual(response.status_code, 200, response.text)
        self.assertEqual((response.json()['verdict'], response.json()['hidden']), ('flawed', True))
        main.SESSIONS.clear()  # the report and evidence replay must survive a restart
        with main.DB.connection() as con:
            complaint = con.execute('SELECT payload FROM complaints').fetchone()
        self.assertIn('"verdict": "flawed"', complaint['payload'])
        self.assertEqual(self.client.get('/api/check/recent').json()['questions'], [])
        owner = main.DB.owner(self.client.cookies.get(main.AUTH_COOKIE))
        saved = main.DB.get_session(self.sid, owner)
        self.assertIn(self.q['id'], saved['reported'])
        self.assertEqual(saved['learner']['history'], [])

    def test_malformed_ai_answer_does_not_hide_question(self):
        for answer in (True, 1.5, 4, -2, None, 'oops', '--1'):
            with self.subTest(answer=answer):
                self.assertEqual(self.check([answer]).status_code, 503)
                self.assertNotIn(self.q['id'], main.SESSIONS[self.sid]['reported'])

    def test_wrong_key_removes_all_attempts_at_repeated_question(self):
        paper = self.client.post('/api/papers/import', json={
            'name': 'Repeated key', 'content': '1. Unit of force?\nA) Joule B) Newton C) Watt D) Pascal\nAnswer: B'
        }).json()
        self.sid = self.client.post('/api/session', json={'level': paper['level'], 'ai_provider': 'none'}).json()['session_id']
        for _ in range(2):
            self.q = self.client.post('/api/next', json={'session_id': self.sid}).json()['question']
            self.client.post('/api/answer', json={'session_id': self.sid, 'choice': 0})
        s = main.SESSIONS[self.sid]
        self.assertEqual(len(s['learner'].history), 2)
        s['ai_provider'] = 'groq'
        response = self.check([2, 2])
        self.assertEqual(response.status_code, 200, response.text)
        self.assertTrue(response.json()['hidden'])
        self.assertEqual(s['learner'].history, [])
        self.assertEqual(len(s['reports']), 2)

    def test_needs_ai_and_owned_question(self):
        with patch.object(main, 'GROQ_KEY', None):
            self.assertEqual(self.check([0]).status_code, 400)
        response = self.client.post('/api/check', json={'session_id': self.sid, 'question_id': 'nope'})
        self.assertEqual(response.status_code, 404)
        with TestClient(main.app) as other:
            other.post('/api/guest')
            self.assertEqual(other.post('/api/check', json={'session_id': self.sid, 'question_id': self.q['id']}).status_code, 404)


if __name__ == '__main__':
    unittest.main()
