"""Offline provider regression checks: python -B test_ai_providers.py."""
import copy
import io
import json
from pathlib import Path
import sys
import unittest
import tempfile
from unittest.mock import patch
from urllib.error import HTTPError

for directory in ('.runtime', '.test-deps'):
    dependency = Path(__file__).parent / directory
    if dependency.exists():
        sys.path.insert(0, str(dependency))
        break
from fastapi.testclient import TestClient
import main
from storage import Database


class ProviderTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        self.original_db = main.DB
        self.test_directory = tempfile.TemporaryDirectory()
        main.DB = Database(Path(self.test_directory.name) / "test.sqlite3")
        self.config = patch.multiple(main, GEMINI_KEY='test-gemini-secret', GROQ_KEY='test-groq-secret')
        self.config.start()
        self.prefetch = patch.object(main, 'prefetch')
        self.prefetch.start()
        self.client = TestClient(main.app)
        self.client.post("/api/guest")
        self.bank = copy.deepcopy(main.BANK)
        self.generated = copy.deepcopy(main.GENERATED)
        main.SESSIONS.clear()
        main.GROQ_COOLDOWN[0] = main.COOLDOWN[0] = 0

    def tearDown(self):
        self.client.close()
        main.DB = self.original_db
        self.test_directory.cleanup()
        self.prefetch.stop()
        self.config.stop()
        main.BANK.clear()
        main.BANK.update(self.bank)
        main.GENERATED.clear()
        main.GENERATED.update(self.generated)
        main.SESSIONS.clear()
        main.GROQ_COOLDOWN[0] = main.COOLDOWN[0] = 0

    def post(self, route, data):
        result = self.client.post('/api/' + route, json=data)
        self.assertEqual(result.status_code, 200, result.text)
        return result.json()

    def test_provider_options_never_expose_keys(self):
        response = self.client.get('/api/providers')
        self.assertEqual(response.json()['default'], 'groq')
        self.assertEqual({p['id'] for p in response.json()['providers']}, {'gemini', 'groq', 'none'})
        self.assertNotIn('test-gemini-secret', response.text)
        self.assertNotIn('test-groq-secret', response.text)
        with patch.object(main, 'GROQ_KEY', None):
            self.assertEqual(main.default_provider(), 'gemini')
        with patch.multiple(main, GEMINI_KEY=None, GROQ_KEY=None):
            self.assertEqual(main.default_provider(), 'none')
            self.assertFalse(self.client.get('/api/catalog?level=JEE').json()['ai'])

    def test_provider_switch_preserves_progress_question_and_break(self):
        sid = self.post('session', {'level': 'JEE', 'ai_provider': 'none'})['session_id']
        self.post('next', {'session_id': sid})
        s = main.SESSIONS[sid]
        self.post('answer', {'session_id': sid, 'choice': s['current']['answer']})
        self.post('next', {'session_id': sid})
        learner, question, clock = s['learner'], s['current'], main.clock(s)
        self.post('provider', {'session_id': sid, 'ai_provider': 'groq'})
        self.assertIs(s['learner'], learner)
        self.assertIs(s['current'], question)
        self.assertEqual(len(learner.history), 1)
        self.assertEqual(s['block_start'], clock['block_start'])
        resumed = self.post('session', {'level': 'JEE', 'resume_id': sid, 'ai_provider': 'groq'})
        self.assertEqual(resumed['session_id'], sid)
        self.assertEqual(self.client.post('/api/break', json={'session_id': sid}).status_code, 423)
        until = s['break_until']
        self.post('provider', {'session_id': sid, 'ai_provider': 'gemini'})
        self.assertEqual(s['break_until'], until)
        self.assertEqual(self.client.post('/api/next', json={'session_id': sid}).status_code, 423)

    def test_unknown_or_unconfigured_providers_rejected(self):
        sid = self.post('session', {'level': 'JEE', 'ai_provider': 'none'})['session_id']
        for provider in ('unknown', 'GROQ_API_KEY'):
            self.assertEqual(self.client.post('/api/provider', json={'session_id': sid, 'ai_provider': provider}).status_code, 400)
        with patch.object(main, 'GROQ_KEY', None):
            self.assertEqual(self.client.post('/api/session', json={'ai_provider': 'groq'}).status_code, 400)
            self.assertEqual(self.client.get('/api/catalog?ai_provider=groq').status_code, 400)
        self.assertEqual(main.SESSIONS[sid]['ai_provider'], 'none')

    def test_groq_json_request_and_text_reply(self):
        payload = {'choices': [{'message': {'content': '{"answer": 2}'}}]}
        with patch.object(main.urllib.request, 'urlopen', return_value=io.BytesIO(json.dumps(payload).encode())) as request:
            self.assertEqual(main.ai_call('Return JSON', True, provider='groq'), {'answer': 2})
            req = request.call_args.args[0]
            body = json.loads(req.data)
            self.assertEqual(req.full_url, 'https://api.groq.com/openai/v1/chat/completions')
            self.assertEqual(req.get_header('Authorization'), 'Bearer test-groq-secret')
            self.assertEqual(body['response_format'], {'type': 'json_object'})
            self.assertIn('JSON', body['messages'][0]['content'])
            self.assertEqual(body['model'], main.GROQ_MODEL)
        payload['choices'][0]['message']['content'] = '  Helpful reply.  '
        with patch.object(main.urllib.request, 'urlopen', return_value=io.BytesIO(json.dumps(payload).encode())):
            self.assertEqual(main.groq('Help'), 'Helpful reply.')

    def test_groq_failures_fall_back_to_gemini(self):
        with patch.object(main.urllib.request, 'urlopen', side_effect=HTTPError('https://api.groq.com', 429, 'limited', {}, None)) as request, patch.object(main, 'gemini') as gemini:
            self.assertIsNotNone(main.ai_call('Help', provider='groq'))
            self.assertGreater(main.GROQ_COOLDOWN[0], 0)
            self.assertEqual(main.COOLDOWN[0], 0)
            self.assertIsNotNone(main.ai_call('Help again', provider='groq'))
            self.assertEqual(request.call_count, 1)
            self.assertEqual(gemini.call_count, 2)  # Groq is down, so Gemini answers both times
        main.GROQ_COOLDOWN[0] = 0
        with patch.object(main.urllib.request, 'urlopen', return_value=io.BytesIO(b'{"choices": []}')):
            self.assertIsNone(main.groq('Help'))

    def test_generation_uses_selected_provider_and_isolates_cache(self):
        question = {'q': '2 + 3?', 'options': ['4', '5', '6', '7'], 'answer': 1,
                    'hints': ['Count on', 'Add three'], 'explanation': '2 + 3 = 5.'}
        with patch.object(main, 'ai_call', side_effect=[question, {'answer': 1}]) as ai:
            q = main.generate_question('Arithmetic', 'Maths', 'Class 2', 0, 'groq')
            self.assertTrue(q)
            self.assertTrue(all(c.kwargs['provider'] == 'groq' for c in ai.call_args_list))
        groq = {'exam': 'Class 2', 'ai_provider': 'groq'}
        gemini = {'exam': 'Class 2', 'ai_provider': 'gemini'}
        self.assertIn(q, main.items(groq, 'Arithmetic'))
        self.assertNotIn(q, main.items(gemini, 'Arithmetic'))

    def test_tutoring_routes_use_selected_provider(self):
        sid = self.post('session', {'level': 'JEE', 'ai_provider': 'groq'})['session_id']
        self.post('next', {'session_id': sid})
        s = main.SESSIONS[sid]
        self.post('answer', {'session_id': sid, 'choice': (s['current']['answer'] + 1) % 4})
        with patch.object(main, 'ai_call', return_value='Tutor reply') as ai:
            self.assertEqual(self.post('ask', {'session_id': sid, 'text': 'Please explain.'})['reply'], 'Tutor reply')
            self.assertEqual(self.post('explain', {'session_id': sid})['explanation'], 'Tutor reply')
            self.assertTrue(all(c.kwargs['provider'] == 'groq' for c in ai.call_args_list))
        self.post('provider', {'session_id': sid, 'ai_provider': 'none'})
        with patch.object(main.urllib.request, 'urlopen') as network:
            self.post('ask', {'session_id': sid, 'text': 'Help'})
            network.assert_not_called()


if __name__ == '__main__':
    unittest.main()

"""Owned daily activity, goals and suggested study plans; isolated/offline."""
import copy
from datetime import date, timedelta
from pathlib import Path
import sys
import tempfile
import time
import unittest
from unittest.mock import patch

for directory in ('.runtime', '.test-deps'):
    location = Path(__file__).parent / directory
    if location.exists():
        sys.path.insert(0, str(location)); break
from fastapi.testclient import TestClient
import main
from storage import Database


class DailyTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        self.temp = tempfile.TemporaryDirectory()
        self.path = Path(self.temp.name) / 'daily.sqlite3'
        self.db = main.DB; main.DB = Database(self.path)
        self.keys = patch.multiple(main, GEMINI_KEY=None, GROQ_KEY=None); self.keys.start()
        self.bank = copy.deepcopy(main.BANK); main.SESSIONS.clear()
        self.client = TestClient(main.app); self.client.post('/api/guest')
        self.today = date(2026, 10, 31)
        self.clock = patch.object(main, 'utc_day', return_value=self.today); self.clock.start()
        self.owner = main.DB.owner(self.client.cookies.get(main.AUTH_COOKIE))

    def tearDown(self):
        self.client.close(); self.clock.stop(); self.keys.stop()
        main.SESSIONS.clear(); main.BANK.clear(); main.BANK.update(self.bank)
        main.DB = self.db; self.temp.cleanup()

    def post(self, path, body):
        response = self.client.post('/api/' + path, json=body)
        self.assertEqual(response.status_code, 200, response.text); return response.json()

    def daily(self):
        response = self.client.get('/api/daily')
        self.assertEqual(response.status_code, 200, response.text); return response.json()

    def session(self):
        return self.post('session', {'level':'JEE', 'ai_provider':'none'})['session_id']

    def test_default_dashboard_does_not_start_quiz_or_count_visits(self):
        for _ in range(2):
            daily = self.daily()
            self.assertEqual((daily['answered'], daily['goal'], daily['streak']), (0, 20, 0))
            self.assertEqual(daily['timezone'], 'UTC')
            self.assertTrue(all(row['n'] == 0 for row in daily['chapters']))
        self.assertIsNone(main.DB.latest_session(self.owner))

    def test_answer_counts_once_skips_and_hints_do_not(self):
        sid = self.session(); self.post('next', {'session_id':sid})
        self.post('hint', {'session_id':sid}); self.post('skip', {'session_id':sid})
        self.assertEqual(self.daily()['answered'], 0)
        self.post('next', {'session_id':sid})
        result = self.post('answer', {'session_id':sid, 'choice':0})
        self.assertEqual(self.client.post('/api/answer',json={'session_id':sid,'choice':0}).status_code,400)
        self.assertFalse(main.DB.record_activity(self.owner,'answer:'+result['answer_id'],self.today.isoformat()))
        self.assertEqual(self.daily()['answered'], 1)

    def test_daily_counts_and_goals_survive_restart(self):
        prefs = {'level':'JEE','subjects':['Maths'],'ai_provider':'none','start_ability':0.0,'daily_goal':12,'exam_date':'2026-11-10'}
        response = self.client.put('/api/preferences',json=prefs)
        self.assertEqual(response.status_code,200,response.text)
        main.DB.record_activity(self.owner,'test-event',self.today.isoformat())
        main.DB = Database(self.path); main.SESSIONS.clear()
        daily = self.daily()
        self.assertEqual((daily['answered'],daily['goal'],daily['days_left']), (1,12,10))
        self.assertTrue(all(row['subject']=='Maths' for row in daily['chapters']))
        self.assertEqual(daily['plan']['goal'],12)

    def test_streak_uses_utc_days_and_allows_yesterday(self):
        for offset in [0,1,2]:
            main.DB.record_activity(self.owner,str(offset),(self.today-timedelta(days=offset)).isoformat())
        self.assertEqual((self.daily()['streak'],self.daily()['milestone']), (3,3))
        with patch.object(main,'utc_day',return_value=self.today+timedelta(days=1)):
            self.assertEqual((self.daily()['answered'],self.daily()['streak']), (0,3))
            self.assertIsNone(self.daily()['milestone'])
        with patch.object(main,'utc_day',return_value=self.today+timedelta(days=2)):
            self.assertEqual(self.daily()['streak'],0)

    def test_preferences_validate_goal_date_and_allow_past_date(self):
        for values in [{'daily_goal':0},{'daily_goal':201},{'exam_date':'2026-02-30'},{'exam_date':'20261031'}]:
            response=self.client.put('/api/preferences',json={'level':'JEE','ai_provider':'none',**values})
            self.assertEqual(response.status_code,400,response.text)
        response=self.client.put('/api/preferences',json={'level':'JEE','ai_provider':'none','start_ability':0.0,'exam_date':'2020-01-01'})
        self.assertEqual(response.status_code,200,response.text)
        self.assertEqual(self.daily()['days_left'],0)
        self.assertEqual(self.daily()['plan']['study_minutes'],10)

    def test_activity_is_private(self):
        main.DB.record_activity(self.owner,'private-event',self.today.isoformat())
        with TestClient(main.app) as other:
            self.assertEqual(other.get('/api/daily').status_code,401)
            other.post('/api/guest')
            self.assertEqual(other.get('/api/daily').json()['answered'],0)
        self.assertEqual(self.daily()['answered'],1)

    def test_due_review_counts_once_and_does_not_change_practice(self):
        sid=self.session(); q=self.post('next',{'session_id':sid})['question']
        full=main.SESSIONS[sid]['current']
        snapshot={'question':dict(full),'subject':'Physics','level':'JEE','level_name':'JEE','unit':86400}
        main.DB.add_review(self.owner,q['id'],snapshot,time.time()-1)
        self.assertEqual(self.daily()['plan']['due_reviews'],1)
        self.assertGreater(self.daily()['plan']['review_minutes'],0)
        response=self.client.post('/api/reviews/'+q['id'],json={'choice':full['answer']})
        self.assertEqual(response.status_code,200,response.text)
        self.assertEqual(self.client.post('/api/reviews/'+q['id'],json={'choice':0}).status_code,409)
        self.assertEqual(self.daily()['answered'],1)
        self.assertEqual(main.SESSIONS[sid]['learner'].history,[])

    def test_latest_unrelated_paper_does_not_replace_syllabus(self):
        self.client.put('/api/preferences',json={'level':'JEE','subjects':['Maths'],'ai_provider':'none','start_ability':0.0})
        sid=self.session(); self.post('next',{'session_id':sid}); self.post('answer',{'session_id':sid,'choice':0})
        paper=self.post('papers/import',{'name':'Other paper','content':'1. Unit of force?\nA) Joule B) Newton C) Watt D) Pascal\nAnswer: B'})
        self.post('session',{'level':paper['level'],'ai_provider':'none'})
        self.assertTrue(all(row['subject']=='Maths' for row in self.daily()['chapters']))


if __name__=='__main__': unittest.main()

"""AI tutor on the learner's own notes: explain, ask, and quiz-from-notes (AI calls faked, runs offline)."""
import copy
import json
from pathlib import Path
import sys
import tempfile
import unittest
from unittest.mock import patch

for directory in ('.runtime', '.test-deps'):
    location = Path(__file__).parent / directory
    if location.exists():
        sys.path.insert(0, str(location))
        break
from fastapi.testclient import TestClient
import main
from storage import Database

NOTES = ("Newton's second law: the net force on a body equals mass times acceleration, F = ma. "
         "Force is measured in newtons. A 2 kg mass accelerating at 3 m/s^2 needs 6 N of net force.")
QUESTIONS = [
    {"q": "Unit of force?", "options": ["Joule", "Newton", "Watt", "Pascal"], "answer": 1,
     "explanation": "Force is measured in newtons.", "difficulty": "easy", "topic": "Newton's laws"},
    {"q": "Net force on 2 kg at 3 m/s^2?", "options": ["5 N", "6 N", "1.5 N", "9 N"], "answer": 1,
     "explanation": "F = ma = 6 N.", "difficulty": "medium", "topic": "Newton's laws"},
    {"q": "Broken item", "options": ["a", "b"], "answer": 0},  # malformed: dropped
]


class FakeAI:
    """Answers each prompt kind with canned JSON; the second solve disagrees on question 2 when told to."""
    def __init__(self, disagree=False):
        self.prompts, self.disagree = [], disagree

    def __call__(self, prompt, as_json=False, provider=None):
        self.prompts.append(prompt)
        if 'key concepts' in prompt:
            return {"summary": "Force and motion.", "concepts": [{"title": "Second law", "explanation": "F = ma."}, {"bad": 1}]}
        if 'Write ' in prompt and 'multiple-choice' in prompt:
            return {"questions": QUESTIONS}
        if 'Solve each question' in prompt:
            return {"answers": [1, 0 if self.disagree else 1]}
        return "It means force depends on mass and acceleration."


class NotesTutorTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_null_model_lists_fail_cleanly(self):
        for replies in ([{'questions':None}], [{'questions':QUESTIONS},{'answers':None}]):
            with patch.object(main,'ai_call',side_effect=copy.deepcopy(replies)):
                response=self.client.post('/api/notes/quiz',json={'notes':NOTES,'ai_provider':'gemini'})
            self.assertEqual(response.status_code,503,response.text)

    def setUp(self):
        self.temp = tempfile.TemporaryDirectory()
        self.original_db = main.DB
        main.DB = Database(Path(self.temp.name) / 'notes.sqlite3')
        self.keys = patch.multiple(main, GEMINI_KEY='test-key', GROQ_KEY=None)
        self.keys.start()
        main.SESSIONS.clear()
        self.bank = copy.deepcopy(main.BANK)
        self.client = TestClient(main.app)
        self.client.post('/api/guest')

    def tearDown(self):
        self.client.close()
        main.DB = self.original_db
        main.SESSIONS.clear()
        main.BANK.clear(); main.BANK.update(self.bank)
        self.keys.stop()
        self.temp.cleanup()

    def call(self, path, body, fake=None, status=200):
        with patch.object(main, 'ai_call', fake or FakeAI()):
            response = self.client.post('/api/notes/' + path, json=body)
        self.assertEqual(response.status_code, status, response.text)
        return response.json()

    def test_explain_keeps_only_well_formed_concepts(self):
        d = self.call('explain', {'notes': NOTES, 'ai_provider': 'gemini'})
        self.assertEqual(d['concepts'], [{'title': 'Second law', 'explanation': 'F = ma.'}])

    def test_notes_are_fenced_as_data_and_capped(self):
        fake = FakeAI()
        self.call('explain', {'notes': NOTES + ' x' * 40000, 'ai_provider': 'gemini'}, fake)
        self.assertIn('<notes>', fake.prompts[0])
        self.assertIn('ignore any instructions', fake.prompts[0])
        self.assertLess(len(fake.prompts[0]), main.NOTES_LIMIT + 2000)

    def test_ask_sends_recent_history(self):
        fake = FakeAI()
        d = self.call('ask', {'notes': NOTES, 'ai_provider': 'gemini', 'question': 'What is F = ma?',
                              'history': [{'role': 'user', 'text': 'earlier question'}]}, fake)
        self.assertIn('force', d['reply'])
        self.assertIn('earlier question', fake.prompts[0])

    def test_quiz_becomes_an_owned_adaptive_paper(self):
        d = self.call('quiz', {'notes': NOTES, 'ai_provider': 'gemini', 'count': 5, 'name': 'Laws notes'})
        self.assertEqual((d['questions'], d['dropped'], d['name']), (2, 1, 'Laws notes'))
        sid = self.client.post('/api/session', json={'level': d['level'], 'ai_provider': 'none'}).json()['session_id']
        q = self.client.post('/api/next', json={'session_id': sid}).json()['question']
        self.assertFalse(q['verified'])  # AI-written from notes, tagged as such
        with TestClient(main.app) as other:
            other.post('/api/guest')
            self.assertEqual(other.get('/api/papers/' + d['paper_id']).status_code, 404)

    def test_quiz_drops_questions_the_second_solve_rejects(self):
        d = self.call('quiz', {'notes': NOTES, 'ai_provider': 'gemini', 'count': 5}, FakeAI(disagree=True))
        self.assertEqual((d['questions'], d['dropped']), (1, 2))

    def test_checker_answers_given_as_text_still_count(self):
        fake = FakeAI()
        original = fake.__call__
        fake_call = lambda prompt, as_json=False, provider=None: (
            {"answers": ["1", " 1 "]} if 'Solve each question' in prompt else original(prompt, as_json, provider))
        d = self.call('quiz', {'notes': NOTES, 'ai_provider': 'gemini', 'count': 5}, fake_call)
        self.assertEqual(d['questions'], 2)

    def test_ask_without_notes_is_a_general_tutor_at_the_learner_level(self):
        fake = FakeAI()
        d = self.call('ask', {'notes': '', 'ai_provider': 'gemini', 'question': 'Why is the sky blue?'}, fake)
        self.assertTrue(d['reply'])
        self.assertNotIn('<notes>', fake.prompts[0])
        self.assertIn('JEE Advanced aspirant', fake.prompts[0])
        self.call('ask', {'notes': '', 'ai_provider': 'none', 'question': 'Why?'}, status=400)
        self.call('ask', {'notes': '', 'ai_provider': 'gemini', 'question': '  '}, status=400)

    def test_guards(self):
        self.call('explain', {'notes': 'too short', 'ai_provider': 'gemini'}, status=400)
        self.call('explain', {'notes': NOTES, 'ai_provider': 'none'}, status=400)
        self.call('quiz', {'notes': NOTES, 'ai_provider': 'gemini'}, fake=lambda *a, **k: None, status=503)
        self.assertEqual(self.client.get('/tutor').status_code, 200)


if __name__ == '__main__':
    unittest.main()

END OF FILE: preppilot/test_notes.py

"""Owned, durable question-bookmark regression checks."""
import copy
from pathlib import Path
import sys
import tempfile
import unittest
from unittest.mock import patch

for directory in ('.runtime', '.test-deps'):
    location=Path(__file__).parent/directory
    if location.exists():
        sys.path.insert(0,str(location))
        break
from fastapi.testclient import TestClient
import main
from storage import Database


class BookmarkTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        self.temp=tempfile.TemporaryDirectory()
        self.path=Path(self.temp.name)/'bookmarks.sqlite3'
        self.original_db=main.DB
        main.DB=Database(self.path)
        self.keys=patch.multiple(main,GEMINI_KEY=None,GROQ_KEY=None)
        self.keys.start()
        main.SESSIONS.clear()
        self.bank=copy.deepcopy(main.BANK)
        self.client=TestClient(main.app)
        self.client.post('/api/signup',json={'name':'Bookmark test','email':'bookmarks@example.com','password':'test-password','level':'JEE'})

    def tearDown(self):
        self.client.close()
        main.DB=self.original_db
        main.SESSIONS.clear()
        main.BANK.clear();main.BANK.update(self.bank)
        self.keys.stop()
        self.temp.cleanup()

    def post(self,path,data):
        response=self.client.post('/api/'+path,json=data)
        self.assertEqual(response.status_code,200,response.text)
        return response.json()

    def question(self):
        sid=self.post('session',{'level':'JEE','ai_provider':'none'})['session_id']
        question=self.post('next',{'session_id':sid})['question']
        return sid,question

    def answered(self):
        sid,q=self.question()
        self.post('answer',{'session_id':sid,'choice':0})
        return sid,q

    def test_unanswered_question_cannot_be_saved(self):
        sid,q=self.question()
        response=self.client.post('/api/bookmarks',json={'session_id':sid,'question_id':q['id']})
        self.assertEqual(response.status_code,409)
        self.assertEqual(self.client.get('/api/bookmarks').json(),[])
        self.post('skip',{'session_id':sid})
        self.post('bookmarks',{'session_id':sid,'question_id':q['id']})  # skipping gives up the question

    def test_save_is_idempotent(self):
        sid,q=self.question()
        self.assertFalse(q['bookmarked'])
        self.assertNotIn('answer',q)
        self.post('answer',{'session_id':sid,'choice':0})
        main.SESSIONS[sid].pop('questions')  # Legacy active sessions predate the question snapshot log.
        for _ in range(2):
            result=self.post('bookmarks',{'session_id':sid,'question_id':q['id']})
            self.assertEqual(result,{'bookmarked':True})
        bookmarks=self.client.get('/api/bookmarks').json()
        self.assertEqual(len(bookmarks),1)
        self.assertEqual(bookmarks[0]['question']['q'],q['q'])

    def test_replacing_an_unanswered_question_does_not_unlock_bookmark(self):
        sid,q=self.question()
        self.post('next',{'session_id':sid})
        self.assertEqual(self.client.post('/api/bookmarks',json={'session_id':sid,'question_id':q['id']}).status_code,409)
        self.assertEqual(self.client.get('/api/bookmarks').json(),[])

    def test_bookmark_after_answer_targets_exact_previous_question(self):
        sid,q=self.question()
        self.post('answer',{'session_id':sid,'choice':0})
        next_q=self.post('next',{'session_id':sid})['question']
        self.post('bookmarks',{'session_id':sid,'question_id':q['id']})
        saved=self.client.get('/api/bookmarks').json()[0]
        self.assertEqual(saved['question_id'],q['id'])
        self.assertEqual(main.SESSIONS[sid]['current']['id'],next_q['id'])
        self.assertEqual(len(main.SESSIONS[sid]['learner'].history),1)

    def test_snapshot_survives_restart_and_source_changes(self):
        sid,q=self.answered()
        self.post('bookmarks',{'session_id':sid,'question_id':q['id']})
        main.SESSIONS[sid]['questions'][q['id']]['question']['q']='Changed source text'
        main.SESSIONS.clear();main.DB=Database(self.path)
        saved=self.client.get('/api/bookmarks').json()
        self.assertEqual(saved[0]['question']['q'],q['q'])
        self.assertEqual(self.client.get('/bookmarks').status_code,200)
        self.client.delete('/api/bookmarks/'+q['id'])
        main.DB=Database(self.path)
        self.assertEqual(self.client.get('/api/bookmarks').json(),[])

    def test_other_learner_cannot_read_or_change_bookmarks(self):
        sid,q=self.answered()
        self.post('bookmarks',{'session_id':sid,'question_id':q['id']})
        with TestClient(main.app) as other:
            self.assertEqual(other.get('/api/bookmarks').status_code,401)
            other.post('/api/guest')
            self.assertEqual(other.get('/api/bookmarks').json(),[])
            self.assertEqual(other.post('/api/bookmarks',json={'session_id':sid,'question_id':q['id']}).status_code,404)
            self.assertEqual(other.delete('/api/bookmarks/'+q['id']).status_code,200)
        self.assertEqual(len(self.client.get('/api/bookmarks').json()),1)

    def test_unseen_question_cannot_be_saved(self):
        sid,q=self.question()
        self.assertEqual(self.client.post('/api/bookmarks',json={'session_id':sid,'question_id':'not-asked'}).status_code,404)
        self.assertEqual(self.client.get('/api/bookmarks').json(),[])

    def test_uploaded_paper_bookmark_keeps_answer_and_explanation(self):
        paper=self.post('papers/import',{'name':'Revision paper','content':'1. Unit of force?\nA) Joule B) Newton C) Watt D) Pascal\nAnswer: B\nExplanation: Force is measured in newtons.'})
        sid=self.post('session',{'level':paper['level'],'ai_provider':'none'})['session_id']
        q=self.post('next',{'session_id':sid})['question']
        self.post('answer',{'session_id':sid,'choice':0})
        self.post('bookmarks',{'session_id':sid,'question_id':q['id']})
        self.client.delete('/api/papers/'+paper['paper_id'])
        main.SESSIONS.clear();main.DB=Database(self.path)
        saved=self.client.get('/api/bookmarks').json()[0]
        self.assertEqual(saved['question']['answer'],1)
        self.assertIn('newtons',saved['question']['explanation'])
        self.assertEqual(saved['level_name'],'📄 Revision paper')


if __name__=='__main__':
    unittest.main()

================================================================================
END OF FILE: preppilot/test_bookmarks.py

FILE: preppilot/test_reviews.py

"""Spaced mistake-review regression checks."""
import copy
from pathlib import Path
import sys
import tempfile
import unittest
from unittest.mock import patch

for directory in ('.runtime', '.test-deps'):
    location = Path(__file__).parent / directory
    if location.exists():
        sys.path.insert(0, str(location))
        break
from fastapi.testclient import TestClient
import main
from storage import Database


class ReviewTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        self.temp = tempfile.TemporaryDirectory()
        self.original_db = main.DB
        main.DB = Database(Path(self.temp.name) / 'reviews.sqlite3')
        self.keys = patch.multiple(main, GEMINI_KEY=None, GROQ_KEY=None)
        self.keys.start()
        main.SESSIONS.clear()
        self.bank = copy.deepcopy(main.BANK)
        self.client = TestClient(main.app)
        self.client.post('/api/guest')

    def tearDown(self):
        self.client.close()
        main.DB = self.original_db
        main.SESSIONS.clear()
        main.BANK.clear(); main.BANK.update(self.bank)
        self.keys.stop()
        self.temp.cleanup()

    def post(self, path, data, client=None):
        response = (client or self.client).post('/api/' + path, json=data)
        self.assertEqual(response.status_code, 200, response.text)
        return response.json()

    def miss_one(self, demo=True):
        sid = self.post('session', {'level': 'JEE', 'ai_provider': 'none', 'demo': demo})['session_id']
        q = self.post('next', {'session_id': sid})['question']
        right = main.SESSIONS[sid]['current']['answer']
        result = self.post('answer', {'session_id': sid, 'choice': (right + 1) % 4})
        return sid, q, right, result

    def make_due(self):
        with main.DB.connection() as con:
            con.execute('UPDATE reviews SET due=0')

    def test_missed_question_is_scheduled_not_due(self):
        _, q, _, result = self.miss_one(demo=False)
        self.assertEqual(result['review_in'], 86400)
        listing = self.client.get('/api/reviews').json()
        self.assertEqual((listing['due'], listing['later']), ([], 1))
        self.assertEqual(self.client.post('/api/reviews/' + q['id'], json={'choice': 0}).status_code, 409)

    def test_right_answer_is_not_queued(self):
        sid = self.post('session', {'level': 'JEE', 'ai_provider': 'none'})['session_id']
        self.post('next', {'session_id': sid})
        result = self.post('answer', {'session_id': sid, 'choice': main.SESSIONS[sid]['current']['answer']})
        self.assertIsNone(result['review_in'])
        self.assertEqual(self.client.get('/api/reviews').json()['later'], 0)

    def test_ladder_to_mastery_and_reset_on_miss(self):
        _, q, right, result = self.miss_one()
        self.assertEqual(result['review_in'], 60)  # demo mode: minutes instead of days
        self.make_due()
        due = self.client.get('/api/reviews').json()['due']
        self.assertEqual([d['question_id'] for d in due], [q['id']])
        self.assertNotIn('answer', due[0])  # the answer stays hidden until the learner answers
        first = self.post('reviews/' + q['id'], {'choice': right})
        self.assertEqual((first['correct'], first['next_in'], first['mastered']), (True, 180, False))
        self.make_due()
        missed = self.post('reviews/' + q['id'], {'choice': (right + 1) % 4})
        self.assertEqual((missed['correct'], missed['next_in']), (False, 60))
        for expected in (180, 420, None):
            self.make_due()
            step = self.post('reviews/' + q['id'], {'choice': right})
            self.assertEqual(step['next_in'], expected)
        self.assertTrue(step['mastered'])
        self.assertEqual(self.client.get('/api/reviews').json(), {'due': [], 'later': 0, 'next_in': None})

    def test_reported_question_leaves_review(self):
        sid, _, _, result = self.miss_one()
        self.post('report', {'session_id': sid, 'answer_id': result['answer_id']})
        self.assertEqual(self.client.get('/api/reviews').json()['later'], 0)

    def test_reviews_are_private(self):
        _, q, right, _ = self.miss_one()
        self.make_due()
        with TestClient(main.app) as other:
            other.post('/api/guest')
            self.assertEqual(other.get('/api/reviews').json()['due'], [])
            self.assertEqual(other.post('/api/reviews/' + q['id'], json={'choice': right}).status_code, 404)
        self.assertEqual(len(self.client.get('/api/reviews').json()['due']), 1)

    def test_dashboard_history_reports_skill_change(self):
        sid, _, _, _ = self.miss_one()
        history = self.client.get('/api/dashboard', params={'session_id': sid}).json()['history']
        self.assertLess(history[-1]['delta'], 0)


if __name__ == '__main__':
    unittest.main()

END OF FILE: preppilot/test_reviews.py

// Offline checks of the real account restoration and Google sign-in scripts.
const assert = require('node:assert/strict');
const fs = require('node:fs');
const path = require('node:path');
const vm = require('node:vm');

async function signIn({preferences = null, google = false, status = 200, stale = false} = {}) {
  const routes = [], requests = [];
  const cache = new Map(stale ? [['pp-prefs', JSON.stringify({level:'NEET'})]] : []);
  const errorNote = {textContent:'', dataset:{}, matches:()=>true};
  const slot = {nextElementSibling:errorNote, hidden:true, clientWidth:300};
  const identity = {user:{email:'student@example.com', name:'Student', level:'JEE'}, preferences, papers:[]};
  let callback;
  const context = {
    URL, Request:class {}, console,
    location:{href:'http://quiz.test/login', pathname:'/login', replace:value=>routes.push(value)},
    localStorage:{getItem:k=>cache.get(k)||null, setItem:(k,v)=>cache.set(k,v), removeItem:k=>cache.delete(k)},
    addEventListener:()=>{}, matchMedia:()=>({matches:false}),
    document:{addEventListener:()=>{}, querySelectorAll:()=>[slot],
      createElement:()=>({}), head:{append:script=>script.onload()}},
    google:{accounts:{id:{initialize:config=>{callback=config.callback}, renderButton:()=>{}}}},
    fetch:async (url,options)=>{
      requests.push([url,options]);
      const code = url === '/api/state' ? status : 200;
      return {ok:code===200,status:code,headers:{get:()=>null},json:async()=>url==='/api/auth/config'?{google_client_id:'synthetic-client'}:identity};
    }
  };
  context.window=context;
  vm.createContext(context);
  vm.runInContext(fs.readFileSync(path.join(__dirname,'account.js'),'utf8'),context);
  if(google){
    await vm.runInContext(fs.readFileSync(path.join(__dirname,'google-signin.js'),'utf8'),context);
    assert.equal(typeof callback,'function');
    await callback({credential:'synthetic-token'});
  }else{
    await context.finishSignIn();
  }
  return {routes,requests,cache,errorNote};
}

(async()=>{
  for(const google of [false,true]){
    assert.deepEqual((await signIn({google,stale:true})).routes,['/setup']);
    const returning=await signIn({google,preferences:{level:'JEE',block_minutes:25}});
    assert.deepEqual(returning.routes,['/study']);
    assert.equal(JSON.parse(returning.cache.get('pp-prefs')).block_minutes,25);
    assert(returning.requests.some(([url])=>url==='/api/state'));
    if(google)assert(returning.requests.some(([url,options])=>url==='/api/auth/google'&&options.method==='POST'));
  }
  await assert.rejects(()=>signIn({status:401}),/sign in/i);
  await assert.rejects(()=>signIn({status:503}),/restore/i);
  const failedGoogle=await signIn({google:true,status:503});
  assert.deepEqual(failedGoogle.routes,[]);
  assert.match(failedGoogle.errorNote.textContent,/restore/i);
  const landing=fs.readFileSync(path.join(__dirname,'landing.html'),'utf8');
  const guestEntry=landing.slice(landing.indexOf('async function continueAsGuest('),landing.indexOf('$("guest").onclick'));
  for(const returning of [false,true]) {
    let created=0,entered=0;
    const sandbox={restoreAccount:async()=>returning?{user:{guest:true},preferences:{level:'JEE'}}:null,
      finishSignIn:async()=>{entered++},fetch:async()=>{created++;return {ok:true}},panelOpen:false};
    vm.createContext(sandbox);vm.runInContext(guestEntry,sandbox);
    await sandbox.continueAsGuest();assert.equal(created,returning?0:1);assert.equal(entered,1);
  }
  console.log('Sign-in routing checks passed: first-time setup, returning Home, server-owned preferences, Google, failed restoration.');
})().catch(error=>{console.error(error);process.exitCode=1;});

// Exercise the actual wizard controller offline, including optional placement.
const fs=require('node:fs'),vm=require('node:vm'),assert=require('node:assert/strict');
const source=fs.readFileSync(require('node:path').join(__dirname,'setup.html'),'utf8');
const controller=source.slice(source.indexOf('function update()'),source.indexOf('fetch("/api/providers")'));
const nodes=new Map(), get=selector=>{
  if(!nodes.has(selector))nodes.set(selector,{classList:{toggle(){},add(){}},textContent:'',disabled:false,hidden:false});
  return nodes.get(selector);
};
const steps=Array.from({length:7},()=>({hidden:true,querySelector:()=>({focus(){}})}));
let saved;
const context={console,$:get,steps,currentStep:0,saving:false,providersReady:true,
  stepNames:['Level','Subjects','Start','Focus','Style','Timing','AI'],
  LEVELS:[{level:'JEE',name:'JEE Advanced',subjects:[{subject:'Maths'}]}],
  P:{level:'JEE',subjects:['Maths'],start_ability:'check',placed:false,block_minutes:40},
  user:{email:'wizard@example.com'},localStorage:{setItem(){}},location:{href:''},
  savePreferences:async prefs=>{saved={...prefs};return prefs;}};
vm.createContext(context);vm.runInContext(controller,context);
(async()=>{
  context.showStep(0,false);
  assert.equal(steps.filter(s=>!s.hidden).length,1);
  await get('#go').onclick();assert.equal(context.currentStep,1);
  context.P.subjects=[];context.update();assert.equal(get('#go').disabled,true);
  await get('#go').onclick();assert.equal(context.currentStep,1);
  context.P.subjects=['Maths'];context.update();
  await get('#go').onclick();assert.equal(context.currentStep,2);
  get('#back').onclick();assert.equal(context.currentStep,1);
  for(let i=1;i<6;i++)await get('#go').onclick();
  assert.equal(context.currentStep,6);assert.equal(context.location.href,'');
  await get('#go').onclick();
  assert.equal(context.location.href,'/study');
  assert.equal(saved.start_ability,'check');assert.equal(saved.placed,false);
  assert.equal(steps.filter(s=>!s.hidden).length,1);
  console.log('Setup wizard checks passed: steps, validation, Back, save, optional placement stays on dashboard.');
})().catch(e=>{console.error(e);process.exitCode=1;});

// Runs the real extractor against PDF.js-style text fragments, without CDN/network access.
// Run from preppilot/: node test_pdf_import.js
const assert = require('node:assert/strict');
const fs = require('node:fs');
const source = fs.readFileSync(require('node:path').join(__dirname, 'extract.js'), 'utf8');
const extractor = source.slice(source.indexOf('async function pdfText('), source.indexOf('async function docxText('));
async function extract(pages) {
  const pdf = {numPages: pages.length, getPage: async n => ({getTextContent: async () => ({items: pages[n - 1]})})};
  const pdfjs = {GlobalWorkerOptions: {}, getDocument: () => ({promise: Promise.resolve(pdf)})};
  return new Function('loadScript', 'CDN', 'pdfjsLib', extractor + '; return pdfText({arrayBuffer:async()=>new ArrayBuffer(0)});')(async () => {}, '', pdfjs);
}
(async () => {
  const text = await extract([[{str:'1. What is 2 + 3?', hasEOL:true},
    {str:'A) 4'}, {str:'B) 5'}, {str:'C) 6'}, {str:'D) 7',hasEOL:true},
    {str:'Answer: B',hasEOL:true}]]);
  assert.match(text, /A\) 4\s+B\) 5\s+C\) 6\s+D\) 7/);
  assert.match(text, /7\nAnswer: B/);
  const wrapped = await extract([[{str:'1. Unit'}, {str:'of force?',hasEOL:true}], [{str:'Answer: C',hasEOL:true}]]);
  assert.match(wrapped, /Unit\s+of force\?/);
  assert.match(wrapped, /force\?\n\nAnswer: C/);
  console.log('PDF extraction regression checks passed');
})().catch(error => {console.error(error);process.exitCode=1;});

# PrepPilot: practice that adapts to every answer

**Hackathon submission · PS 2: Adaptive AI Study Companion**

PrepPilot is an adaptive study companion for **Class 1–12 (CBSE/NCERT), JEE Main, JEE Advanced and NEET**. It picks every question from what you got wrong, **explains why it chose it**, diagnoses *how* you fail, spaces your reviews so they stick, and enforces healthy study breaks. It runs as a website and an installable phone app.

> **Judges, the quickest way in:** run it (2 commands below), open http://localhost:8000, click **Continue as guest**, then answer a few questions in a chapter like *Laws of Motion*. Watch the **Live adaptation** graph and **Why this question** panel on the right.

---

## What to look at (5-minute tour)
| Where | What it shows |
|---|---|
| **Landing page** `/` | 3D *knowledge galaxy* (subjects are planets, chapters orbit them, coloured by readiness) and the pitch |
| **Home** `/study` | Recommended next chapter, mistakes due for review, daily goal, streak, exam plan |
| **Quiz** `/app` | Adaptive questions. After each answer: right/wrong animation, an **error diagnosis** (concept gap, careless slip, trap option, time pressure), the **live adaptation graph** with ↑ harder / ↓ easier, and **Why this question** |
| **Progress tab** (quiz, right side) | Score estimate, mistake patterns, **confidence vs reality** with Attempt/Skip advice for negative marking, ability vs difficulty graph |
| **Mistake review** `/review` | Spaced repetition: missed questions return after 1, 3 and 7 days |
| **AI tutor** `/tutor` | Ask anything, or upload notes (PDF/Word/PowerPoint) to get concepts explained and turned into a quiz |
| **Question papers** `/papers` | Paste or upload your own paper (or a Google Sheet) and it becomes an adaptive quiz |
| **Breaks** | `/app?demo=1` uses 2-minute focus blocks, so you can see the enforced break |

## What makes it different
1. **Explainable adaptation:** every question shows *why* it was picked, plus a live graph of skill vs difficulty.
2. **Understands how you fail:** confidence, time, hints and answer switching feed a per-chapter skill model, and each miss gets a specific fix.
3. **Exam strategy:** confidence calibration and negative-marking advice (JEE Advanced +3/−1, JEE Main and NEET +4/−1), plus a predicted score.
4. **Wellbeing built in:** 40-minute focus blocks, **server-enforced breaks**, and fatigue and frustration detection.
5. **Your own material:** question papers and notes become adaptive practice; disputed answer keys are checked by two independent AI solves.
6. **Honest content:** 154 verified questions, including **74 real JEE Advanced questions with official keys** (JEEBench, credited). AI questions are tagged and checked by a second solve. **It works with no AI at all.**

In simulation, adaptive selection reaches the readiness target with **21–44% fewer questions** than a fixed bank (`python engine.py`).

## Run it locally
Requires **Python 3.11+**.
```bash
cd preppilot
pip install -r requirements.txt
python -m uvicorn main:app --port 8000
```
Open **http://localhost:8000** and click **Continue as guest**.

**Optional AI** (tutor, explanations and extra questions): get a free Groq key at console.groq.com, then:
```bash
# from the repository root
cp .env.example .env.local      # then put GROQ_API_KEY=... (and optionally GEMINI_API_KEY=...) in it
cd preppilot && python start_local.py
```
Groq is used first, with Gemini as fallback. Without keys, everything except the AI features works.

**Install on a phone:** open the site over HTTPS (for example via a tunnel such as `cloudflared tunnel --url http://localhost:8000`), then use *Install app* (Android) or *Share → Add to Home Screen* (iPhone).

## Checks (all pass)
Run from `preppilot/`:
```bash
python engine.py            # adaptive engine self-check + simulation, prints "engine ok"
python verify_bank.py       # re-checks every answer key, prints "all answer keys verified"
python -B test_regressions.py
python -B test_persistence.py
python -B test_bookmarks.py
python -B test_reviews.py
python -B test_ai_providers.py
python -B test_notes.py
python -B test_account_extras.py
node test_pdf_import.js
```
These also run in GitHub Actions (`.github/workflows/checks.yml`). All tests run offline, with the AI faked.

## How it works
| Part | File |
|---|---|
| Learner model and policy: per-chapter IRT/Elo ability, question difficulty targeted at ~60% chance of success, chapter ranking (weakness + exploration + misconceptions), error diagnosis, wellbeing, calibration, predicted score, spaced-review schedule, simulation | `preppilot/engine.py` (pure Python, assert-based self-check) |
| API, sessions, focus blocks and breaks, AI routing, accounts, reviews, bookmarks, papers, notes tutor | `preppilot/main.py` (FastAPI) |
| Storage: accounts (PBKDF2 hashes), hashed expiring login tokens, sessions, preferences, papers, bookmarks, reviews | `preppilot/storage.py` (SQLite) |
| Syllabus maps (Class 1–12, JEE, NEET) | `preppilot/syllabus.py` |
| Question bank and answer-key verification | `preppilot/questions*.json`, `preppilot/verify_bank.py` |
| Pages | `preppilot/*.html`, `site.css`; 3D galaxy in `galaxy.js` (Three.js); installable app via `manifest.webmanifest`, `sw.js` |

**Stack:** Python, FastAPI, SQLite, vanilla JavaScript, Three.js, Chart.js, KaTeX. No build step.
More detail: [`preppilot/README.md`](preppilot/README.md), [`docs/ARCHITECTURE.md`](docs/ARCHITECTURE.md), [`docs/PITCH_FACTS.md`](docs/PITCH_FACTS.md), [`PROJECT_REPORT.md`](PROJECT_REPORT.md). The pitch deck is in `docs/PrepPilot_Pitch_Deck.pptx`.

## Honest limits
- The verified question bank is small (154). Other chapters use AI-generated, self-checked questions, which need an AI key.
- No video lessons, and no real-student study yet.
- It's a single-server prototype; learner data lives in a local SQLite file.

## Credits
- **JEE Advanced questions:** JEEBench (Arora et al., EMNLP 2023), MIT licence. See `docs/JEEBENCH_LICENSE.txt` and `docs/THIRD_PARTY_NOTICES.md`.
- **Libraries** (from cdnjs): Three.js, Chart.js, KaTeX, pdf.js, mammoth, JSZip. **Fonts** from Google Fonts.
- **Study buddy art** (Pip, Mika, Leo): original characters, generated with Canva's AI image generator.

================================================================================
FILE: preppilot/README.md

# PrepPilot: adaptive quiz bot for JEE Advanced and NEET

The pure Python engine selects practice. Optional Gemini and Groq provide content and tutoring.

## Daily tools

Home shows your UTC-day answer/review count and streak. Preferences lets you set
a daily goal (1–200; default 20) and optional exam date. The suggested day plan
uses chapter learning gain and due reviews; it starts nothing automatically.
Expand Chapter heatmap to choose a chapter. Unassessed tiles stay faded.
Read aloud appears on quiz questions/explanations when speech synthesis is available.
Session summary offers Save as image for a local PNG download.

## Run

```bash
pip install -r requirements.txt
# PowerShell: $env:GEMINI_API_KEY="..."   (optional: without it the quiz uses the verified bank only)
uvicorn main:app --reload
```

Open http://localhost:8000. The landing page is at `/`, sign-in at `/login`, and the quiz app at `/app` (`/app?demo=1` gives short focus blocks).

After the first sign-in, the setup page at `/setup` asks for:
- class or exam,
- subjects,
- starting difficulty (just starting / comfortable / confident, which seeds the ability model at −1 / 0 / +1),
- focus-block length (25/40/60 min; breaks scale to ¼ and ⅜ of the block),
- explanation style (step by step / short / analogies; used in the Gemini prompts),
- timed mode on or off.

Choices are saved in the browser. You can reopen the page any time from **⚙ Preferences** in the account menu.

Accounts are stored in `users.json`, with passwords salted and hashed (PBKDF2-SHA256), never in plain text. Login tokens live in memory, so a server restart signs everyone out. "Continue as guest" skips sign-up. The quiz API itself is not locked behind login, which is fine for a demo but must be added before real users.

Optional: `GEMINI_MODEL` (default `gemini-3.5-flash`; `gemini-flash-lite-latest` is the automatic fallback).

## Files

| File | What it does |
|---|---|
| `engine.py` | Student model + question selection + simulation. `python engine.py` runs the self-check. |
| `syllabus.py` | Chapter map for Class 1 to Class 12 (CBSE / NCERT aligned), JEE Advanced and NEET. `python syllabus.py` lists every level. |
| `questions.json` | Verified question bank. Add questions here: `id, chapter, b, q, options, answer, hints, explanation`. |
| `main.py` | FastAPI server, sessions, Gemini question generation and explanations. |
| `index.html` | Chat UI, "Why this question" panel, dashboard, simulation. |
| `buddy/` | Study-buddy pictures, `<name>-<idle\|happy\|sad>.png`, served at `/buddy/<file>`. |

## How the adaptivity works

1. **Student model (1-parameter IRT / Elo).** Each chapter has an ability θ. Each question has a difficulty b. P(correct) = 1 / (1 + e^-(θ - b)).
2. **Response patterns change the update weight.**
   - Confident and wrong: a misconception. Bigger penalty, and the chapter gets flagged.
   - Answered in under 3 s: treated as a guess, so it counts for less.
   - Correct but slow (over 150 s): weaker evidence of mastery.
   - Each hint used lowers the credit for a correct answer.
3. **Hierarchical prior.** An unseen chapter starts at its subject's ability. Every answer nudges the subject, so the model has an estimate for all chapters.
4. **Chapter choice.** Score = weakness for the target exam + exploration bonus + misconception boost − penalty for a chapter just asked. Highest score wins.
5. **Difficulty choice.** Pick the question whose P(correct) is about 60% (desirable difficulty).
6. **After a miss.** The next question is an easier follow-up in the same chapter.
7. **Item calibration.** Verified questions' b values drift with real answers.
8. **AI questions.** For chapters without a close-enough bank question, Gemini writes one at the target difficulty. A second, independent solve must agree (it may answer "no option is correct"). Questions are prefetched in the background so the student doesn't wait. Students can report a specific answered question: it is hidden only for that session, the report is recorded in memory, and learner progress is recalculated without that answer. Shared bank questions remain available to other learners.

## "Understands how you fail, not just how often"

All logic is in `engine.py` and covered by its self-check.

- **Error-type diagnosis** (`diagnose`). Each wrong answer is classified from the response pattern, and each type gets a different fix:
  - **Time pressure:** the timer ran out, or more than 85% of it was used.
  - **Careless slip:** the student had the right option and switched away from it, or the model expected them to know it (P ≥ 0.65) and they rushed.
  - **Trap option:** marked "sure" but wrong.
  - **Concept gap:** everything else.

  Concept gaps and traps get an easier follow-up question; slips and time errors don't.
- **Fatigue and frustration** (`wellbeing`):
  - Three misses in a row means frustrated. The next question is a confidence-builder from a strong chapter, at about 85% chance of success.
  - At least two of these together means tired: accuracy dropping 30 points or more, answers 50% slower, repeated skips, or lots of answer switching. The bot suggests a break and switches to an easier, different topic.
- **Confidence calibration and negative marking** (`calibration`): accuracy for each confidence level ("sure", "unsure", "guessing"), and the expected marks of answering at that level under +3/−1 (JEE) or +4/−1 (NEET). The bot advises skipping when guessing loses marks; break-even is 25% for JEE and 20% for NEET.
- **Predicted score** (`predicted_score`): expected % of max marks on a paper with a mix of easy and hard questions. It is updated after every answer, and also shows how much skipping negative-value questions adds.
- **Highest-payoff chapters** (`best_gains`): where extra ability adds the most expected marks.
- **Timed exam mode:** the ⏱ toggle in the header gives each question a countdown (JEE 150 s, NEET 60 s, classes 90 s) and auto-submits at zero.

## Study rhythm: focus blocks and mandatory breaks

- **40-minute focus blocks.** The header shows a countdown ("🎯 Focus 32:10"). When it runs out, the current question can still be finished. After that, a full-screen break screen with a countdown and rest tips locks the quiz.
- **Break length.** The break is 10 minutes, or 15 if the block ended with fatigue or frustration signals (`engine.wellbeing`).
- **The server enforces it.** `/api/next` returns HTTP 423 during a break, so it can't be clicked away. The break state is stored in the browser, so reloading the page or switching class doesn't skip it either.
- **Early breaks.** "☕ Break" starts one at any time. The "you seem tired" nudge also offers "Take my break now".
- **Demo mode.** Open `http://localhost:8000/?demo=1` for 2-minute blocks and 30–45-second breaks, so judges can see a full cycle.
- The timings are in `RHYTHM` in `main.py`.

## Levels

Pick a class (1 to 12) or exam (JEE Advanced, NEET) from the header. The left sidebar lists every subject and chapter at that level with live readiness. Click a chapter to practise only that chapter, "Practise" on a subject for only that subject, or "All subjects" to let the engine choose.

- Classes 1 to 5: Maths, English, EVS. Classes 6 to 10: Maths, Science, Social Science, English. Classes 11 and 12: Physics, Chemistry, Maths, Biology.
- Readiness target: class level for Classes 1 to 10, board/NEET level for Classes 11, 12 and NEET, Advanced level for JEE.
- Verified bank questions apply to Class 11, Class 12, JEE and NEET. Every other level needs `GEMINI_API_KEY` (questions are generated per level, so Class 4 "Fractions" and Class 7 "Fractions and Decimals" stay age-appropriate).

## Question bank and answer checking

`questions.json` holds 80 original questions (10 per chapter in 8 chapters), written on common JEE Main / NEET past-paper patterns: same concepts and traps, new wording and numbers. They are not copied from past papers, books or Testbook, which are copyrighted.

`python verify_bank.py` re-checks every answer key:
- 54 numeric questions: the answer is recomputed from the question's data, and exactly one option must equal it.
- 26 conceptual questions: an independent review names the correct option text, which must match the key.
- Any question missing from both lists fails.

The server runs the same check at startup and drops any question that fails, so items that fail these checks are excluded (passing is not a correctness guarantee). When you add a question, add its check to `verify_bank.py` too.

## Real past-paper questions (JEEBench importer)

```bash
python import_jeebench.py
```

Downloads [JEEBench](https://github.com/dair-iitd/jeebench) (MIT license, EMNLP 2023): JEE Advanced 2016–2023 with official answer keys. Writes `questions_imported.json`, which the server loads next to `questions.json`.

- Imports single-correct MCQs only: 74 of 110. Skips 15 whose options don't split into (A)–(D) and 21 List-I/List-II tables. Multi-correct, integer and numeric types aren't supported by the quiz UI.
- Chapters are assigned by Gemini (keyword fallback without a key).
- Answer keys are the official ones (`"key_source": "official"`), and `verify_bank.py` checks their structure.
- Difficulty starts at b = 1.8 (Advanced). The engine serves them once a student is within reach, and real questions are preferred over AI ones.
- `--explain` asks Gemini for hints and a worked solution, kept only if Gemini reaches the official answer.
- In the app they're tagged "✓ Real past paper · JEE Adv 2019 Paper 1".
- Cite the JEEBench paper (see their README) in your pitch or credits.

## Adding questions (team task)

Use `b` from -2 (very easy) to +2.5 (hard JEE Advanced). Rough guide: -1.5 NCERT, -0.5 easy Mains/NEET, 0.5 Mains/NEET, 1.5 Advanced.
Chapter names must match `syllabus.py` exactly.


## Reliability fixes and checks

Reloading `/app` now resumes the browser's session, including learner progress, focus,
active question, and break countdown, when the level and setup preferences match.
Changing preferences starts a new session. Sessions and reports still live in memory:
a server restart resets them. This is not account-based permanent progress storage.

Question reports identify an exact answer, including on older result cards. Reporting
rebuilds the learner model and predicted-score history without that answer; it never
deletes a shared question. Repeated reports are safe.

Account creation serializes read/modify/write operations and atomically replaces the
JSON file. This protects concurrent sign-ups in the current single-process server.
Use one worker; a database is still needed before running multiple worker processes.
Operations that modify a quiz session are also serialized per session.

Run regression checks with FastAPI and httpx installed:

```bash
python -B test_regressions.py
python -B engine.py
python -B verify_bank.py
```

`test_regressions.py` uses temporary accounts and disables Gemini for deterministic
checks. It uses `.runtime/` or `.test-deps/` when present; a clean checkout uses the active Python environment.


## Starting a focus session

After sign-in or Continue as guest, the original setup page lets learners choose
class, subjects, starting difficulty, focus time (25/40/60 minutes), and other preferences.
Choose a chapter opens `/study`. The first Start quiz creates the session and starts the selected timer.
Picking another chapter later keeps the same session (learner model, history, timer) and only switches its focus.
There is no extra start overlay. Reloading the quiz resumes progress and the existing timer.
Changing preferences starts a new learner, but a running break carries over, so setup can't be used to skip it.
New sessions ignore browser-supplied clock values. Breaks start after the focus period, once the current question finishes.
Pages are served with `Cache-Control: no-cache`, so a normal reload always picks up edits.


## Study space

Setup now leads to `/study`, a subject and chapter dashboard. Learners choose a
subject, search its chapters, and click Start quiz. The quiz opens focused on that
chapter and the selected focus timer begins then, not while browsing the dashboard.
Chapters without bank questions are disabled when Gemini is not configured.
The quiz account menu links back to Subjects & chapters; the dashboard offers
Resume quiz when a matching browser session exists.

## Your own question papers (Google Sheets or CSV)

`/papers` (linked from the study space and the account menu) turns a teacher's or student's own questions into an adaptive quiz.

**One box.** Paste the questions the way you'd write a question paper, paste a Google Sheets link, or upload a **PDF, Word (.docx), PowerPoint (.pptx)**, `.txt` or `.csv`. Files are read in the browser with pdf.js, mammoth.js and JSZip from cdnjs, and their text fills the box (slides in order). A scanned PDF or picture-only slides have no text, so they get a clear message; old `.ppt`/`.doc` files must be re-saved as `.pptx`/`.docx`. The format is detected automatically and the name is optional.

```
Chapter: Laws of Motion
1. A 2 kg body has a net force of 10 N. Its acceleration is
(1) 20 m/s² (2) 5 m/s² (3) 0.2 m/s² (4) 12 m/s²
Answer: (2)
```

- Options can be `A)`–`D)`, `(a)`–`(d)` or `(1)`–`(4)`, written on separate lines or on one line, including the question's own line.
- The answer can be `B`, `(2)`, `b.` or the option's text.
- Optional lines: `Chapter:`, `Subject:`, `Explanation:`, `Hint:` and `Difficulty:`. A `Chapter:` line before the first question applies to all of them.
- Google Sheets and CSV need the columns `question`, `option_a`…`option_d` and `answer` (template at `/api/papers/template.csv`). The sheet must be shared as **Anyone with the link: Viewer**.
- An **Answer key** section at the end (`1-B 2-C 3-(4)`) fills in answers by question number. Titles and instruction lines are ignored.
- Questions that can't be read are skipped and reported, e.g. "Question 3: add a line like Answer: B".

How it works (`papers.py`):
- The server only ever requests a `docs.google.com` CSV-export URL that it builds from the sheet ID. It never fetches an arbitrary URL. Private sheets get a clear "share it" message.
- Every row is validated: four distinct options and a resolvable answer. Bad rows are skipped and reported by row number. The limits are 500 questions and 2 MB.
- The paper becomes its own level (`paper:<id>`). Chapters from the sheet are tracked by the same ability model, and difficulty seeds each question's `b`. The paper's questions are the only ones used, never AI-generated ones.
- Papers are saved in `preppilot/papers/<id>.json` (git-ignored). The browser remembers its papers in `localStorage`, and they appear in the quiz's level picker under "My question papers".
- Anyone with a paper's ID can quiz on it. There are no per-account papers yet.

## User journey

`/` landing → `/login` → first visit only: `/setup` (optionally a 5-question placement check in `/app?placement=1`) → **`/study`, the home hub** → quiz `/app` → **End session** summary → back to the hub. Returning users go straight to the hub; preferences are saved per account and restored at sign-in.

- **Home hub (`/study`)**:
  - a **Continue** card (resumes the last session, from `/api/brief`),
  - a **Recommended next** card: the chapter with the biggest expected score gain (`engine.best_gains`). Without AI it only picks chapters that have questions.
  - subject cards with chapter lists,
  - **My question papers** cards.
- **Placement check**: 5 medium questions rotating across the chosen subjects. Each answer moves the whole subject's prior, not just 30% of the chapter's change. Afterwards the starting level per subject is shown and the student goes to the hub.
- **Quiz (`/app`)**: a slim header with 🏠 Home, one **📖 chapter menu**, ⏱ Timed, the focus clock, Break and **✓ End session**. The quiz page no longer has a level picker or a permanent sidebar.
- **Session summary** (`/api/summary`): questions answered, accuracy, time, predicted score change, before→after readiness per chapter, the top mistake pattern with its fix, and the next recommended chapter.
- **After a break**: "Continue <chapter>" or "Switch to <recommended>", instead of auto-continuing.
- **Progress page (`/progress`)**: predicted score and its trend, session stats, subject and chapter readiness, mistake patterns, confidence vs reality, highest-payoff chapters, and the adaptive-vs-static simulation (moved here from the quiz).

## Study buddy

A painted chibi buddy floats in the bottom-right corner of the quiz to keep the mood up. Pick from **Pip**, **Mika** or **Leo** with the ⇄ button; the choice is remembered in the browser.

- **After each answer**: right answers swap to the happy picture with a spin-jump and sparkles ("Very good, you got that right!"); 3 or more in a row makes the buddy dance. Wrong or timed-out answers swap to the sad picture with a droop and a reassuring line ("Don't worry, you'll get it next time.").
- **Click it**: cycles a spin-jump, squish, giggle and dance with a short line. It is a real button, so Tab + Enter works too.
- **Idle**: a soft breathing bob with a slight sway, and it leans a little toward the pointer. All three pose pictures stay stacked, so mood changes crossfade instead of swapping. Reactions play on a separate layer above the idle motion, so they start and end without snapping. All motion stops under `prefers-reduced-motion`.
- **Move it anywhere**: drag it with a mouse, finger or pen (it swings with the drag and bounces on landing), or focus it and use the arrow keys (Shift for bigger steps). The spot is remembered per browser, the speech bubble flips to whichever side has room, and **Options → Put buddy back in the corner** resets it.
- **Art**: original characters made with Canva's AI image generator; backgrounds removed and poses aligned locally, saved at 3× display size. Lines live in `CHEERS` in `index.html`. Leo has no sad pose yet, so `leo-sad.png` is a copy of his idle picture. After replacing pictures, bump the `?v=` number in `index.html` so browsers fetch the new ones.


## Placement and PDF regression checks

`python -B test_regressions.py` now runs 18 API regression tests. Placement requests
start a check rather than resuming an ordinary quiz; reloading an active check keeps
its progress. Reports preserve placement-specific subject updates and reopen the
missing assessment slot, including after the check has completed.

With Node.js installed, run `node test_pdf_import.js` to check the browser's actual
PDF text extractor against fragmented options, wrapped questions and page boundaries.
The test uses PDF.js-style text items without network access. It does not add OCR
or guarantee reading order for multi-column/scanned PDFs. CI runs this check too.


## Choosing Gemini or Groq

Setup and the quiz header include an AI provider selector: Gemini, Groq, or Bank only.
Options without a server key are disabled. Switching in the quiz preserves its learner
model, current question and break timer. Generated-question caches and pending jobs
are separated by provider; errors do not silently send content to another provider.
Question generation, second-solve checking, explanations and doubts use the selected
provider. Uploaded papers continue to use only their uploaded questions.

Set the required key(s) in the server terminal, then restart the server:

```powershell
$env:GEMINI_API_KEY="your-gemini-key"  # optional
$env:GROQ_API_KEY="your-groq-key"      # optional
$env:GROQ_MODEL="llama-3.3-70b-versatile"  # optional override
```

```bash
export GROQ_API_KEY="your-groq-key"
export GROQ_MODEL="llama-3.3-70b-versatile"  # optional override
```

Do not enter keys in the browser or commit them. `.env.example` is reference only;
process environment variables are used. With no saved preference, the default is
Groq when configured, then Gemini, otherwise Bank only. Configured does not mean
that the key, quota or model has been validated. A provider outage still falls back
to available bank questions; tutoring displays an unavailable message.

Groq uses its [chat completions endpoint](https://console.groq.com/docs/api-reference)
and JSON-object mode for generation/checking. The configured model must support
that mode; see [Groq models](https://console.groq.com/docs/models). There is no new
Python dependency. The optional JEEBench import utility retains its Gemini-specific
classification/explanation calls.

Run `python -B test_ai_providers.py` from `preppilot/` for offline transport,
provider selection, failure handling, cache separation and session-preservation tests.
These use synthetic keys and mocked responses, not live provider requests.


### Private local launcher

`python start_local.py` loads the optional, Git-ignored `QUIZBOT/.env.local` before
starting the server. It accepts only GEMINI_API_KEY, GEMINI_MODEL, GROQ_API_KEY,
GROQ_MODEL and GOOGLE_CLIENT_ID; values are not printed. Direct uvicorn startup continues to use process
environment variables. Keep `.env.local` private and out of upload packages.


## Interface cleanup (2026-10-31)

The study hub orders Continue/Recommended, subjects, chapters, then personal papers.
The quiz header prioritizes navigation, chapter and focus time. Options contains timed
mode, AI provider, buddy visibility, early break and session summary. Buddy visibility
is remembered in browser storage. Initial readiness is labelled Building rather than
a definite percentage; score estimates are hidden until five answers and chapter
readiness until three. These are display thresholds, not statistical validation.


## Durable storage and account access (2026-10-31)

SQLite (`preppilot.sqlite3`) now stores accounts, preferences, sessions, reports,
authentication and owned papers. Sign in again after the upgrade; existing JSON
accounts keep their passwords. Returning users can resume after a restart or sign in
from a second browser. Guests also have isolated saved sessions, accessible through
their browser cookie. JSON paper files from the old version are preserved and require
re-import to attach ownership. Older memory-only sessions cannot be migrated.

The frontend restores server state via `account.js`; browser caches are not identity.
Auth uses an HttpOnly 30-day cookie, with hashed tokens in the database. All learner
API reads/writes enforce ownership. Set `PREPPILOT_SECURE_COOKIES=1` when deploying
behind HTTPS. Continue using one worker because the active learner cache is in-process.

Run `python -B test_persistence.py` for 11 tests of restart recovery, access isolation,
authentication, migration, archives and long-absence timer behavior. See the root README
and `docs/PERSISTENCE_MIGRATION.md` for current behavior; older milestone notes above
may describe the superseded in-memory implementation.


## Bookmarked questions

Every quiz question has a Bookmark toggle, including questions from uploaded papers.
The control becomes available only after answering or skipping. Saving an active,
unanswered question is rejected by the server. Home has a Bookmarked
questions navigation option opening `/bookmarks`: search saved questions, reveal an
answer/explanation for revision, or remove a bookmark. Question snapshots are owned
by the learner and stored in SQLite independently of their original paper. Guests
use the same feature through their guest cookie. This is personal revision, not a
secure graded-exam interface. `python -B test_bookmarks.py` runs seven regression tests.

## Live adaptation graph

The top of the quiz's side panel shows **Live adaptation**: a small chart redrawn (animated) after every answer. The line is your skill estimate (θ) in the chapter you just answered; each dot is that question's difficulty (b), green if you got it right and red if you missed. A one-line caption says what just happened, e.g. "✓ Right → Kinematics skill +0.21. Its next question aims a little harder." The fuller chart and the rest of the learner model stay on the Progress tab.

## Mistake review (spaced repetition)

Every missed question goes into the learner's review list (SQLite `reviews` table, per owner). It comes back after **1 day**; answering it right moves it to **3 days**, then **7 days**, and a third right answer marks it **mastered** and removes it. Any miss restarts at 1 day (`engine.next_review`, `REVIEW_DAYS`).

- **`/review`**: due questions as a mini quiz. Answers and explanations are withheld until the learner answers (`GET /api/reviews`, `POST /api/reviews/{question_id}`), and a question can't be answered before it is due.
- **Home hub**: a "Due for review" card when questions are due, or "All caught up · next in …" when some are scheduled.
- **Quiz**: the result line of a miss says "added to mistake review, back in 1 day". Reporting a question removes it from review.
- **Demo mode** (`/app?demo=1`): the gaps are minutes instead of days, so judges can see a question come back.
- Review answers don't change the adaptive learner model, because a review can come from a different class, exam or paper than the current session.

## Install as an app (PWA)

PrepPilot is an installable web app. On Chrome, Edge or Android an **Install app** button appears on the landing page and the home hub (or use the browser's install icon); on iPhone/iPad it explains Safari's Share → Add to Home Screen. Once installed it opens full-screen from its own icon (Pip), with shortcuts to Continue practice and Mistake review.

- `manifest.webmanifest`: name, colours, icons and shortcuts. Icons in `icons/` are cut from Pip's full-size art.
- `sw.js` (served from `/sw.js` so it controls every page): pages are network-first with the last good copy offline, then `offline.html`; static files and CDN libraries are cached and refreshed in the background. **`/api/` is never cached**, so progress, answers and accounts always come from the server. Bump `CACHE` in `sw.js` when the cached file list changes.
- `pwa.js`: registers the worker and wires every `[data-install]` button.
- Installing needs `localhost` or HTTPS. To install on a phone for a demo, expose the server over HTTPS (for example `cloudflared tunnel --url http://localhost:8000`) and open that link on the phone.

## Knowledge galaxy

The study hub (`/study`) and progress page (`/progress`) include **Your knowledge galaxy** (`galaxy.js`), an interactive Three.js 3D view of the learner's syllabus and chapter model:

- **Planets = subjects**: each subject sits at the centre of its own orbital system with a clickable subject label.
- **Moons = chapters**: each chapter orbits its subject planet.
  - **Colour = readiness**: smoothly interpolated from red (`0%` ready) through amber to green (`≥ 70%` ready); **faded** when `n == 0` (not assessed yet).
  - **Size = practice**: moon radius grows with the number of questions answered (`n`), capped so heavily practised chapters stay legible.
  - **Pulsing ring = misconception**: chapters with confident wrong answers (`flags > 0`) display a soft pulsing halo ring.
- **Interaction**: drag or touch to rotate, scroll or pinch to zoom, hover or tap a moon to inspect its readiness tooltip, and click a moon to ease the camera toward it and open a **Practise** button (`/app?new=1&focus=<chapter>`).
- **Accessibility and fallback**: a keyboard-reachable and toggleable **List view** renders every subject and chapter with readiness and a **Practise** link using `textContent`. When WebGL is unavailable or `prefers-reduced-motion: reduce` is enabled, the flat list is shown automatically instead of the 3D canvas. Rendering starts lazily via `IntersectionObserver` and pauses when off-screen or when the tab is hidden.

## AI tutor on your notes (`/tutor`)

Learners paste or upload their own notes (PDF, Word, PowerPoint or text, read in the browser by `extract.js`). Notes stay in the browser (`localStorage`) and are sent to the chosen AI provider only for a request, capped at `NOTES_LIMIT` (30,000 characters) and fenced as study material so instructions inside them are ignored.

- **Explain concepts** (`POST /api/notes/explain`): up to 10 key concepts in the learner's explanation style, with gentle corrections of mistakes in the notes.
- **Ask a question** (`POST /api/notes/ask`): a chat grounded in the notes; the last 6 turns are sent for context, and it says when the notes don't cover something.
- **Quiz me** (`POST /api/notes/quiz`): 5, 10 or 15 MCQs written from the notes. A second, independent solve must agree with each answer key or the question is dropped. Survivors become an owned question paper (`key_source: "ai-notes"`, not verified, labelled AI-generated) that runs through the normal adaptive quiz.
- Needs Gemini or Groq on the server; with Bank only the page explains that. `test_notes.py` covers all of this offline with a fake AI.

## Account menu, Google sign-in and AI question checks

- **Account menu**: the avatar on the home hub (and any `[data-account-menu]` button, via `account.js`) opens a menu with every study page, *Check a question with AI*, Preferences and **Sign out** (guests: *Create an account* and *Leave guest session*). Keyboard: arrows, Esc.
- **Google sign-in**: set `GOOGLE_CLIENT_ID` (Google Cloud Console → APIs & Services → Credentials → OAuth client ID → Web application; add every origin you open the app from, e.g. `http://localhost:8000` and your tunnel's `https://…` URL, under *Authorized JavaScript origins*) in `.env.local` and start with `python start_local.py`. The button appears only when configured. The server verifies Google's ID token (audience, issuer, expiry, verified email) via Google's tokeninfo endpoint, then signs the learner in with the same cookie as password accounts; a verified Google email also signs in to an existing password account with that email. Note: a quick `trycloudflare.com` tunnel gets a new URL each run, which must be added as an origin again.
- **Check a question** (`/check`): pick one of your recently answered questions and optionally say why the key looks wrong. If the first AI solve disagrees with the key, a second independent solve checks it. Only if both agree on a different answer (or both find no correct option) is the question hidden for you. All of your attempts at that question are removed through evidence replay; other learners and the shared bank are unaffected. Official exam keys get a caution note. Every completed check is logged in the `complaints` table. `test_account_extras.py` covers this and Google sign-in offline.

- **Ask without notes**: the tutor's *Ask a question* tab also works with no notes. It then answers as a general tutor at the learner's class or exam level (from Preferences), and opens on that tab when no notes are saved.

## Sign-in and first-time preferences

First-time accounts choose their preferences before entering the study space. Once
saved, password and Google sign-in take them directly to Home (`/study`), including
after signing in from another browser. Accounts already signed in also skip the
landing/sign-in screen. Change settings through **profile avatar → Preferences**;
saving returns to Home (or an explicitly selected placement check).
Run `node test_signin.js` for offline sign-in routing checks.
